--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillingService.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillingService.h"

#include "billing/BillCalculator.h"
#include "common/RecordText.h"

#include <algorithm>

namespace barcodehub::billing {
namespace {

void clearError(std::string* error) {
    if (error) error->clear();
}

bool fail(std::string* error, const std::string& why) {
    if (error) *error = why;
    return false;
}

} // namespace

BillingService::BillingService(order::OrderManager& orders, BillRepository& bills,
                               ProductLookup lookup, PaymentMethodRegistry methods)
    : orders_(orders), bills_(bills), lookup_(std::move(lookup)), methods_(std::move(methods)) {}

bool BillingService::preview(const std::string& orderId, Bill& bill, std::string* error) const {
    clearError(error);
    order::Order order;
    if (!orders_.findOrder(orderId, order, error)) return false;

    std::vector<order::OrderDetail> details;
    if (!orders_.loadOrderDetails(orderId, details, error)) return false;
    if (details.empty()) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " has no products.");

    Bill result;
    result.customerName = order.customerName;
    result.orderId = order.id;
    result.paymentStatus = order::toString(order.status);
    for (const auto& detail : details) {
        order::ProductSnapshot product;
        if (!lookup_ || !lookup_(detail.productId, product))
            return fail(error, "Product " + detail.productId + " was not found.");
        BillLine line;
        line.productAcronym = product.acronym;
        line.unitPrice = product.unitPrice;
        line.taxPerUnit = product.taxPerUnit;
        line.discountPerUnit = product.discountPerUnit;
        line.quantity = detail.quantity;
        result.lines.push_back(line);
    }
    if (!BillCalculator::calculate(result.lines, result.totals, error)) return false;

    bill = std::move(result);
    return true;
}

bool BillingService::checkout(const std::string& orderId, const PaymentRequest& payment,
                              Bill& bill, double* change, std::string* error,
                              const BillAdjustments& adjustments) {
    clearError(error);
    if (change) *change = 0.0;

    order::Order order;
    if (!orders_.findOrder(orderId, order, error)) return false;
    if (order.status == order::OrderStatus::Paid) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " is already paid.");
    if (order.status == order::OrderStatus::Archived) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " is archived.");

    Bill result;
    if (!preview(orderId, result, error)) return false;

    // 0. bill-level discounts (Increment 3): seasonal (Seasons.txt) and special (at most 8 percent)
    const double seasonal = common::roundMoney(adjustments.seasonalDiscount);
    const double special = common::roundMoney(adjustments.specialDiscount);
    if (seasonal < 0 || special < 0) return fail(error, "Seasonal and special discounts cannot be negative.");
    if (seasonal + special > result.totals.payable + 0.005)
        return fail(error, "Seasonal and special discounts are larger than the bill.");
    {
        const double base = result.totals.withoutTax - result.totals.discount - seasonal;
        double units = 0; // the checkout screen rounds per unit, so allow half a paisa per unit
        for (const auto& line : result.lines) units += line.quantity;
        if (special > common::roundMoney(base * kMaxSpecialDiscountPercent / 100.0) + 0.01 + 0.005 * units)
            return fail(error, "The special discount cannot be more than 8 percent of the bill.");
    }
    result.seasonalDiscount = seasonal;
    result.specialDiscount = special;
    result.totals.payable = common::roundMoney(result.totals.payable - seasonal - special);

    // 1. payment rules of the chosen mode
    const IPaymentMethod* method = methods_.find(payment.mode);
    if (!method) return fail(error, "This payment mode is not available.");
    const PaymentResult paid = method->process(payment, result.totals.payable);
    if (!paid.ok) return fail(error, paid.error);

    // 2. Bill ID
    std::vector<Bill> existing;
    if (!bills_.loadAll(existing, error)) return false;
    result.date = common::currentDate();
    result.time = common::currentTime();
    result.id = BillRepository::nextBillId(result.date, existing, error);
    if (result.id.empty()) return false;
    // amount received / balance returned: cash = what was handed over; card and wallet pay exactly the bill
    result.amountReceived = payment.mode == order::PaymentMode::Cash ? common::roundMoney(payment.amountTendered)
                                                                     : result.totals.payable;
    result.balanceReturned = common::roundMoney(paid.change);

    // 3. order becomes paid (Order.txt)
    order::Order paidOrder = order;
    paidOrder.status = order::OrderStatus::Paid;
    paidOrder.paymentAmount = result.totals.payable;
    paidOrder.paymentMode = payment.mode;
    paidOrder.paymentMerchant = payment.mode == order::PaymentMode::Cash ? std::string() : payment.merchant;
    if (!orders_.updateOrder(paidOrder, error)) return false;
    result.paymentStatus = order::toString(paidOrder.status);

    // 4. bill is saved (Bill.txt); if that fails the order goes back to how it was
    if (!bills_.append(result, error)) {
        std::string ignored;
        orders_.updateOrder(order, &ignored);
        return false;
    }

    bill = std::move(result);
    if (change) *change = paid.change;
    return true;
}

bool BillingService::rebill(const std::string& orderId, const PaymentRequest& payment,
                            Bill& bill, double* change, std::string* error,
                            const BillAdjustments& adjustments) {
    clearError(error);
    if (change) *change = 0.0;

    order::Order order;
    if (!orders_.findOrder(orderId, order, error)) return false;
    if (order.status != order::OrderStatus::Paid) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " is not paid.");

    std::vector<Bill> existing;
    if (!bills_.loadAll(existing, error)) return false;
    const auto old = std::find_if(existing.begin(), existing.end(), [&](const Bill& b) { return b.orderId == orderId; });
    if (old == existing.end()) return fail(error, "Order " + orderId + " has no bill to update.");

    Bill result;
    if (!preview(orderId, result, error)) return false;
    const double seasonal = common::roundMoney(adjustments.seasonalDiscount);
    const double special = common::roundMoney(adjustments.specialDiscount);
    if (seasonal < 0 || special < 0) return fail(error, "Seasonal and special discounts cannot be negative.");
    if (seasonal + special > result.totals.payable + 0.005)
        return fail(error, "Seasonal and special discounts are larger than the bill.");
    {
        const double base = result.totals.withoutTax - result.totals.discount - seasonal;
        double units = 0; // the checkout screen rounds per unit, so allow half a paisa per unit
        for (const auto& line : result.lines) units += line.quantity;
        if (special > common::roundMoney(base * kMaxSpecialDiscountPercent / 100.0) + 0.01 + 0.005 * units)
            return fail(error, "The special discount cannot be more than 8 percent of the bill.");
    }
    result.seasonalDiscount = seasonal;
    result.specialDiscount = special;
    result.totals.payable = common::roundMoney(result.totals.payable - seasonal - special);

    const IPaymentMethod* method = methods_.find(payment.mode);
    if (!method) return fail(error, "This payment mode is not available.");
    const PaymentResult paid = method->process(payment, result.totals.payable);
    if (!paid.ok) return fail(error, paid.error);

    result.id = old->id;
    result.date = old->date;
    result.time = old->time;
    result.amountReceived = payment.mode == order::PaymentMode::Cash ? common::roundMoney(payment.amountTendered)
                                                                     : result.totals.payable;
    result.balanceReturned = common::roundMoney(paid.change);

    order::Order paidOrder = order;
    paidOrder.paymentAmount = result.totals.payable;
    paidOrder.paymentMode = payment.mode;
    paidOrder.paymentMerchant = payment.mode == order::PaymentMode::Cash ? std::string() : payment.merchant;
    if (!orders_.updateOrder(paidOrder, error)) return false;
    result.paymentStatus = order::toString(paidOrder.status);

    if (!bills_.replace(result, error)) {
        std::string ignored;
        orders_.updateOrder(order, &ignored);
        return false;
    }
    bill = std::move(result);
    if (change) *change = paid.change;
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillRecordCodec.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"

#include "common/RecordText.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <vector>

namespace barcodehub::billing {
namespace {

bool fail(std::string* error, const std::string& why) {
    if (error) *error = why;
    return false;
}

bool isBillId(const std::string& s) {
    return s.size() == 9 &&
           std::all_of(s.begin(), s.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c) != 0; });
}

bool isDate(const std::string& s) {   // YYYY-MM-DD
    return s.size() == 10 && s[4] == '-' && s[7] == '-' &&
           std::all_of(s.begin(), s.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c) || c == '-'; });
}

bool isTime(const std::string& s) {   // HH:MM:SS
    return s.size() == 8 && s[2] == ':' && s[5] == ':' &&
           std::all_of(s.begin(), s.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c) || c == ':'; });
}

} // namespace

int BillRecordCodec::fieldCount(const std::string& record) {
    std::vector<std::string> t;
    if (!common::splitRecord(record, t)) return -1;
    return static_cast<int>(t.size());
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------- Bill.txt
std::string BillRecordCodec::format(const Bill& b) {
    using common::encodeField;
    using common::formatMoney;
    return b.id + ' ' +
           encodeField(b.customerName) + ' ' +
           b.orderId + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.totals.withoutTax) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.totals.tax) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.totals.discount) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.totals.payable) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.amountReceived) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.balanceReturned) + ' ' +
           encodeField(b.paymentStatus) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.seasonalDiscount) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(b.specialDiscount) + ' ' +
           b.date + ' ' +
           b.time + " #";
}

bool BillRecordCodec::parse(const std::string& record, Bill& out, std::string* error) {
    std::vector<std::string> t;
    if (!common::splitRecord(record, t)) return fail(error, "record must end with '#'.");
    if (t.size() != 14) return fail(error, "expected 14 fields, found " + std::to_string(t.size()) + ".");
    if (!isBillId(t[0])) return fail(error, "Bill ID must contain exactly 9 digits.");

    Bill b;
    b.id = t[0];
    b.orderId = t[2];
    if (!common::decodeField(t[1], b.customerName) || !common::decodeField(t[9], b.paymentStatus))
        return fail(error, "bad text encoding.");
    if (!common::parseNumber(t[3], b.totals.withoutTax) || !common::parseNumber(t[4], b.totals.tax) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[5], b.totals.discount) || !common::parseNumber(t[6], b.totals.payable) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[7], b.amountReceived) || !common::parseNumber(t[8], b.balanceReturned) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[10], b.seasonalDiscount) || !common::parseNumber(t[11], b.specialDiscount))
        return fail(error, "a numeric field is not a number.");
    if (!isDate(t[12])) return fail(error, "bill date must be YYYY-MM-DD.");
    if (!isTime(t[13])) return fail(error, "bill time must be HH:MM:SS.");
    b.date = t[12];
    b.time = t[13];
    out = b;
    return true;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------- BillDetail.txt
std::string BillRecordCodec::formatDetail(const std::string& billId, const BillLine& line) {
    using common::formatMoney;
    return billId + ' ' +
           common::encodeField(line.productAcronym) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(line.unitPrice) + ' ' +
           common::formatQuantity(line.quantity) + ' ' +
           formatMoney(common::roundMoney(line.discountPerUnit * line.quantity)) + " #";
}

bool BillRecordCodec::parseDetail(const std::string& record, std::string& billId, BillLine& line,
                                  std::string* error) {
    std::vector<std::string> t;
    if (!common::splitRecord(record, t)) return fail(error, "record must end with '#'.");
    if (t.size() != 5) return fail(error, "expected 5 fields, found " + std::to_string(t.size()) + ".");
    if (!isBillId(t[0])) return fail(error, "Bill ID must contain exactly 9 digits.");

    BillLine l;
    double lineDiscount = 0;
    if (!common::decodeField(t[1], l.productAcronym)) return fail(error, "bad text encoding.");
    if (!common::parseNumber(t[2], l.unitPrice) || !common::parseNumber(t[3], l.quantity) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[4], lineDiscount))
        return fail(error, "a numeric field is not a number.");
    if (l.quantity <= 0) return fail(error, "quantity must be greater than zero.");
    l.discountPerUnit = lineDiscount / l.quantity;
    billId = t[0];
    line = l;
    return true;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------- old Increment 2 Bill.txt
bool BillRecordCodec::parseLegacy(const std::string& record, Bill& header, BillLine& line,
                                  std::string* error) {
    std::vector<std::string> t;
    if (!common::splitRecord(record, t)) return fail(error, "record must end with '#'.");
    if (t.size() != 12) return fail(error, "expected 12 fields, found " + std::to_string(t.size()) + ".");
    if (!isBillId(t[0])) return fail(error, "Bill ID must contain exactly 9 digits.");

    Bill h;
    BillLine l;
    h.id = t[0];
    h.orderId = t[2];
    if (!common::decodeField(t[1], h.customerName) || !common::decodeField(t[3], l.productAcronym) ||
        !common::decodeField(t[11], h.paymentStatus))
        return fail(error, "bad text encoding.");
    if (!common::parseNumber(t[4], l.unitPrice) || !common::parseNumber(t[5], l.discountPerUnit) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[6], l.quantity) || !common::parseNumber(t[7], h.totals.withoutTax) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[8], h.totals.tax) || !common::parseNumber(t[9], h.totals.discount) ||
        !common::parseNumber(t[10], h.totals.payable))
        return fail(error, "a numeric field is not a number.");
    h.amountReceived = h.totals.payable; // not stored in the old format
    header = h;
    line = l;
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/BillRepository.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"

#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>
#include <system_error>

namespace barcodehub::billing {

namespace {

// Writes `lines` to `path` through a temporary file, then swaps it in (no half-written file on a crash).
bool writeAtomically(const std::filesystem::path& path, const std::vector<std::string>& lines, std::string* error) {
    std::error_code ec;
    if (!path.parent_path().empty()) std::filesystem::create_directories(path.parent_path(), ec);
    const auto temp = path.string() + ".tmp";
    {
        std::ofstream out(temp, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!out) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot write temporary file: " + temp;
            return false;
        }
        for (const auto& line : lines) out << line << '\n';
        out.flush();
        if (!out) {
            if (error) *error = "Failed while writing " + path.filename().string() + " (disk full or no permission?).";
            std::filesystem::remove(temp, ec);
            return false;
        }
    }
    std::filesystem::rename(temp, path, ec);
    if (ec) {   // some platforms refuse to rename over an existing file
        ec.clear();
        std::filesystem::copy_file(temp, path, std::filesystem::copy_options::overwrite_existing, ec);
        std::error_code ignored;
        std::filesystem::remove(temp, ignored);
        if (ec) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot replace " + path.filename().string() + ": " + ec.message();
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

} // namespace

BillRepository::BillRepository(std::filesystem::path billFile, std::filesystem::path detailFile)
    : file_(std::move(billFile)),
      detailFile_(detailFile.empty() ? file_.parent_path() / "BillDetail.txt" : std::move(detailFile)) {}

bool BillRepository::loadAll(std::vector<Bill>& bills, std::string* error) const {
    bills.clear();
    std::error_code ec;
    auto findBill = [&](const std::string& id) {
        return std::find_if(bills.begin(), bills.end(), [&](const Bill& b) { return b.id == id; });
    };

    // 1. Bill.txt: one line per bill (old Increment 2 files have one line per product instead)
    if (std::filesystem::exists(file_, ec)) {
        std::ifstream in(file_);
        if (!in) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot open bill file: " + file_.string();
            return false;
        }
        std::string text;
        int lineNo = 0;
        while (std::getline(in, text)) {
            ++lineNo;
            if (text.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n") == std::string::npos) continue;   // blank line
            std::string why;
            const auto where = file_.filename().string() + " line " + std::to_string(lineNo) + ": ";
            if (BillRecordCodec::fieldCount(text) == 12) {   // old format: header + one product
                Bill header;
                BillLine line;
                if (!BillRecordCodec::parseLegacy(text, header, line, &why)) {
                    if (error) *error = where + why;
                    return false;
                }
                // the old format has no date/time: take the date from the bill id (YYMMDD###)
                header.date = "20" + header.id.substr(0, 2) + "-" + header.id.substr(2, 2) + "-" + header.id.substr(4, 2);
                header.time = "00:00:00";
                const auto same = findBill(header.id);
                if (same == bills.end()) {
                    header.lines.push_back(line);
                    bills.push_back(std::move(header));
                } else {
                    same->lines.push_back(line);
                }
                continue;
            }
            Bill bill;
            if (!BillRecordCodec::parse(text, bill, &why)) {
                if (error) *error = where + why;
                return false;
            }
            if (findBill(bill.id) != bills.end()) {
                if (error) *error = where + "Bill ID " + bill.id + " appears twice.";
                return false;
            }
            bills.push_back(std::move(bill));
        }
    }

    // 2. BillDetail.txt: the products of each bill
    if (std::filesystem::exists(detailFile_, ec)) {
        std::ifstream in(detailFile_);
        if (!in) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot open bill detail file: " + detailFile_.string();
            return false;
        }
        std::string text;
        int lineNo = 0;
        while (std::getline(in, text)) {
            ++lineNo;
            if (text.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n") == std::string::npos) continue;
            std::string billId, why;
            BillLine line;
            if (!BillRecordCodec::parseDetail(text, billId, line, &why)) {
                if (error) *error = detailFile_.filename().string() + " line " + std::to_string(lineNo) + ": " + why;
                return false;
            }
            const auto owner = findBill(billId);
            if (owner != bills.end()) owner->lines.push_back(line);   // lines of unknown bills are ignored
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool BillRepository::append(const Bill& bill, std::string* error) {
    if (bill.lines.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot save a bill without products.";
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Bill> all;
    if (!loadAll(all, error)) return false;
    for (const auto& existing : all) {
        if (existing.id == bill.id) {
            if (error) *error = "Bill ID " + bill.id + " already exists.";
            return false;
        }
    }
    all.push_back(bill);
    return writeAll(all, error);
}

bool BillRepository::replace(const Bill& bill, std::string* error) {
    if (bill.lines.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot save a bill without products.";
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Bill> all;
    if (!loadAll(all, error)) return false;
    const auto found = std::find_if(all.begin(), all.end(), [&](const Bill& b) { return b.id == bill.id; });
    if (found == all.end()) {
        if (error) *error = "Bill ID " + bill.id + " was not found.";
        return false;
    }
    *found = bill;   // same Bill ID, so Bill IDs stay unique; its old BillDetail lines are dropped
    return writeAll(all, error);
}

bool BillRepository::writeAll(const std::vector<Bill>& all, std::string* error) {
    // Both files are rewritten in the Increment 3 format (old bills are converted on the way).
    std::vector<std::string> billLines, detailLines;
    for (const auto& b : all) {
        billLines.push_back(BillRecordCodec::format(b));
        for (const auto& line : b.lines) detailLines.push_back(BillRecordCodec::formatDetail(b.id, line));
    }
    return writeAtomically(detailFile_, detailLines, error) && writeAtomically(file_, billLines, error);
}

std::string BillRepository::nextBillId(const std::string& date, const std::vector<Bill>& existing,
                                       std::string* error) {
    if (date.size() != 10 || date[4] != '-' || date[7] != '-') {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot generate a Bill ID from an invalid date.";
        return {};
    }
    const std::string prefix = date.substr(2, 2) + date.substr(5, 2) + date.substr(8, 2);   // YYMMDD
    int highest = 0;
    for (const auto& bill : existing) {
        if (bill.id.size() == 9 && bill.id.compare(0, 6, prefix) == 0) {
            try {
                highest = std::max(highest, std::stoi(bill.id.substr(6, 3)));
            } catch (...) {
            }
        }
    }
    if (highest >= 999) {
        if (error) *error = "Daily Bill ID limit reached (999 bills for this date).";
        return {};
    }
    std::ostringstream id;
    id << prefix << std::setw(3) << std::setfill('0') << (highest + 1);
    return id.str();
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/PaymentMethod.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/PaymentMethod.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::billing {
namespace {

bool validMerchant(const std::string& name) {
    const auto first = name.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
    if (first == std::string::npos) return false;
    return name.find('#') == std::string::npos;
}

PaymentResult electronic(const PaymentRequest& request, const char* what) {
    PaymentResult result;
    if (!validMerchant(request.merchant)) {
        result.error = std::string("Enter the ") + what + " name (for example ABL or JazzCash).";
        return result;
    }
    result.ok = true;
    return result;
}

} // namespace

PaymentResult CashPayment::process(const PaymentRequest& request, double payable) const {
    PaymentResult result;
    if (!std::isfinite(request.amountTendered) || request.amountTendered < 0.0) {
        result.error = "Invalid cash amount tendered.";
        return result;
    }
    if (request.amountTendered + 1e-9 < payable) {
        result.error = "Cash received is less than the amount payable.";
        return result;
    }
    result.ok = true;
    result.change = std::round((request.amountTendered - payable) * 100.0) / 100.0;
    return result;
}

PaymentResult CardPayment::process(const PaymentRequest& request, double) const {
    return electronic(request, "bank");
}

PaymentResult WalletPayment::process(const PaymentRequest& request, double) const {
    return electronic(request, "wallet");
}

PaymentMethodRegistry PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults() {
    PaymentMethodRegistry registry;
    registry.add(std::make_unique<CashPayment>());
    registry.add(std::make_unique<CardPayment>());
    registry.add(std::make_unique<WalletPayment>());
    return registry;
}

void PaymentMethodRegistry::add(std::unique_ptr<IPaymentMethod> method) {
    methods_.push_back(std::shared_ptr<const IPaymentMethod>(std::move(method)));
}

const IPaymentMethod* PaymentMethodRegistry::find(order::PaymentMode mode) const {
    const auto it = std::find_if(methods_.begin(), methods_.end(),
                                 [&](const auto& m) { return m->mode() == mode; });
    return it == methods_.end() ? nullptr : it->get();
}

} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/billing/SeasonRepository.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "billing/SeasonRepository.h"

#include <cctype>
#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>

namespace barcodehub::billing
{
    namespace
    {
        // YYYY-MM-DD with a real month (01-12) and day (01-31).
        bool isDate(const std::string &s)
        {
            if (s.size() != 10 || s[4] != '-' || s[7] != '-')
                return false;
            for (int i : {0, 1, 2, 3, 5, 6, 8, 9})
                if (!std::isdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(s[i])))
                    return false;
            const int month = std::stoi(s.substr(5, 2));
            const int day = std::stoi(s.substr(8, 2));
            return month >= 1 && month <= 12 && day >= 1 && day <= 31;
        }

        std::string trim(const std::string &s)
        {
            const auto a = s.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n");
            if (a == std::string::npos)
                return std::string();
            const auto b = s.find_last_not_of(" \t\r\n");
            return s.substr(a, b - a + 1);
        }
    } // namespace

    SeasonRepository::SeasonRepository(std::string filePath) : m_path(std::move(filePath)) {}

    std::string SeasonRepository::encode(const SeasonRecord &s)
    {
        std::ostringstream out;
        out << s.startDate << ' ' << s.endDate << ' ' << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << s.percent << ' '
            << (s.active ? "true" : "false") << '#';
        return out.str();
    }

    bool SeasonRepository::decode(const std::string &rawLine, SeasonRecord &out, std::string *error)
    {
        auto fail = [error](const std::string &msg)
        {
            if (error)
                *error = msg;
            return false;
        };
        std::string line = trim(rawLine);
        if (line.empty() || line.back() != '#')
            return fail("line must end with '#'");
        line.pop_back();

        std::istringstream in(line);
        std::string start, end, percent, active, extra;
        if (!(in >> start >> end >> percent >> active))
            return fail("expected 4 fields: startDate endDate percent isActive");
        if (in >> extra)
            return fail("too many fields");
        if (!isDate(start) || !isDate(end))
            return fail("dates must be YYYY-MM-DD");
        if (end < start) // ISO dates compare correctly as text
            return fail("end date is before start date");
        double pct = 0;
        try
        {
            size_t used = 0;
            pct = std::stod(percent, &used);
            if (used != percent.size())
                return fail("percent is not a number");
        }
        catch (...)
        {
            return fail("percent is not a number");
        }
        if (pct <= 0 || pct > 100)
            return fail("percent must be greater than 0 and at most 100");
        if (active != "true" && active != "false")
            return fail("isActive must be true or false");

        out = SeasonRecord{start, end, pct, active == "true"};
        return true;
    }

    std::vector<SeasonRecord> SeasonRepository::load(std::vector<std::string> *errors) const
    {
        std::vector<SeasonRecord> seasons;
        std::ifstream file(m_path);
        if (!file)
            return seasons; // no file yet: no seasons
        std::string line;
        int number = 0;
        while (std::getline(file, line))
        {
            ++number;
            if (trim(line).empty())
                continue;
            SeasonRecord s;
            std::string err;
            if (decode(line, s, &err))
                seasons.push_back(s);
            else if (errors)
                errors->push_back("Seasons.txt line " + std::to_string(number) + ": " + err);
        }
        return seasons;
    }

    bool SeasonRepository::save(const std::vector<SeasonRecord> &seasons, std::string *error) const
    {
        const std::string tmp = m_path + ".tmp";
        {
            std::ofstream out(tmp, std::ios::trunc);
            if (!out)
            {
                if (error)
                    *error = "cannot write " + tmp;
                return false;
            }
            for (const auto &s : seasons)
                out << encode(s) << '\n';
            if (!out)
            {
                if (error)
                    *error = "write failed for " + tmp;
                return false;
            }
        }
        std::remove(m_path.c_str()); // Windows rename() will not overwrite an existing file
        if (std::rename(tmp.c_str(), m_path.c_str()) != 0)
        {
            if (error)
                *error = "cannot replace " + m_path;
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
} // namespace barcodehub::billing


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/common/RecordText.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "common/RecordText.h"

#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>
#include <ctime>
#include <iomanip>
#include <locale>
#include <sstream>

namespace barcodehub::common {

std::string encodeField(const std::string& text) {
    if (text.empty()) return "-";
    if (text == "-") return "%2D";
    std::string out;
    for (const unsigned char c : text) {
        if (c == '%') out += "%25";
        else if (c == '_') out += "%5F";
        else if (c == '#') out += "%23";
        else if (std::isspace(c)) out += '_';
        else out += static_cast<char>(c);
    }
    return out;
}

bool decodeField(const std::string& token, std::string& out) {
    out.clear();
    if (token == "-") return true;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < token.size(); ++i) {
        const char c = token[i];
        if (c == '_') {
            out += ' ';
        } else if (c == '%') {
            if (i + 2 >= token.size()) return false;   // truncated %XX
            const std::string hex = token.substr(i + 1, 2);
            if (!std::isxdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(hex[0])) ||
                !std::isxdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(hex[1]))) return false;
            out += static_cast<char>(std::stoi(hex, nullptr, 16));
            i += 2;
        } else {
            out += c;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool splitRecord(const std::string& rawLine, std::vector<std::string>& tokens) {
    tokens.clear();
    std::string line = rawLine;
    while (!line.empty() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(line.back()))) line.pop_back();
    if (line.empty() || line.back() != '#') return false;
    line.pop_back();
    std::istringstream in(line);
    for (std::string token; in >> token;) tokens.push_back(token);
    return true;
}

bool parseNumber(const std::string& token, double& value) {
    try {
        std::size_t used = 0;
        value = std::stod(token, &used);
        return used == token.size() && std::isfinite(value);
    } catch (...) {
        return false;
    }
}

std::string formatMoney(double value) {
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2) << value;
    return out.str();
}

std::string formatQuantity(double value) {
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << value;
    std::string text = out.str();
    while (!text.empty() && text.back() == '0') text.pop_back();
    if (!text.empty() && text.back() == '.') text.pop_back();
    return text.empty() ? "0" : text;
}

double roundMoney(double value) {
    return std::round(value * 100.0) / 100.0;
}

std::string currentDate() {
    const std::time_t now = std::time(nullptr);
    std::tm local{};
#ifdef _WIN32
    localtime_s(&local, &now);
#else
    localtime_r(&now, &local);
#endif
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::put_time(&local, "%Y-%m-%d");
    return out.str();
}

std::string currentTime() {
    const std::time_t now = std::time(nullptr);
    std::tm local{};
#ifdef _WIN32
    localtime_s(&local, &now);
#else
    localtime_r(&now, &local);
#endif
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::put_time(&local, "%H:%M:%S");
    return out.str();
}

} // namespace barcodehub::common

#include "integration/ProductAdapter.h"

#include <string>

namespace barcodehub::integration {

order::ProductSnapshot toSnapshot(const Product& p) {
    order::ProductSnapshot s;
    s.productId = std::to_string(p.getProductID());   // Product keeps an int id, Order stores text
    s.barcode = p.getBarcode();
    s.acronym = p.getAcronym();
    s.name = p.getProductName();
    s.measurementUnit = p.getMeasurementUnitName();
    s.unitPrice = p.getUnitPrice();
    s.taxPerUnit = p.getTax();
    s.discountPerUnit = p.getDiscount();
    s.availableQuantity = p.getAvailableQuantity();   // int -> double
    return s;
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/order/Order.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "order/Order.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::order {
namespace {
bool digits(const std::string& s) {
    return !s.empty() && std::all_of(s.begin(), s.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c); });
}
bool fail(std::string* error, const char* message) {
    if (error) *error = message;
    return false;
}
}

std::string toString(OrderStatus status) {
    switch (status) {
    case OrderStatus::InProgress: return "in-progress";
    case OrderStatus::OnHold: return "on-hold";
    case OrderStatus::Paid: return "paid";
    case OrderStatus::Archived: return "archived";
    }
    return "unknown";
}

std::string toString(PaymentMode mode) {
    switch (mode) {
    case PaymentMode::Cash: return "cash";
    case PaymentMode::Card: return "card";
    case PaymentMode::Wallet: return "wallet";
    }
    return "unknown";
}

bool orderStatusFromString(const std::string& value, OrderStatus& status) {
    if (value == "in-progress") status = OrderStatus::InProgress;
    else if (value == "on-hold") status = OrderStatus::OnHold;
    else if (value == "paid") status = OrderStatus::Paid;
    else if (value == "archived") status = OrderStatus::Archived;
    else return false;
    return true;
}

bool paymentModeFromString(const std::string& value, PaymentMode& mode) {
    if (value == "cash") mode = PaymentMode::Cash;
    else if (value == "card") mode = PaymentMode::Card;
    else if (value == "wallet") mode = PaymentMode::Wallet;
    else return false;
    return true;
}

bool Order::isValid(std::string* error) const {
    if (id.size() != 8 || !digits(id)) return fail(error, "Order ID must contain eight digits.");
    if (date.size() != 10 || date[4] != '-' || date[7] != '-' ||
        !digits(date.substr(0, 4)) || !digits(date.substr(5, 2)) || !digits(date.substr(8, 2)))
        return fail(error, "Date must use YYYY-MM-DD.");
    if (time.size() != 8 || time[2] != ':' || time[5] != ':' ||
        !digits(time.substr(0, 2)) || !digits(time.substr(3, 2)) || !digits(time.substr(6, 2)))
        return fail(error, "Time must use HH:MM:SS.");
    if (customerName.empty()) return fail(error, "Customer name is required.");
    if (!std::isfinite(paymentAmount) || paymentAmount < 0.0)
        return fail(error, "Payment amount must be non-negative.");
    if (status == OrderStatus::Paid && paymentAmount <= 0.0)
        return fail(error, "Paid orders require a positive payment amount.");
    if (toString(status) == "unknown" || toString(paymentMode) == "unknown")
        return fail(error, "Unknown order status or payment method.");
    if (error) error->clear();
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::order

#include "order/OrderDetail.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::order {
bool OrderDetail::isValid(std::string* error) const {
    if (orderId.size() != 8 || !std::all_of(orderId.begin(), orderId.end(),
            [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c); })) {
        if (error) *error = "Detail needs an eight-digit Order ID.";
        return false;
    }
    if (productId.empty() || productBarcode.empty() ||
        productId.find_first_of(" \t\r\n#") != std::string::npos ||
        productBarcode.find_first_of(" \t\r\n#") != std::string::npos) {
        if (error) *error = "Detail needs a valid product ID and barcode.";
        return false;
    }
    if (!std::isfinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0.0) {
        if (error) *error = "Detail quantity must be positive.";
        return false;
    }
    if (error) error->clear();
    return true;
}
} // namespace barcodehub::order

#include "order/OrderManager.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cmath>
#include <ctime>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <limits>
#include <sstream>
#include <system_error>

namespace barcodehub::order {
namespace {

std::string currentDate() {
    const std::time_t now = std::time(nullptr);
    std::tm local{};
#ifdef _WIN32
    localtime_s(&local, &now);
#else
    localtime_r(&now, &local);
#endif
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::put_time(&local, "%Y-%m-%d");
    return out.str();
}

std::string currentTime() {
    const std::time_t now = std::time(nullptr);
    std::tm local{};
#ifdef _WIN32
    localtime_s(&local, &now);
#else
    localtime_r(&now, &local);
#endif
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::put_time(&local, "%H:%M:%S");
    return out.str();
}

bool isWholeNumber(double value) {
    return std::isfinite(value) && std::fabs(value - std::round(value)) < 1e-9;
}

bool hasWhitespace(const std::string& text) {
    return std::any_of(text.begin(), text.end(),
                       [](unsigned char c) { return std::isspace(c) != 0; });
}

double roundMoney(double value) {
    return std::round(value * 100.0) / 100.0;
}

void clearError(std::string* error) {
    if (error) error->clear();
}

bool ensureParentDirectory(const std::filesystem::path& file, std::string* error) {
    std::error_code ec;
    if (!file.parent_path().empty()) {
        std::filesystem::create_directories(file.parent_path(), ec);
    }
    if (ec) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot create data directory: " + ec.message();
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// ---- Field encoding -------------------------------------------------------
// Records are one line each, fields separated by single spaces, line ended by '#'
// (same style as Product.txt). To keep that rule true for names containing spaces,
// text fields are escaped: ' ' -> '_', '_' -> %5F, '%' -> %25, '#' -> %23,
// and an empty (optional) field is written as '-'.
std::string encodeField(const std::string& text) {
    if (text.empty()) return "-";
    if (text == "-") return "%2D";
    std::string out;
    for (const unsigned char c : text) {
        if (c == '%') out += "%25";
        else if (c == '_') out += "%5F";
        else if (c == '#') out += "%23";
        else if (std::isspace(c)) out += '_';
        else out += static_cast<char>(c);
    }
    return out;
}

bool decodeField(const std::string& token, std::string& out) {
    out.clear();
    if (token == "-") return true;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < token.size(); ++i) {
        const char c = token[i];
        if (c == '_') {
            out += ' ';
        } else if (c == '%') {
            if (i + 2 >= token.size()) return false;   // truncated %XX
            const std::string hex = token.substr(i + 1, 2);
            if (hex.size() != 2 || !std::isxdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(hex[0])) ||
                !std::isxdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(hex[1]))) return false;
            out += static_cast<char>(std::stoi(hex, nullptr, 16));
            i += 2;
        } else {
            out += c;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

std::string formatQuantity(double value) {
    std::ostringstream out;
    out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(3) << value;
    std::string text = out.str();
    while (!text.empty() && text.back() == '0') text.pop_back();
    if (!text.empty() && text.back() == '.') text.pop_back();
    return text.empty() ? "0" : text;
}

bool parseNumber(const std::string& token, double& value) {
    try {
        std::size_t used = 0;
        value = std::stod(token, &used);
        return used == token.size() && std::isfinite(value);
    } catch (...) {
        return false;
    }
}

// Splits one record line into tokens. The line must end with '#'.
bool splitRecord(const std::string& rawLine, std::vector<std::string>& tokens) {
    tokens.clear();
    std::string line = rawLine;
    while (!line.empty() && std::isspace(static_cast<unsigned char>(line.back()))) line.pop_back();
    if (line.empty() || line.back() != '#') return false;
    line.pop_back();
    std::istringstream in(line);
    std::string token;
    while (in >> token) tokens.push_back(token);
    return true;
}

std::string lineError(const std::filesystem::path& path, int lineNo, const std::string& why) {
    return path.filename().string() + " line " + std::to_string(lineNo) + ": " + why;
}

bool replaceFile(const std::filesystem::path& temp,
                 const std::filesystem::path& destination,
                 std::string* error) {
    std::error_code ec;
    std::filesystem::rename(temp, destination, ec);
    if (!ec) return true;

    // Some platforms refuse to rename over an existing file: overwrite by copying instead
    // (the destination is never deleted first, so a failure cannot lose the old data).
    ec.clear();
    std::filesystem::copy_file(temp, destination,
                               std::filesystem::copy_options::overwrite_existing, ec);
    std::error_code ignored;
    std::filesystem::remove(temp, ignored);
    if (ec) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot replace data file: " + ec.message();
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool readOrdersFrom(const std::filesystem::path& path,
                    std::vector<Order>& orders,
                    std::string* error) {
    orders.clear();
    std::error_code existsError;
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(path, existsError)) return true;   // no file yet = no orders

    std::ifstream in(path);
    if (!in) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot open order file: " + path.string();
        return false;
    }

    std::string line;
    int lineNo = 0;
    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
        ++lineNo;
        std::vector<std::string> t;
        if (line.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n") == std::string::npos) continue;  // blank line
        if (!splitRecord(line, t)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "record must end with '#'.");
            return false;
        }
        if (t.size() != 9) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "expected 9 fields, found " + std::to_string(t.size()) + ".");
            return false;
        }

        Order order;
        order.id = t[0];
        order.date = t[1];
        order.time = t[2];
        if (!decodeField(t[3], order.customerName) ||
            !decodeField(t[4], order.customerContact) ||
            !decodeField(t[8], order.paymentMerchant)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "bad text encoding.");
            return false;
        }
        if (!orderStatusFromString(t[5], order.status)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "unknown order status '" + t[5] + "'.");
            return false;
        }
        if (!parseNumber(t[6], order.paymentAmount)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "payment amount is not a number.");
            return false;
        }
        if (!paymentModeFromString(t[7], order.paymentMode)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "unknown payment mode '" + t[7] + "'.");
            return false;
        }
        std::string why;
        if (!order.isValid(&why)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, why);
            return false;
        }
        for (const auto& existing : orders) {
            if (existing.id == order.id) {
                if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "duplicate Order ID " + order.id + ".");
                return false;
            }
        }
        orders.push_back(std::move(order));
    }
    return true;
}

bool readDetailsFrom(const std::filesystem::path& path,
                     std::vector<OrderDetail>& details,
                     std::string* error) {
    details.clear();
    std::error_code existsError;
    if (!std::filesystem::exists(path, existsError)) return true;

    std::ifstream in(path);
    if (!in) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot open order-detail file: " + path.string();
        return false;
    }

    std::string line;
    int lineNo = 0;
    while (std::getline(in, line)) {
        ++lineNo;
        std::vector<std::string> t;
        if (line.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n") == std::string::npos) continue;
        if (!splitRecord(line, t)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "record must end with '#'.");
            return false;
        }
        if (t.size() != 4) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "expected 4 fields, found " + std::to_string(t.size()) + ".");
            return false;
        }
        OrderDetail detail;
        detail.orderId = t[0];
        detail.productId = t[1];
        detail.productBarcode = t[2];
        if (!parseNumber(t[3], detail.quantity)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, "quantity is not a number.");
            return false;
        }
        std::string why;
        if (!detail.isValid(&why)) {
            if (error) *error = lineError(path, lineNo, why);
            return false;
        }
        details.push_back(std::move(detail));
    }
    return true;
}

bool writeOrdersTo(const std::filesystem::path& path,
                   const std::vector<Order>& orders,
                   std::string* error) {
    if (!ensureParentDirectory(path, error)) return false;
    const auto temp = path.string() + ".tmp";
    {
        std::ofstream out(temp, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!out) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot write temporary order file: " + temp;
            return false;
        }
        out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2);
        for (const auto& order : orders) {
            out << order.id << ' '
                << order.date << ' '
                << order.time << ' '
                << encodeField(order.customerName) << ' '
                << encodeField(order.customerContact) << ' '
                << toString(order.status) << ' '
                << order.paymentAmount << ' '
                << toString(order.paymentMode) << ' '
                << encodeField(order.paymentMerchant) << " #\n";
        }
        out.flush();
        if (!out) {
            std::error_code ignored;
            std::filesystem::remove(temp, ignored);
            if (error) *error = "Failed while writing order data (disk full or no permission?).";
            return false;
        }
    }
    return replaceFile(temp, path, error);
}

bool writeDetailsTo(const std::filesystem::path& path,
                    const std::vector<OrderDetail>& details,
                    std::string* error) {
    if (!ensureParentDirectory(path, error)) return false;
    const auto temp = path.string() + ".tmp";
    {
        std::ofstream out(temp, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!out) {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot write temporary order-detail file: " + temp;
            return false;
        }
        for (const auto& detail : details) {
            out << detail.orderId << ' '
                << detail.productId << ' '
                << detail.productBarcode << ' '
                << formatQuantity(detail.quantity) << " #\n";
        }
        out.flush();
        if (!out) {
            std::error_code ignored;
            std::filesystem::remove(temp, ignored);
            if (error) *error = "Failed while writing order-detail data (disk full or no permission?).";
            return false;
        }
    }
    return replaceFile(temp, path, error);
}

} // namespace

OrderManager::OrderManager(std::filesystem::path orderFile,
                           std::filesystem::path orderDetailFile)
    : orderFile_(std::move(orderFile)),
      orderDetailFile_(std::move(orderDetailFile)) {}

bool OrderManager::validateProduct(const ProductSnapshot& product, double quantity,
                                   std::string* error) const {
    clearError(error);
    if (product.productId.empty() || product.barcode.empty() || product.name.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Product ID, barcode, and name are required.";
        return false;
    }
    if (hasWhitespace(product.productId) || hasWhitespace(product.barcode)) {
        if (error) *error = "Product ID and barcode must not contain spaces.";
        return false;
    }
    if (!std::isfinite(quantity) || quantity <= 0.0 ||
        !std::isfinite(product.availableQuantity) || product.availableQuantity < 0.0) {
        if (error) *error = "Quantity and available stock must be valid positive values.";
        return false;
    }
    if (product.measurementUnit == "count" && !isWholeNumber(quantity)) {
        if (error) *error = "Count-based products require a whole-number quantity.";
        return false;
    }
    if (quantity > product.availableQuantity + 1e-9) {
        if (error) {
            std::ostringstream msg;
            msg << "Requested quantity exceeds available stock for " << product.name
                << " (available: " << formatQuantity(product.availableQuantity) << ").";
            *error = msg.str();
        }
        return false;
    }
    if (!std::isfinite(product.unitPrice) || product.unitPrice < 0.0 ||
        !std::isfinite(product.taxPerUnit) || product.taxPerUnit < 0.0 ||
        !std::isfinite(product.discountPerUnit) || product.discountPerUnit < 0.0) {
        if (error) *error = "Product price, tax, and discount must be non-negative.";
        return false;
    }
    if (product.measurementUnit != "count" && product.measurementUnit != "weight") {
        if (error) *error = "Measurement unit must be 'count' or 'weight'.";
        return false;
    }
    if (product.discountPerUnit > product.unitPrice + product.taxPerUnit + 1e-9) {
        if (error) *error = "Discount per unit cannot exceed the unit price plus tax for " + product.name + ".";
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool OrderManager::addToCart(const ProductSnapshot& product, double quantity,
                             std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    if (!validateProduct(product, quantity, error)) return false;

    const auto found = std::find_if(cart_.begin(), cart_.end(), [&](const CartItem& item) {
        return item.product.productId == product.productId;
    });

    if (found != cart_.end()) {
        const double combined = found->quantity + quantity;
        if (!validateProduct(product, combined, error)) return false;
        found->product = product;
        found->quantity = combined;
    } else {
        cart_.push_back(CartItem{product, quantity});
    }
    return true;
}

bool OrderManager::setCartQuantity(const std::string& productId, double newQuantity,
                                   std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    const auto found = std::find_if(cart_.begin(), cart_.end(), [&](const CartItem& item) {
        return item.product.productId == productId;
    });
    if (found == cart_.end()) {
        if (error) *error = "Product is not present in the cart.";
        return false;
    }
    if (!validateProduct(found->product, newQuantity, error)) return false;
    found->quantity = newQuantity;
    return true;
}

bool OrderManager::removeFromCart(const std::string& productId) {
    const auto oldSize = cart_.size();
    cart_.erase(std::remove_if(cart_.begin(), cart_.end(), [&](const CartItem& item) {
        return item.product.productId == productId;
    }), cart_.end());
    return cart_.size() != oldSize;
}

void OrderManager::clearCart() {
    cart_.clear();
}

const std::vector<CartItem>& OrderManager::cart() const noexcept {
    return cart_;
}

double OrderManager::subtotalBeforeTax() const noexcept {
    double total = 0.0;
    for (const auto& item : cart_) {
        total += item.product.unitPrice * item.quantity;
    }
    return roundMoney(total);
}

double OrderManager::totalTax() const noexcept {
    double total = 0.0;
    for (const auto& item : cart_) {
        total += item.product.taxPerUnit * item.quantity;
    }
    return roundMoney(total);
}

double OrderManager::totalDiscount() const noexcept {
    double total = 0.0;
    for (const auto& item : cart_) {
        total += item.product.discountPerUnit * item.quantity;
    }
    return roundMoney(total);
}

double OrderManager::totalPayable() const noexcept {
    return roundMoney(subtotalBeforeTax() + totalTax() - totalDiscount());
}

std::string OrderManager::generateOrderId(const std::string& date,
                                          const std::vector<Order>& existing,
                                          std::string* error) const {
    // ID layout: YYMM + daily sequence (01-99) + DD.
    // Example: 26110115 = November 2026, order 01 of the 15th.
    if (date.size() != 10 || date[4] != '-' || date[7] != '-') {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot generate Order ID from invalid date.";
        return {};
    }
    const std::string yearMonth = date.substr(2, 2) + date.substr(5, 2);
    const std::string day = date.substr(8, 2);
    int highestSequence = 0;

    for (const auto& order : existing) {
        if (order.id.size() == 8 &&
            order.id.substr(0, 4) == yearMonth &&
            order.id.substr(6, 2) == day) {
            try {
                highestSequence = std::max(highestSequence, std::stoi(order.id.substr(4, 2)));
            } catch (...) {
                // Ignore malformed legacy IDs; valid records are checked elsewhere.
            }
        }
    }

    if (highestSequence >= 99) {
        if (error) *error = "Daily Order ID limit reached (99 orders for this date).";
        return {};
    }

    std::ostringstream id;
    id << yearMonth << std::setw(2) << std::setfill('0')
       << (highestSequence + 1) << day;
    return id.str();
}

bool OrderManager::createOrder(const std::string& customerName,
                               const std::string& customerContact,
                               Order& createdOrder,
                               std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    if (cart_.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot create an order with an empty cart.";
        return false;
    }
    if (customerName.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Customer name is required.";
        return false;
    }

    std::vector<Order> orders;
    std::vector<OrderDetail> details;
    if (!loadOrders(orders, error) || !loadOrderDetails(details, error)) return false;

    Order order;
    order.date = currentDate();
    order.time = currentTime();
    order.id = generateOrderId(order.date, orders, error);
    if (order.id.empty()) return false;
    order.customerName = customerName;
    order.customerContact = customerContact;
    order.status = OrderStatus::InProgress;
    order.paymentAmount = 0.0;
    order.paymentMode = PaymentMode::Cash;
    order.paymentMerchant.clear();

    if (!order.isValid(error)) return false;

    std::vector<OrderDetail> newDetails = details;
    for (const auto& item : cart_) {
        newDetails.push_back(OrderDetail{
            order.id,
            item.product.productId,
            item.product.barcode,
            item.quantity
        });
    }

    orders.push_back(order);

    // Save detail rows first. If order saving fails, the extra detail rows are harmless
    // only if the application does not expose orphan details; rollback them immediately.
    if (!writeDetailsTo(orderDetailFile_, newDetails, error)) return false;
    if (!writeOrdersTo(orderFile_, orders, error)) {
        std::string rollbackError;
        writeDetailsTo(orderDetailFile_, details, &rollbackError);
        return false;
    }

    createdOrder = order;
    clearCart();
    return true;
}

std::string OrderManager::nextOrderId(std::string* error) const {
    std::vector<Order> existing;
    if (!loadOrders(existing, error)) return {};
    return generateOrderId(currentDate(), existing, error);
}

bool OrderManager::discardOrder(const std::string& orderId, std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    std::vector<Order> orders;
    std::vector<OrderDetail> details;
    if (!loadOrders(orders, error) || !loadOrderDetails(details, error)) return false;
    const auto it = std::find_if(orders.begin(), orders.end(), [&](const Order& order) {
        return order.id == orderId;
    });
    if (it == orders.end() ||
        (it->status != OrderStatus::InProgress && it->status != OrderStatus::OnHold)) {
        if (error) *error = "Only existing unpaid (in-progress or on-hold) orders may be discarded.";
        return false;
    }
    const auto oldDetails = details;
    orders.erase(it);
    details.erase(std::remove_if(details.begin(), details.end(), [&](const OrderDetail& detail) {
        return detail.orderId == orderId;
    }), details.end());
    if (!writeDetailsTo(orderDetailFile_, details, error)) return false;
    if (!writeOrdersTo(orderFile_, orders, error)) {
        std::string ignored;
        writeDetailsTo(orderDetailFile_, oldDetails, &ignored);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

bool OrderManager::updateOrder(const Order& order, std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    if (!order.isValid(error)) return false;

    std::vector<Order> orders;
    if (!loadOrders(orders, error)) return false;

    const auto found = std::find_if(orders.begin(), orders.end(), [&](const Order& existing) {
        return existing.id == order.id;
    });
    if (found == orders.end()) {
        if (error) *error = "Order ID was not found.";
        return false;
    }

    if (found->status == OrderStatus::Archived && order.status != OrderStatus::Archived) {
        if (error) *error = "An archived order cannot be reopened.";
        return false;
    }
    // Date and time belong to the moment of creation and must not be rewritten.
    Order updated = order;
    updated.date = found->date;
    updated.time = found->time;
    *found = updated;
    return writeOrdersTo(orderFile_, orders, error);
}

bool OrderManager::saveCartToOrder(const std::string& orderId,
                                   const std::string& customerName,
                                   const std::string& customerContact,
                                   std::string* error) {
    clearError(error);
    if (cart_.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Cannot save an order with an empty cart.";
        return false;
    }
    if (customerName.empty()) {
        if (error) *error = "Customer name is required.";
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Order> orders;
    std::vector<OrderDetail> details;
    if (!loadOrders(orders, error) || !loadOrderDetails(details, error)) return false;
    const auto found = std::find_if(orders.begin(), orders.end(), [&](const Order& o) {
        return o.id == orderId;
    });
    if (found == orders.end()) {
        if (error) *error = "Order ID was not found.";
        return false;
    }
    if (found->status == OrderStatus::Archived) {
        if (error) *error = "An archived order cannot be changed.";
        return false;
    }
    found->customerName = customerName;
    found->customerContact = customerContact;
    if (!found->isValid(error)) return false;

    const auto oldDetails = details;
    details.erase(std::remove_if(details.begin(), details.end(), [&](const OrderDetail& d) {
        return d.orderId == orderId;
    }), details.end());
    for (const auto& item : cart_) {
        details.push_back(OrderDetail{orderId, item.product.productId, item.product.barcode, item.quantity});
    }
    if (!writeDetailsTo(orderDetailFile_, details, error)) return false;
    if (!writeOrdersTo(orderFile_, orders, error)) {
        std::string ignored;
        writeDetailsTo(orderDetailFile_, oldDetails, &ignored);
        return false;
    }
    clearCart();
    return true;
}

bool OrderManager::loadOrders(std::vector<Order>& orders, std::string* error) const {
    clearError(error);
    return readOrdersFrom(orderFile_, orders, error);
}

bool OrderManager::loadOrderDetails(std::vector<OrderDetail>& details,
                                    std::string* error) const {
    clearError(error);
    return readDetailsFrom(orderDetailFile_, details, error);
}

bool OrderManager::loadOrderDetails(const std::string& orderId,
                                    std::vector<OrderDetail>& details,
                                    std::string* error) const {
    std::vector<OrderDetail> all;
    if (!loadOrderDetails(all, error)) return false;
    details.clear();
    for (auto& detail : all) {
        if (detail.orderId == orderId) details.push_back(std::move(detail));
    }
    return true;
}

bool OrderManager::findOrder(const std::string& orderId, Order& order,
                             std::string* error) const {
    std::vector<Order> orders;
    if (!loadOrders(orders, error)) return false;
    const auto found = std::find_if(orders.begin(), orders.end(), [&](const Order& o) {
        return o.id == orderId;
    });
    if (found == orders.end()) {
        if (error) *error = "Order ID was not found.";
        return false;
    }
    order = *found;
    return true;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::order


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/product/CategoryStorage.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "CategoryStorage.h"

#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>

using namespace std;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- small helper functions

// Removes spaces, tabs and line-end characters from both ends of a string.
static string trim(const string &s)
{
    int start = 0;
    int end = (int)s.size() - 1;
    while (start <= end && (s[start] == ' ' || s[start] == '\t' || s[start] == '\r' || s[start] == '\n'))
        start++;
    while (end >= start && (s[end] == ' ' || s[end] == '\t' || s[end] == '\r' || s[end] == '\n'))
        end--;
    return s.substr(start, end - start + 1);
}

// True if s is exactly two digits, e.g. "10".
static bool isTwoDigits(const string &s)
{
    return s.size() == 2 && s[0] >= '0' && s[0] <= '9' && s[1] >= '0' && s[1] <= '9';
}

// True if s contains the character c.
static bool contains(const string &s, char c)
{
    return s.find(c) != string::npos;
}

// Splits a line into words (separated by one or more spaces).
static vector<string> splitWords(const string &line)
{
    vector<string> words;
    stringstream in(line);
    string word;
    while (in >> word)
        words.push_back(word);
    return words;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- CategoryRepository

CategoryRepository::CategoryRepository(string filePath)
{
    m_path = filePath;
}

string CategoryRepository::validate(const ProductCategory &c)
{
    if (!isTwoDigits(c.id) || c.id == "00")
        return "Category ID must be 2 digits from 01 to 99.";
    if (trim(c.name) == "")
        return "Category name is required.";
    if (contains(c.name, '#'))
        return "Category name cannot contain '#'.";
    if (c.acronym == "" || contains(c.acronym, ' ') || contains(c.acronym, '#'))
        return "Category acronym must be one word without spaces or '#'.";
    return ""; // valid
}

string CategoryRepository::encode(const ProductCategory &c)
{
    return c.id + " " + trim(c.name) + " " + c.acronym + "#";
}

bool CategoryRepository::decode(const string &rawLine, ProductCategory &out, string *error)
{
    string line = trim(rawLine);

    // 1. the line must end with '#'
    if (line == "" || line[line.size() - 1] != '#')
    {
        if (error)
            *error = "line must end with '#'";
        return false;
    }
    line = line.substr(0, line.size() - 1); // remove the '#'

    // 2. we need at least 3 words: id, name (one or more words), acronym
    vector<string> words = splitWords(line);
    if (words.size() < 3)
    {
        if (error)
            *error = "expected 3 fields: categoryId categoryName categoryAcronym";
        return false;
    }

    // 3. first word = id, last word = acronym, everything in between = name
    ProductCategory c;
    c.id = words[0];
    c.acronym = words[words.size() - 1];
    for (int i = 1; i < (int)words.size() - 1; i++)
    {
        if (c.name != "")
            c.name += " ";
        c.name += words[i];
    }

    // 4. check the values
    string problem = validate(c);
    if (problem != "")
    {
        if (error)
            *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    out = c;
    return true;
}

bool CategoryRepository::exists() const
{
    ifstream file(m_path);
    return file.is_open();
}

vector<ProductCategory> CategoryRepository::load(vector<string> *errors) const
{
    vector<ProductCategory> categories;
    ifstream file(m_path);
    if (!file.is_open())
        return categories; // no file yet: no categories

    string line;
    int lineNumber = 0;
    while (getline(file, line))
    {
        lineNumber++;
        if (trim(line) == "")
            continue; // skip empty lines

        ProductCategory c;
        string why;
        if (!decode(line, c, &why))
        {
            if (errors)
                errors->push_back("PCategory.txt line " + to_string(lineNumber) + ": " + why);
            continue;
        }

        // the same id may appear only once (linear search through what we have read so far)
        bool repeated = false;
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)categories.size(); i++)
            if (categories[i].id == c.id)
                repeated = true;
        if (repeated)
        {
            if (errors)
                errors->push_back("PCategory.txt line " + to_string(lineNumber) + ": category id " + c.id +
                                  " appears twice");
            continue;
        }
        categories.push_back(c);
    }
    return categories;
}

bool CategoryRepository::save(const vector<ProductCategory> &categories, string *error) const
{
    // check every category first, so a bad one never reaches the file
    for (int i = 0; i < (int)categories.size(); i++)
    {
        string problem = validate(categories[i]);
        if (problem != "")
        {
            if (error)
                *error = "category " + categories[i].id + ": " + problem;
            return false;
        }
    }

    ofstream file(m_path); // opening for writing empties the old file
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        if (error)
            *error = "cannot write " + m_path;
        return false;
    }
    for (int i = 0; i < (int)categories.size(); i++)
        file << encode(categories[i]) << "\n";
    return true;
}

FILE: /Backend/src/product/ProductModel.cpp
