#include "ProductModel.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cstdio>
#include <stdexcept>

// ======================= Measurement unit =======================
std::string toString(MeasurementUnit unit)
{
    return unit == MeasurementUnit::Count ? "count" : "weight";
}

bool parseMeasurementUnit(const std::string &text, MeasurementUnit &out)
{
    if (text == "count")
        out = MeasurementUnit::Count;
    else if (text == "weight")
        out = MeasurementUnit::Weight;
    else
        return false;
    return true;
}

// ======================= Money =======================
std::string formatPKR(double amount)
{
    char buf[64];
    std::snprintf(buf, sizeof buf, "PKR %.2f", amount);
    return buf;
}

// ======================= EAN-8 =======================
namespace ean8
{
    bool isEightDigits(const std::string &code)
    {
        return code.size() == 8 &&
               std::all_of(code.begin(), code.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c); });
    }

    // EAN-8: first 7 digits weighted 3,1,3,1,3,1,3; check digit makes the sum a multiple of 10.
    bool hasValidCheckDigit(const std::string &code)
    {
        if (!isEightDigits(code))
            return false;
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i)
            sum += (code[i] - '0') * (i % 2 == 0 ? 3 : 1);
        return (10 - sum % 10) % 10 == code[7] - '0';
    }
}

// ======================= Product =======================
Product::Product(int id, std::string barcode, std::string acronym, std::string name, MeasurementUnit unit,
                 double unitPrice, double tax, double discount, int availableQuantity,
                 std::string barcodeFilePath, std::string categoryId)
    : id_(id), barcode_(std::move(barcode)), acronym_(std::move(acronym)), name_(std::move(name)),
      unit_(unit), unitPrice_(unitPrice), tax_(tax), discount_(discount),
      availableQuantity_(availableQuantity), barcodeFilePath_(std::move(barcodeFilePath)),
      categoryId_(std::move(categoryId))
{
    if (categoryId_.empty() && barcode_.size() >= 2)
        categoryId_ = barcode_.substr(0, 2); // older Product.txt lines have no Category Id field
}

void Product::deductStock(int qty)
{
    if (qty < 0 || qty > availableQuantity_)
        throw std::invalid_argument("cannot deduct " + std::to_string(qty) + " from stock of " +
                                    std::to_string(availableQuantity_));
    availableQuantity_ -= qty;
}

// ======================= ProductDescriber =======================
ProductDescription ProductDescriber::describe(const Product &p) const
{
    ProductDescription d;
    d.title = p.getProductName();
    const int qty = p.getAvailableQuantity();
    d.stockLevel = qty <= 0 ? "out" : qty <= lowStockThreshold_ ? "low" : "in-stock";
    const std::string unit = p.getMeasurementUnitName();
    d.fields = {
        {"Product ID", std::to_string(p.getProductID())},
        {"Category ID", p.getCategoryId()},
        {"Barcode", p.getBarcode()},
        {"Acronym", p.getAcronym()},
        {"Name", p.getProductName()},
        {"Measured by", unit},
        {"Unit price", formatPKR(p.getUnitPrice())},
        {"Tax per unit", formatPKR(p.getTax())},
        {"Discount per unit", formatPKR(p.getDiscount())},
        {"You pay per unit", formatPKR(p.getUnitPrice() - p.getDiscount() + p.getTax())},
        {"Available", std::to_string(qty) + (d.stockLevel == "out"   ? " (out of stock)"
                                             : d.stockLevel == "low" ? " (low stock)"
                                                                     : "")},
        {"Barcode file", p.getBarcodeFilePath()},
    };
    return d;
}

// ======================= TextProductFormatter =======================
std::string TextProductFormatter::format(const ProductDescription &d) const
{
    std::size_t w = 0;
    for (const auto &f : d.fields)
        w = std::max(w, f.first.size());
    std::string out = d.title + "\n" + std::string(d.title.size(), '-') + "\n";
    for (const auto &f : d.fields)
        out += f.first + std::string(w - f.first.size() + 2, ' ') + f.second + "\n";
    return out;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/product/ProductStorage.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "ProductStorage.h"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cctype>
#include <cerrno>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <istream>
#include <locale>
#include <set>
#include <sstream>

// Shared helpers used only inside this file
namespace
{
    std::string trim(const std::string &s)
    {
        const char *ws = " \t\r\n";
        const auto b = s.find_first_not_of(ws);
        if (b == std::string::npos)
            return {};
        return s.substr(b, s.find_last_not_of(ws) - b + 1);
    }

    std::string lower(std::string s)
    {
        std::transform(s.begin(), s.end(), s.begin(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::tolower(c); });
        return s;
    }

    bool toInt(const std::string &s, int &out)
    {
        char *end = nullptr;
        errno = 0;
        const long v = std::strtol(s.c_str(), &end, 10);
        if (s.empty() || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE || v < -2147483647L || v > 2147483647L)
            return false;
        out = static_cast<int>(v);
        return true;
    }

    bool toDouble(const std::string &s, double &out)
    {
        char *end = nullptr;
        errno = 0;
        const double v = std::strtod(s.c_str(), &end);
        if (s.empty() || *end != '\0' || errno == ERANGE)
            return false;
        out = v;
        return true;
    }

    // Whole-token numeric conversion in the "C" locale: "12abc" and "1,5" are rejected.
    template <typename T>
    bool toNumber(const std::string &token, T &out)
    {
        std::istringstream in(token);
        in.imbue(std::locale::classic());
        in >> out;
        return !in.fail() && in.peek() == std::char_traits<char>::eof();
    }
}

// ======================= ProductParser =======================
ParsedProduct ProductParser::parseLine(const std::string &rawLine) const
{
    ParsedProduct out;
    std::string line = trim(rawLine);
    if (line.empty() || line.back() != kEndMarker)
    {
        out.error = "missing '#' end-of-line marker";
        return out;
    }
    line.pop_back();

    std::vector<std::string> t;
    std::istringstream in(line);
    for (std::string tok; in >> tok;)
        t.push_back(tok);

    constexpr std::size_t kMinFields = 10; // name needs at least one word
    if (t.size() < kMinFields)
    {
        out.error = "expected 10 fields but found " + std::to_string(t.size());
        return out;
    }

    // Increment 3 lines end with the 2-digit Category Id after the barcode file path; older lines do not.
    std::string categoryId;
    if (t.size() >= kMinFields + 1 && t.back().size() == 2 &&
        std::isdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(t.back()[0])) && std::isdigit(static_cast<unsigned char>(t.back()[1])))
    {
        categoryId = t.back();
        t.pop_back();
    }
    const std::size_t n = t.size();
    int id = 0, quantity = 0;
    double price = 0, tax = 0, discount = 0;
    MeasurementUnit unit = MeasurementUnit::Count;

    if (!toInt(t[0], id))
        out.error = "product id '" + t[0] + "' is not a whole number";
    else if (!parseMeasurementUnit(t[n - 6], unit))
        out.error = "measurement unit '" + t[n - 6] + "' must be 'count' or 'weight'";
    else if (!toDouble(t[n - 5], price))
        out.error = "unit price '" + t[n - 5] + "' is not a number";
    else if (!toDouble(t[n - 4], tax))
        out.error = "tax '" + t[n - 4] + "' is not a number";
    else if (!toDouble(t[n - 3], discount))
        out.error = "discount '" + t[n - 3] + "' is not a number";
    else if (!toInt(t[n - 2], quantity))
        out.error = "available quantity '" + t[n - 2] + "' is not a whole number";
    if (!out.error.empty())
        return out;

    std::string name;
    for (std::size_t i = 3; i < n - 6; ++i)
        name += (name.empty() ? "" : " ") + t[i];

    out.product = Product(id, t[1], t[2], name, unit, price, tax, discount, quantity, t[n - 1], categoryId);
    return out;
}

// ======================= ProductRecordCodec =======================
ProductRecordCodec::ParseResult ProductRecordCodec::parse(const std::string &rawLine)
{
    ParseResult r;
    std::string line = trim(rawLine); // also strips the '\r' of Windows line endings

    if (line.empty() || line.back() != '#')
    {
        r.error = "missing '#' end-of-line marker";
        return r;
    }
    line.pop_back();

    std::vector<std::string> t;
    std::istringstream in(line);
    for (std::string tok; in >> tok;)
        t.push_back(tok);

    std::string categoryId;
    if (t.size() == 11)
    {
        categoryId = t[10];
        t.pop_back();
    }
    if (t.size() != 10)
    {
        r.error = "expected 11 fields (or 10 without Category Id) but found " + std::to_string(t.size());
        return r;
    }

    int id = 0, quantity = 0;
    double price = 0, tax = 0, discount = 0;
    MeasurementUnit unit = MeasurementUnit::Count;
    if (!toNumber(t[0], id))
        r.error = "product id '" + t[0] + "' is not a whole number";
    else if (!parseMeasurementUnit(t[4], unit))
        r.error = "measurement unit '" + t[4] + "' must be 'count' or 'weight'";
    else if (!toNumber(t[5], price))
        r.error = "unit price '" + t[5] + "' is not a number";
    else if (!toNumber(t[6], tax))
        r.error = "tax '" + t[6] + "' is not a number";
    else if (!toNumber(t[7], discount))
        r.error = "discount '" + t[7] + "' is not a number";
    else if (!toNumber(t[8], quantity))
        r.error = "available quantity '" + t[8] + "' is not a whole number";
    if (!r.error.empty())
        return r;

    r.product = Product(id, t[1], t[2], t[3], unit, price, tax, discount, quantity, t[9], categoryId);
    r.ok = true;
    return r;
}

std::string ProductRecordCodec::format(const Product &p)
{
    std::ostringstream out;
    out.imbue(std::locale::classic());
    out << std::fixed << std::setprecision(2);
    out << p.getProductID() << ' ' << p.getBarcode() << ' ' << p.getAcronym() << ' '
        << p.getProductName() << ' ' << p.getMeasurementUnitName() << ' '
        << p.getUnitPrice() << ' ' << p.getTax() << ' ' << p.getDiscount() << ' '
        << p.getAvailableQuantity() << ' ' << p.getBarcodeFilePath() << ' ' << p.getCategoryId() << '#';
    return out.str();
}

// ======================= ProductFileWriter =======================
std::string ProductFileWriter::serialize(const Product &p) const
{
    char nums[96];
    std::snprintf(nums, sizeof nums, "%.2f %.2f %.2f", p.getUnitPrice(), p.getTax(), p.getDiscount());
    return std::to_string(p.getProductID()) + " " + p.getBarcode() + " " + p.getAcronym() + " " +
           p.getProductName() + " " + p.getMeasurementUnitName() + " " + nums + " " +
           std::to_string(p.getAvailableQuantity()) + " " + p.getBarcodeFilePath() + " " + p.getCategoryId() + "#";
}

bool ProductFileWriter::save(const std::string &path, const std::vector<Product> &products, std::string *error) const
{
    namespace fs = std::filesystem;
    const std::string tmp = path + ".tmp";
    {
        std::ofstream out(tmp, std::ios::trunc);
        if (!out)
        {
            if (error)
                *error = "cannot write '" + tmp + "'";
            return false;
        }
        for (const auto &p : products)
            out << serialize(p) << '\n';
        if (!out)
        {
            if (error)
                *error = "write failed for '" + tmp + "'";
            return false;
        }
    }
    std::error_code ec;
    fs::rename(tmp, path, ec);
    if (ec)
    {
        // Do not delete the original if Windows does not support rename-over-existing.
        ec.clear();
        fs::copy_file(tmp, path, fs::copy_options::overwrite_existing, ec);
        std::error_code ignored;
        fs::remove(tmp, ignored);
        if (ec)
        {
            if (error)
                *error = "cannot replace '" + path + "': " + ec.message();
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

// ======================= ProductLoadResult =======================
int ProductLoadResult::errorCount() const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto &i : issues)
        n += i.severity == IssueSeverity::Error;
    return n;
}

int ProductLoadResult::warningCount() const
{
    return static_cast<int>(issues.size()) - errorCount();
}

std::string ProductLoadResult::summary() const
{
    if (!fileOpened)
        return issues.empty() ? "Product file could not be opened" : issues.front().message;
    std::string s = std::to_string(products.size()) + " product(s) loaded";
    if (rejectedLines)
        s += ", " + std::to_string(rejectedLines) + " line(s) rejected";
    s += ".\n";
    for (const auto &i : issues)
        s += std::string(i.severity == IssueSeverity::Error ? "  error   " : "  warning ") + "line " +
             std::to_string(i.line) + ": " + i.message + "\n";
    return s;
}

// ======================= ProductFileLoader =======================
ProductLoadResult ProductFileLoader::load(const std::string &path) const
{
    std::ifstream file(path);
    if (!file.is_open())
    {
        ProductLoadResult r;
        r.issues.push_back({0, IssueSeverity::Error, "cannot open '" + path + "'"});
        return r;
    }
    return loadFrom(file);
}

ProductLoadResult ProductFileLoader::loadFrom(std::istream &in) const
{
    ProductLoadResult result;
    result.fileOpened = true;
    std::set<int> seenIds;
    std::set<std::string> seenBarcodes;

    std::string line;
    int lineNo = 0;
    while (std::getline(in, line))
    {
        ++lineNo;
        if (lineNo == 1 && line.size() >= 3 && line.compare(0, 3, "\xEF\xBB\xBF") == 0)
            line.erase(0, 3); // UTF-8 byte-order mark
        if (line.find_first_not_of(" \t\r\n") == std::string::npos)
            continue; // blank line

        const ParsedProduct parsed = parser_.parseLine(line);
        if (!parsed.ok())
        {
            result.issues.push_back({lineNo, IssueSeverity::Error, parsed.error});
            ++result.rejectedLines;
            continue;
        }
        const Product &p = *parsed.product;

        const ValidationReport report = validator_.validate(p);
        for (const auto &w : report.warnings)
            result.issues.push_back({lineNo, IssueSeverity::Warning, w});
        if (!report.ok())
        {
            for (const auto &e : report.errors)
                result.issues.push_back({lineNo, IssueSeverity::Error, e});
            ++result.rejectedLines;
            continue;
        }
        if (seenIds.count(p.getProductID()) || seenBarcodes.count(p.getBarcode()))
        {
            result.issues.push_back({lineNo, IssueSeverity::Error,
                                     "duplicate product id " + std::to_string(p.getProductID()) +
                                         " or barcode " + p.getBarcode()});
            ++result.rejectedLines;
            continue;
        }
        seenIds.insert(p.getProductID());
        seenBarcodes.insert(p.getBarcode());
        result.products.push_back(p);
    }
    return result;
}

// ======================= ProductManager =======================
bool ProductManager::loadProducts(const std::string &filename)
{
    lastLoad_ = loader_.load(filename);
    path_ = filename;
    products_ = lastLoad_.products;
    rebuildIndex();
    return !lastLoad_.hasErrors();
}

bool ProductManager::saveProducts(std::string *error) const
{
    if (path_.empty())
    {
        if (error)
            *error = "no product file has been loaded";
        return false;
    }
    return ProductFileWriter().save(path_, products_, error);
}

void ProductManager::rebuildIndex()
{
    byBarcode_.clear();
    byId_.clear();
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < products_.size(); ++i)
    {
        byBarcode_[products_[i].getBarcode()] = i;
        byId_[products_[i].getProductID()] = i;
    }
}

void ProductManager::clearProducts()
{
    products_.clear();
    rebuildIndex();
}

const Product *ProductManager::searchByBarcode(const std::string &barcode) const
{
    const auto it = byBarcode_.find(barcode);
    return it == byBarcode_.end() ? nullptr : &products_[it->second];
}

const Product *ProductManager::searchByID(int id) const
{
    const auto it = byId_.find(id);
    return it == byId_.end() ? nullptr : &products_[it->second];
}

const Product *ProductManager::searchByCode(const std::string &codeIn) const
{
    const std::string code = lower(codeIn);
    if (const Product *p = searchByBarcode(codeIn))
        return p;
    if (!code.empty() && std::all_of(code.begin(), code.end(), [](unsigned char c) { return std::isdigit(c); }) &&
        code.size() < 8)
        if (const Product *p = searchByID(std::atoi(code.c_str())))
            return p;
    for (const auto &p : products_)
        if (lower(p.getAcronym()) == code)
            return &p;
    return nullptr;
}

std::vector<const Product *> ProductManager::searchByText(const std::string &query) const
{
    std::vector<const Product *> out;
    const std::string q = lower(query);
    for (const auto &p : products_)
        if (q.empty() || lower(p.getProductName()).find(q) != std::string::npos ||
            lower(p.getAcronym()).find(q) != std::string::npos || p.getBarcode().find(q) != std::string::npos)
            out.push_back(&p);
    return out;
}

Product *ProductManager::mutableById(int id)
{
    const auto it = byId_.find(id);
    return it == byId_.end() ? nullptr : &products_[it->second];
}

bool ProductManager::deductStock(int productId, int quantity)
{
    Product *p = mutableById(productId);
    if (!p || quantity < 0 || quantity > p->getAvailableQuantity())
        return false;
    p->deductStock(quantity);
    return true;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/product/ProductValidation.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "ProductValidation.h"

// ======================= Product rules =======================
void ProductIdRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    if (p.getProductID() <= 0)
        r.errors.push_back("product id must be a positive number");
}

void BarcodeRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    const std::string &b = p.getBarcode();
    if (b.empty())
    {
        r.errors.push_back("barcode is empty");
        return;
    }
    if (!ean8::isEightDigits(b))
    {
        r.errors.push_back("barcode '" + b + "' must be exactly 8 digits (EAN-8)");
        return;
    }
    // Increment 3, requirement 1: the first two digits are the product's Category Id (01-99; 00 is not a category).
    if (b.compare(0, 2, "00") == 0)
        r.errors.push_back("barcode '" + b + "' cannot start with 00 (the first two digits are the Category Id, 01-99)");
    if (!ean8::hasValidCheckDigit(b))
        r.warnings.push_back("barcode '" + b + "' has an invalid EAN-8 check digit");
}

void AcronymRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    if (p.getAcronym().empty())
        r.errors.push_back("acronym is empty");
    else if (p.getAcronym().size() > maxLength_)
        r.errors.push_back("acronym '" + p.getAcronym() + "' is longer than " + std::to_string(maxLength_) +
                           " characters");
}

void ProductNameRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    if (p.getProductName().empty())
        r.errors.push_back("product name is empty");
}

void PriceRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    if (p.getUnitPrice() < 0)
        r.errors.push_back("unit price cannot be negative");
    if (p.getTax() < 0)
        r.errors.push_back("tax cannot be negative");
    if (p.getDiscount() < 0)
        r.errors.push_back("discount cannot be negative");
    if (p.getDiscount() > p.getUnitPrice())
        r.errors.push_back("discount cannot be greater than the unit price");
}

void QuantityRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    if (p.getAvailableQuantity() < 0)
        r.errors.push_back("available quantity cannot be negative");
}

void CategoryIdRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    const std::string &c = p.getCategoryId();
    if (c.size() != 2 || c[0] < '0' || c[0] > '9' || c[1] < '0' || c[1] > '9' || c == "00")
    {
        r.errors.push_back("category id '" + c + "' must be 2 digits from 01 to 99");
        return;
    }
    if (p.getBarcode().compare(0, 2, c) != 0)
        r.errors.push_back("barcode '" + p.getBarcode() + "' must start with its category id " + c);
}

void BarcodePathRule::check(const Product &p, ValidationReport &r) const
{
    if (p.getBarcodeFilePath().empty())
        r.errors.push_back("barcode file path is empty");
}

// ======================= ProductValidator =======================
ProductValidator ProductValidator::standard()
{
    ProductValidator v;
    v.addRule(std::make_unique<ProductIdRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<BarcodeRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<AcronymRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<ProductNameRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<PriceRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<QuantityRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<BarcodePathRule>())
        .addRule(std::make_unique<CategoryIdRule>());
    return v;
}

ProductValidator &ProductValidator::addRule(std::unique_ptr<IProductRule> rule)
{
    rules_.push_back(std::move(rule));
    return *this;
}

ValidationReport ProductValidator::validate(const Product &product) const
{
    ValidationReport report;
    for (const auto &rule : rules_)
        rule->check(product, report);
    return report;
}

// ======================= StockValidator =======================
StockCheck StockValidator::check(const Product &p, int requested) const
{
    StockCheck c;
    c.requested = requested;
    c.available = p.getAvailableQuantity();
    if (requested <= 0)
    {
        c.status = StockStatus::InvalidQuantity;
        c.message = "quantity must be at least 1";
    }
    else if (c.available <= 0)
    {
        c.status = StockStatus::OutOfStock;
        c.message = p.getProductName() + " is out of stock";
    }
    else if (!policy_->allows(requested, c.available))
    {
        c.status = StockStatus::InsufficientStock;
        c.message = "only " + std::to_string(c.available) + " of " + p.getProductName() + " available (" +
                    policy_->describe() + ")";
    }
    return c;
}

StockCheck StockValidator::checkAdd(const Product &p, int alreadyInOrder, int toAdd) const
{
    if (toAdd <= 0)
        return check(p, toAdd);
    return check(p, alreadyInOrder + toAdd);
}

FILE: /Backend/src/scanner/CameraScanner.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "cameraScanner.h"
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
#include <opencv2/videoio.hpp>
#if __has_include(<opencv2/geometry.hpp>)
#include <opencv2/geometry.hpp>
#endif
using namespace std;
cameraScanner::cameraScanner(int idx, int width, int height)
{
    index = idx;
    this->width = width;
    this->height = height;
}
// Open the camera
bool cameraScanner::openCamera()
{
#ifdef _WIN32
    camera.open(index, cv::CAP_DSHOW);
#else
    camera.open(index);
#endif
    if (!camera.isOpened())
    {
        errorMsg = "Camera cannot open";
        return false;
    }
    // produces an MJPG of 640x480 screen
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FOURCC, cv::VideoWriter::fourcc('M', 'J', 'P', 'G'));
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, width);
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, height);
    camera.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FPS, 30);
    return true;
}
// locate the barcode
bool cameraScanner::find(const cv::Mat &gray, cv::Rect &box) const
{
    cv::Mat gx, gy, score, mask;
    cv::Sobel(gray, gx, CV_32F, 1, 0, 3);
    cv::Sobel(gray, gy, CV_32F, 0, 1, 3);
    score = cv::max(cv::abs(gx) - cv::abs(gy), 0.0);
    score.convertTo(score, CV_8U, 0.25);
    // find the blob
    cv::blur(score, score, cv::Size(9, 9));
    cv::threshold(score, mask, 0, 255, cv::THRESH_BINARY | cv::THRESH_OTSU);
    cv::Mat k = cv::getStructuringElement(cv::MORPH_RECT, cv::Size(21, 7));
    cv::morphologyEx(mask, mask, cv::MORPH_CLOSE, k);
    cv::erode(mask, mask, cv::Mat(), cv::Point(-1, -1), 4);
    cv::dilate(mask, mask, cv::Mat(), cv::Point(-1, -1), 4);
    vector<vector<cv::Point>> contours;
    cv::findContours(mask, contours, cv::RETR_EXTERNAL, cv::CHAIN_APPROX_SIMPLE);
    cv::Rect Best;
    double bestArea = 0;
    for (const vector<cv::Point> &c : contours)
    {
        double a = cv::contourArea(c);
        if (a > bestArea)
        {
            bestArea = a;
            Best = cv::boundingRect(c);
        }
    }
    if (bestArea > 2000 && Best.width > Best.height && Best.height > 40)
    {
        box = Best;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}
// read each frame and locate the barcode in grayscale
bool cameraScanner::frameRead(std::string &six, cv::Rect &box, const cv::Mat &frame)
{
    box = cv::Rect();
    six.clear();
    errorMsg.clear();
    if (frame.empty())
    {
        errorMsg = "Camera stopped sending frames";
        return false;
    }
    cv::Mat gray;
    if (frame.channels() == 1)
        gray = frame;
    else if (frame.channels() == 3)
        cv::cvtColor(frame, gray, cv::COLOR_BGR2GRAY);
    else if (frame.channels() == 4)
        cv::cvtColor(frame, gray, cv::COLOR_BGRA2GRAY);
    else
        return false;
    // Decode across several scan lines before relying on the contour detector.
    if (decoder.decodeCropping(gray, six))
    {
        cv::Rect detected;
        box = find(gray, detected) ? detected : cv::Rect(0, 0, gray.cols, gray.rows);
        return true;
    }
    // barcode will be located on the grayscale
    cv::Rect Best;
    if (!find(gray, Best))
    {
        cv::Mat rotated;
        cv::rotate(gray, rotated, cv::ROTATE_90_CLOCKWISE);
        if (decoder.decodeCropping(rotated, six))
        {
            box = cv::Rect(0, 0, gray.cols, gray.rows);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    box = Best;
    cv::Rect pad(Best.x - 15, Best.y, Best.width + 30, Best.height);
    pad &= cv::Rect(0, 0, gray.cols, gray.rows);
    cv::Mat barcode = gray(pad).clone();
    return decoder.decodeCropping(barcode, six);
}
// scanning the located barcode for the six digits
bool cameraScanner::scan(std::string &sixDigits)
{
    sixDigits.clear();
    if (!camera.isOpened() && !openCamera())
        return false;
    const auto start = cv::getTickCount();
    // tick count
    while ((cv::getTickCount() - start) / cv::getTickFrequency() < 30.0)
    {
        cv::Mat frame;
        if (!camera.read(frame) || frame.empty())
        {
            errorMsg = "Camera stopped sending frames";
            camera.release();
            return false;
        }
        cv::Rect box;
        if (frameRead(sixDigits, box, frame))
        {
            camera.release();
            return true;
        }
    }
    camera.release();
    errorMsg = "No barcode found within 30 seconds";
    return false;
}
// store the error message
std::string cameraScanner::errMessage() const
{
    return errorMsg;
}
// grab one frame from the camera (the GUI calls this on every timer tick)
bool cameraScanner::readFrame(cv::Mat &frame)
{
    if (!camera.isOpened() || !camera.read(frame) || frame.empty())
    {
        errorMsg = "Camera stopped sending frames";
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}
// close the camera
cameraScanner::~cameraScanner()
{
    camera.release();
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Backend/src/scanner/decoder.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "decoder.h"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>

const char *const Decoder::patterns[10] = {"0001101", "0011001", "0010011", "0111101", "0100011",
                                           "0110001", "0101111", "0111011", "0110111", "0001011"};

std::vector<double> Decoder::lineRuns(const std::vector<float> &line, double &firstEdge) const
{
    firstEdge = 0;
    if (line.size() < 3)
        return {};
    const auto mm = std::minmax_element(line.begin(), line.end());
    if (*mm.second - *mm.first < 25.0f) // flat picture: no bars here
        return {};
    const float T = (*mm.first + *mm.second) / 2.0f;
    std::vector<double> edges;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 1 < line.size(); ++i)
    {
        const float a = line[i], b = line[i + 1];
        if ((a < T) != (b < T))
            edges.push_back(static_cast<double>(i) + (T - a) / (b - a));
    }
    if (edges.size() < 2)
        return {};
    firstEdge = edges.front();
    std::vector<double> runs;
    for (std::size_t j = 0; j + 1 < edges.size(); ++j)
        runs.push_back(edges[j + 1] - edges[j]);
    return runs;
}

std::string Decoder::runsToModules(const std::vector<double> &runs) const
{
    if (runs.size() != static_cast<std::size_t>(stripes))
        return "";
    double total = 0;
    for (double r : runs)
        total += r;
    const double module = total / modules;
    std::string bits;
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < runs.size(); ++i)
    {
        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::lround(runs[i] / module));
        if (n < 1 || n > 4)
            return "";
        bits += std::string(static_cast<std::size_t>(n), i % 2 == 0 ? '1' : '0');
    }
    return bits.size() == static_cast<std::size_t>(modules) ? bits : "";
}

int Decoder::digitMatch(const std::string &seven, bool right)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
    {
        std::string pattern = patterns[i];
        if (right)
            for (char &c : pattern)
                c = (c == '1') ? '0' : '1';
        if (pattern == seven)
            return i;
    }
    return -1;
}

std::string Decoder::modulesToDigits(const std::string &bits) const
{
    if (bits.size() != static_cast<std::size_t>(modules) || bits.compare(0, 3, "101") != 0 ||
        bits.compare(64, 3, "101") != 0 || bits.compare(31, 5, "01010") != 0)
        return "";
    std::string digits;
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
    {
        const int d = digitMatch(bits.substr(3 + 7 * i, 7), false);
        if (d < 0)
            return "";
        digits += static_cast<char>('0' + d);
    }
    for (int j = 0; j < 4; ++j)
    {
        const int d = digitMatch(bits.substr(36 + 7 * j, 7), true);
        if (d < 0)
            return "";
        digits += static_cast<char>('0' + d);
    }
    return digits;
}

bool Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit(const std::string &d)
{
    if (d.size() != 8)
        return false;
    int sum = 0;
    for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i)
        sum += (d[i] - '0') * ((i % 2 == 0) ? 3 : 1);
    return (10 - sum % 10) % 10 == d[7] - '0';
}

bool Decoder::decodeModules(const std::string &bits, std::string &ean8) const
{
    ean8.clear();
    for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 2; ++attempt) // straight, then upside down
    {
        const std::string b = attempt == 0 ? bits : std::string(bits.rbegin(), bits.rend());
        const std::string d = modulesToDigits(b);
        if (d.size() != 8)
            continue;
        if (!hasValidCheckDigit(d))
        {
            badCheckDigit = true;
            continue;
        }
        ean8 = d;
        badCheckDigit = false;
        return true;
    }
    return false;
}

// Average a thin band of lines (tolerates slightly tilted bars), pad with white, find the runs.
bool Decoder::scanLines(const GrayImage &g, bool vertical, std::string &ean8, int preferredPercent) const
{
    const int across = vertical ? g.height : g.width; // length of the scan line
    const int lines = vertical ? g.width : g.height;  // how many lines are available
    constexpr int pad = 12;
    std::vector<int> order{preferredPercent, preferredPercent - 4, preferredPercent + 4, preferredPercent - 8,
                           preferredPercent + 8, 50, 40, 60, 30, 70, 20, 80, 10, 90};
    for (int percent : order)
    {
        if (percent < 2 || percent > 98)
            continue;
        const int centre = lines * percent / 100;
        const int from = std::max(0, centre - 3), to = std::min(lines, centre + 4);
        if (to <= from)
            continue;
        std::vector<float> line(static_cast<std::size_t>(across) + 2 * pad, 255.0f);
        for (int i = 0; i < across; ++i)
        {
            float sum = 0;
            for (int k = from; k < to; ++k)
                sum += vertical ? g.at(k, i) : g.at(i, k);
            line[static_cast<std::size_t>(i) + pad] = sum / static_cast<float>(to - from);
        }
        // light smoothing against camera noise
        std::vector<float> smooth(line);
        for (std::size_t i = 1; i + 1 < line.size(); ++i)
            smooth[i] = (line[i - 1] + 2 * line[i] + line[i + 1]) / 4.0f;

        double firstEdge = 0;
        const auto runs = lineRuns(smooth, firstEdge);
        // other dark objects may share the line: try every starting dark stripe
        for (std::size_t start = 0; start + stripes <= runs.size(); start += 2)
        {
            const std::vector<double> candidate(runs.begin() + static_cast<long>(start),
                                                runs.begin() + static_cast<long>(start) + stripes);
            const std::string bits = runsToModules(candidate);
            if (!bits.empty() && decodeModules(bits, ean8))
            {
                double before = 0, len = 0;
                for (std::size_t k = 0; k < start; ++k) before += runs[k];
                for (double r : candidate) len += r;
                const double a = firstEdge + before - pad, b = a + len; // along the scan line
                const double mid = (from + to) / 2.0, half = len * 0.28; // box height ~ barcode aspect
                hit_.valid = true;
                if (vertical) { hit_.x0 = mid - half; hit_.x1 = mid + half; hit_.y0 = a; hit_.y1 = b; }
                else { hit_.x0 = a; hit_.x1 = b; hit_.y0 = mid - half; hit_.y1 = mid + half; }
                return true;
            }
        }
    }
    return false;
}

bool Decoder::decodeCropping(const GrayImage &gray, std::string &ean8, double lineFraction) const
{
    ean8.clear();
    badCheckDigit = false;
    hit_ = Hit{};
    if (gray.empty())
        return false;
    bool badCheck = false;
    for (bool vertical : {false, true})
    {
        if (scanLines(gray, vertical, ean8, vertical ? 50 : static_cast<int>(lineFraction * 100.0 + 0.5)))
            return true;
        badCheck = badCheck || badCheckDigit;
    }
    badCheckDigit = badCheck;
    return false;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /cmake/FindMinGWOpenCV.cmake
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Finds an OpenCV built for MinGW even when it has no OpenCVConfig.cmake (e.g. a folder that only
# contains headers + libopencv_*.dll). Looks in OPENCV_ROOT, OpenCV_DIR and the usual places.
# Sets: MINGW_OPENCV_FOUND, MINGW_OPENCV_INCLUDE, MINGW_OPENCV_LIBS, MINGW_OPENCV_DLL_DIR
set(MINGW_OPENCV_FOUND OFF)
set(_roots "${OPENCV_ROOT}" "${OpenCV_DIR}" "$ENV{OPENCV_DIR}"
    "$ENV{USERPROFILE}/Downloads/opencv" "$ENV{USERPROFILE}/Desktop/opencv" "$ENV{USERPROFILE}/opencv"
    "C:/opencv" "C:/OpenCV" "D:/opencv" "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/build/deps/opencv" "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/opencv")
set(_inc "")
set(_dlls "")
foreach(_r IN LISTS _roots)
    if(NOT _r OR NOT IS_DIRECTORY "${_r}")
        continue()
    endif()
    file(GLOB_RECURSE _h "${_r}/*/opencv2/core.hpp" "${_r}/opencv2/core.hpp")
    foreach(_f IN LISTS _h)
        get_filename_component(_d "${_f}" DIRECTORY)   # .../opencv2
        get_filename_component(_d "${_d}" DIRECTORY)   # include dir
        if(NOT _inc)
            set(_inc "${_d}")
        endif()
    endforeach()
    file(GLOB_RECURSE _l "${_r}/*libopencv_world*.dll" "${_r}/*libopencv_world*.dll.a"
                         "${_r}/*libopencv_core*.dll" "${_r}/*libopencv_core*.dll.a")
    foreach(_f IN LISTS _l)
        string(TOLOWER "${_f}" _lf)
        if(_lf MATCHES "mingw|gcc|\\.dll\\.a$|libopencv_" AND NOT _lf MATCHES "vc1[0-9]|msvc")
            list(APPEND _dlls "${_f}")
        endif()
    endforeach()
    if(_inc AND _dlls)
        break()
    endif()
endforeach()
if(_dlls)
    # Use the headers that belong to these DLLs (same package), not another OpenCV version:
    # walk up from the DLL folder looking for include/opencv2/core.hpp.
    list(GET _dlls 0 _firstdll)
    get_filename_component(_up "${_firstdll}" DIRECTORY)
    foreach(_i RANGE 6)
        foreach(_try "${_up}/include" "${_up}/include/opencv4")
            if(EXISTS "${_try}/opencv2/core.hpp")
                set(_inc "${_try}")
                break()
            endif()
        endforeach()
        if(EXISTS "${_up}/include/opencv2/core.hpp" OR EXISTS "${_up}/include/opencv4/opencv2/core.hpp")
            break()
        endif()
        get_filename_component(_up "${_up}" DIRECTORY)
    endforeach()
endif()
if(_inc AND _dlls)
    # prefer the world library; otherwise pick core + imgproc + videoio (+ imgcodecs) next to it
    set(_libs "")
    set(_world "")
    foreach(_f IN LISTS _dlls)
        if(_f MATCHES "world")
            set(_world "${_f}")
        endif()
    endforeach()
    if(_world)
        get_filename_component(_dir "${_world}" DIRECTORY)
        file(GLOB _cands "${_dir}/libopencv_world*.dll.a" "${_dir}/libopencv_world*.dll")
        list(GET _cands 0 _one)
        set(_libs "${_one}")
    else()
        list(GET _dlls 0 _first)
        get_filename_component(_dir "${_first}" DIRECTORY)
        foreach(_m core imgproc videoio imgcodecs)
            file(GLOB _c "${_dir}/libopencv_${_m}*.dll.a" "${_dir}/libopencv_${_m}*.dll")
            list(GET _c 0 _x)
            if(_x)
                list(APPEND _libs "${_x}")
            endif()
        endforeach()
    endif()
    if(_libs)
        set(MINGW_OPENCV_FOUND ON)
        set(MINGW_OPENCV_INCLUDE "${_inc}")
        set(MINGW_OPENCV_LIBS "${_libs}")
        get_filename_component(_one "${_libs}" DIRECTORY)
        set(MINGW_OPENCV_DLL_DIR "${_one}")
        message(STATUS "MinGW OpenCV: headers ${_inc}")
        message(STATUS "MinGW OpenCV: libs    ${_libs}")
    endif()
endif()

FILE: /cmake/seed-data.cmake

# Seed a NEW application data directory without overwriting real retail records.
if(NOT DEFINED SOURCE_DATA OR NOT DEFINED DEST_DATA)
    message(FATAL_ERROR "Provide -DSOURCE_DATA and -DDEST_DATA")
endif()
file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${DEST_DATA}")
file(GLOB_RECURSE _seed RELATIVE "${SOURCE_DATA}" "${SOURCE_DATA}/*")
foreach(_entry IN LISTS _seed)
    if(IS_DIRECTORY "${SOURCE_DATA}/${_entry}")
        continue()
    endif()
    if(NOT EXISTS "${DEST_DATA}/${_entry}")
        get_filename_component(_parent "${DEST_DATA}/${_entry}" DIRECTORY)
        file(MAKE_DIRECTORY "${_parent}")
        file(COPY_FILE "${SOURCE_DATA}/${_entry}" "${DEST_DATA}/${_entry}")
    endif()
endforeach()


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /cmake/toolchain-qt-mingw.cmake
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Used by the "qt-mingw" preset. Qt Creator's preset kit sometimes has no compiler and CMake then
# picks Visual Studio's cl.exe, which cannot build against Qt's MinGW libraries
# ("Qt requires a C++17 compiler ... /Zc:__cplusplus"). This makes sure Qt's own MinGW g++ is used.
if(NOT CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER OR CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER MATCHES "[cC][lL]\\.exe$")
    set(_roots "$ENV{QT_INSTALL_ROOT}" "C:/Qt" "D:/Qt" "E:/Qt")
    set(_gxx "")
    foreach(_r IN LISTS _roots)
        if(_r)
            file(GLOB _found "${_r}/Tools/mingw*_64/bin/g++.exe")
            list(SORT _found COMPARE NATURAL ORDER DESCENDING)
            if(_found)
                list(GET _found 0 _gxx)
                break()
            endif()
        endif()
    endforeach()
    if(_gxx)
        get_filename_component(_bin "${_gxx}" DIRECTORY)
        set(CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER "${_bin}/g++.exe" CACHE FILEPATH "Qt MinGW C++ compiler" FORCE)
        set(CMAKE_C_COMPILER "${_bin}/gcc.exe" CACHE FILEPATH "Qt MinGW C compiler" FORCE)
        set(ENV{PATH} "${_bin};$ENV{PATH}")
    endif()
endif()

#pragma once
// Qt-facing bridge to the Product, Order, and Billing backend modules.
// Data is loaded from and persisted to Product.txt, Order.txt, OrderDetail.txt and Bill.txt.
#include "product/ProductModel.h"
#include "product/ProductStorage.h"
#include "product/ProductValidation.h"
#include "order/OrderManager.h"
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"
#include <memory>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QMap>
#include <QObject>
#include <QPair>
#include <QSet>
#include <QString>
#include <QVector>
#include <optional>

struct ProductInfo
{
    int id = 0; // 0 = custom (manual) line, not in the catalogue
    QString sku;   // acronym (max 8 chars)
    QString ean;   // barcode
    QString name;
    QString unit;  // "count" | "weight"
    double price = 0;    // unit price, no tax
    double tax = 0;      // tax per unit (PKR)
    double discount = 0; // discount per unit (PKR)
    int stock = 0;
    QString categoryId;  // 2-digit Category Id stored in Product.txt (= first two barcode digits)
    QString imagePath;   // resolved barcode image (may not exist)
    static ProductInfo from(const Product &p, const QString &rootDir);
};

struct ProductDescriptionView
{
    QString title, level; // level: in-stock | low | out
    QVector<QPair<QString, QString>> fields;
    QString imagePath;
};

struct ReceiptLine
{
    QString name, acronym;
    int qty = 1;
    double price = 0;    // unit price, no tax
    double tax = 0;      // per unit
    double discount = 0; // per unit
    int productId = 0;   // 0 = manually entered product
};

struct OrderRecord
{
    QString id;
    QString billId; // only nonempty when an actual Bill.txt record exists
    QDateTime time;   // order created (Order.txt)
    QDateTime paidAt; // payment moment (Bill.txt date/time); the 7-day edit window counts from here
    QString customer, contact, tier, payment, merchant, status, shift;
    QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;
    double subtotal = 0, discount = 0, tax = 0, total = 0; // total = subtotal - discount + tax; discount = ALL discounts
    double tendered = 0; // amount received (cash); 0 for card / wallet
    // Breakdown of discount stored on the bill. Legacy bills only store the combined discount.
    double seasonal = 0, special = 0;
    double seasonalPercent = 0, specialPercent = 0;
    QString seasonName;
    double itemDiscount() const { return discount - seasonal - special; }
    double change() const { return tendered > total ? tendered - total : 0.0; } // balance returned
    QDateTime paymentTime() const { return paidAt.isValid() ? paidAt : time; }
    int itemCount() const;
    void recompute();
    QString summary() const; // "3 items  Milk, Bread, +1"
};

struct Settings
{
    int lowStockThreshold = 10;
    bool autoAddScans = true; // requirement 10: a scan adds the item with no extra click
    bool beepOnScan = true;
    barcodehub::order::PaymentMode defaultPayment = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    bool receiptA4 = false;
    QString receiptFolder;

    static Settings load();
    bool save() const;
};

class AppState : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    static AppState &instance();

    // ---- products (backed by Product.txt) ----
    bool loadProducts(const QString &path);
    QString productFile() const { return m_productPath; }
    QStringList productIssues() const; // "line 4: acronym ... " (errors and warnings)
    int productErrorCount() const { return m_manager.getLastLoad().errorCount(); }
    const QVector<ProductInfo> &products() const { return m_products; }
    std::optional<ProductInfo> findByCode(const QString &code) const; // barcode, id or acronym
    QVector<ProductInfo> searchText(const QString &query) const;
    ProductDescriptionView describe(int productId) const;
    // Empty when `toAdd` more units may be added; otherwise the reason (from the StockValidator).
    QString stockProblem(int productId, int alreadyInOrder, int toAdd) const;
    QVector<ProductInfo> lowStock() const;
    bool isReordered(int productId) const { return m_reordered.contains(productId); }
    void reorder(int productId);
    void reorderAllLow();
    static QString locateDataDir();
    QString dataDir() const; // folder that holds Product.txt, Order.txt, Bill.txt, barcodes/
    void reloadAll();        // re-read Product.txt, Order.txt, OrderDetail.txt and Bill.txt (after an external change)

    // ---- manager: catalogue and inventory (written to Product.txt with the backend's ProductFileWriter) ----
    int nextProductId() const;
    // 8-digit barcode "cc" + 5-digit serial + EAN-8 check digit for category `categoryId`; empty if that category is full.
    QString nextBarcode(const QString &categoryId) const;
    int nextSerial(const QString &categoryId) const; // the serial nextBarcode() will use
    // True when the back end accepts barcodes with this 2-digit Category Id as prefix (any of 01-99).
    bool backendAcceptsPrefix(const QString &categoryId) const;
    // Empty when `p` may be saved; otherwise the first problem (backend ProductValidator + duplicate barcode/acronym).
    QString validateProduct(const ProductInfo &p, bool isNew) const;
    bool addProduct(ProductInfo &p, QString *error = nullptr);          // p.id 0 = assign the next id; writes the barcode PNG
    bool updateProduct(const ProductInfo &p, QString *error = nullptr); // matched by p.id
    // Inventory commit: productId -> new available quantity. All-or-nothing; saved to Product.txt.
    bool setStockLevels(const QMap<int, int> &levels, QString *error = nullptr);

    // ---- orders ----
    const QVector<OrderRecord> &orders() const { return m_orders; } // newest first
    QString nextOrderId();
    // Pays an order: creates the order (or, with `existingId`, pays that held / in-progress order under the SAME
    // Order ID), writes the bill and takes the stock. All-or-nothing: on failure every file is put back.
    bool addOrder(OrderRecord &o, QString *error = nullptr, const QString &existingId = QString());
    // Editing a paid order (requirement 3): allowed for 7 days after payment, only after manager approval.
    static constexpr int kEditWindowDays = 7;
    // -1 = not a paid order (no window), 0 = the 7 days are over, 1..7 = days left (rounded up).
    int editDaysLeft(const OrderRecord &o) const;
    // Edits a paid order IN PLACE: same Order ID and Bill ID, new lines/amounts, stock adjusted by the difference.
    bool replaceOrder(const QString &orderId, OrderRecord &edited, QString *error = nullptr);
    // Quantity of each product a paid order already took from stock (credited back while that order is edited).
    QMap<int, int> stockHeldBy(const QString &orderId) const;

    // ---- on-hold orders (requirement 10): real orders in Order.txt with status on-hold ----
    // Saves the cart as an on-hold order (a new one, or `existingId` updated). Returns the Order ID, empty on failure.
    QString holdOrder(const QString &customer, const QString &contact, const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines,
                      const QString &existingId = QString(), QString *error = nullptr);
    bool discardHeldOrder(const QString &orderId, QString *error = nullptr);
    QVector<OrderRecord> heldOrders() const; // newest first
    double salesToday() const { return m_baseSales + m_sessionSales; }
    int ordersToday() const { return m_baseOrders + m_sessionOrders; }

    Settings &settings() { return m_settings; }
    void emitChanged() { emit changed(); }
    void emitScan(const QString &code) { emit scanned(code); }

signals:
    void changed();
    void scanned(const QString &code);

private:
    AppState();
    void refreshCache();
    void refreshOrders();
    bool settle(OrderRecord &o, const QString &existingId, bool editPaid, QString *error);
    bool cartFromLines(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, QString *error); // fills the backend cart
    QStringList recordFiles() const; // Product/Order/OrderDetail/Bill/BillDetail.txt (for all-or-nothing saves)
    ProductManager m_manager;
    StockValidator m_stock;
    QVector<ProductInfo> m_products;
    QString m_root;
    QString m_productPath;
    std::unique_ptr<barcodehub::order::OrderManager> m_orderManager;
    std::unique_ptr<barcodehub::billing::BillRepository> m_billRepository;
    QVector<OrderRecord> m_orders;
    QSet<int> m_reordered;
    Settings m_settings;
    double m_baseSales = 0, m_sessionSales = 0;
    int m_baseOrders = 0, m_sessionOrders = 0;
};


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/BarcodeArt.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Draws EAN-8 barcodes (bars + digits) with QPainter. Used to preview a barcode in the product editor and to write
// the data/barcodes/<code>.png file that Product.txt points to. Pure frontend code: the check digit rule is the
// same one the Increment 2 backend validates (Backend/include/product/Ean8.h).
#include <QImage>
#include <QString>

namespace BarcodeArt
{
    // "01001020" -> the 67-module bit pattern ('1' = bar). Empty if `code` is not 8 digits.
    QString modules(const QString &code);
    // 7 digits -> the EAN-8 check digit character ("0".."9").
    QString checkDigit(const QString &sevenDigits);
    // Full 8-digit code from category (2 digits) + serial (up to 5 digits).
    QString makeCode(const QString &categoryId, int serial);
    // Same look as the existing data/barcodes PNGs: white background, black bars, digits underneath.
    QImage render(const QString &code, int moduleWidth = 3, int barHeight = 100, bool withDigits = true);
    bool savePng(const QString &code, const QString &path, QString *error = nullptr);
}

FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/DarkModeManager.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file DarkModeManager.h
 * @brief Self-contained Dark Mode subsystem and top-bar toggle component for BarcodeHub.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements a standalone theme management module adhering to Separation of Concerns:
 * - Manages application-wide theme switching (Light <-> Dark mode) dynamically at runtime.
 * - Generates high-contrast, polished dark stylesheets tailored for Point of Sale terminals.
 * - Persists theme state across sessions via QSettings.
 * - Provides a ready-to-mount, interactive toggle button widget for the application top bar.
 */

#include <QObject>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QIcon>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QString>

class QWidget;

namespace barcodehub::ui {

/**
 * @class DarkModeManager
 * @brief Central controller managing application theme state, stylesheet injection, and persistence.
 */
class DarkModeManager : public QObject {
    Q_OBJECT

public:
    /**
     * @brief Accesses the singleton instance of the DarkModeManager.
     * @return Reference to the DarkModeManager instance.
     */
    static DarkModeManager& instance();

    /**
     * @brief Checks if Dark Mode is currently active.
     * @return True if dark mode is active, false if light mode.
     */
    bool isDarkMode() const;

    /**
     * @brief Sets the application theme mode and immediately reapplies the global stylesheet.
     * @param enable True to enable dark mode, false for light mode.
     */
    void setDarkMode(bool enable);

    /**
     * @brief Toggles between light and dark modes.
     */
    void toggle();

    /**
     * @brief Generates the comprehensive QSS stylesheet for the current active theme.
     * @return QString containing complete Qt stylesheet rules.
     */
    QString getActiveStyleSheet() const;

    /**
     * @brief Factory creating a styled, pre-wired toggle button for the application top bar.
     *
     * The button features dynamic icons (Sun for Light mode, Moon for Dark mode),
     * a pill-shaped modern layout, and tooltips. Clicking automatically calls toggle().
     *
     * @param parent Optional parent widget.
     * @return Pointer to the configured QPushButton.
     */
    QPushButton* createTopBarToggle(QWidget* parent = nullptr);

    /**
     * @brief Renders a vector sun icon using QPainter.
     * @param color Icon color.
     * @param size Pixel dimensions.
     * @return QIcon rendered with crisp antialiasing.
     */
    static QIcon createSunIcon(const QColor& color, int size = 20);

    /**
     * @brief Renders a vector moon icon using QPainter.
     * @param color Icon color.
     * @param size Pixel dimensions.
     * @return QIcon rendered with crisp antialiasing.
     */
    static QIcon createMoonIcon(const QColor& color, int size = 20);

signals:
    /**
     * @brief Emitted whenever the theme changes.
     * @param isDark True if switched to dark mode, false if light mode.
     */
    void themeChanged(bool isDark);

private:
    explicit DarkModeManager(QObject* parent = nullptr);
    ~DarkModeManager() override = default;
    DarkModeManager(const DarkModeManager&) = delete;
    DarkModeManager& operator=(const DarkModeManager&) = delete;

    void applyTheme();
    QString buildDarkPaletteStyleSheet() const;

    bool m_isDark{false};
};

} // namespace barcodehub::ui

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/Dialogs.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

/**
 * @file Dialogs.h
 * @brief Modal dialog windows and receipt generation utilities for the POS GUI.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements the interactive dialog workflows assigned for Increment 2:
 * - Payment selection modal (supporting Cash, Card, and Mobile Wallet).
 * - Receipt viewer modal (displaying finalized bill details and printable format).
 * - Product Description modal (displaying specifications, stock, and barcode graphic).
 * - Order details inspector.
 */

#include "AppState.h"
#include "UserRole.h"
#include <QString>
#include <QVector>

class QWidget;

namespace Dialogs
{
    /** What the payment dialog needs to know about the order being settled. */
    struct PaymentRequest
    {
        QString orderId;                // e.g. "26110122"
        QString customer, contact;
        QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;     // cart lines; per-unit discount = the product's own discount only (Pricing.h adds the rest)
    };

    /** What the cashier chose in the payment dialog. */
    struct PaymentResult
    {
        QString mode;              // "Cash" | "Card" | "Wallet"
        QString merchant;          // gateway / wallet name for Card and Wallet
        double tendered = 0;       // cash received (Cash only)
        double specialPercent = 0; // special discount typed in the dialog, 0..8 (Pricing::clampSpecial)
    };

    /**
     * @brief "Complete Order Payment": billing breakdown (item / seasonal / special discount, tax), tender mode,
     *        cash numpad with quick-tender keys, balance to return. Returns false when cancelled.
     */
    bool payment(QWidget *parent, const PaymentRequest &req, PaymentResult &res);

    /**
     * @brief "Request edit" (cashier): asks for the reason to send to a manager, who approves or rejects the
     *        request on the Orders page. Returns false when the cashier cancels.
     */
    bool requestEditReason(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order, QString *reason);

    /**
     * @brief "Add New Product to Catalog" (existing == nullptr) or edit an existing product: category, specifications,
     *        EAN-8 barcode generator. Saves through AppState::addProduct / updateProduct. Returns true if saved.
     */
    bool editProduct(QWidget *parent, const ProductInfo *existing);

    /**
     * @brief Displays a clean printable-style receipt preview dialog for a finalized order.
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     * @param order Fully recorded order with items, customer details, and payment metadata.
     */
    void receipt(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order);

    /**
     * @brief Displays detailed line items, timestamps, and customer metadata for an order.
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     * @param order Target OrderRecord to inspect.
     */
    void orderDetails(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order);

    /**
     * @brief Displays the Product Description popup dialog.
     *
     * Fulfills Increment 2 requirement: Cashier can inspect comprehensive product
     * attributes (acronym, description, pricing, tax, discount, available inventory)
     * along with rendered barcode graphic.
     *
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     * @param productId Numeric identifier of the product to query.
     */
    void productDescription(QWidget *parent, int productId);

    /**
     * @brief Displays any syntax or data integrity issues detected during Product.txt parsing.
     * @param parent Owning parent widget.
     */
    void loadIssues(QWidget *parent);

    /**
     * @brief Generates formatted monospace text representation of an order's finalized bill receipt.
     *
     * Contains business header, itemized quantities, unit prices, discounts, taxes,
     * total payable, amount tendered, change returned, and payment method details.
     *
     * @param order Recorded order data.
     * @return Formatted multi-line receipt text.
     */
    QString receiptText(const OrderRecord &order);

    /** @brief The bill as formatted HTML (used on screen and for the PDF). */
    QString receiptHtml(const OrderRecord &order);

    /** @brief Saves the bill as a PDF file; returns false if it could not be written. */
    bool saveReceiptPdf(const OrderRecord &order, const QString &path);
    /**
     * @brief Sign-in screen: the user types a name and presses Cashier or Manager.
     * @param[out] role Chosen role.
     * @param[out] name Typed name (the role name if left empty).
     * @return False if the window was closed without signing in.
     */
    bool login(QWidget *parent, UserRole &role, QString &name);
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/OrderRules.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Order-editing rules (Increment 3, requirement 3). One place, no widgets (SRP):
//  - a PAID order may be edited within 7 days of its payment, and only after a manager approves it;
//  - an ON HOLD / IN PROGRESS order is simply resumed (no approval);
//  - an ARCHIVED order is never opened again.
// Front-end helper only: it works on the OrderRecord that AppState already loads from Order.txt.
#include "AppState.h"
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QString>

namespace OrderRules
{
    constexpr int kEditWindowDays = 7;

    // Moment the edit window closes (payment time + 7 days).
    inline QDateTime editDeadline(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.time.addDays(kEditWindowDays); }

    inline bool isPaid(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.status == "Paid"; }
    inline bool isResumable(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.status == "On Hold" || o.status == "In Progress"; }

    // Whole days still left to edit a paid order (rounded up: 6 days 3 h left -> 7). 0 = window closed.
    inline int editDaysLeft(const OrderRecord &o, const QDateTime &now = QDateTime::currentDateTime())
    {
        if (!isPaid(o))
            return 0;
        const qint64 secs = now.secsTo(editDeadline(o));
        if (secs <= 0)
            return 0;
        return int((secs + 86399) / 86400);
    }

    inline bool paidEditable(const OrderRecord &o, const QDateTime &now = QDateTime::currentDateTime())
    {
        return editDaysLeft(o, now) > 0;
    }

    // "Editable for 5 days" / "Editable for 1 day"
    inline QString editBadgeText(int daysLeft)
    {
        return QString("Editable for %1 day%2").arg(daysLeft).arg(daysLeft == 1 ? "" : "s");
    }

    // Badge colour: green while there is plenty of time, amber for the last three days (W::badge kinds).
    inline QString editBadgeKind(int daysLeft) { return daysLeft <= 3 ? "amber" : "green"; }

    // Whole days since the order was paid (for the "window closed" message).
    inline int daysSincePaid(const OrderRecord &o, const QDateTime &now = QDateTime::currentDateTime())
    {
        return int(o.time.daysTo(now));
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/Pricing.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// Seasonal + special discount arithmetic for the checkout screens.
//
// The Increment 2 backend stores one "discount per unit" for every bill line. The seasonal discount (active season
// from the Discounts page) and the special discount (cashier, at most 8 percent) are therefore *folded into that
// per-unit discount* before the order is handed to the backend, so Order.txt / Bill.txt totals always equal what the
// customer paid. The breakdown (item / seasonal / special) is kept on the OrderRecord for the receipt of this session.
#include "AppState.h"
#include <QDate>

namespace Pricing
{
    struct Quote
    {
        double gross = 0;        // sum of unit price * qty (no tax)
        double itemDiscount = 0; // product discounts from Product.txt
        double seasonal = 0;     // seasonal discount (active season, restricted to its categories)
        double special = 0;      // special discount (cashier typed percent, capped at 8)
        double tax = 0;
        double payable = 0;      // gross - itemDiscount - seasonal - special + tax
        double seasonalPercent = 0;
        double specialPercent = 0; // after capping
        QString seasonName;
        int units = 0, lines = 0;
        double totalDiscount() const { return itemDiscount + seasonal + special; }
    };

    // Cap a typed special-discount percent to 0..8 (UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent).
    double clampSpecial(double percent);

    // `lines` carry only the product's own discount per unit. Returns the quote for `on` (default today).
    Quote quote(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent = 0, const QDate &on = QDate::currentDate());

    // Same lines with the seasonal and special amounts folded into each line's per-unit discount (2 decimals).
    QVector<ReceiptLine> fold(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent = 0,
                              const QDate &on = QDate::currentDate());

    // Copy the quote's breakdown into an OrderRecord (subtotal/discount/tax/total + seasonal/special parts).
    void apply(OrderRecord &order, const Quote &q);
}

#pragma once

/**
 * @file RecordOrderPage.h
 * @brief Primary interactive POS cashier screen for recording orders and completing checkout.
 * @author Ali Abdullah (25L-3022)
 *
 * Implements the core Point of Sale GUI workflow for Increment 2:
 * - Barcode entry, product preview, cart and a fixed checkout summary.
 * - Keyboard shortcuts: F2 (Hold), F4 (Product Description), F9 (Camera), F10 (Checkout).
 * - Real-time stock validation and dynamic bill subtotal calculation.
 * - Integration with Dialogs::payment() and Dialogs::receipt().
 */

#include "AppState.h"
#include "UserRole.h"
#include <QMap>
#include <QPair>
#include <QVector>
#include <QWidget>
#include <optional>

class QFrame;
class QHBoxLayout;
class QLabel;
class ElidedLabel;
class QLineEdit;
class QPushButton;
class QTableWidget;
class CameraFeedWidget;

/**
 * @class RecordOrderPage
 * @brief Main cashier interface managing active order drafts, shopping cart items, and billing settlement.
 */
class RecordOrderPage : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    /**
     * @brief Constructs the RecordOrderPage and builds its user interface components.
     * @param parent Optional parent widget.
     */
    explicit RecordOrderPage(QWidget* parent = nullptr);

public slots:
    /** @brief Shifts keyboard focus to the barcode input field. */
    void focusInput();

    /** @brief Starts the camera scanner. */
    void simulateScan();

    /** @brief Opens the catalog picker. */
    void quickLookup();

    /** @brief Triggers order checkout and launches the payment settlement dialog (F10 shortcut). */
    void checkout();

    /**
     * @brief Processes a scanned or typed barcode string, looking up matching inventory.
     * @param code Decoded barcode string.
     * @param fromScanner True if emitted from camera scanner; false if typed manually.
     */
    void showCode(const QString& code, bool fromScanner);

    /**
     * @brief Adds a batch of checked barcode-scanned items (EAN, quantity) directly to the cart
     *        so the cashier can generate a collective bill.
     */
    QVector<QPair<QString, int>> addScannedBatch(const QVector<QPair<QString, int>>& items);

    /** @brief Discards the current cart and resets the interface for a new customer transaction. */
    void newDraft();

    /** @brief Opens the Product Description for the product on screen (F4). */
    void describeCurrent();

    /** @brief Puts the current order on hold (parked in UiStore) and starts a fresh one (F2). One press, no dialog. */
    void holdOrder();

    /** @brief Resumes a parked (on-hold) order by its code, e.g. "HLD-108" (click on the parked strip). */
    void resumeParked(const QString& code);

    /**
     * @brief Opens an existing order in this screen for editing. Called after the manager approved the edit of a paid
     *        order (Orders page) or when resuming an in-progress order. The screen shows an "Editing order" banner.
     */
    void loadOrderForEdit(const QString& orderId);

    /** @brief Role of the signed-in user (Manager may also approve edits on the spot). */
    void setRole(UserRole role);

signals:
    /** @brief Emits a transient user notification toast message on the main window. */
    void toast(const QString& message);

    /** @brief A bill was just created for this order: the shell opens it on the Bills page ("New Order [Space]" there). */
    void billReady(const QString& orderId);

private:
    /**
     * @struct CartLine
     * @brief In-memory shopping cart item tracking product metadata and selected quantity.
     */
    struct CartLine
    {
        ProductInfo p;  ///< Cached snapshot of product details.
        int qty = 1;    ///< Quantity added to cart.
    };

    QWidget* buildBarcodeStrip();
    QWidget* buildCustomerStrip();
    QWidget* buildEditBanner();  ///< amber "Editing order #..." bar (hidden unless an order is being edited)
    void setEditMode(const QString& orderId, const QString& text); ///< empty id = leave edit mode
    QWidget* buildParkedStrip(); ///< "Parked (n)" chips of the orders on hold
    QWidget* buildProductPane();
    QWidget* buildCart();
    QWidget* buildSummary();
    void setCameraActive(bool active);
    void setPreview(const std::optional<ProductInfo>& p);
    void processBarcode(const QString& codeIn, bool fromScanner, bool playAudio);
    bool addToCart(const ProductInfo& p, int qty = 1);
    void changeQty(int row, int delta);
    void removeRow(int row);
    void rebuildTable();
    void updateTotals();
    void loadDemoDraft();
    void rebuildParked();        ///< redraws the on-hold chips (also keeps the "4m ago" texts fresh)
    QVector<ReceiptLine> cartLines() const;
    // Empty when `add` more units of product `id` fit (the cart already has `have`); otherwise the reason.
    // While a paid order is edited, the units that order already took from stock count as available.
    QString stockIssue(int id, int have, int add) const;
    void startFresh(const QString& message = QString()); ///< empty cart, new order number, ready to scan

    UserRole m_role = UserRole::Cashier;
    QVector<CartLine> m_cart;
    std::optional<ProductInfo> m_preview;
    int m_nextCustomLine = 1;

    QLineEdit* m_customer;
    QLineEdit* m_contact;
    QLineEdit* m_manual;
    QLabel* m_sessionId;

    CameraFeedWidget* m_feed;
    QWidget* m_cameraPanel;
    QPushButton* m_camToggle;
    QPushButton* m_addBtn;
    QPushButton* m_descBtn;
    ElidedLabel* m_pName;
    QLabel* m_pPrice;
    QLabel* m_pMeta;
    QLabel* m_pStock;
    QLabel* m_pBadge;

    QTableWidget* m_table;
    QLabel* m_itemsBadge;
    QLabel* m_gross;
    QLabel* m_disc;
    QLabel* m_tax;
    QLabel* m_total;
    QPushButton* m_checkout;
    QPushButton* m_hold;
    QLabel* m_parkTitle;
    QLabel* m_parkEmpty;
    QWidget* m_parkedList;
    QHBoxLayout* m_parkChips;
    QFrame* m_editBanner = nullptr;
    QLabel* m_editText = nullptr;
    QString m_editingId; ///< paid order being edited (empty = a normal new order)
    QString m_heldId;    ///< on-hold order resumed into the cart (paid / held again under the same Order ID)
    QMap<int, int> m_stockCredit; ///< productId -> units the edited paid order already took from stock
};

#pragma once
// Business Reports (manager): six tabular reports with totals, CSV export and PDF print.
//   1 incomplete orders (not paid / not archived) + total      4 all sales with total amount and discounts
//   2 completed orders + total                                 5 top ten most sold items with remaining stock
//   3 cash sales by period                                    6 top ten items by current remaining stock
// Reports read current orders, catalogue stock, and saved bill quantities without writing records.
#include <QDate>
#include <QHash>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVector>
#include <QWidget>

class QDateEdit;
class QHBoxLayout;
class QLabel;
class QPushButton;
class QTableWidget;
class QVBoxLayout;

class ReportsPage : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit ReportsPage(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void refresh();
signals:
    void toast(const QString &message);

private:
    enum Tab { Incomplete, Completed, CashSales, AllSales, TopSold, TopRemaining, TabCount };
    enum Period { All, Daily, Weekly, Monthly, Yearly, Custom };

    struct Tile { QString caption, icon, value, sub; };
    struct TopItem { QString name, acronym; double units = 0, lineValue = 0; int stock = -1; };
    struct Report
    {
        QString title, subtitle;
        QStringList headers;
        QVector<bool> right;        // right-aligned columns
        QVector<int> accent;        // blue + bold columns
        QVector<QStringList> rows;
        QVector<Tile> tiles;        // three summary tiles
        QString footLabel, footValue, footNote;
        bool showTopSold = false;   // "Top sold staples" side panel
    };

    Report build(int tab, const QHash<QString, double> &paidUnits) const;
    QVector<TopItem> topSold(int limit, bool cashOnly = false) const;
    QString periodText() const;
    void setPeriod(int period);
    void setTab(int tab);
    void render();
    void exportCsv();
    void exportPdf();

    int m_tab = CashSales;
    int m_period = All;
    QDate m_from, m_to; // inclusive; invalid = no limit
    Report m_report;

    QVector<QPushButton *> m_tabButtons, m_periodButtons;
    QDateEdit *m_fromEdit = nullptr, *m_toEdit = nullptr;
    QWidget *m_periodBar = nullptr;
    QWidget *m_outstandingNotice = nullptr;
    QHBoxLayout *m_tiles = nullptr;
    QTableWidget *m_table = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_ledgerTitle = nullptr, *m_ledgerSub = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_empty = nullptr;
    QWidget *m_side = nullptr;
    QVBoxLayout *m_sideList = nullptr;
    QLabel *m_footLabel = nullptr, *m_footValue = nullptr, *m_footNote = nullptr;
};

FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/ScanAudio.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once

#include <QByteArray>
#include <QElapsedTimer>
#include <QObject>
#include <QString>
#include <memory>

#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
class QMediaDevices;
class QSoundEffect;
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
class QTemporaryFile;
#endif

/**
 * @class ScanAudio
 * @brief Reusable low-latency POS confirmation audio service for successful cashier barcode scans.
 *
 * Independent of order storage, billing, and UI layout. Reads the existing
 * AppState::instance().settings().beepOnScan flag, suppresses unintended duplicate triggers
 * within a short cooldown interval, and degrades gracefully when audio hardware or Qt Multimedia
 * is unavailable.
 */
class ScanAudio : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    static constexpr qint64 kDefaultMinIntervalMs = 150;

    static ScanAudio &instance();

    /** @brief Pre-initializes the audio backend and asset so the first scan has minimal latency. */
    void warmUp();

    /** @brief Plays one scan confirmation beep if Settings::beepOnScan is enabled and not debounced. */
    bool playSuccess();

    /** @brief Plays one scan confirmation beep if @p enabled is true and not debounced. */
    bool playIfEnabled(bool enabled);

    /** @brief Pure trigger-gating rule for setting state and duplicate-suppression interval. */
    static bool shouldPlay(bool beepEnabled, qint64 elapsedSinceLastMs, qint64 minIntervalMs = kDefaultMinIntervalMs);

    /** @brief Synthesizes a clean 110 ms, 44.1 kHz 16-bit mono PCM WAV retail scanner beep. */
    static QByteArray buildSuccessToneWav();

    bool isAvailable() const;
    QString backendName() const;
    void setMinIntervalMs(qint64 ms);
    qint64 minIntervalMs() const { return m_minIntervalMs; }
    void resetDebounce();

signals:
    /** @brief Emitted whenever confirmation feedback passes setting and debounce checks. */
    void feedbackTriggered();

private:
    ScanAudio();
    ~ScanAudio() override;
    ScanAudio(const ScanAudio &) = delete;
    ScanAudio &operator=(const ScanAudio &) = delete;

    void ensureInitialized();
    void shutdown();
    void refreshDeviceAvailability();
    bool triggerBackendPlayback();

    qint64 m_minIntervalMs = kDefaultMinIntervalMs;
    QElapsedTimer m_lastPlayTimer;
    bool m_initialized = false;
    bool m_shuttingDown = false;
    bool m_available = false;
    bool m_warnedUnavailable = false;
    QByteArray m_wavBuffer;
#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
    std::unique_ptr<QMediaDevices> m_mediaDevices;
    std::unique_ptr<QSoundEffect> m_effect;
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
    std::unique_ptr<QTemporaryFile> m_temporaryWav;
    QString m_temporaryWavPath;
#endif
};

#pragma once
// Shared building blocks for the redesigned screens (tables, page headers, stat tiles, design-style modal).

namespace Ui
{
    // ---- tables ----
    QTableWidget *makeTable(const QStringList &headers);
    QTableWidgetItem *item(const QString &s, Qt::Alignment a = Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter,
                           const QString &color = "", bool bold = false);
    QWidget *wrapW(QWidget *w, Qt::Alignment a = Qt::AlignLeft); // put a widget in a table cell
    bool writeCsv(QWidget *parent, const QString &suggested, const QStringList &rows);

    // ---- page scaffolding ----
    QWidget *pageShell(QWidget *page, QVBoxLayout *&body, bool scrollable = false);

    // EYEBROW caption (blue, optional) / big title / description on the left, action widgets on the right.
    QWidget *sectionHeader(const QString &eyebrow, const QString &title, const QString &subtitle,
                           const QList<QWidget *> &actions = {});
    // Stat tile: CAPTION, big value, small sub-line, round icon on the right.
    QFrame *statTile(const QString &caption, const QString &icon, QLabel *&value, QLabel *&sub,
                     const QString &tint = "blue"); // tint: blue | green | amber | red
    QLabel *kbd(const QString &text); // little "Ctrl+S" key hint chip
    QLabel *dotLabel(const QString &text, const QColor &dot); // "● Text" status
    // "Prev  Page 1 of 61  Next" footers are page-specific; this makes just the pill buttons.
    QPushButton *pageButton(const QString &text, bool current = false);

    // ---- modal in the style of the Stitch dialogs ----
    // Frameless rounded card, icon tile + title (+ tag chip) + subtitle + close button, body, footer.

    class Modal : public QDialog
    {

              const QString &tag = QString(), int width = 720);
