--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/include/ui/UiStore.h
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#pragma once
// State for the Increment 3 manager screens: product categories (PCategory.txt), promotional seasons
// (Seasons.txt), on-hold orders (Order.txt through AppState) and the manager sign-off check.
#include "AppState.h"
#include <QDate>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QMap>
#include <QObject>
#include <QString>
#include <QStringList>
#include <QVector>

struct Category
{
    QString id;      // 2 digits, becomes the first two digits of every barcode in the category
    QString name;
    QString acronym; // up to 3 letters
    QString color;   // theme tag, e.g. "#2554d8"
};

struct Season
{
    // Seasons.txt (requirement 13) stores exactly: start date, end date, discount percentage, isActive.
    QDate start, end;
    double percent = 0;
    bool active = false;       // isActive in Seasons.txt
    // Not stored - derived for the screen only:
    QString id;   // row handle "SEA-2026-01" (position in Seasons.txt)
    QString name; // label made from the dates, e.g. "23 Nov - 30 Nov 2026"
    // "active" | "scheduled" | "inactive" | "expired" for a given day
    QString statusOn(const QDate &d) const;
};

struct ParkedOrder
{
    QString code; // the Order ID of the on-hold order (Order.txt)
    QString customer, contact;
    QDateTime parkedAt;
    QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;
    int itemCount() const;
    double total() const; // subtotal - discount + tax
};

class UiStore : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    static UiStore &instance();
    static constexpr double kSpecialDiscountCapPercent = 8.0; // requirement 8: special discount is at most 8 percent

    // ---- categories ----
    const QVector<Category> &categories() const { return m_categories; }
    const Category *category(const QString &id) const;
    int productCount(const QString &id) const; // products whose barcode starts with this id (from Product.txt)
    QString rangeText(const QString &id) const; // "10000000 - 10999999"
    QStringList freeIds(int n = 3) const;       // unused ids from 10 to 99, e.g. {"80","85","90"}
    // Empty string when valid; otherwise the message to show (e.g. the "already in use" validation error).
    QString validateCategory(const QString &id, const QString &name, const QString &acronym,
                             const QString &ignoreId = QString()) const;
    bool addCategory(const Category &c, QString *error = nullptr);
    bool updateCategory(const QString &oldId, const Category &c, QString *error = nullptr);
    bool removeCategory(const QString &id, QString *error = nullptr); // refused while products use it

    // ---- seasons (Seasons.txt: start date, end date, percentage, isActive) ----
    const QVector<Season> &seasons() const { return m_seasons; }
    void addSeason(const Season &s);
    void updateSeason(const QString &id, const Season &s);
    void removeSeason(const QString &id);
    // Empty on success; otherwise why it was refused (an active season may not overlap another active one).
    QString setSeasonActive(const QString &id, bool on);
    QString nextSeasonId() const;
    const Season *activeSeason(const QDate &d = QDate::currentDate()) const; // first active season covering d
    // Requirement 13: a season is only start date, end date, discount percentage and isActive (Seasons.txt).
    // Two ACTIVE seasons may not share a day, so a bill date always matches at most one discount (requirement 15).
    QString validateSeason(const QDate &start, const QDate &end, double percent, bool active,
                           const QString &ignoreId = QString()) const;
    static QString seasonLabel(const QDate &start, const QDate &end); // "23 Nov - 30 Nov 2026"

    // ---- on-hold orders: saved in Order.txt / OrderDetail.txt with status on-hold (see AppState::holdOrder) ----
    QVector<ParkedOrder> parked() const; // newest first
    // Saves the cart as an on-hold order (`existingId` = update that held order). Returns the Order ID, empty on failure.
    QString park(const QString &customer, const QString &contact, const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines,
                 const QString &existingId = QString(), QString *error = nullptr);
    bool takeParked(const QString &code, ParkedOrder &out); // reads it (it stays on hold until it is paid)
    bool discardParked(const QString &code, QString *error = nullptr);

    // ---- edit requests (requirement 3): a cashier asks, a manager approves or rejects ----
    // Saved in data/EditRequests.txt, so a request survives a restart and is seen by every terminal on the same data
    // folder. An approval is good for one edit: it is used up when the edit is saved or cancelled.
    struct EditRequest
    {
        QString orderId;
        QString status; // "pending" | "approved" | "rejected" | "used"
        QString cashier, reason;
        QDateTime requestedAt;
        QString manager; // who decided
        QDateTime decidedAt;
    };
    QVector<EditRequest> editRequests() const;            // newest first
    QVector<EditRequest> pendingEditRequests() const;     // waiting for a manager, oldest first
    const EditRequest *latestEditRequest(const QString &orderId) const;
    QString requestEdit(const QString &orderId, const QString &cashier, const QString &reason); // empty = sent
    QString decideEdit(const QString &orderId, const QString &manager, bool approve);          // empty = done
    int approvedUnusedCount() const;                      // approved, not yet used (cashier's to-do)
    void reloadEditRequests();                            // re-read the file (another terminal may have changed it)

    // Used by Record Order: an approved request for this order (a signed-in manager approves his own edit).
    struct Approval { QString manager, reason; QDateTime at; };
    void grantApproval(const QString &orderId, const QString &manager, const QString &reason);
    bool approvalFor(const QString &orderId, Approval *out = nullptr) const;
    void revokeApproval(const QString &orderId); // the approval is used up

signals:
    void categoriesChanged();
    void seasonsChanged();
    void parkedChanged();
    void editRequestsChanged();

private:
    UiStore();
    // Seasons.txt (start end percent isActive#) via the backend SeasonRepository.
    QString categoriesPath() const; // PCategory.txt
    void loadCategories();
    bool saveCategories(const QVector<Category> &categories, QString *error);
    QString seasonsPath() const;
    void loadSeasons();
    void saveSeasons();
    QVector<Category> m_categories;
    QVector<Season> m_seasons;
    QString editRequestsPath() const;
    bool saveEditRequests(const QVector<EditRequest> &requests, QString *error = nullptr);
    void setEditRequests(const QVector<EditRequest> &requests); // keeps memory + file + watcher in step
    QVector<EditRequest> m_requests;
    class QFileSystemWatcher *m_watcher = nullptr;
};

#pragma once
#include <QString>

// Who is using the terminal. Chosen once at sign-in.
enum class UserRole
{
    Cashier, // records orders, edits orders (paid ones after manager approval), views products, prints bills
    Manager  // everything a cashier can, plus categories, products, inventory, discounts and reports
};

// Sidebar pages, in sidebar order. MainWindow::Page has exactly the same values.
namespace Pages
{
    enum Id
    {
        Dashboard,
        RecordOrder,
        Orders,
        Inventory,
        Products,
        Categories,
        Scanner,
        Bills,
        Reports,
        Discounts,
        Settings,
        Count
    };
}

// Single place that decides what each role may open (SRP).
// Adding a role or changing a permission only touches this file (OCP for the rest of the UI).
namespace RolePolicy
{
inline bool isManagerOnly(int page)
{
    return page == Pages::Inventory || page == Pages::Categories || page == Pages::Reports ||
           page == Pages::Discounts;
}

inline bool canOpen(UserRole role, int page)
{
    if (page < 0 || page >= Pages::Count)
        return false;
    return role == UserRole::Manager || !isManagerOnly(page);
}

// Only a manager may add or edit products, generate barcodes and change stock (Products page is view-only for cashiers).
inline bool canEditCatalogue(UserRole role) { return role == UserRole::Manager; }

// First page shown after signing in.
inline int startPage(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? int(Pages::Dashboard) : int(Pages::RecordOrder);
}

inline QString name(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? QStringLiteral("Manager") : QStringLiteral("Cashier");
}

// "Terminal Manager" / "Terminal Cashier" (sidebar user card)
inline QString title(UserRole role)
{
    return role == UserRole::Manager ? QStringLiteral("Terminal Manager") : QStringLiteral("Terminal Cashier");
}
} // namespace RolePolicy

FILE: /Frontend/legacy-camera/CameraWidget.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "CameraWidget.h"
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <opencv2/imgcodecs.hpp>
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
CameraWidget::CameraWidget(QWidget *parent)
    : QWidget(parent), view(new QLabel(this)), status(new QLabel("Camera is off", this)),
      toggle(new QPushButton("Start camera", this)), upload(new QPushButton("Upload image", this))
{
    auto *Layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    Layout->addWidget(view);
    view->setMinimumSize(640, 480);
    view->setScaledContents(true);
    // NEW: buttons + status label under the picture
    auto *buttons = new QHBoxLayout();
    buttons->addWidget(toggle);
    buttons->addWidget(upload);
    Layout->addLayout(buttons);
    Layout->addWidget(status);
    connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &CameraWidget::onTick);
    connect(toggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CameraWidget::onToggle);
    connect(upload, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &CameraWidget::onUpload);
}
void CameraWidget::start()
{
    if (scanner.openCamera())
    {
        timer.start(30);
        toggle->setText("Stop camera");
        setStatus("Point a barcode at the camera");
    }
    else
    {
        // NEW: error handling - tell the user, and offer the upload instead
        const QString message = QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage());
        setStatus(message + " - use Upload image");
        emit scanError(message);
    }
}
void CameraWidget::stop()
{
    timer.stop();
    scanner.closeCamera(); // NEW: really release the camera, otherwise its light stays on
    toggle->setText("Start camera");
    setStatus("Camera is off");
}
// NEW: Start / Stop button
void CameraWidget::onToggle()
{
    if (timer.isActive())
        stop();
    else
        start();
}
// NEW: upload fallback, choose a picture of a barcode
void CameraWidget::onUpload()
{
    const QString path = QFileDialog::getOpenFileName(this, "Choose a barcode picture", QString(),
                                                      "Pictures (*.png *.jpg *.jpeg *.bmp)");
    if (!path.isEmpty())
        scanImage(path);
}
void CameraWidget::scanImage(const QString &path)
{
    QFile file(path); // read the bytes ourselves, so file names with special letters also work on Windows
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
    {
        setStatus("Cannot open the picture");
        emit scanError("Cannot open the picture");
        return;
    }
    const QByteArray bytes = file.readAll();
    const cv::Mat picture = cv::imdecode(cv::Mat(1, bytes.size(), CV_8UC1, (void *)bytes.constData()), cv::IMREAD_COLOR);
    if (picture.empty())
    {
        setStatus("This file is not a picture");
        emit scanError("This file is not a picture");
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect box;
    if (scanner.frameRead(six, box, picture))
    {
        setStatus("Scanned " + QString::fromStdString(six));
        emit barcodeScan(QString::fromStdString(six));
    }
    else
    {
        const QString message = scanner.badCheckDigit() ? "Wrong check digit in the picture" : "No barcode found in the picture";
        setStatus(message);
        emit scanError(message);
    }
}
void CameraWidget::setStatus(const QString &text)
{
    status->setText(text);
}
QImage CameraWidget::toQImage(const cv::Mat &BGR)
{
    cv::Mat RGB;
    cv::cvtColor(BGR, RGB, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
    QImage img(RGB.data, RGB.cols, RGB.rows, (int)RGB.step, QImage::Format_RGB888);
    return img.copy();
}
void CameraWidget::onTick()
{
    cv::Mat frame;

    if (!scanner.readFrame(frame))
    {
        stop();
        setStatus(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()) + " - use Upload image");
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect Box;
    bool found = scanner.frameRead(six, Box, frame);
    if (Box.area() > 0)
        cv::rectangle(frame, Box, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 2);
    view->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(toQImage(frame)));
    if (found)
    {
        // NEW: duplicate-scan prevention - the same barcode still in front of the camera is ignored for 2 seconds
        const QString code = QString::fromStdString(six);
        if (code == lastCode && lastClock.isValid() && lastClock.elapsed() < 2000)
            return;
        lastCode = code;
        lastClock.restart();
        setStatus("Scanned " + code);
        emit barcodeScan(code);
    }
    else if (scanner.badCheckDigit())
        setStatus("Wrong check digit - hold the barcode steady");
}

#pragma once
#include <QWidget>

class QLabel;
class CameraWidget : public QWidget
{
    Q_OBJECT
public:
    explicit CameraWidget(QWidget *parent = nullptr);
    void start();
    void stop();
signals:
    void barcodeScan(const QString &six);
    void scanError(const QString &message);
private slots:
    void onTick();

private:
    // OpenCV picture to Qt picture
    static QImage toQImage(constcv::Mat &BGR);
    // backend work of decoding and scanning
    cameraScanner scanner;
    QLabel *view;

};

FILE: /Frontend/legacy-camera/main.cpp

#include <QApplication>
#include "CameraWidget.h"

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // starts Qt
    CameraWidget cam;             // your widget, as its own window
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::barcodeScan, [&cam](const QString &six)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Scanned: " + six); // show the digits in the title bar
                     });
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::scanError, [&cam](const QString &msg)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Error: " + msg); // show the problem instead
                     });
    cam.show();        // open the window
    cam.start();       // open the camera (connect first, start last)
    return app.exec(); // keep the window alive
}

// "Request edit" (cashier): asks a manager for permission to edit a paid order (requirement 3).
// The request is saved by UiStore::requestEdit; a manager approves or rejects it on the Orders page.
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace Dialogs
{
    bool requestEditReason(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &order, QString *reason)
    {
        Ui::Modal d(parent, "unlock", "Request edit", "A manager has to approve before a paid order can be changed.",
                    QString(), 500);

        auto *summary = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *sv = new QVBoxLayout(summary);
        sv->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 10);
        sv->setSpacing(2);
        auto *title = W::label(QString("Order #%1  ·  %2").arg(order.id, W::money(order.total)), "h2");
        sv->addWidget(title);
        sv->addWidget(W::label((order.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in Customer") : order.customer) + "  ·  " +
                                   order.paymentTime().toString("d MMM yyyy, h:mm AP"),
                               "muted"));
        d.body()->addWidget(summary);

        d.body()->addSpacing(4);
        d.body()->addWidget(W::label("Reason", "caption"));
        auto *why = new QComboBox;
        why->setEditable(true);
        why->setMinimumHeight(40);
        why->addItems({"Wrong quantity entered", "Wrong item scanned", "Price or discount correction",
                       "Customer returned an item", "Customer request"});
        d.body()->addWidget(why);

        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel", "flat");
        auto *send = W::button("Send to manager", "primary", "check");
        send->setMinimumHeight(40);
        send->setDefault(true);
        d.footer()->addStretch();
        d.footer()->addWidget(cancel);
        d.footer()->addWidget(send);
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);
        QObject::connect(send, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);

        why->setFocus();
        if (d.exec() != QDialog::Accepted)
            return false;
        if (reason)
            *reason = why->currentText().trimmed();
        return true;
    }
}

#include "AppState.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "BarcodeArt.h"
#include "integration/ProductAdapter.h"
#include "billing/BillingService.h"
#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QFile>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QSettings>
#include <QtMath>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <map>

#ifndef BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR
#define BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR ""
#endif

ProductInfo ProductInfo::from(const Product& p, const QString& rootDir)
{
    ProductInfo i;
    i.id = p.getProductID();
    i.sku = QString::fromStdString(p.getAcronym());
    i.ean = QString::fromStdString(p.getBarcode());
    i.name = QString::fromStdString(p.getProductName());
    i.unit = QString::fromStdString(p.getMeasurementUnitName());
    i.price = p.getUnitPrice();
    i.tax = p.getTax();
    i.discount = p.getDiscount();
    i.stock = p.getAvailableQuantity();
    i.categoryId = QString::fromStdString(p.getCategoryId());
    const QString path = QString::fromStdString(p.getBarcodeFilePath());
    i.imagePath = QFileInfo(path).isAbsolute() ? path : QDir(rootDir).filePath(path);
    return i;
}

int OrderRecord::itemCount() const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
        n += l.qty;
    return n;
}

void OrderRecord::recompute()
{
    subtotal = discount = tax = 0;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
    {
        subtotal += l.price * l.qty;
        discount += l.discount * l.qty;
        tax += l.tax * l.qty;
    }
    total = subtotal - discount + tax;
}

QString OrderRecord::summary() const
{
    if (lines.isEmpty())
        return "-";
    QStringList names;
    for (const auto& l : lines)
        names << l.name;
    QString s = QString("%1 item%2  ").arg(itemCount()).arg(itemCount() == 1 ? "" : "s");
    s += names.mid(0, 2).join(", ");
    if (names.size() > 2)
        s += QString(", +%1").arg(names.size() - 2);
    return s;
}

AppState& AppState::instance()
{
    static AppState s;
    return s;
}

QString AppState::locateDataDir()
{
    QStringList candidates;
    const QByteArray env = qgetenv("BARCODEHUB_DATA");
    if (!env.isEmpty())
        candidates << QString::fromLocal8Bit(env);
    const QString app = QCoreApplication::applicationDirPath();
    candidates << app + "/data" << app + "/../data" << app + "/../../data" << app + "/../../../data"
        << QDir::currentPath() + "/data" << QString(BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR) + "/data";
    for (const auto& c : candidates)
        if (QFileInfo::exists(c + "/Product.txt"))
            return QDir::cleanPath(c);
    return QString();
}

Settings Settings::load()
{
    QSettings store(QSettings::defaultFormat(), QSettings::UserScope, "BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS");
    store.beginGroup("terminal");
    Settings settings;
    settings.lowStockThreshold = qBound(0, store.value("lowStockThreshold", settings.lowStockThreshold).toInt(), 1000);
    settings.autoAddScans = store.value("addScansToCart", settings.autoAddScans).toBool();
    settings.beepOnScan = store.value("beepOnScan", settings.beepOnScan).toBool();
    using Payment = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode;
    const auto payment = static_cast<Payment>(store.value("defaultPayment", int(Payment::Cash)).toInt());
    if (payment == Payment::Card || payment == Payment::Wallet)
        settings.defaultPayment = payment;
    settings.receiptA4 = store.value("receiptA4", settings.receiptA4).toBool();
    settings.receiptFolder = store.value("receiptFolder").toString();
    return settings;
}

bool Settings::save() const
{
    QSettings store(QSettings::defaultFormat(), QSettings::UserScope, "BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS");
    store.beginGroup("terminal");
    store.setValue("lowStockThreshold", lowStockThreshold);
    store.setValue("addScansToCart", autoAddScans);
    store.setValue("beepOnScan", beepOnScan);
    store.setValue("defaultPayment", int(defaultPayment));
    store.setValue("receiptA4", receiptA4);
    store.setValue("receiptFolder", receiptFolder);
    store.sync();
    return store.status() == QSettings::NoError;
}

AppState::AppState() : m_settings(Settings::load())
{
    const QString dir = locateDataDir();
    if (!dir.isEmpty())
        loadProducts(dir + "/Product.txt");
    if (!dir.isEmpty())
    {
        const std::string root = dir.toStdString();
        m_orderManager = std::make_unique<barcodehub::order::OrderManager>(root + "/Order.txt", root + "/OrderDetail.txt");
        m_billRepository = std::make_unique<barcodehub::billing::BillRepository>(root + "/Bill.txt");
        refreshOrders();
    }
}

bool AppState::loadProducts(const QString& path)
{
    m_root = QDir::cleanPath(QFileInfo(path).absolutePath() + "/..");
    m_productPath = path;
    const bool ok = m_manager.loadProducts(path.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    emit changed();
    return ok;
}

void AppState::refreshCache()
{
    m_products.clear();
    for (const auto& p : m_manager.getProducts())
        m_products.push_back(ProductInfo::from(p, m_root));
}

QStringList AppState::productIssues() const
{
    QStringList out;
    const auto& r = m_manager.getLastLoad();
    if (!r.fileOpened)
        out << QString::fromStdString(r.summary());
    for (const auto& i : r.issues)
        if (i.line > 0)
            out << QString("%1 line %2: %3")
            .arg(i.severity == IssueSeverity::Error ? "Error  " : "Warning")
            .arg(i.line)
            .arg(QString::fromStdString(i.message));
    return out;
}

std::optional<ProductInfo> AppState::findByCode(const QString& code) const
{
    if (const Product* p = m_manager.searchByCode(code.trimmed().toStdString()))
        return ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root);
    return std::nullopt;
}

QVector<ProductInfo> AppState::searchText(const QString& query) const
{
    QVector<ProductInfo> out;
    for (const Product* p : m_manager.searchByText(query.trimmed().toStdString()))
        out.push_back(ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root));
    return out;
}

ProductDescriptionView AppState::describe(int productId) const
{
    ProductDescriptionView v;
    const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(productId);
    if (!p)
        return v;
    const ProductDescription d = ProductDescriber(m_settings.lowStockThreshold).describe(*p);
    v.title = QString::fromStdString(d.title);
    v.level = QString::fromStdString(d.stockLevel);
    for (const auto& f : d.fields)
        v.fields.push_back({ QString::fromStdString(f.first), QString::fromStdString(f.second) });
    v.imagePath = ProductInfo::from(*p, m_root).imagePath;
    return v;
}

QString AppState::stockProblem(int productId, int alreadyInOrder, int toAdd) const
{
    const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(productId);
    if (!p)
        return QString(); // custom lines are not stock-controlled
    const StockCheck c = m_stock.checkAdd(*p, alreadyInOrder, toAdd);
    return c.ok() ? QString() : QString::fromStdString(c.message);
}

QVector<ProductInfo> AppState::lowStock() const
{
    QVector<ProductInfo> v;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        if (p.stock <= m_settings.lowStockThreshold)
            v << p;
    std::sort(v.begin(), v.end(), [](const ProductInfo& a, const ProductInfo& b) { return a.stock < b.stock; });
    return v;
}

void AppState::reorder(int id)
{
    m_reordered.insert(id);
    emit changed();
}

void AppState::reorderAllLow()
{
    for (const auto& p : lowStock())
        m_reordered.insert(p.id);
    emit changed();
}

QString AppState::nextOrderId()
{
    if (!m_orderManager)
        return "--------";
    std::string error;
    const std::string id = m_orderManager->nextOrderId(&error);
    return id.empty() ? QString("--------") : QString::fromStdString(id);
}

// ======================================================================== orders: paying, editing, holding
QStringList AppState::recordFiles() const
{
    const QString d = dataDir();
    return { m_productPath, d + "/Order.txt", d + "/OrderDetail.txt", d + "/Bill.txt", d + "/BillDetail.txt" };
}

bool AppState::cartFromLines(const QVector<ReceiptLine>& lines, QString* error)
{
    namespace bh = barcodehub;
    m_orderManager->clearCart();
    std::string why;
    for (const auto& line : lines)
    {
        bh::order::ProductSnapshot snapshot;
        if (line.productId > 0)
        {
            const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(line.productId);
            if (!p || QString::fromStdString(p->getAcronym()) != line.acronym)
            {
                if (error) *error = "A product in the order no longer exists. Refresh and retry.";
                m_orderManager->clearCart();
                return false;
            }
            snapshot = bh::integration::toSnapshot(*p);
            snapshot.discountPerUnit = line.discount; // product discount (seasonal/special are bill-level)
            snapshot.availableQuantity = 1000000;     // stock is checked separately (holds take no stock)
        }
        else
        {
            snapshot.productId = "CUSTOM-" + line.acronym.toStdString();
            snapshot.barcode = snapshot.productId;
            snapshot.acronym = line.acronym.toStdString();
            snapshot.name = line.name.toStdString();
            snapshot.measurementUnit = "count";
            snapshot.unitPrice = line.price;
            snapshot.taxPerUnit = line.tax;
            snapshot.discountPerUnit = line.discount;
            snapshot.availableQuantity = 1000000;
        }
        if (!m_orderManager->addToCart(snapshot, line.qty, &why))
        {
            if (error) *error = QString::fromStdString(why);
            m_orderManager->clearCart();
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

bool AppState::addOrder(OrderRecord& o, QString* error, const QString& existingId)
{
    return settle(o, existingId, false, error);
}

bool AppState::replaceOrder(const QString& orderId, OrderRecord& edited, QString* error)
{
    return settle(edited, orderId, true, error);
}

QMap<int, int> AppState::stockHeldBy(const QString& orderId) const
{
    QMap<int, int> held;
    for (const auto& o : m_orders)
        if (o.id == orderId && o.status == "Paid")
            for (const auto& l : o.lines)
                if (l.productId > 0)
                    held[l.productId] += l.qty;
    return held;
}

// One routine for every payment, so a new order, a resumed held order and an edited paid order follow the
// same rules. Everything it writes (Product, Order, OrderDetail, Bill, BillDetail) is backed up first and put
// back if any step fails, so a failed checkout never leaves half-saved records or wrong stock.
bool AppState::settle(OrderRecord& o, const QString& existingId, bool editPaid, QString* error)
{
    namespace bh = barcodehub;
    if (error) error->clear();
    if (!m_orderManager || !m_billRepository)
    {
        if (error) *error = "Data directory is unavailable. Check BARCODEHUB_DATA and Product.txt.";
        return false;
    }
    if (o.lines.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = "The order is empty.";
        return false;
    }

    std::string why;
    bh::order::Order existing;
    if (!existingId.isEmpty())
    {
        if (!m_orderManager->findOrder(existingId.toStdString(), existing, &why))
        {
            if (error) *error = "Order " + existingId + " was not found.";
            return false;
        }
        const auto st = existing.status;
        if (st == bh::order::OrderStatus::Archived)
        {
            if (error) *error = "Order " + existingId + " is archived and cannot be changed.";
            return false;
        }
        if (editPaid && st != bh::order::OrderStatus::Paid)
        {
            if (error) *error = "Order " + existingId + " is not a paid order.";
            return false;
        }
        if (!editPaid && st == bh::order::OrderStatus::Paid)
        {
            if (error) *error = "Order " + existingId + " is already paid.";
            return false;
        }
        if (editPaid)
            for (const auto& rec : m_orders)
                if (rec.id == existingId && editDaysLeft(rec) == 0)
                {
                    if (error) *error = "The 7-day edit window of order " + existingId + " is over.";
                    return false;
                }
    }

    bh::order::PaymentMode mode = bh::order::PaymentMode::Cash;
    if (!bh::order::paymentModeFromString(o.payment.toLower().toStdString(), mode))
    {
        if (error) *error = "Unsupported payment method.";
        return false;
    }
    bh::billing::PaymentRequest payment;
    payment.mode = mode;
    payment.merchant = o.merchant.toStdString();
    payment.amountTendered = o.tendered;
    const auto methods = bh::billing::PaymentMethodRegistry::withDefaults();
    if (!methods.find(mode))
    {
        if (error) *error = "Payment method unavailable.";
        return false;
    }

    // ---- back up every record file, so any failure below can put them all back
    QMap<QString, QByteArray> backup;
    QSet<QString> absent;
    for (const QString& f : recordFiles())
    {
        QFile file(f);
        if (!file.exists()) { absent.insert(f); continue; }
        if (!file.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
        {
            if (error) *error = "Cannot back up " + QFileInfo(f).fileName() + " before saving.";
            return false;
        }
        backup[f] = file.readAll();
    }
    auto fail = [&](const QString& message) {
        bool clean = true;
        for (auto it = backup.constBegin(); it != backup.constEnd(); ++it)
        {
            QSaveFile out(it.key());
            clean = out.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly) && out.write(it.value()) == it.value().size() && out.commit() && clean;
        }
        for (const QString& f : absent)
            QFile::remove(f);
        m_orderManager->clearCart();
        m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
        refreshCache();
        refreshOrders();
        emit changed();
        if (error) *error = message + (clean ? QString() : QString(" (restoring the files failed - check the data folder)"));
        return false;
    };

    // ---- an edited paid order first gives back the stock it took, then takes what the edited lines need
    if (editPaid)
    {
        QMap<int, int> levels;
        const auto held = stockHeldBy(existingId);
        for (auto it = held.constBegin(); it != held.constEnd(); ++it)
            if (const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(it.key()))
                levels[it.key()] = p->getAvailableQuantity() + it.value();
        QString stockError;
        if (!levels.isEmpty() && !setStockLevels(levels, &stockError))
            return fail(stockError);
    }

    // ---- the backend cart (validates every line)
    std::map<std::string, bh::order::ProductSnapshot> manualProducts;
    std::map<std::string, double> lineDiscount; // product id -> the product's own per-unit discount
    std::map<int, int> stockToDeduct;
    QString cartError;
    if (!cartFromLines(o.lines, &cartError))
        return fail(cartError);
    for (const auto& item : m_orderManager->cart())
    {
        if (item.product.productId.rfind("CUSTOM-", 0) == 0)
            manualProducts[item.product.productId] = item.product;
        else
        {
            lineDiscount[item.product.productId] = item.product.discountPerUnit;
            stockToDeduct[std::stoi(item.product.productId)] += static_cast<int>(item.quantity);
        }
    }
    for (const auto& [id, qty] : stockToDeduct)
        if (const auto* p = m_manager.searchByID(id); !p || qty > p->getAvailableQuantity())
            return fail(QString("Not enough stock of %1 (%2 available).")
                            .arg(p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getProductName()) : QString::number(id))
                            .arg(p ? p->getAvailableQuantity() : 0));

    // ---- totals must match the backend (seasonal and special discounts are bill-level amounts)
    const double seasonal = std::max(0.0, o.seasonal), special = std::max(0.0, o.special);
    o.recompute(); // line totals only
    o.seasonal = seasonal;
    o.special = special;
    o.total = std::round((o.total - seasonal - special) * 100.0) / 100.0;
    const double payable = std::round((m_orderManager->totalPayable() - seasonal - special) * 100.0) / 100.0;
    if (std::fabs(o.total - payable) > 0.011)
        return fail("Cart totals differ from backend totals. Please refresh the order.");
    const auto accepted = methods.find(mode)->process(payment, payable);
    if (!accepted.ok)
        return fail(QString::fromStdString(accepted.error));

    // ---- Order.txt / OrderDetail.txt: a new order, or the existing one keeps its Order ID
    const std::string name = o.customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : o.customer.trimmed().toStdString();
    const std::string contact = (o.contact == "-" ? QString() : o.contact.trimmed()).toStdString();
    std::string orderId;
    if (existingId.isEmpty())
    {
        bh::order::Order created;
        if (!m_orderManager->createOrder(name, contact, created, &why))
            return fail(QString::fromStdString(why));
        orderId = created.id;
    }
    else
    {
        if (!m_orderManager->saveCartToOrder(existingId.toStdString(), name, contact, &why))
            return fail(QString::fromStdString(why));
        orderId = existingId.toStdString();
    }

    // ---- stock (Product.txt)
    for (const auto& [id, qty] : stockToDeduct)
        m_manager.deductStock(id, qty);
    if (!m_manager.saveProducts(&why))
        return fail("Could not save stock: " + QString::fromStdString(why));

    // ---- bill (Bill.txt / BillDetail.txt); an edited paid order keeps its Bill ID and payment date
    bh::billing::BillingService service(*m_orderManager, *m_billRepository,
        [this, &manualProducts, &lineDiscount](const std::string& id, bh::order::ProductSnapshot& snapshot) {
            const auto custom = manualProducts.find(id);
            if (custom != manualProducts.end()) { snapshot = custom->second; return true; }
            try {
                const Product* p = m_manager.searchByID(std::stoi(id));
                if (p) {
                    snapshot = bh::integration::toSnapshot(*p);
                    const auto d = lineDiscount.find(id);
                    if (d != lineDiscount.end()) snapshot.discountPerUnit = d->second;
                    return true;
                }
            }
            catch (...) {}
            return false;
        });
    bh::billing::Bill bill;
    double change = 0;
    bh::billing::BillAdjustments adjustments;
    adjustments.seasonalDiscount = seasonal;
    adjustments.specialDiscount = special;
    const bool billed = editPaid ? service.rebill(orderId, payment, bill, &change, &why, adjustments)
                                 : service.checkout(orderId, payment, bill, &change, &why, adjustments);
    if (!billed)
        return fail("Checkout failed: " + QString::fromStdString(why));

    bh::order::Order saved;
    m_orderManager->findOrder(orderId, saved, &why);
    o.id = QString::fromStdString(orderId);
    o.billId = QString::fromStdString(bill.id);
    o.time = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(saved.date + " " + saved.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    o.paidAt = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(bill.date + " " + bill.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
    o.status = "Paid";
    o.total = bill.totals.payable;
    o.subtotal = bill.totals.withoutTax;
    o.discount = bill.totals.discount + bill.seasonalDiscount + bill.specialDiscount; // OrderRecord: all discounts
    o.seasonal = bill.seasonalDiscount;
    o.special = bill.specialDiscount;
    o.tax = bill.totals.tax;
    o.tendered = bill.amountReceived;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    // The current session knows the manual item descriptions even if the catalogue doesn't.
    for (auto& history : m_orders)
        if (history.id == o.id) { history = o; break; }
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

int AppState::editDaysLeft(const OrderRecord& o) const
{
    if (o.status != "Paid")
        return -1;
    const QDateTime paid = o.paymentTime();
    const qint64 window = qint64(kEditWindowDays) * 86400;
    const qint64 secs = paid.secsTo(QDateTime::currentDateTime());
    if (secs > window)
        return 0;
    return qBound(1, int((window - secs + 86399) / 86400), kEditWindowDays);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- on-hold orders
QString AppState::holdOrder(const QString& customer, const QString& contact, const QVector<ReceiptLine>& lines,
                            const QString& existingId, QString* error)
{
    namespace bh = barcodehub;
    if (error) error->clear();
    if (!m_orderManager)
    {
        if (error) *error = "Data directory is unavailable.";
        return {};
    }
    if (lines.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = "Nothing to hold - the order is empty.";
        return {};
    }
    QString cartError;
    if (!cartFromLines(lines, &cartError))
    {
        if (error) *error = cartError;
        return {};
    }
    const std::string name = customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : customer.trimmed().toStdString();
    const std::string phone = (contact.trimmed() == "-" ? QString() : contact.trimmed()).toStdString();
    std::string why;
    bh::order::Order order;
    bool ok = false;
    if (!existingId.isEmpty() && m_orderManager->findOrder(existingId.toStdString(), order, &why) &&
        (order.status == bh::order::OrderStatus::OnHold || order.status == bh::order::OrderStatus::InProgress))
    {
        ok = m_orderManager->saveCartToOrder(order.id, name, phone, &why) &&
             m_orderManager->findOrder(order.id, order, &why);
    }
    else
        ok = m_orderManager->createOrder(name, phone, order, &why);
    if (ok)
    {
        order.status = bh::order::OrderStatus::OnHold;
        ok = m_orderManager->updateOrder(order, &why);
    }
    m_orderManager->clearCart();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
    if (!ok)
    {
        if (error) *error = "Could not put the order on hold: " + QString::fromStdString(why);
        return {};
    }
    return QString::fromStdString(order.id);
}

bool AppState::discardHeldOrder(const QString& orderId, QString* error)
{
    if (!m_orderManager)
        return false;
    std::string why;
    const bool ok = m_orderManager->discardOrder(orderId.toStdString(), &why);
    if (!ok && error)
        *error = QString::fromStdString(why);
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
    return ok;
}

QVector<OrderRecord> AppState::heldOrders() const
{
    QVector<OrderRecord> held;
    for (const auto& o : m_orders)
        if (o.status == "On Hold" || o.status == "In Progress")
            held << o;
    return held;
}

void AppState::refreshOrders()
{
    m_orders.clear();
    m_baseSales = 0;
    m_baseOrders = 0;
    if (!m_orderManager || !m_billRepository) return;
    std::vector<barcodehub::order::Order> orders;
    std::vector<barcodehub::order::OrderDetail> details;
    std::vector<barcodehub::billing::Bill> bills;
    std::string error;
    if (!m_orderManager->loadOrders(orders, &error) ||
        !m_orderManager->loadOrderDetails(details, &error) ||
        !m_billRepository->loadAll(bills, &error))
    {
        qWarning("Could not load persisted orders/bills: %s", error.c_str());
        return;
    }
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    for (const auto& entry : orders)
    {
        OrderRecord rec;
        rec.id = QString::fromStdString(entry.id);
        rec.time = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(entry.date + " " + entry.time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
        rec.customer = QString::fromStdString(entry.customerName);
        rec.contact = entry.customerContact.empty() ? "-" : QString::fromStdString(entry.customerContact);
        rec.tier = "Guest";
        rec.shift = "POS #01";
        rec.status = QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::order::toString(entry.status));
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::InProgress) rec.status = "In Progress";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::OnHold) rec.status = "On Hold";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::Paid) rec.status = "Paid";
        if (entry.status == barcodehub::order::OrderStatus::Archived) rec.status = "Archived";
        rec.payment = QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::order::toString(entry.paymentMode));
        rec.merchant = QString::fromStdString(entry.paymentMerchant);
        const auto bill = std::find_if(bills.begin(), bills.end(), [&](const auto& b) { return b.orderId == entry.id; });
        if (bill != bills.end())
        {
            rec.billId = QString::fromStdString(bill->id);
            rec.paidAt = QDateTime::fromString(QString::fromStdString(bill->date + " " + bill->time), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss");
        }
        int detailIndex = 0;
        for (const auto& detail : details)
        {
            if (detail.orderId != entry.id) continue;
            const Product* p = nullptr;
            try { p = m_manager.searchByID(std::stoi(detail.productId)); }
            catch (...) {}
            ReceiptLine line;
            line.name = p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getProductName()) : QString::fromStdString(detail.productId);
            line.acronym = p ? QString::fromStdString(p->getAcronym()) : QString::fromStdString(detail.productId);
            line.productId = p ? p->getProductID() : 0;
            line.qty = static_cast<int>(detail.quantity);
            line.price = p ? p->getUnitPrice() : 0;
            line.tax = p ? p->getTax() : 0;
            line.discount = p ? p->getDiscount() : 0;
            if (bill != bills.end() && detailIndex < static_cast<int>(bill->lines.size()))
            {
                const auto& saved = bill->lines[detailIndex];
                line.acronym = QString::fromStdString(saved.productAcronym);
                if (!p) line.name = line.acronym;
                line.price = saved.unitPrice;
                line.discount = saved.discountPerUnit;
            }
            rec.lines.push_back(line);
            ++detailIndex;
        }
        rec.recompute();
        if (bill != bills.end())
        {
            rec.subtotal = bill->totals.withoutTax;
            rec.discount = bill->totals.discount + bill->seasonalDiscount + bill->specialDiscount; // all discounts
            rec.seasonal = bill->seasonalDiscount;
            rec.special = bill->specialDiscount;
            rec.tax = bill->totals.tax;
            rec.total = bill->totals.payable;
            rec.tendered = bill->amountReceived;
        }
        if (rec.time.date() == today)
            ++m_baseOrders;
        if (rec.status == "Paid" && rec.paymentTime().date() == today)
            m_baseSales += rec.total;
        m_orders.push_back(std::move(rec));
    }
    std::sort(m_orders.begin(), m_orders.end(), [](const OrderRecord& a, const OrderRecord& b) { return a.time > b.time; });
}

// ======================================================================== data directory / reload
QString AppState::dataDir() const
{
    return m_productPath.isEmpty() ? QString() : QFileInfo(m_productPath).absolutePath();
}

void AppState::reloadAll()
{
    if (!m_productPath.isEmpty())
        m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
}

// ======================================================================== catalogue (manager)
namespace
{
    Product toProduct(const ProductInfo& p, const QString& barcodeFile)
    {
        MeasurementUnit unit = MeasurementUnit::Count;
        parseMeasurementUnit(p.unit.toStdString(), unit);
        const QString category = p.categoryId.isEmpty() ? p.ean.left(2) : p.categoryId;
        return Product(p.id, p.ean.toStdString(), p.sku.toStdString(), p.name.toStdString(), unit, p.price, p.tax,
                       p.discount, p.stock, barcodeFile.toStdString(), category.toStdString());
    }
}

int AppState::nextProductId() const
{
    int maxId = 0;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        maxId = qMax(maxId, p.id);
    return maxId + 1;
}

int AppState::nextSerial(const QString& categoryId) const
{
    int maxSerial = 0;
    for (const auto& p : m_products)
        if (p.ean.size() == 8 && p.ean.left(2) == categoryId)
            maxSerial = qMax(maxSerial, p.ean.mid(2, 5).toInt());
    return maxSerial + 1;
}

QString AppState::nextBarcode(const QString& categoryId) const
{
    if (categoryId.size() != 2)
        return QString();
    int serial = nextSerial(categoryId);
    while (serial <= 99999)
    {
        const QString code = BarcodeArt::makeCode(categoryId, serial);
        bool taken = false;
        for (const auto& p : m_products)
            if (p.ean == code) { taken = true; break; }
        if (!taken)
            return code;
        ++serial;
    }
    return QString();
}

bool AppState::backendAcceptsPrefix(const QString& categoryId) const
{
    const QString code = BarcodeArt::makeCode(categoryId, 1);
    if (code.isEmpty())
        return false;
    const Product probe(1, code.toStdString(), "PROBE", "Probe", MeasurementUnit::Count, 1, 0, 0, 0, "data/barcodes/probe.png");
    return ProductValidator::standard().validate(probe).ok();
}

QString AppState::validateProduct(const ProductInfo& p, bool isNew) const
{
    if (p.name.trimmed().isEmpty())
        return "Product name is required.";
    if (p.sku.trimmed().isEmpty())
        return "Acronym is required.";
    if (p.ean.isEmpty())
        return "Generate a barcode first.";
    const QString category = p.categoryId.isEmpty() ? p.ean.left(2) : p.categoryId;
    if (!UiStore::instance().category(category))
        return QString("Category %1 is not in PCategory.txt. Add it on the Categories page first.").arg(category);
    const Product candidate = toProduct(p, "data/barcodes/" + p.ean + ".png");
    const ValidationReport report = ProductValidator::standard().validate(candidate);
    if (!report.ok())
        return QString::fromStdString(report.errors.front());
    for (const auto& o : m_products)
    {
        if (!isNew && o.id == p.id)
            continue;
        if (o.ean == p.ean)
            return QString("Barcode %1 already belongs to \"%2\".").arg(p.ean, o.name);
        if (o.sku.compare(p.sku, Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0)
            return QString("Acronym %1 is already used by \"%2\".").arg(p.sku.toUpper(), o.name);
    }
    return QString();
}

namespace
{
    // Saves `products` to Product.txt; on failure puts the previous file content back.
    bool saveCatalogue(const std::string& path, const std::vector<Product>& products, QString* error)
    {
        QFile f(QString::fromStdString(path));
        QByteArray backup;
        if (f.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly)) { backup = f.readAll(); f.close(); }
        std::string why;
        if (ProductFileWriter().save(path, products, &why))
            return true;
        if (!backup.isEmpty())
        {
            QSaveFile restore(QString::fromStdString(path));
            if (restore.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly)) { restore.write(backup); restore.commit(); }
        }
        if (error) *error = "Could not save Product.txt: " + QString::fromStdString(why);
        return false;
    }
}

bool AppState::addProduct(ProductInfo& p, QString* error)
{
    if (m_productPath.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = "Product.txt was not found.";
        return false;
    }
    if (p.id <= 0)
        p.id = nextProductId();
    p.sku = p.sku.trimmed().toUpper();
    p.name = p.name.trimmed();
    const QString problem = validateProduct(p, true);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    const QString rel = "data/barcodes/" + p.ean + ".png";
    QString imgError;
    if (!BarcodeArt::savePng(p.ean, QDir(m_root).filePath(rel), &imgError))
    {
        if (error) *error = imgError;
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Product> all = m_manager.getProducts();
    all.push_back(toProduct(p, rel));
    if (!saveCatalogue(m_productPath.toStdString(), all, error))
        return false;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

bool AppState::updateProduct(const ProductInfo& p, QString* error)
{
    ProductInfo q = p;
    q.sku = q.sku.trimmed().toUpper();
    q.name = q.name.trimmed();
    const QString problem = validateProduct(q, false);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        if (error) *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    std::vector<Product> all = m_manager.getProducts();
    bool found = false;
    for (auto& e : all)
        if (e.getProductID() == q.id)
        {
            e = toProduct(q, QString::fromStdString(e.getBarcodeFilePath()));
            found = true;
        }
    if (!found)
    {
        if (error) *error = "That product no longer exists.";
        return false;
    }
    if (!saveCatalogue(m_productPath.toStdString(), all, error))
        return false;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    refreshOrders();
    emit changed();
    return true;
}

bool AppState::setStockLevels(const QMap<int, int>& levels, QString* error)
{
    std::vector<Product> all = m_manager.getProducts();
    for (auto& e : all)
    {
        const auto it = levels.constFind(e.getProductID());
        if (it == levels.constEnd())
            continue;
        if (it.value() < 0)
        {
            if (error) *error = "Quantity cannot be negative.";
            return false;
        }
        ProductInfo info = ProductInfo::from(e, m_root);
        info.stock = it.value();
        e = toProduct(info, QString::fromStdString(e.getBarcodeFilePath()));
    }
    if (!saveCatalogue(m_productPath.toStdString(), all, error))
        return false;
    m_manager.loadProducts(m_productPath.toStdString());
    refreshCache();
    emit changed();
    return true;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/BarcodeArt.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "BarcodeArt.h"
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QFont>
#include <QPainter>

namespace
{
    // EAN-8: left half uses the "L" codes, right half the "R" codes (R = bitwise complement of L).
    const char *kL[10] = {"0001101", "0011001", "0010011", "0111101", "0100011",
                          "0110001", "0101111", "0111011", "0110111", "0001011"};
    const char *kR[10] = {"1110010", "1100110", "1101100", "1000010", "1011100",
                          "1001110", "1010000", "1000100", "1001000", "1110100"};
    bool eightDigits(const QString &s)
    {
        if (s.size() != 8)
            return false;
        for (QChar c : s)
            if (!c.isDigit())
                return false;
        return true;
    }
}

namespace BarcodeArt
{
    QString checkDigit(const QString &seven)
    {
        if (seven.size() != 7)
            return QString();
        int sum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < 7; ++i)
            sum += seven[i].digitValue() * (i % 2 == 0 ? 3 : 1);
        return QString::number((10 - sum % 10) % 10);
    }

    QString makeCode(const QString &categoryId, int serial)
    {
        if (categoryId.size() != 2 || serial < 0 || serial > 99999)
            return QString();
        const QString seven = categoryId + QString::number(serial).rightJustified(5, '0');
        return seven + checkDigit(seven);
    }

    QString modules(const QString &code)
    {
        if (!eightDigits(code))
            return QString();
        QString m = "101";
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
            m += kL[code[i].digitValue()];
        m += "01010";
        for (int i = 4; i < 8; ++i)
            m += kR[code[i].digitValue()];
        m += "101";
        return m;
    }

    QImage render(const QString &code, int moduleWidth, int barHeight, bool withDigits)
    {
        const QString m = modules(code);
        if (m.isEmpty())
            return QImage();
        const int quiet = moduleWidth * 7;
        const int digitsH = withDigits ? moduleWidth * 8 : 0;
        const int w = quiet * 2 + m.size() * moduleWidth;
        const int h = quiet / 2 + barHeight + digitsH + quiet / 2;
        QImage img(w, h, QImage::Format_RGB32);
        img.fill(Qt::white);
        QPainter p(&img);
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(Qt::black);
        for (int i = 0; i < m.size(); ++i)
            if (m[i] == '1')
                p.drawRect(quiet + i * moduleWidth, quiet / 2, moduleWidth, barHeight);
        if (withDigits)
        {
            QFont f("Courier New");
            f.setStyleHint(QFont::Monospace);
            f.setPixelSize(moduleWidth * 6);
            p.setFont(f);
            p.setPen(Qt::black);
            p.drawText(QRect(0, quiet / 2 + barHeight, w, digitsH + quiet / 3), Qt::AlignHCenter | Qt::AlignVCenter, code);
        }
        return img;
    }

    bool savePng(const QString &code, const QString &path, QString *error)
    {
        const QImage img = render(code);
        if (img.isNull())
        {
            if (error) *error = "Barcode must be exactly 8 digits.";
            return false;
        }
        QDir().mkpath(QFileInfo(path).absolutePath());
        if (!img.save(path, "PNG"))
        {
            if (error) *error = "Could not write " + path;
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }
}

BillsPage::BillsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    root->setSpacing(8);
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader({}, "Bills & Invoices", "Find paid transactions and open their receipts."));
    auto* card = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(16, 16, 16, 12);
    v->setSpacing(8);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addWidget(W::label("Saved Bills", "h1"));
    h->addStretch();
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search by bill ID, order ID or customer");
    m_search->setAccessibleName("Search saved bills");
    m_search->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_search->setClearButtonEnabled(true);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    h->addWidget(m_search, 1);
    v->addLayout(h);
    m_table = makeTable({ "BILL NO.", "ORDER", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "PAYMENT", "TOTAL", "ACTIONS" });
    m_table->setProperty("managementTable", true);
    m_table->setAlternatingRowColors(true);
    m_table->setWordWrap(false);
    m_table->setAccessibleName("Saved bills");
    auto *header = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    for (int column : {0, 1, 2, 4, 5})
        header->setSectionResizeMode(column, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    header->setSectionResizeMode(6, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    header->setMinimumSectionSize(100);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(6, 110);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    m_summary = W::label("", "muted");
    v->addWidget(m_summary);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &BillsPage::rebuild);
    rebuild();
}

void BillsPage::rebuild()
{
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    QVector<OrderRecord> rows;
    int savedCount = 0;
    for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
    {
        if (o.status != "Paid" || o.billId.isEmpty())
            continue;
        ++savedCount;
        if (q.isEmpty() || o.id.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) || o.billId.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) || o.customer.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive))
            rows << o;
    }
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& o = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 52);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(o.billId, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(o.id));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(o.paymentTime().toString("d MMM yyyy HH:mm")));
        auto *customer = item(o.customer);
        customer->setToolTip(o.customer);
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, customer);
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(o.payment));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(o.total), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        auto* view = W::button("View Bill", "mini");
        view->setAccessibleName("View bill " + o.billId);
        h->addWidget(view);
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 6, w);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        connect(view, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::receipt(this, copy); });
    }
    m_summary->setText(savedCount == 0 ? "No paid bills yet."
                         : rows.isEmpty() ? "No paid bills match your search."
                         : QString("Showing %1 of %2 paid bills").arg(rows.size()).arg(savedCount));
}

void BillsPage::showOrder(const QString& orderId) { m_search->setText(orderId); }

#include "CameraWidget.h"
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <opencv2/imgproc.hpp>
CameraWidget::CameraWidget(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent), view(new QLabel(this))
{
    auto *Layout = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    Layout->addWidget(view);
    view->setMinimumSize(640, 480);
    view->setScaledContents(true);
    connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &CameraWidget::onTick);
}
void CameraWidget::start()
{
    if (scanner.openCamera())
        timer.start(30);
    else
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
}
void CameraWidget::stop()
{
    timer.stop();
}
QImage CameraWidget::toQImage(const cv::Mat &BGR)
{
    cv::Mat RGB;
    cv::cvtColor(BGR, RGB, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
    QImage img(RGB.data, RGB.cols, RGB.rows, (int)RGB.step, QImage::Format_RGB888);
    return img.copy();
}
void CameraWidget::onTick()
{
    cv::Mat frame;

    if (!scanner.readFrame(frame))
    {
        timer.stop();
        emit scanError(QString::fromStdString(scanner.errMessage()));
        return;
    }
    std::string six;
    cv::Rect Box;
    bool found = scanner.frameRead(six, Box, frame);
    if (Box.area() > 0)
        cv::rectangle(frame, Box, cv::Scalar(0, 255, 0), 2);
    view->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(toQImage(frame)));
    if (found)
    {
        emit barcodeScan(QString::fromStdString(six));
    }
}

void CategoriesPage::refresh() { rebuild(); validate(); }

void CategoriesPage::rebuild()
{
    auto &store = UiStore::instance();
    const QString q = m_filter->text().trimmed();
    QVector<Category> rows;
    int skus = 0;
    for (const auto &c : store.categories())
    {
        skus += store.productCount(c.id);
        if (!q.isEmpty() && !c.id.contains(q) && !c.name.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) &&
            !c.acronym.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive))
            continue;
        rows << c;
    }
    m_summary->setText(QString("%1 of %2 categories · %3 linked products").arg(rows.size()).arg(store.categories().size()).arg(skus));
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const Category c = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 46);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(c.id, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        auto *name = item(c.name, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true);
        name->setToolTip(c.name);
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, name);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, wrapW(W::badge(c.acronym, "blue")));
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, item(QString::number(store.productCount(c.id))));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(store.rangeText(c.id)));
        auto *cell = new QWidget;
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        h->setSpacing(4);
        auto *e = W::button("", "flat", "edit");
        auto *x = W::button("", "flat", "trash");
        e->setToolTip("Edit");
        x->setToolTip("Delete");
        e->setAccessibleName("Edit " + c.name);
        x->setAccessibleName("Delete " + c.name);
        h->addWidget(e);
        h->addWidget(x);
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 5, cell);
        const QString id = c.id;
        connect(e, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id] { edit(id); });
        connect(x, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id]
                {
            QString err;
            if (QMessageBox::question(this, "Delete category", "Delete category " + id + "?") != QMessageBox::Yes)
                return;
            if (!UiStore::instance().removeCategory(id, &err)) { emit toast(err); return; }
            emit toast("Category " + id + " deleted"); });
    }
}

void CategoriesPage::validate()
{
    const QString id = m_id->text().trimmed();
    m_range->setText(id.size() == 2 ? "Barcode range: " + UiStore::instance().rangeText(id)
                                   : "Enter a category ID to see its barcode range.");
    const bool pristine = id.isEmpty() && m_name->text().isEmpty() && m_acr->text().isEmpty();
    QString why;
    if (!pristine)
        why = UiStore::instance().validateCategory(id, m_name->text(), m_acr->text(), m_editing);
    // do not nag about fields the user has not reached yet
    if (id.size() == 2 && m_name->text().trimmed().isEmpty() && m_acr->text().trimmed().isEmpty() && why.startsWith("Category name"))
        why.clear();
    if (why.startsWith("Acronym must") && m_acr->text().isEmpty())
        why.clear();
    m_errorBox->setVisible(!why.isEmpty());
    m_error->setText(why);
    const bool idTaken = why.contains("already in use");
    m_suggest->setVisible(idTaken);
    m_suggest->setText("Suggested free ids:  " + UiStore::instance().freeIds(3).join("   "));
    m_submit->setEnabled(!pristine && UiStore::instance().validateCategory(id, m_name->text(), m_acr->text(), m_editing).isEmpty());
}

void CategoriesPage::resetForm()
{
    m_editing.clear();
    m_id->setEnabled(true);
    m_id->clear();
    m_name->clear();
    m_acr->clear();
    m_formTitle->setText("Create New Category");
    m_submit->setText("Add Category");
    validate();
}

void CategoriesPage::edit(const QString &id)
{
    const Category *c = UiStore::instance().category(id);
    if (!c)
        return;
    m_editing = id;
    m_id->setText(c->id);
    m_name->setText(c->name);
    m_acr->setText(c->acronym);
    m_formTitle->setText("Edit Category " + id);
    m_submit->setText("Save Category");
    validate();
}

void CategoriesPage::submit()
{
    Category c;
    c.id = m_id->text().trimmed();
    c.name = m_name->text().trimmed();
    c.acronym = m_acr->text().trimmed().toUpper();
    c.color = "#2554d8";
    QString err;
    const bool editing = !m_editing.isEmpty();
    const bool ok = editing ? UiStore::instance().updateCategory(m_editing, c, &err)
                            : UiStore::instance().addCategory(c, &err);
    if (!ok)
    {
        emit toast(err);
        return;
    }
    emit toast(QString("Category %1 %2").arg(c.id, editing ? "updated" : "added"));
    resetForm();
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/DarkModeManager.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "DarkModeManager.h"
#include "Theme.h"

#include <QApplication>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QSettings>
#include <QShortcut>
#include <QWidget>
#include <cmath>

namespace barcodehub::ui {

DarkModeManager::DarkModeManager(QObject* parent)
    : QObject(parent), m_isDark(Theme::isDark())
{
}

DarkModeManager& DarkModeManager::instance()
{
    static DarkModeManager s_instance;
    return s_instance;
}

bool DarkModeManager::isDarkMode() const
{
    return m_isDark;
}

void DarkModeManager::setDarkMode(bool enable)
{
    if (m_isDark == enable && qApp->styleSheet() == getActiveStyleSheet())
        return;

    m_isDark = enable;
    Theme::setDark(enable);
    applyTheme();
    emit themeChanged(m_isDark);
}

void DarkModeManager::toggle()
{
    setDarkMode(!m_isDark);
}

void DarkModeManager::applyTheme()
{
    if (!qApp)
        return;

    qApp->setStyleSheet(getActiveStyleSheet());

    // Repolish all active top-level windows
    for (QWidget* w : QApplication::topLevelWidgets()) {
        Theme::repolish(w);
        for (QWidget* child : w->findChildren<QWidget*>()) {
            Theme::repolish(child);
        }
    }
}

QString DarkModeManager::buildDarkPaletteStyleSheet() const
{
    return Theme::styleSheet();
}

QString DarkModeManager::getActiveStyleSheet() const
{
    QString base = Theme::styleSheet();

    // Append dedicated styles for the Dark Mode top-bar toggle button
    QString toggleStyle;
    if (m_isDark) {
        toggleStyle = QStringLiteral(R"(
            #darkModeToggle {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #233150, stop:1 #172138);
                border: 1px solid #33446c;
                border-bottom: 2px solid #0f1627;
                border-radius: 12px;
                padding: 5px 12px 5px 10px;
                font-weight: 700;
                font-size: 12.5px;
                color: #e0e9ff;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:hover {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #2d3e64, stop:1 #1e2a47);
                border-color: #4b629b;
                color: #ffffff;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:pressed {
                border-bottom: 1px solid #0f1627;
                padding-top: 6px;
                padding-bottom: 4px;
                background: #19233a;
            }
        )");
    } else {
        toggleStyle = QStringLiteral(R"(
            #darkModeToggle {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #ffffff, stop:1 #eef3ff);
                border: 1px solid #d9e3f8;
                border-bottom: 2px solid #cbd8f2;
                border-radius: 12px;
                padding: 5px 12px 5px 10px;
                font-weight: 700;
                font-size: 12.5px;
                color: #1b2a4d;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:hover {
                background: qlineargradient(x1:0, y1:0, x2:0, y2:1, stop:0 #ffffff, stop:1 #e2ecfd);
                border-color: #b9ccf2;
                color: #0f1b33;
            }
            #darkModeToggle:pressed {
                border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd8f2;
                padding-top: 6px;
                padding-bottom: 4px;
                background: #dce7fb;
            }
        )");
    }

    return base + "\n" + toggleStyle;
}

QIcon DarkModeManager::createSunIcon(const QColor& color, int size)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(size * dpr), int(size * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);

    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(size / 24.0, size / 24.0);

    // Center circular sun disk
    p.setPen(QPen(color, 1.8, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap));
    p.setBrush(QBrush(color));
    p.drawEllipse(QPointF(12.0, 12.0), 4.2, 4.2);

    // 8 radial sun rays
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
    const qreal r1 = 6.8;
    const qreal r2 = 9.8;
    for (int i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
        const qreal angle = i * (3.14159265358979323846 / 4.0);
        const qreal cosA = std::cos(angle);
        const qreal sinA = std::sin(angle);
        p.drawLine(QPointF(12.0 + r1 * cosA, 12.0 + r1 * sinA),
                   QPointF(12.0 + r2 * cosA, 12.0 + r2 * sinA));
    }

    return QIcon(pm);
}

QIcon DarkModeManager::createMoonIcon(const QColor& color, int size)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(size * dpr), int(size * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);

    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(size / 24.0, size / 24.0);

    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(QBrush(color));

    // Smooth crescent moon geometry
    QPainterPath path;
    path.moveTo(17.5, 4.5);
    path.cubicTo(11.0, 4.5, 5.8, 9.7, 5.8, 16.2);
    path.cubicTo(5.8, 20.0, 7.6, 23.3, 10.4, 25.3);
    path.cubicTo(9.0, 23.2, 8.2, 20.6, 8.2, 17.8);
    path.cubicTo(8.2, 11.5, 13.3, 6.4, 19.6, 6.4);
    path.cubicTo(20.3, 6.4, 21.0, 6.5, 21.6, 6.7);
    path.cubicTo(20.4, 5.3, 19.0, 4.5, 17.5, 4.5);
    path.closeSubpath();

    // Adjust scale/offset to fit 24x24 box
    QTransform tr;
    tr.translate(1.0, -1.5);
    tr.scale(0.9, 0.9);
    p.setTransform(tr);

    p.drawPath(path);

    return QIcon(pm);
}

QPushButton* DarkModeManager::createTopBarToggle(QWidget* parent)
{
    auto* btn = new QPushButton(parent);
    btn->setObjectName("darkModeToggle");
    btn->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);

    auto updateButtonState = [this, btn]() {
        if (m_isDark) {
            btn->setIcon(createSunIcon(QColor("#facc15"), 18)); // Vibrant warm yellow sun
            btn->setText("  Light Mode");
            btn->setToolTip("Switch to Light Theme (Alt+D)");
        } else {
            btn->setIcon(createMoonIcon(QColor("#4338ca"), 18)); // Indigo crescent moon
            btn->setText("  Dark Mode");
            btn->setToolTip("Switch to Dark Theme (Alt+D)");
        }
    };

    updateButtonState();

    connect(btn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &DarkModeManager::toggle);

    connect(this, &DarkModeManager::themeChanged, btn, [updateButtonState]() {
        updateButtonState();
    });

    // Register Alt+D keyboard shortcut
    auto* shortcut = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Alt+D"), btn);
    shortcut->setContext(Qt::ApplicationShortcut);
    connect(shortcut, &QShortcut::activated, this, &DarkModeManager::toggle);

    return btn;
}

} // namespace barcodehub::ui

#include "DashboardPage.h"

    // One KPI tile: icon, caption, big number, one supporting line.
    QFrame *statCard(const QString &caption, const QString &icon, const char *tone, const QColor &ink,
                     QLabel *&value, QLabel *&sub)
    {
        auto *c = W::card(nullptr, "dashboardPanel");
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(14, 14, 14, 14);
        auto *tile = new QLabel;
        tile->setObjectName("dashboardIcon");
        tile->setProperty("tone", tone);
        tile->setFixedSize(32, 32);
        tile->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        tile->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, ink, 18));
        h->addWidget(tile, 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(3);
        v->addWidget(W::label(caption, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "dashboardKpi");
        value->setWordWrap(true);
        v->addWidget(value);
        sub = W::label("", "muted");
        sub->setWordWrap(true);
        v->addWidget(sub);
        v->addStretch();
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        return c;
    }

    QTableWidget *table(const QStringList &headers)
    {
        auto *t = new QTableWidget(0, headers.size());
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers);
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView::SelectRows);
        t->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
        t->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setHighlightSections(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        return t;
    }
}

DashboardPage::DashboardPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    build();
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &DashboardPage::refresh);
    refresh();
    auto *dateTimer = new QTimer(this);
    connect(dateTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this]
            { if (m_dashboardDate != QDate::currentDate()) refresh(); });
    dateTimer->start(1000);
}

void DashboardPage::focusLookup()
{
    m_search->setFocus();
    m_search->selectAll();
}

void DashboardPage::build()
{
    setObjectName("page");
    auto *outer = new QVBoxLayout(this);
    outer->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    auto *scroll = new QScrollArea;
    scroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
    scroll->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    scroll->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    auto *inner = new QWidget;
    inner->setObjectName("dashboardBody");
    inner->setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground);
    scroll->setWidget(inner);
    outer->addWidget(scroll);
    auto *root = new QVBoxLayout(inner);
    root->setContentsMargins(20, 18, 20, 20);
    root->setSpacing(14);

    auto *hero = W::card(nullptr, "dashboardHero");
    auto *heroLayout = new QHBoxLayout(hero);
    heroLayout->setContentsMargins(24, 20, 24, 20);
    heroLayout->setSpacing(20);
    auto *overview = new QVBoxLayout;
    overview->setSpacing(5);
    overview->addWidget(W::label("Store activity", "dashboardHeroTitle"));
    m_date = W::label({}, "dashboardHeroDate");
    overview->addWidget(m_date);
    auto *checkout = W::button("Open checkout", "primary", "cart");
    checkout->setObjectName("dashboardCheckout");
    overview->addSpacing(5);
    overview->addWidget(checkout, 0, Qt::AlignLeft);
    connect(checkout, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] { emit navigate(Pages::RecordOrder); });
    heroLayout->addLayout(overview, 1);

    auto *searchPanel = W::card(nullptr, "dashboardSearch");
    auto *searchLayout = new QVBoxLayout(searchPanel);
    searchLayout->setContentsMargins(13, 12, 13, 12);
    auto *searchTitle = W::label("Find an order or product", "dashboardSearchTitle");
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setAccessibleName(searchTitle->text());
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Order ID or barcode");
    m_search->setMinimumWidth(210);
    m_search->setMinimumHeight(36);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    searchLayout->addWidget(searchTitle);
    searchLayout->addWidget(m_search);
    heroLayout->addWidget(searchPanel, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    root->addWidget(hero);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, [this]
            { emit lookup(m_search->text()); m_search->clear(); });

    // ---- KPI row
    auto *stats = new QHBoxLayout;
    stats->setSpacing(12);
    stats->addWidget(statCard("Sales today", "cash", "blue", "#244fc4", m_sales, m_salesSub), 1);
    stats->addWidget(statCard("Orders today", "orders", "green", "#116944", m_orders, m_ordersSub), 1);
    stats->addWidget(statCard("Pending", "clock", "amber", "#865210", m_pending, m_pendingSub), 1);
    stats->addWidget(statCard("Low stock", "warn", "rose", "#9f344b", m_lowCount, m_lowSub), 1);
    root->addLayout(stats);

    auto *mid = new QHBoxLayout;
    mid->setSpacing(14);
    auto *cc = W::card(nullptr, "dashboardPanel");
    auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(cc);
    cv->setContentsMargins(18, 14, 18, 12);
    cv->setSpacing(6);
    auto *chartHeader = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_revenueTitle = W::label("Hourly revenue", "h1");
    chartHeader->addWidget(m_revenueTitle, 1);
    auto *periods = new QButtonGroup(this);
    for (const auto &text : {QString("Today"), QString("Last 7 days")})
    {
        auto *period = W::button(text, "tab");
        period->setCheckable(true);
        period->setProperty("reportTab", true);
        periods->addButton(period, periods->buttons().size());
        chartHeader->addWidget(period);
    }
    periods->button(0)->setChecked(true);
    connect(periods, &QButtonGroup::idClicked, this, [this](int id)
            {
                m_showRecentDays = id == 1;
                refreshRevenue();
            });
    cv->addLayout(chartHeader);
    cv->addWidget(W::label("Paid orders · PKR", "muted"));
    m_chart = new RevenueChart;
    m_chart->setObjectName("dashboardRevenue");
    m_chart->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Expanding, QSizePolicy::Expanding);
    cv->addWidget(m_chart, 1);
    mid->addWidget(cc, 3);

    auto *quick = W::card(nullptr, "dashboardPanel");
    auto *actions = new QVBoxLayout(quick);
    actions->setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 14);
    actions->setSpacing(8);

    rv->addWidget(m_showing);
    root->addWidget(rc, 1);
