void DashboardPage::refresh()
{
    auto &st = AppState::instance();
    const auto &orders = st.orders();
    m_dashboardDate = QDate::currentDate();
    m_date->setText(QLocale().toString(m_dashboardDate, "dddd, d MMMM yyyy"));
    int pending = 0;
    int ordersToday = 0;
    int paidToday = 0;
    double salesToday = 0.0;
    for (const auto &o : orders)
    {
        if (o.time.date() == m_dashboardDate)
            ++ordersToday;
        if (o.status == "On Hold" || o.status == "In Progress")
            ++pending;
        if (o.status == "Paid" && o.paymentTime().date() == m_dashboardDate)
        {
            ++paidToday;
            salesToday += o.total;
        }
    }
    m_sales->setText(W::money(salesToday));
    m_salesSub->setText("Net revenue so far today");
    m_orders->setText(QString::number(ordersToday));
    m_ordersSub->setText("Average " + W::money(salesToday / qMax(1, paidToday)) + " per paid order");
    refreshRevenue();
    m_pending->setText(QString::number(pending));
    m_pendingSub->setText("On hold or in progress");
    const auto low = st.lowStock();
    m_lowCount->setText(QString::number(low.size()));
    m_lowSub->setText("At or below stock limit");

    const int m = qMin(5, orders.size());
    m_recent->setRowCount(m);
    for (int r = 0; r < m; ++r)
    {
        const auto &o = orders[r];
        m_recent->setRowHeight(r, 48);
        auto id = new QTableWidgetItem(o.id);
        id->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor("#2554d8")));
        QFont f = id->font();
        f.setBold(true);
        id->setFont(f);
        m_recent->setItem(r, 0, id);
        m_recent->setItem(r, 1, new QTableWidgetItem(o.time.toString("HH:mm")));
        m_recent->setItem(r, 2, new QTableWidgetItem(o.customer));
        m_recent->setItem(r, 3, new QTableWidgetItem(QString::number(o.itemCount())));
        auto *am = new QTableWidgetItem(W::money(o.total));
        am->setFont(f);
        m_recent->setItem(r, 4, am);
        auto *sw = new QWidget;
        auto *swl = new QHBoxLayout(sw);
        swl->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        swl->addWidget(W::badge(o.status, W::statusBadgeKind(o.status)), 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        swl->addStretch();
        m_recent->setCellWidget(r, 5, sw);
        auto *ab = W::button("View", "mini");
        auto *aw = new QWidget;
        auto *al = new QHBoxLayout(aw);
        al->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 4, 0);
        al->addWidget(ab);
        m_recent->setCellWidget(r, 6, aw);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        connect(ab, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
                { Dialogs::orderDetails(this, copy); });
    }
    m_showing->setText(m == 0 ? QStringLiteral("No saved orders yet")
                             : QString("Showing the latest %1 of %2 saved orders").arg(m).arg(orders.size()));
}

void DashboardPage::refreshRevenue()
{
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    const QDate start = m_showRecentDays ? today.addDays(-6) : today;
    const int bins = m_showRecentDays ? 7 : 24;
    QVector<double> totals(bins, 0.0);
    QStringList labels;
    for (int i = 0; i < bins; ++i)
        labels << (m_showRecentDays ? QLocale().toString(start.addDays(i), "dd MMM")
                                   : QTime(i, 0).toString("HH:mm"));

    bool hasPaidOrders = false;
    for (const auto &order : AppState::instance().orders())
    {
        const auto paid = order.paymentTime();
        if (order.status != "Paid" || !paid.isValid() || paid.date() < start || paid.date() > today)
            continue;
        const auto bin = m_showRecentDays ? start.daysTo(paid.date()) : paid.time().hour();
        totals[bin] += order.total;
        hasPaidOrders = true;
    }

    m_revenueTitle->setText(m_showRecentDays ? "Daily revenue" : "Hourly revenue");
    m_chart->setAccessibleName(m_revenueTitle->text());
    m_chart->setData(labels, totals, hasPaidOrders);
}

#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"

namespace Dialogs
{
    QString receiptText(const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        const int w = 36;
        auto center = [&](const QString &s)
        { return QString((w - s.size()) / 2, ' ') + s; };
        auto two = [&](const QString &l, const QString &r)
        { return l + QString(qMax(1, w - l.size() - r.size()), ' ') + r; };
        const QString rule(w, '-');
        QString t;
        t += center("SYNTRO-CHECK") + "\n" + center("Retail receipt") + "\n" + rule + "\n";
        t += two("Order", o.id) + "\n";
        if (!o.billId.isEmpty()) t += two("Bill", o.billId) + "\n";
        t += two("Date", o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")) + "\n";
        t += two("Customer", o.customer) + "\n" + rule + "\n";
        for (const auto &l : o.lines)
        {
            const double sub = l.price * l.qty;
            QString name = l.name;
            if (name.size() > w - 12)
                name = name.left(w - 13) + "…";
            t += two(QString("%1 x %2").arg(l.qty).arg(name), W::money(sub)) + "\n";
        }
        t += rule + "\n";
        t += two("Subtotal", W::money(o.subtotal)) + "\n";
        if (o.discount > 0)
            t += two("Discount", W::money(-o.discount)) + "\n";
        t += two("Tax", W::money(o.tax)) + "\n";
        t += two("TOTAL", W::money(o.total)) + "\n" + rule + "\n";
        t += two("Payment", o.payment) + "\n";
        if (o.tendered > 0)
        {
            t += two("Tendered", W::money(o.tendered)) + "\n";
            t += two("Change", W::money(o.tendered - o.total)) + "\n";
        }
        t += "\n" + center("Thank you for shopping!") + "\n";
        return t;
    }

    // Bill as a small HTML page: header, details, item table, totals, payment.
    // The same HTML is shown on screen and saved as the PDF, so both always match.
    QString receiptHtml(const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        auto esc = [](const QString &x) { return x.toHtmlEscaped(); };
        auto row = [&](const QString &l, const QString &r, bool strong = false)
        {
            const QString w = strong ? "font-weight:700;" : "";
            return QString("<tr><td style='padding:2px 0;color:#5b6478;%3'>%1</td>"
                           "<td align='right' style='padding:2px 0;%3'>%2</td></tr>").arg(l, r, w);
        };
        QString h;
        h += "<div style='font-family:Segoe UI,Arial,sans-serif;font-size:10pt;color:#1b2b55;'>";
        h += "<p align='center' style='margin:0;font-size:18pt;font-weight:800;color:#2554d8;'>Syntro-Check</p>";
        h += "<p align='center' style='margin:0 0 10px 0;color:#5b6478;'>Sales receipt</p>";

        // order details
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0'>";
        h += row("Bill no.", o.billId.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : esc(o.billId));
        h += row("Order no.", esc(o.id));
        h += row("Date", o.time.toString("dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm AP"));
        h += row("Customer", esc(o.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in") : o.customer));
        if (!o.contact.isEmpty())
            h += row("Contact", esc(o.contact));
        h += "</table><hr style='color:#dce4f5;'/>";

        // items
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0' cellpadding='3'>"
             "<tr style='background:#eef3ff;color:#2554d8;font-weight:700;'>"
             "<td>Item</td><td align='center'>Qty</td><td align='right'>Price</td>"
             "<td align='right'>Disc.</td><td align='right'>Amount</td></tr>";
        for (const auto &l : o.lines)
        {
            const double amount = (l.price - l.discount) * l.qty;
            h += QString("<tr><td>%1<br/><span style='color:#8a94ab;font-size:8pt;'>%2</span></td>"
                         "<td align='center'>%3</td><td align='right'>%4</td><td align='right'>%5</td>"
                         "<td align='right'>%6</td></tr>")
                     .arg(esc(l.name), esc(l.acronym)).arg(l.qty)
                     .arg(QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(l.price, 'f', 2),
                          l.discount > 0 ? QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(l.discount * l.qty, 'f', 2) : QString("-"),
                          QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(amount, 'f', 2));
        }
        h += "</table><hr style='color:#dce4f5;'/>";

        // totals
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0'>";
        h += row("Subtotal", W::money(o.subtotal));
        h += row("Discount", o.discount > 0 ? W::money(-o.discount) : W::money(0));
        h += row("Tax", W::money(o.tax));
        h += "</table>";
        h += QString("<table width='100%' cellspacing='0' cellpadding='6' style='margin-top:6px;'>"
                     "<tr style='background:#2554d8;color:white;font-weight:800;font-size:13pt;'>"
                     "<td>TOTAL</td><td align='right'>%1</td></tr></table>").arg(W::money(o.total));

        // payment
        h += "<table width='100%' cellspacing='0' style='margin-top:8px;'>";
        QString mode = o.payment.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : esc(o.payment);
        if (!o.merchant.isEmpty() && o.merchant != "-")
            mode += " (" + esc(o.merchant) + ")";
        h += row("Payment", mode);
        if (o.tendered > 0)
        {
            h += row("Amount received", W::money(o.tendered));
            h += row("Balance returned", W::money(o.tendered - o.total));
        }
        if (!o.status.isEmpty())
            h += row("Status", esc(o.status));
        h += "</table>";
        h += "<p align='center' style='margin-top:14px;color:#5b6478;'>Thank you for shopping with us!</p></div>";
        return h;
    }

    // Saves the bill as a PDF using the terminal's preferred paper size.
    bool saveReceiptPdf(const OrderRecord &o, const QString &path)
    {
        QPdfWriter pdf(path);
        pdf.setPageSize(QPageSize(AppState::instance().settings().receiptA4 ? QPageSize::A4 : QPageSize::A5));
        pdf.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(12, 12, 12, 12), QPageLayout::Millimeter);
        pdf.setTitle("Bill " + (o.billId.isEmpty() ? o.id : o.billId));
        pdf.setCreator("Syntro-Check");
        QTextDocument doc;
        doc.setHtml(receiptHtml(o));
        doc.print(&pdf);
        return QFileInfo(path).exists();
    }

    void receipt(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Bill - " + (o.billId.isEmpty() ? o.id : o.billId));
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(18, 18, 18, 14);
        v->setSpacing(12);
        auto *view = new QTextBrowser;
        view->setHtml(receiptHtml(o));
        view->setStyleSheet("QTextBrowser{background:white;border:1px solid #dce9ff;border-radius:10px;padding:14px;}");
        view->setMinimumSize(470, 600);
        v->addWidget(view);

        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        auto *pdfBtn = W::button("Save as PDF", "soft", "print");
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        h->addWidget(pdfBtn);
        h->addStretch();
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        QObject::connect(pdfBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&d, &o]
        {
            const QString name = QString("Bill_%1.pdf").arg(o.billId.isEmpty() ? o.id : o.billId);
            QString folder = AppState::instance().settings().receiptFolder;
            if (folder.isEmpty() || !QFileInfo(folder).isDir())
                folder = QStandardPaths::writableLocation(QStandardPaths::DocumentsLocation);
            const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(&d, "Save bill as PDF",
                QDir(folder).filePath(name), "PDF files (*.pdf)");
            if (path.isEmpty())
                return;
            if (saveReceiptPdf(o, path))
                QMessageBox::information(&d, "Bill saved", "Saved to:\n" + QDir::toNativeSeparators(path));
            else
                QMessageBox::warning(&d, "Bill not saved", "Could not write the PDF file.");
        });
        d.exec();
    }

    void orderDetails(QWidget *parent, const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Order " + o.id);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(20, 18, 20, 16);
        v->setSpacing(10);
        auto *top = new QHBoxLayout;
        top->addWidget(W::label("Order " + o.id, "h1"));
        top->addWidget(W::badge(o.status, W::statusBadgeKind(o.status)));
        top->addStretch();
        v->addLayout(top);
        // date • customer • contact (empty parts are skipped, so no stray dots)
        QStringList meta{o.time.toString("dd MMM yyyy, hh:mm AP"), o.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in") : o.customer};
        if (!o.contact.isEmpty()) meta << o.contact;
        v->addWidget(W::label(meta.join("  •  "), "muted"));
        auto *t = new QTableWidget(o.lines.size(), 5);
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels({"ITEM", "QTY", "PRICE", "DISCOUNT", "AMOUNT"});
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::NoSelection);
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView::Stretch);
        for (int i = 0; i < o.lines.size(); ++i)
        {
            const auto &l = o.lines[i];
            auto cell = [&](int col, const QString &text, Qt::Alignment a)
            {
                auto *it = new QTableWidgetItem(text);
                it->setTextAlignment(a | Qt::AlignVCenter);
                t->setItem(i, col, it);
            };
            cell(0, l.name, Qt::AlignLeft);
            cell(1, QString::number(l.qty), Qt::AlignCenter);
            cell(2, W::money(l.price), Qt::AlignRight);
            cell(3, l.discount > 0 ? W::money(-l.discount * l.qty) : QString("-"), Qt::AlignRight);
            cell(4, W::money((l.price - l.discount) * l.qty), Qt::AlignRight);
        }
        t->setMinimumSize(660, 200);
        t->setStyleSheet("QTableWidget{border:1px solid #dce9ff;border-radius:8px;}");
        v->addWidget(t);
        // totals breakdown
        auto *sum = new QFormLayout;
        sum->setLabelAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft);
        auto right = [](const QString &x, bool big = false)
        {
            auto *l = new QLabel(x);
            l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignRight);
            if (big) l->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:15px;");
            return l;
        };
        sum->addRow("Subtotal", right(W::money(o.subtotal)));
        sum->addRow("Discount", right(o.discount > 0 ? W::money(-o.discount) : W::money(0)));
        sum->addRow("Tax", right(W::money(o.tax)));
        sum->addRow("Payment", right(o.payment.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : o.payment));
        auto *totalLbl = new QLabel("Total");
        totalLbl->setStyleSheet("font-weight:800; font-size:15px;");
        sum->addRow(totalLbl, right(W::money(o.total), true));
        v->addLayout(sum);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->addStretch();
        auto *bill = W::button("View Bill", "", "bills");
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        h->addWidget(bill);
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        QObject::connect(bill, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         { receipt(&d, o); });
        d.exec();
    }

    void productDescription(QWidget *parent, int productId)
    {
        const ProductDescriptionView pd = AppState::instance().describe(productId);
        if (pd.title.isEmpty())
            return;
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Product description");
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(22, 18, 22, 16);
        v->setSpacing(10);
        auto *top = new QHBoxLayout;
        top->addWidget(W::label(pd.title, "h1"));
        const bool out = pd.level == "out", low = pd.level == "low";
        top->addWidget(W::badge(out ? "Out of stock" : low ? "Low stock" : "In stock", out ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green"));
        top->addStretch();
        v->addLayout(top);
        auto *body = new QHBoxLayout;
        auto *form = new QFormLayout;
        form->setHorizontalSpacing(24);
        form->setVerticalSpacing(8);
        for (const auto &f : pd.fields)
            form->addRow(W::label(f.first.toUpper(), "caption"), W::label(f.second, "h2"));
        body->addLayout(form, 1);
        QPixmap px(pd.imagePath);
        if (!px.isNull())
        {
            auto *img = new QLabel;
            img->setPixmap(px.scaledToWidth(200, Qt::SmoothTransformation));
            body->addWidget(img, 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        }
        v->addLayout(body);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->addStretch();
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        d.exec();
    }

    void loadIssues(QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto &st = AppState::instance();
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Product.txt - data check");
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 20, 14);
        v->setSpacing(10);
        v->addWidget(W::label("Product data check", "h1"));
        v->addWidget(W::label(st.productFile().isEmpty() ? "Product.txt was not found." : st.productFile(), "muted"));
        auto *list = new QListWidget;
        const QStringList issues = st.productIssues();
        list->addItems(issues.isEmpty() ? QStringList{"No problems found."} : issues);
        list->setMinimumSize(560, 260);
        v->addWidget(list);
        auto *close = W::button("Close", "primary");
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->addStretch();
        h->addWidget(close);
        v->addLayout(h);
        QObject::connect(close, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);
        d.exec();
    }

    bool login(QWidget *parent, UserRole &role, QString &name)
    {
        QDialog d(parent);
        d.setWindowTitle("Syntro-Check - Sign in");
        d.setMinimumWidth(420);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(&d);
        v->setContentsMargins(28, 24, 28, 22);
        v->setSpacing(12);

        auto *title = W::label("Sign in to Syntro-Check", "modalTitle");
        auto *sub = W::label("Enter your name and choose your role.", "muted");
        v->addWidget(title);
        v->addWidget(sub);

        auto *nameEdit = new QLineEdit;
        nameEdit->setPlaceholderText("Your name");
        nameEdit->setMinimumHeight(38);
        v->addWidget(nameEdit);

        // One button per role; the button pressed decides the role.
        auto *cashier = W::button("Cashier", "primary", "cart");
        auto *manager = W::button("Manager", "soft", "products");
        for (auto *b : {cashier, manager})
            b->setMinimumHeight(46);
        auto *row = new QHBoxLayout;
        row->setSpacing(10);
        row->addWidget(cashier);
        row->addWidget(manager);
        v->addLayout(row);

        auto *hint = W::label("Cashier: record orders and generate bills.\nManager: manage the catalog and view reports.", "muted");
        v->addWidget(hint);

        QObject::connect(cashier, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&] { role = UserRole::Cashier; d.accept(); });
        QObject::connect(manager, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&] { role = UserRole::Manager; d.accept(); });
        QObject::connect(nameEdit, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, cashier, &QPushButton::click);

        if (d.exec() != QDialog::Accepted)
            return false;
        name = nameEdit->text().trimmed();
        if (name.isEmpty())
            name = RolePolicy::name(role);
        return true;
    }
}

#include "DiscountsPage.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QDateEdit>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QMessageBox>
#include <QScrollArea>
#include <QScrollBar>
#include <QSizePolicy>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

using namespace Ui;

DiscountsPage::DiscountsPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout *root;
    pageShell(this, root, true);
    root->setSpacing(8);
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader({}, "Seasonal Discounts", "Manage campaigns and automatic seasonal price reductions."));

    // ---- banner
    m_banner = new QFrame;
    m_banner->setObjectName("seasonBanner");
    auto *bh = new QHBoxLayout(m_banner);
    bh->setContentsMargins(16, 8, 16, 8);
    auto *bv = new QVBoxLayout;
    bv->setSpacing(2);
    bv->addWidget(W::label("CURRENT PROMOTION", "caption"));
    m_bannerTitle = W::label("", "h1");
    m_bannerSub = W::label("", "muted");
    m_bannerTitle->setWordWrap(true);
    m_bannerSub->setWordWrap(true);
    bv->addWidget(m_bannerTitle);
    bv->addWidget(m_bannerSub);
    bh->addLayout(bv, 1);
    root->addWidget(m_banner);

    auto *tiles = new QHBoxLayout;
    tiles->setSpacing(8);
    auto tile = [tiles](const QString &title, QLabel *&value, QLabel *&detail)
    {
        auto *card = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *layout = new QVBoxLayout(card);
        layout->setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8);
        layout->setSpacing(2);
        layout->addWidget(W::label(title, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "bigValue");
        detail = W::label("", "muted");
        layout->addWidget(value);
        layout->addWidget(detail);
        tiles->addWidget(card, 1);
    };
    QLabel *cap, *capDetails;
    tile("ACTIVE RATE", m_rate, m_rateSub);
    tile("UPCOMING CAMPAIGNS", m_upcoming, m_upSub);
    tile("CASHIER SPECIAL CAP", cap, capDetails);
    cap->setText(QString("%1%").arg(UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent));
    capDetails->setText("At checkout");
    root->addLayout(tiles);

    auto *row = new QHBoxLayout;
    row->setSpacing(8);

    // ---- list
    auto *list = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto *lv = new QVBoxLayout(list);
    lv->setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 12);
    lv->setSpacing(8);
    lv->addWidget(W::label("Configured Promotional Seasons", "h1"));
    auto *lh = new QHBoxLayout;
    lh->setSpacing(4);
    for (const QString &name : {"All", "Active", "Scheduled", "Inactive", "Expired"})
    {
        auto *btn = W::button(name, "tab");
        btn->setProperty("compactSeasonTab", true);
        btn->setCheckable(true);
        btn->setChecked(name == "All");
        lh->addWidget(btn);
        m_tabs << btn;
        connect(btn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, name]
                {
            m_filter = name;
            for (auto *t : m_tabs) t->setChecked(t->text() == name);
            rebuild(); });
    }
    lh->addStretch();
    lv->addLayout(lh);
    // the four fields of a Seasons.txt line, then today's status and the actions
    m_table = makeTable({"START DATE", "END DATE", "RATE", "ACTIVE", "STATUS", "ACTIONS"});
    m_table->setProperty("managementTable", true);
    m_table->setAlternatingRowColors(true);
    m_table->setAccessibleName("Configured promotional seasons");
    auto *hh = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    // fixed widths; the last (actions) column takes whatever room is left over
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Fixed);
    const int widths[] = {100, 100, 70, 72, 108, 88};
    for (int cidx = 0; cidx <= 5; ++cidx)
        m_table->setColumnWidth(cidx, widths[cidx]);
    hh->setStretchLastSection(true);
    hh->setMinimumSectionSize(60);
    m_table->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAsNeeded); // narrow window: scroll sideways, never clip
    lv->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    lv->addWidget(W::label("Status reflects dates and activation.", "muted"));
    row->addWidget(list, 62);

    // ---- form
    auto *form = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto *fv = new QVBoxLayout(form);
    fv->setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12);
    fv->setSpacing(8);
    m_formTitle = W::label("Add Promotional Season", "h1");
    m_formTitle->setWordWrap(true);
    fv->addWidget(m_formTitle);
    m_seasonLabel = W::label("", "muted");
    m_seasonLabel->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Ignored, QSizePolicy::Preferred);
    m_seasonLabel->setWordWrap(true);
    fv->addWidget(m_seasonLabel);
    auto *dates = new QHBoxLayout;
    dates->setSpacing(10);
    auto *sv = new QVBoxLayout;
    sv->setSpacing(4);
    sv->addWidget(W::label("START DATE", "caption"));
    m_start = dateEdit(QDate::currentDate(), "dd MMM yyyy", 36);
    m_start->setAccessibleName("Season start date");
    auto *ev = new QVBoxLayout;
    ev->setSpacing(4);
    ev->addWidget(W::label("END DATE", "caption"));
    m_end = dateEdit(QDate::currentDate().addDays(7), "dd MMM yyyy", 36);
    m_end->setAccessibleName("Season end date");
    sv->addWidget(m_start);
    ev->addWidget(m_end);
    dates->addLayout(sv, 1);
    dates->addLayout(ev, 1);
    fv->addLayout(dates);
    const auto updateSeasonLabel = [this] {
        m_seasonLabel->setText(UiStore::seasonLabel(m_start->date(), m_end->date()));
    };
    connect(m_start, &QDateEdit::dateChanged, this, updateSeasonLabel);
    connect(m_end, &QDateEdit::dateChanged, this, updateSeasonLabel);
    updateSeasonLabel();
    fv->addSpacing(4);
    fv->addWidget(W::label("DISCOUNT PERCENTAGE", "caption"));
    m_percent = new QDoubleSpinBox;
    m_percent->setRange(0.5, 100);
    m_percent->setAccessibleName("Seasonal discount percentage");
    m_percent->setDecimals(2);
    m_percent->setSingleStep(0.5);
    m_percent->setSuffix(" %");
    m_percent->setValue(10);
    m_percent->setMinimumHeight(36);
    fv->addWidget(m_percent);
    m_active = new QCheckBox("Apply automatically during this season");
    m_active->setMinimumHeight(30);
    m_active->setChecked(true);
    fv->addWidget(m_active);
    m_error = W::label("", "errorText");
    m_error->setWordWrap(true);
    m_error->hide();
    fv->addWidget(m_error);
    fv->addStretch();
    auto *note = W::card(form, "field");
    auto *noteLayout = new QVBoxLayout(note);
    noteLayout->setContentsMargins(8, 6, 8, 6);
    auto *nl = W::label(QString("The special checkout discount is capped at %1%.")
                            .arg(UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent), "muted");
    nl->setWordWrap(true);
    noteLayout->addWidget(nl);
    fv->addWidget(note);
    auto *btns = new QHBoxLayout;
    auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel", "soft");
    m_save = W::button("Save Season", "primary", "check");
    m_save->setMinimumHeight(38);
    btns->addWidget(cancel);
    btns->addWidget(m_save, 1);
    fv->addLayout(btns);
    auto *formScroll = new QScrollArea;
    formScroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
    formScroll->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    formScroll->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    formScroll->setWidget(form);
    // wide enough for the form plus the vertical scroll bar, so the card is never cut off on the right
    formScroll->setMinimumWidth(form->minimumSizeHint().width() + formScroll->verticalScrollBar()->sizeHint().width() + 4);
    row->addWidget(formScroll, 38);
    root->addLayout(row, 1);

    connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &DiscountsPage::resetForm);
    connect(m_save, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &DiscountsPage::submit);
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::seasonsChanged, this, &DiscountsPage::refresh);
    refresh();
}

void DiscountsPage::refresh() { rebuild(); }

void DiscountsPage::rebuild()
{
    auto &store = UiStore::instance();
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    const Season *act = store.activeSeason(today);
    m_banner->setProperty("activeSeason", act != nullptr);
    Theme::repolish(m_banner);
    if (act)
    {
        m_bannerTitle->setText(QString("%1% seasonal discount active · %2")
                                   .arg(act->percent, 0, 'g', 4).arg(act->name));
        m_bannerSub->setText("Applied automatically to every bill and shown in the bill's seasonal discount field. " +
                             QString::number(today.daysTo(act->end)) + " days remaining.");
        m_rate->setText(QString("%1%").arg(act->percent, 0, 'g', 4));
        m_rateSub->setText("Until " + act->end.toString("d MMM yyyy"));
    }
    else
    {
        m_bannerTitle->setText("No seasonal discount is active right now");
        m_bannerSub->setText("An enabled season applies automatically within its date range.");
        m_rate->setText("0%");
        m_rateSub->setText("No active season");
    }
    int upcoming = 0;
    for (const auto &s : store.seasons())
        if (s.active && s.start > today)
            ++upcoming;
    m_upcoming->setText(QString::number(upcoming));
    m_upSub->setText("Scheduled");

    QVector<Season> rows;
    for (const auto &s : store.seasons())
    {
        const QString st = s.statusOn(today);
        if (m_filter != "All" && st.compare(m_filter, Qt::CaseInsensitive) != 0)
            continue;
        rows << s;
    }
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const Season s = rows[r];
        const QString st = s.statusOn(today);
        const bool expired = st == "expired";
        const QString dim = expired ? "#8a94ab" : "";
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 48);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(s.start.toString("dd MMM yyyy"), Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, dim, true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(s.end.toString("dd MMM yyyy"), Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, dim, true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(QString("%1%").arg(s.percent, 0, 'g', 4),
                                    Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, dim, true));
        auto *tog = W::button(s.active ? "ON" : "OFF", "tab");
        tog->setProperty("compactSeasonTab", true);
        tog->setCheckable(true);
        tog->setChecked(s.active);
        tog->setMinimumWidth(54);
        tog->setEnabled(!expired);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 3, wrapW(tog));
        QString status = st;
        status[0] = status.at(0).toUpper();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 4, wrapW(W::badge(status,
                                                   st == "active" ? "green" : st == "scheduled" ? "amber" : st == "inactive" ? "red" : "grey")));
        auto *cell = new QWidget;
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        h->setSpacing(4);
        auto *e = W::button("", "flat", "edit");
        auto *x = W::button("", "flat", "trash");
        e->setToolTip("Edit season");
        x->setToolTip("Delete season");
        h->addWidget(e);
        h->addWidget(x);
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 5, cell);
        const QString id = s.id;
        connect(tog, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id](bool on)
                {
            const QString why = UiStore::instance().setSeasonActive(id, on);
            if (!why.isEmpty())
            {
                rebuild();
                QMessageBox::warning(this, "Cannot activate", why);
            }
            else
                emit toast(on ? "Seasonal discount set active" : "Seasonal discount set inactive"); });
        connect(e, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id] { edit(id); });
        connect(x, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id]
                {
            if (QMessageBox::question(this, "Delete season", "Delete this seasonal discount from Seasons.txt?") != QMessageBox::Yes) return;
            UiStore::instance().removeSeason(id);
            if (m_editing == id) resetForm();
            emit toast("Seasonal discount deleted"); });
    }
}

void DiscountsPage::resetForm()
{
    m_editing.clear();
    m_start->setDate(QDate::currentDate());
    m_end->setDate(QDate::currentDate().addDays(7));
    m_percent->setValue(10);
    m_active->setChecked(true);
    m_error->clear();
    m_error->hide();
    m_formTitle->setText("Add Promotional Season");
    m_formTitle->setToolTip({});
    m_save->setText("Save Season");
}

void DiscountsPage::edit(const QString &id)
{
    for (const auto &s : UiStore::instance().seasons())
        if (s.id == id)
        {
            m_editing = id;
            m_start->setDate(s.start);
            m_end->setDate(s.end);
            m_percent->setValue(s.percent);
            m_active->setChecked(s.active);
            m_error->clear();
            m_error->hide();
            m_formTitle->setText("Edit Promotional Season");
            m_formTitle->setToolTip(s.name);
            m_save->setText("Save Season");
            return;
        }
}

void DiscountsPage::submit()
{
    auto &store = UiStore::instance();
    const QString why = store.validateSeason(m_start->date(), m_end->date(), m_percent->value(), m_active->isChecked(), m_editing);
    m_error->setText(why);
    m_error->setVisible(!why.isEmpty());
    if (!why.isEmpty())
        return;
    Season s;
    s.start = m_start->date();
    s.end = m_end->date();
    s.percent = m_percent->value();
    s.active = m_active->isChecked();
    if (m_editing.isEmpty())
    {
        s.id = store.nextSeasonId();
        store.addSeason(s);
        emit toast("Seasonal discount added to Seasons.txt");
    }
    else
    {
        s.id = m_editing;
        store.updateSeason(m_editing, s);
        emit toast("Seasonal discount updated in Seasons.txt");
    }
    resetForm();
}

#include <iterator>
#include "InventoryPage.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

using namespace Ui;

namespace
{
    QString stockKind(int qty, int limit)
    {
        return qty <= 0 ? "red" : qty <= limit ? "amber" : "green";
    }
    QString stockText(int qty, int limit)
    {
        return qty <= 0 ? "Out of stock" : qty <= limit ? "Low stock" : "In stock";
    }
}

InventoryPage::InventoryPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout *root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    root->setSpacing(8);

    auto *exportBtn = W::button("Export Stock Ledger", "soft", "export");
    auto *newBtn = W::button("New SKU Entry", "primary", "plus");
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader({}, "Inventory Management",
                                  "Monitor stock and stage quantity adjustments.",
                                  {exportBtn, newBtn}));
    connect(newBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &InventoryPage::newProduct);

    auto *tiles = new QHBoxLayout;
    tiles->setSpacing(8);
    tiles->addWidget(statTile("CATALOGUE DEPTH", "products", m_total, m_totalSub, "blue"));
    tiles->addWidget(statTile("LOW STOCK", "warn", m_low, m_lowSub, "amber"));
    tiles->addWidget(statTile("OUT OF STOCK", "cart", m_out, m_outSub, "red"));
    root->addLayout(tiles);

    auto *card = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 12);
    v->setSpacing(8);

    auto *tools = new QHBoxLayout;
    tools->setSpacing(8);
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search inventory");
    m_search->setToolTip("Search by product ID, name, acronym, or barcode.");
    m_search->setAccessibleName("Search inventory");
    m_search->setClearButtonEnabled(true);
    m_search->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    m_category = new QComboBox;
    m_category->setAccessibleName("Inventory category");
    m_category->setSizeAdjustPolicy(QComboBox::AdjustToMinimumContentsLengthWithIcon);
    m_category->setMinimumContentsLength(14);
    m_category->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_lowOnly = new QCheckBox("Low stock only");
    m_saveTop = W::button("Save Changes", "primary", "check");
    m_saveTop->setMinimumHeight(40);
    tools->addWidget(m_search, 3);
    tools->addWidget(m_category, 2);
    tools->addWidget(m_lowOnly);
    tools->addWidget(m_saveTop);
    v->addLayout(tools);

    m_table = makeTable({"PRODUCT ID", "PRODUCT", "CATEGORY / ACRONYM", "AVAILABLE QUANTITY", "STATUS", "UNIT PRICE"});
    m_table->setProperty("managementTable", true);
    m_table->setAlternatingRowColors(true);
    m_table->setAccessibleName("Inventory stock");
    auto *hh = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView::Interactive);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(1, 224);
    for (int c : {0, 5})
        hh->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(2, QHeaderView::Interactive);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(2, 142);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(4, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(3, 155);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(4, 112);
    hh->setStretchLastSection(true);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);

    m_footer = W::label("", "muted");
    v->addWidget(m_footer);

    // Unsaved changes bar
    m_bar = new QFrame;
    m_bar->setObjectName("totalBox");
    auto *bh = new QHBoxLayout(m_bar);
    bh->setContentsMargins(16, 10, 12, 10);
    bh->setSpacing(10);
    bh->addWidget(W::iconLabel("warn", QColor("#ffc24a"), 20));
    m_barText = W::label("");
    m_barText->setStyleSheet("color:white; font-weight:700;");
    bh->addWidget(m_barText, 1);
    auto *disc = W::button("Discard All", "soft", "refresh");
    auto *save = W::button("Save && Commit Stock", "primary", "check");
    bh->addWidget(disc);
    bh->addWidget(save);
    v->addWidget(m_bar);
    m_bar->hide();
    root->addWidget(card, 1);

    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this] { rebuild(); });
    connect(m_category, &QComboBox::currentIndexChanged, this, [this] { if (!m_building) rebuild(); });
    connect(m_lowOnly, &QCheckBox::toggled, this, [this] { rebuild(); });
    connect(m_saveTop, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &InventoryPage::save);
    connect(save, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &InventoryPage::save);
    connect(disc, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &InventoryPage::discard);
    connect(exportBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            {
        QStringList rows{"Product ID,Barcode,Acronym,Name,Category,Quantity,Status"};
        const int limit = AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold;
        for (const auto &p : AppState::instance().products())
        {
            const int q = m_edits.value(p.id, p.stock);
            rows << QString("%1,%2,%3,%4,%5,%6,%7").arg(p.id).arg(p.ean, p.sku, csvField(p.name), p.categoryId).arg(q).arg(stockText(q, limit).toUpper());
        }
        if (writeCsv(this, "inventory.csv", rows)) emit toast("Stock ledger exported"); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &InventoryPage::refresh);
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::categoriesChanged, this, &InventoryPage::refresh);
    refresh();
}

void InventoryPage::refresh()
{
    m_building = true;
    const QString keep = m_category->currentData().toString();
    m_category->clear();
    m_category->addItem("All Categories", QString());
    for (const auto &c : UiStore::instance().categories())
        m_category->addItem(QString("%1 - %2").arg(c.id, c.name), c.id);
    const int idx = m_category->findData(keep);
    m_category->setCurrentIndex(idx < 0 ? 0 : idx);
    m_building = false;
    rebuild();
}

void InventoryPage::rebuild()
{
    auto &st = AppState::instance();
    const int limit = st.settings().lowStockThreshold;
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    const QString cat = m_category->currentData().toString();

    // drop staged values for products that no longer exist
    for (auto it = m_edits.begin(); it != m_edits.end();)
    {
        bool found = false;
        for (const auto &p : st.products())
            if (p.id == it.key()) { found = true; break; }
        it = found ? std::next(it) : m_edits.erase(it);
    }

    int low = 0, out = 0;
    double value = 0;
    for (const auto &p : st.products())
    {
        if (p.stock <= 0) ++out;
        else if (p.stock <= limit) ++low;
        value += p.price * p.stock;
    }
    m_total->setText(QString::number(st.products().size()) + " SKUs");
    m_totalSub->setText("Stock value: " + W::money(value));
    m_low->setText(QString::number(low) + " items");
    m_lowSub->setText(QString("At or below %1 units").arg(limit));
    m_out->setText(QString("%1 SKU%2").arg(out).arg(out == 1 ? "" : "s"));
    m_outSub->setText("Zero units available");

    QVector<ProductInfo> rows;
    const QVector<ProductInfo> base = q.isEmpty() ? st.products() : st.searchText(q);
    for (const auto &p : base)
    {
        const int qty = m_edits.value(p.id, p.stock);
        if (!cat.isEmpty() && p.categoryId != cat) continue;
        if (m_lowOnly->isChecked() && qty > limit) continue;
        rows << p;
    }

    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const ProductInfo p = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 52);
        const Category *c = UiStore::instance().category(p.categoryId);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item("PRD-" + QString::number(p.id).rightJustified(4, '0'), Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        auto *product = item(p.name + "\nEAN: " + p.ean + "  -  " + p.unit);
        product->setToolTip(product->text());
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, product);
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(p.sku + "\n" + (c ? c->name : QString("Category " + p.categoryId))));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(p.price), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));

        auto *cell = new QWidget;
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        h->setContentsMargins(6, 0, 6, 0);
        h->setSpacing(4);
        auto *minus = W::button("-", "step");
        auto *plus = W::button("+", "step");
        auto *spin = new QSpinBox;
        spin->setAccessibleName("Quantity for " + p.name);
        minus->setAccessibleName("Decrease quantity for " + p.name);
        plus->setAccessibleName("Increase quantity for " + p.name);
        spin->setRange(0, 999999);
        spin->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
        spin->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        spin->setValue(m_edits.value(p.id, p.stock));
        spin->setMinimumWidth(64);
        h->addWidget(minus);
        h->addWidget(spin);
        h->addWidget(plus);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 3, cell);
        auto *badge = W::badge(stockText(spin->value(), limit), stockKind(spin->value(), limit));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 4, wrapW(badge));
        connect(minus, &QPushButton::clicked, spin, [spin] { spin->setValue(spin->value() - 1); });
        connect(plus, &QPushButton::clicked, spin, [spin] { spin->setValue(spin->value() + 1); });
        connect(spin, qOverload<int>(&QSpinBox::valueChanged), this, [this, spin, badge, p, limit](int v)
                {
            if (v == p.stock) m_edits.remove(p.id); else m_edits[p.id] = v;
            W::setBadge(badge, stockText(v, limit), stockKind(v, limit));
            spin->setProperty("staged", v != p.stock);
            Theme::repolish(spin);
            updateBar(); });
        spin->setProperty("staged", m_edits.contains(p.id));
        Theme::repolish(spin);
    }
    m_footer->setText(QString("Displaying %1 of %2 registered items").arg(rows.size()).arg(st.products().size()));
    updateBar();
}

void InventoryPage::updateBar()
{
    const int n = m_edits.size();
    m_bar->setVisible(n > 0);
    m_barText->setText(QString("Unsaved quantity changes: %1 item%2 modified").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s"));
    m_saveTop->setEnabled(n > 0);
}

void InventoryPage::save()
{
    if (m_edits.isEmpty())
        return;
    QString err;
    const int n = m_edits.size();
    if (!AppState::instance().setStockLevels(m_edits, &err))
    {
        emit toast(err.isEmpty() ? "Could not save stock levels" : err);
        return;
    }
    m_edits.clear();
    rebuild();
    emit toast(QString("Stock updated for %1 product%2").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s"));
}

void InventoryPage::discard()
{
    m_edits.clear();
    rebuild();
    emit toast("Changes discarded");
}

#include <QApplication>
#include "CameraWidget.h"

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv); // starts Qt
    CameraWidget cam;             // your widget, as its own window
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::barcodeScan, [&cam](const QString &six)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Scanned: " + six); // show the digits in the title bar
                     });
    QObject::connect(&cam, &CameraWidget::scanError, [&cam](const QString &msg)
                     {
                         cam.setWindowTitle("Error: " + msg); // show the problem instead
                     });
    cam.show();        // open the window
    cam.start();       // open the camera (connect first, start last)
    return app.exec(); // keep the window alive
}

#include "MainWindow.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "BillsPage.h"
#include "CategoriesPage.h"
#include "DarkModeManager.h"
#include "DashboardPage.h"
#include "DiscountsPage.h"
#include "InventoryPage.h"
#include "OrdersPage.h"
#include "ProductsPage.h"
#include "RecordOrderPage.h"
#include "ReportsPage.h"
#include "ScannerPage.h"
#include "SettingsPage.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QCloseEvent>
#include <QDateTime>
#include <QFontMetrics>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QMenu>
#include <QMessageBox>
#include <QToolButton>
#include <QWidgetAction>
#include <QShortcut>
#include <QStackedWidget>

namespace
{
    struct NavDef
    {
        const char *text, *icon, *subtitle;
    };
    // Same order as Pages::Id / the sidebar of the design.
    const NavDef kNav[Pages::Count] = {
        {"Dashboard", "dashboard", "Track sales and recent activity"},
        {"Record Order", "cart", "Build and check out a sale"},
        {"Orders", "orders", "Find saved orders and resume drafts"},
        {"Inventory", "inventory", "Review stock levels and low-stock items"},
        {"Products", "products", "Search the product catalog"},
        {"Categories", "categories", "Organize products into categories"},
        {"Barcode Scanner", "barcode", "Scan or enter a barcode to find an item"},
        {"Bills", "bills", "Find bills and print receipts"},
        {"Reports", "reports", "Review sales and inventory reports"},
        {"Discounts", "percent", "Manage discount rules"},
        {"Settings", "settings", "Adjust terminal preferences"}};

    constexpr int sidebarWidth = 232;
    const QColor navIconColor("#b9c6da");
}

MainWindow::MainWindow(UserRole role, const QString &userName, QWidget *parent)
    : QMainWindow(parent), m_role(role), m_userName(userName.isEmpty() ? RolePolicy::name(role) : userName)
{
    setWindowTitle("Syntro-Check");
    setMinimumSize(1100, 700);
    resize(1440, 900);

    auto *root = new QWidget;
    root->setObjectName("root");
    setCentralWidget(root);
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(root);
    h->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    h->setSpacing(0);
    h->addWidget(buildSidebar());

    auto *right = new QWidget;
    auto *rv = new QVBoxLayout(right);
    rv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    rv->setSpacing(0);
    rv->addWidget(buildTopBar());

    m_stack = new QStackedWidget;
    m_dashboard = new DashboardPage;
    m_record = new RecordOrderPage;
    m_orders = new OrdersPage;
    m_inventory = new InventoryPage;
    m_products = new ProductsPage;
    m_categories = new CategoriesPage;
    m_scanner = new ScannerPage;
    m_bills = new BillsPage;
    m_reports = new ReportsPage;
    m_discounts = new DiscountsPage;
    m_settings = new SettingsPage(nullptr, m_role);
    const QList<QWidget *> pages = {m_dashboard, m_record,  m_orders,  m_inventory, m_products, m_categories,
                                    m_scanner,   m_bills,   m_reports, m_discounts, m_settings};
    Q_ASSERT(pages.size() == PageCount);
    for (QWidget *w : pages)
        m_stack->addWidget(w);
    rv->addWidget(m_stack, 1);
    h->addWidget(right, 1);

    // Cashier: products and scanner are look-up only. Manager: full catalogue tools.
    m_products->setRole(m_role);
    m_products->setViewOnly(!RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, RecordOrder));
    m_scanner->setViewOnly(!RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, RecordOrder));
    m_orders->setUser(m_userName);
    m_orders->setRole(m_role);
    m_record->setRole(m_role);

    m_toast = new QLabel(root);
    m_toast->setObjectName("toast");
    m_toast->hide();
    m_toastTimer = new QTimer(this);
    m_toastTimer->setSingleShot(true);
    connect(m_toastTimer, &QTimer::timeout, m_toast, &QWidget::hide);

    // ---- edit requests: badge on "Orders", and a notice when a manager signs in with requests waiting ----
    auto updateRequestBadge = [this]
    {
        if (m_nav.size() <= Orders)
            return;
        auto *count = m_nav[Orders]->findChild<QLabel *>("navCount");
        if (!count)
            return;
        const auto &store = UiStore::instance();
        const int n = m_role == UserRole::Manager ? store.pendingEditRequests().size() : store.approvedUnusedCount();
        count->setText(QString::number(n));
        count->setVisible(n > 0);
        count->setToolTip(m_role == UserRole::Manager ? "Edit requests waiting for your approval"
                                                      : "Approved edit requests - open them on Orders");
    };
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::editRequestsChanged, this, updateRequestBadge);
    updateRequestBadge();
    if (m_role == UserRole::Manager && !UiStore::instance().pendingEditRequests().isEmpty())
        QTimer::singleShot(600, this, [this] {
            showToast(QString("%1 edit request(s) waiting for your approval on Orders")
                          .arg(UiStore::instance().pendingEditRequests().size())); });

    // ---- page wiring ----
    for (QObject *o : QList<QObject *>(pages.begin(), pages.end()))
        connect(o, SIGNAL(toast(QString)), this, SLOT(showToast(QString)));

    connect(m_dashboard, &DashboardPage::navigate, this, &MainWindow::goTo);
    connect(m_dashboard, &DashboardPage::lookup, this, [this](const QString &text)
            {
        if (text.trimmed().isEmpty()) return;
        if (AppState::instance().findByCode(text)) { m_products->setSearch(text); goTo(Products); }
        else { m_orders->setSearch(text); goTo(Orders); } });
    connect(m_dashboard, &DashboardPage::resumeOrder, this, &MainWindow::openOrderForEdit);

    connect(m_products, &ProductsPage::addToOrder, this, [this](const QString &ean)
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->showCode(ean, true); });
    connect(m_scanner, &ScannerPage::sendToOrder, this, [this](const QString &ean)
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->showCode(ean, true); });
    connect(m_scanner, &ScannerPage::sendBatchToOrder, this, [this](const QVector<QPair<QString, int>> &items)
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_scanner->removeTransferredItems(m_record->addScannedBatch(items)); });

    connect(m_orders, &OrdersPage::resumeOrder, this, &MainWindow::openOrderForEdit);
    connect(m_orders, &OrdersPage::editOrder, this, &MainWindow::openOrderForEdit);
    connect(m_orders, &OrdersPage::showBill, this, &MainWindow::showBillFor);

    connect(m_record, &RecordOrderPage::billReady, this, &MainWindow::showBillFor);
    connect(m_bills, &BillsPage::newOrderRequested, this, [this]
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->newDraft(); });
    connect(m_inventory, &InventoryPage::newProduct, this, [this]
            { if (goTo(Products)) m_products->addNew(); });

    // ---- shell shortcuts (pages register their own) ----
    auto *f1 = new QShortcut(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F1), this);
    f1->setContext(Qt::ApplicationShortcut);
    connect(f1, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this] { goTo(Dashboard); });
    auto *k = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+K"), this);
    connect(k, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this]
            { if (goTo(Dashboard)) m_dashboard->focusLookup(); });
    auto *nd = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+N"), this);
    connect(nd, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this]
            { if (goTo(RecordOrder)) m_record->newDraft(); });
    // Ctrl+1 .. Ctrl+0: the pages in sidebar order (only the ones this role can see)
    QVector<int> visible;
    for (int i = 0; i < PageCount; ++i)
        if (RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, i))
            visible << i;
    for (int n = 0; n < visible.size() && n < 10; ++n)
    {
        auto *sc = new QShortcut(QKeySequence(QString("Ctrl+%1").arg((n + 1) % 10)), this);
        const int page = visible[n];
        connect(sc, &QShortcut::activated, this, [this, page] { goTo(page); });
    }

    goTo(RolePolicy::startPage(m_role));
}

QWidget *MainWindow::buildSidebar()
{
    auto *sb = new QFrame;
    sb->setObjectName("sidebar");
    sb->setFixedWidth(sidebarWidth);
    auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(sb);
    v->setContentsMargins(12, 16, 12, 16);
    v->setSpacing(4);

    auto *brand = new QHBoxLayout;
    brand->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
    brand->setSpacing(11);
    auto *logo = new QFrame;
    logo->setObjectName("logoBox");
    logo->setFixedSize(40, 40);
    auto *ll = new QVBoxLayout(logo);
    ll->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    auto *ls = new QLabel("S");
    ls->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    ll->addWidget(ls);
    brand->addWidget(logo);
    auto *bt = new QVBoxLayout;
    bt->setSpacing(3);
    bt->addWidget(W::label("Syntro-Check", "brand"));
    auto *brandDetails = new QHBoxLayout;
    brandDetails->setSpacing(5);
    brandDetails->addWidget(W::label("Point of sale", "tagBlue"));
    brandDetails->addWidget(W::label(QStringLiteral("· ") + RolePolicy::name(m_role), "tagGrey"));
    brandDetails->addStretch();
    bt->addLayout(brandDetails);
    brand->addLayout(bt, 1);
    v->addLayout(brand);

    const auto section = [v](const QString &text)
    {
        v->addSpacing(10);
        v->addWidget(W::label(text, "navSection"));
    };
    for (int i = 0; i < PageCount; ++i)
    {
        switch (i)
        {
        case Dashboard: section("Sales"); break;
        case Inventory: section("Catalog"); break;
        case Scanner: section("Operations"); break;
        default: break;
        }
        const auto title = QString::fromLatin1(kNav[i].text);
        auto *b = new QPushButton;
        b->setObjectName("nav");
        b->setProperty("active", false);
        b->setAccessibleName(title);
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        b->setFocusPolicy(Qt::StrongFocus);
        b->setFixedHeight(36);
        b->setToolTip(title);
        auto *bh = new QHBoxLayout(b);
        bh->setContentsMargins(12, 0, 12, 0);
        bh->setSpacing(11);
        auto *ic = new QLabel;
        ic->setObjectName("navIcon");
        ic->setFixedSize(18, 18);
        ic->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(kNav[i].icon, navIconColor, ic->width()));
        auto *tx = new QLabel(title);
        for (QWidget *l : {static_cast<QWidget *>(ic), static_cast<QWidget *>(tx)})
            l->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TransparentForMouseEvents);
        bh->addWidget(ic);
        bh->addWidget(tx, 1);
        if (i == Orders)
        {
            // edit requests: waiting for a manager (manager) / approved and ready to open (cashier)
            auto *count = new QLabel;
            count->setObjectName("navCount");
            count->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
            count->setMinimumWidth(20);
            count->setStyleSheet("background:#e8a317; color:#1b1b1b; border-radius:9px; font-weight:800; font-size:11px; padding:1px 6px;");
            count->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TransparentForMouseEvents);
            count->hide();
            bh->addWidget(count);
        }
        v->addWidget(b);
        m_nav << b;
        b->setVisible(RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, i)); // hide pages this role cannot use
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, i] { goTo(i); });
    }

    v->addStretch();
    return sb;
}

QWidget *MainWindow::buildTopBar()
{
    auto *bar = new QFrame;
    bar->setObjectName("topbar");
    bar->setMinimumHeight(70);
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(bar);
    h->setContentsMargins(20, 10, 16, 10);
    h->setSpacing(12);
    auto *t = new QVBoxLayout;
    t->setSpacing(2);
    t->addStretch();
    m_pageTitle = W::label("", "pageTitle");
    m_pageSubtitle = W::label("", "pageSub");
    m_pageSubtitle->setWordWrap(true);
    t->addWidget(m_pageTitle);
    t->addWidget(m_pageSubtitle);
    t->addStretch();
    h->addLayout(t, 1);

    auto *ready = new QFrame;
    ready->setObjectName("readyPill");
    ready->setToolTip("Type a barcode to look up an item. The scanning page shows camera status.");
    auto *rh = new QHBoxLayout(ready);
    rh->setContentsMargins(10, 6, 10, 6);
    rh->addWidget(W::label("Barcode input available", "readyText"));
    h->addWidget(ready, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);

    auto *chip = new QFrame;
    chip->setObjectName("chip");
    auto *ch = new QHBoxLayout(chip);
    ch->setContentsMargins(12, 5, 12, 5);
    m_clockTime = W::label("", "clockTime");
    m_clockDate = W::label("", "clockDate");
    m_clockTime->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    m_clockDate->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    ch->addWidget(m_clockTime);
    ch->addWidget(m_clockDate);
    h->addWidget(chip, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);

    // Dark Mode toggle (DarkModeManager)
    auto *darkToggle = barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance().createTopBarToggle(bar);
    h->addWidget(darkToggle, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
    connect(&barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance(), &barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::themeChanged,
            this, [this](bool isDark)
            {
                showToast(isDark ? "Switched to Dark Mode" : "Switched to Light Mode");
                AppState::instance().emitChanged(); // rebuild tables so their text colours match the theme
            });

    auto *avatar = new QToolButton;
    avatar->setObjectName("topAvatar");
    avatar->setFixedSize(36, 36);
    const auto names = m_userName.simplified().split(' ', Qt::SkipEmptyParts);
    const auto initials = names.size() > 1 ? names.first().left(1) + names.last().left(1)
                                           : m_userName.trimmed().left(2);
    avatar->setText(initials.toUpper());
    avatar->setAccessibleName("User menu");
    avatar->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
    avatar->setFocusPolicy(Qt::StrongFocus);
    avatar->setToolTip(m_userName + " (" + RolePolicy::name(m_role) + ")");
    avatar->setPopupMode(QToolButton::InstantPopup);
    auto *menu = new QMenu(avatar);
    menu->setObjectName("accountMenu");
    menu->setMinimumWidth(208);
    auto *who = new QWidget;
    auto *wv = new QVBoxLayout(who);
    wv->setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8);
    wv->setSpacing(2);
    auto *nm = W::label("", "h2", who);
    nm->setTextFormat(Qt::PlainText);
    nm->setAccessibleName(m_userName);
    nm->ensurePolished();
    nm->setText(nm->fontMetrics().elidedText(m_userName.simplified(), Qt::ElideRight, 220));
    wv->addWidget(nm);
    wv->addWidget(W::label(RolePolicy::title(m_role), "muted"));
    auto *header = new QWidgetAction(menu);
    header->setDefaultWidget(who);
    menu->addAction(header);
    menu->addSeparator();
    QAction *logout = menu->addAction("Log out");
    connect(logout, &QAction::triggered, this, [this]
            { m_loggedOut = true; if (!close()) m_loggedOut = false; });
    avatar->setMenu(menu);
    h->addWidget(avatar, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);

    auto *timer = new QTimer(this);
    auto tick = [this]
    {
        const auto now = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
        m_clockTime->setText(now.toString("HH:mm:ss"));
        m_clockDate->setText(now.toString("d MMM yyyy"));
    };
    connect(timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, tick);
    timer->start(1000);
    tick();
    return bar;
}

bool MainWindow::goTo(int page)
{
    if (page < 0 || page >= PageCount)
        return false;
    if (!RolePolicy::canOpen(m_role, page))
    {
        showToast(QString("%1 cannot open %2.").arg(RolePolicy::name(m_role), QString::fromLatin1(kNav[page].text)));
        return false;
    }
    m_stack->setCurrentIndex(page);
    m_pageTitle->setText(QString::fromLatin1(kNav[page].text));
    m_pageSubtitle->setText(QString::fromLatin1(kNav[page].subtitle));
    for (int i = 0; i < m_nav.size(); ++i)
    {
        auto *button = m_nav[i];
        const bool active = i == page;
        if (button->property("active").toBool() == active)
            continue;
        button->setProperty("active", active);
        if (auto *ic = button->findChild<QLabel *>("navIcon"))
            ic->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(kNav[i].icon, active ? Theme::white : navIconColor, ic->width()));
        Theme::repolish(button);
        for (auto *c : button->findChildren<QLabel *>())
            Theme::repolish(c);
    }
    switch (page)
    {
    case Dashboard: m_dashboard->refresh(); break;
    case RecordOrder: m_record->focusInput(); break;
    case Inventory: m_inventory->refresh(); break;
    case Categories: m_categories->refresh(); break;
    case Reports: m_reports->refresh(); break;
    case Discounts: m_discounts->refresh(); break;
    default: break;
    }
    return true;
}

QPixmap MainWindow::grabPage(int page)
{
    goTo(page);
    QApplication::processEvents();
    return grab();
}

void MainWindow::openOrderForEdit(const QString &orderId)
{
    if (goTo(RecordOrder))
        m_record->loadOrderForEdit(orderId);
}

void MainWindow::showBillFor(const QString &orderId)
{
    if (goTo(Bills))
        m_bills->showOrder(orderId);
}

void MainWindow::showToast(const QString &text)
{
    m_toast->setText(text);
    m_toast->adjustSize();
    positionToast();
    m_toast->raise();
    m_toast->show();
    m_toastTimer->start(2600);
}

void MainWindow::positionToast()
{
    if (!m_toast)
        return;
    const QWidget *root = centralWidget();
    int bottom = root->height() - 36;
    if (m_stack->currentIndex() == RecordOrder)
        if (const auto *summary = m_record->findChild<QWidget *>("checkoutSummary"))
            bottom = summary->mapTo(root, QPoint()).y();
    m_toast->move((root->width() + sidebarWidth - m_toast->width()) / 2, bottom - m_toast->height());
}

void MainWindow::resizeEvent(QResizeEvent *e)
{
    QMainWindow::resizeEvent(e);
    positionToast();
}

void MainWindow::closeEvent(QCloseEvent *e)
{
    const auto r = m_loggedOut
        ? QMessageBox::question(this, "Log out", "Log out and return to the sign-in screen? The current draft will be lost.")
        : QMessageBox::question(this, "Exit Terminal", "Close Syntro-Check? The current draft will be lost.");
    if (r == QMessageBox::Yes)
        e->accept();
    else
        e->ignore();
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/NavigationModel.h

#pragma once

#include <QString>
#include <QKeySequence>
#include <QVector>

/**
 * Represents a single navigation entry in the sidebar.
 * Used by MainWindow to build the UI dynamically.
 */
struct NavItem {
    QString id;          // internal identifier (e.g. "dashboard")
    QString text;        // visible label
    QString icon;        // icon name used by Theme::iconPixmap
    QKeySequence shortcut; // optional hot‑key (F1, F2, …)
};

/**
 * NavigationModel supplies the list of pages for the POS UI.
 * It can later be backed by a JSON file or a plugin system – for now it
 * returns a static vector, making it OCP‑friendly.
 */
class NavigationModel {
public:
    static QVector<NavItem> items();
};

#include "OrdersPage.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"

using namespace Ui; // makeTable, item, wrapW, pageShell, writeCsv (see UiKit.h)


// =================================================================== OrdersPage
OrdersPage::OrdersPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    root->setSpacing(8);
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader({}, "Orders Management", "Search, review, and resume customer orders."));

    // ---- manager: edit requests waiting for approval (hidden when there are none)
    m_requestsCard = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto* rv = new QVBoxLayout(m_requestsCard);
    rv->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
    rv->setSpacing(8);
    auto* rh = new QHBoxLayout;
    rh->setSpacing(8);
    rh->addWidget(W::iconLabel("clock", Theme::primary, 18));
    m_requestsTitle = W::label("", "h2");
    rh->addWidget(m_requestsTitle);
    rh->addStretch();
    rv->addLayout(rh);
    m_requestsList = new QVBoxLayout;
    m_requestsList->setSpacing(6);
    rv->addLayout(m_requestsList);
    m_requestsCard->hide();
    root->addWidget(m_requestsCard);

    auto* card = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(16, 16, 16, 12);
    v->setSpacing(8);

    auto* tools = new QHBoxLayout;
    tools->setSpacing(8);
    m_orderSearch = new QLineEdit;
    m_orderSearch->setAccessibleName("Search orders");
    m_orderSearch->setClearButtonEnabled(true);
    m_orderSearch->setPlaceholderText("Search by order number, customer or phone…");
    m_orderSearch->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_orderSearch->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    m_custSearch = new QLineEdit; // kept hidden: legacy field, not shown
    m_custSearch->hide();
    tools->addWidget(m_orderSearch, 1);
    auto* exp = W::button("Export CSV", "soft", "export");
    exp->setMinimumHeight(40);
    tools->addWidget(exp);
    v->addLayout(tools);

    auto* tabs = new QHBoxLayout;
    tabs->setSpacing(6);
    for (const QString& name : { "All", "Paid", "In Progress", "On Hold" })
    {
        auto* b = W::button(name, "tab");
        b->setCheckable(true);
        b->setChecked(name == "All");
        tabs->addWidget(b);
        m_tabs << b;
        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, name]
            {
                m_filter = name;
                for (auto* t : m_tabs) t->setChecked(t->text() == name);
                rebuild(); });
    }
    tabs->addStretch();
    v->addLayout(tabs);

    m_table = makeTable({ "ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS SUMMARY", "PAYMENT", "AMOUNT", "STATUS", "EDIT WINDOW", "ACTIONS" });
    m_table->setProperty("managementTable", true);
    m_table->setAlternatingRowColors(true);
    m_table->setAccessibleName("Orders");
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setMinimumSectionSize(70);
    for (int c : {0, 1, 4, 5})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    for (int c : {2, 6, 7, 8})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(2, 152);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(6, 112);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(7, 132);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(8, 204);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    m_empty = W::label("", "muted");
    m_empty->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    m_empty->setMinimumHeight(64);
    m_empty->setWordWrap(true);
    v->addWidget(m_empty);
    m_showing = W::label("", "muted");
    v->addWidget(m_showing);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);

    connect(m_orderSearch, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(m_custSearch, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &OrdersPage::rebuild);
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::editRequestsChanged, this, &OrdersPage::rebuild);
    connect(exp, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            QStringList rows{ "Order ID,Time,Customer,Contact,Items,Payment,Amount,Status" };
            for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
                rows << QString("%1,%2,\"%3\",\"%4\",%5,\"%6\",%7,%8").arg(o.id, o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss"), o.customer, o.contact)
                .arg(o.itemCount()).arg(o.payment).arg(o.total, 0, 'f', 2).arg(o.status);
            if (writeCsv(this, "orders.csv", rows)) emit toast("Orders exported"); });
    rebuild();
}

void OrdersPage::setSearch(const QString& text)
{
    m_custSearch->clear();
    m_orderSearch->setText(text);
}

void OrdersPage::rebuild()
{
    rebuildRequests();
    const auto& all = AppState::instance().orders();
    QString q = m_orderSearch->text().trimmed();
    q.remove('#');
    QVector<OrderRecord> rows;
    for (const auto& o : all)
    {
        if (m_filter != "All" && o.status != m_filter)
            continue;
        if (!q.isEmpty() && !o.id.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) && !o.customer.contains(q, Qt::CaseInsensitive) &&
            !o.contact.contains(q))
            continue;
        rows << o;
    }
    auto& st = AppState::instance();
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& o = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 54);
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item("#" + o.id, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(o.time.toString("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm") + "\n" + o.shift));
        {
            auto* cw = new QWidget;
            auto* cl = new QVBoxLayout(cw);
            cl->setContentsMargins(8, 4, 8, 4);
            cl->setSpacing(0);
            auto* n = W::label(o.customer + (o.tier == "VIP" ? "  [VIP]" : ""));
            n->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
            cw->setToolTip(n->text() + "\n" + o.contact);
            auto* c = W::label(o.contact, "muted");
            cl->addWidget(n);
            cl->addWidget(c);
            m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, cw);
        }
        auto* summary = item(o.summary());
        summary->setToolTip(o.summary());
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, summary);
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(o.payment));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(o.total), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 6, wrapW(W::badge(o.status, W::statusBadgeKind(o.status))));
        // ---- edit window (requirement 3): paid orders can be edited for 7 days after payment, with manager approval
        const int daysLeft = st.editDaysLeft(o);
        QLabel* windowBadge = nullptr;
        const UiStore::EditRequest* req = UiStore::instance().latestEditRequest(o.id);
        const QString reqStatus = (o.status == "Paid" && daysLeft > 0 && req) ? req->status : QString();
        if (reqStatus == "pending")
            windowBadge = W::badge("Requested", "amber");
        else if (reqStatus == "approved")
            windowBadge = W::badge("Approved", "green");
        else if (reqStatus == "rejected")
            windowBadge = W::badge("Rejected", "red");
        else if (o.status == "Paid")
            windowBadge = daysLeft > 0
                ? W::badge(QString("Editable · %1d").arg(daysLeft), daysLeft <= 2 ? "amber" : "green")
                : W::badge("Window closed", "grey");
        if (req && !reqStatus.isEmpty())
            windowBadge->setToolTip(QString("Requested by %1 (%2)%3").arg(req->cashier, req->reason,
                req->manager.isEmpty() ? QString() : " - " + req->status + " by " + req->manager));
        else if (o.status == "In Progress" || o.status == "On Hold")
            windowBadge = W::badge("Not paid yet", "blue");
        else
            windowBadge = W::badge("Archived", "grey");
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 7, wrapW(windowBadge));

        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 0, 4, 0);
        h->setSpacing(4);
        const OrderRecord copy = o;
        auto* view = W::button("", "flat", "eye");
        view->setFixedWidth(34);
        view->setToolTip("View details");
        view->setAccessibleName("View details for order " + o.id);
        h->addWidget(view);
        connect(view, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::orderDetails(this, copy); });
        auto* rc = W::button("", "flat", "bills");
        rc->setFixedWidth(34);
        rc->setToolTip(o.billId.isEmpty() ? "Receipt" : "View Bill");
        rc->setAccessibleName(rc->toolTip() + " for order " + o.id);
        h->addWidget(rc);
        connect(rc, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
            { Dialogs::receipt(this, copy); });
        if (o.status == "In Progress" || o.status == "On Hold")
        {
            auto* resume = W::button("Resume", "mini", "play");
            resume->setToolTip("Open this order in Record Order");
            h->addWidget(resume);
            connect(resume, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
                { emit resumeOrder(copy.id); });
        }
        else if (o.status == "Paid")
        {
            QString text = "Open", icon = "unlock", tip = "Edit this order";
            bool enabled = daysLeft > 0;
            if (daysLeft <= 0)
            {
                text = "Locked";
                icon = "lock";
                tip = "The 7-day edit window is over";
            }
            else if (m_role != UserRole::Manager)
            {
                if (reqStatus == "pending")
                {
                    text = "Waiting";
                    icon = "clock";
                    tip = "Waiting for a manager to approve";
                    enabled = false;
                }
                else if (reqStatus == "approved")
                    tip = "Approved by " + req->manager + " - open it to edit";
                else
                {
                    text = "Request edit";
                    tip = reqStatus == "rejected" ? "The last request was rejected by " + req->manager + ". Ask again."
                                                  : "Ask a manager for permission to edit this paid order";
                }
            }
            auto* open = W::button(text, "mini", icon);
            open->setEnabled(enabled);
            open->setToolTip(tip);
            h->addWidget(open);
            connect(open, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
                { openPaid(copy); });
        }
        h->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 8, w);
    }
    m_empty->setText(all.isEmpty() ? "No orders recorded yet." : "No orders match the current search and status.");
    m_empty->setVisible(rows.isEmpty());
    m_showing->setText(QString("Showing %1 of %2 orders").arg(rows.size()).arg(all.size()));
}

void OrdersPage::setRole(UserRole role)
{
    m_role = role;
    rebuild();
}

// Manager: edits straight away (his own approval is recorded). Cashier: opens it when a manager approved the request,
// otherwise sends a request - no PIN on the cashier's screen.
void OrdersPage::openPaid(const OrderRecord& order)
{
    auto& store = UiStore::instance();
    if (AppState::instance().editDaysLeft(order) <= 0)
    {
        emit toast("Order " + order.id + " can no longer be edited (7 days after payment are over)");
        return;
    }
    if (m_role == UserRole::Manager)
    {
        store.grantApproval(order.id, m_user.isEmpty() ? QString("Manager") : m_user, "Edited by manager");
        emit editOrder(order.id);
        return;
    }
    store.reloadEditRequests();
    if (store.approvalFor(order.id))
    {
        emit editOrder(order.id);
        return;
    }
    QString reason;
    if (!Dialogs::requestEditReason(this, order, &reason))
        return;
    const QString error = store.requestEdit(order.id, m_user, reason);
    emit toast(error.isEmpty() ? "Request sent - order " + order.id + " opens once a manager approves it" : error);
}

void OrdersPage::showEvent(QShowEvent* e)
{
    UiStore::instance().reloadEditRequests(); // another terminal may have approved / requested meanwhile
    QWidget::showEvent(e);
}

void OrdersPage::rebuildRequests()
{
    while (auto* item = m_requestsList->takeAt(0))
    {
        if (auto* w = item->widget())
            w->deleteLater();
        delete item;
    }
    const auto pending = UiStore::instance().pendingEditRequests();
    m_requestsCard->setVisible(m_role == UserRole::Manager && !pending.isEmpty());
    if (m_role != UserRole::Manager)
        return;
    m_requestsTitle->setText(QString("Waiting for your approval (%1)").arg(pending.size()));
    for (const auto& r : pending)
    {
        OrderRecord order;
        for (const auto& o : AppState::instance().orders())
            if (o.id == r.orderId) { order = o; break; }
        auto* row = new QWidget;
        auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(row);
        h->setContentsMargins(0, 2, 0, 2);
        h->setSpacing(10);
        auto* what = W::label(QString("<b>#%1</b> &nbsp;%2 &nbsp;·&nbsp; %3").arg(r.orderId,
            (order.customer.isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in Customer") : order.customer).toHtmlEscaped(), W::money(order.total)));
        what->setTextFormat(Qt::RichText);
        h->addWidget(what);
        auto* why = W::label(QString("%1 asked: %2  ·  %3").arg(r.cashier, r.reason, r.requestedAt.toString("h:mm AP")), "muted");
        h->addWidget(why, 1);
        auto* reject = W::button("Reject", "flat", "close");
        auto* approve = W::button("Approve", "primary", "check");
        h->addWidget(reject);
        h->addWidget(approve);
        const QString id = r.orderId;
        connect(approve, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id] {
            const QString err = UiStore::instance().decideEdit(id, m_user, true);
            emit toast(err.isEmpty() ? "Approved - the cashier can now open order " + id : err); });
        connect(reject, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, id] {
            const QString err = UiStore::instance().decideEdit(id, m_user, false);
            emit toast(err.isEmpty() ? "Request for order " + id + " rejected" : err); });
        m_requestsList->addWidget(row);
    }
}
