// "Payment" dialog: the bill on the left (only the discounts that apply are shown), how the customer pays on the
// right (cash with quick amounts, or card / wallet), and the change to return.
// Frontend only: the numbers come from Pricing::quote (the same function the order is saved with), so what the
// cashier sees here is exactly what lands in Order.txt / Bill.txt.
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Pricing.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QButtonGroup>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QStackedWidget>
#include <QVBoxLayout>
#include <QtMath>

namespace Dialogs
{
    bool payment(QWidget *parent, const PaymentRequest &req, PaymentResult &res)
    {
        Pricing::Quote q = Pricing::quote(req.lines, 0);
        const QString who = req.customer.trimmed().isEmpty() ? QString("Walk-in Customer") : req.customer.trimmed();
        Ui::Modal d(parent, "cash", "Payment",
                    QString("Order #%1  ·  %2  ·  %3 item%4").arg(req.orderId, who).arg(q.units).arg(q.units == 1 ? "" : "s"),
                    QString(), 820);

        auto *cols = new QHBoxLayout;
        cols->setSpacing(18);

        // ============================================================ left: the bill
        auto *bill = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *bv = new QVBoxLayout(bill);
        bv->setContentsMargins(18, 16, 18, 16);
        bv->setSpacing(10);
        bv->addWidget(W::label("Bill", "caption"));

        // one "label ......... amount" line; returns the row so it can be hidden when it is zero
        auto line = [&](const QString &k, QLabel *&key, QLabel *&val)
        {
            auto *row = new QWidget;
            auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(row);
            h->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
            key = W::label(k, "muted");
            val = W::label("PKR 0.00");
            val->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
            h->addWidget(key, 1);
            h->addWidget(val);
            bv->addWidget(row);
            return row;
        };
        QLabel *grossK, *gross, *itemK, *itemDisc, *seasonK, *seasonal, *taxK, *tax;
        line("Subtotal", grossK, gross);
        auto *itemRow = line("Item discount", itemK, itemDisc);
        auto *seasonRow = line("Seasonal discount", seasonK, seasonal);

        // special discount: typed percent, at most 8 percent (requirement 8)
        auto *spRow = new QHBoxLayout;
        spRow->setSpacing(8);
        spRow->addWidget(W::label("Special discount", "muted"));
        auto *pct = new QDoubleSpinBox;
        pct->setRange(0, UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent);
        pct->setDecimals(1);
        pct->setSingleStep(0.5);
        pct->setSuffix(" %");
        pct->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
        pct->setAlignment(Qt::AlignRight);
        pct->setFixedWidth(70);
        pct->setMinimumHeight(32);
        pct->setToolTip(QString("At most %1%").arg(UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent, 0, 'f', 0));
        spRow->addWidget(pct);
        spRow->addWidget(W::label(QString("max %1%").arg(UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent, 0, 'f', 0), "muted"));
        spRow->addStretch();
        auto *special = W::label("PKR 0.00");
        special->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
        spRow->addWidget(special);
        bv->addLayout(spRow);

        line("Tax", taxK, tax);
        bv->addStretch();

        auto *totalBox = W::card(nullptr, "totalBox");
        auto *th = new QHBoxLayout(totalBox);
        th->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
        auto *totalK = W::label("Total");
        totalK->setStyleSheet("font-size:15px; font-weight:700; color:white; background:transparent;");
        auto *payable = W::label("PKR 0.00");
        payable->setStyleSheet("font-size:28px; font-weight:800; color:white; background:transparent;");
        th->addWidget(totalK);
        th->addStretch();
        th->addWidget(payable);
        bv->addWidget(totalBox);
        cols->addWidget(bill, 1);

        // ============================================================ right: how the customer pays
        auto *right = new QVBoxLayout;
        right->setSpacing(10);
        right->addWidget(W::label("Pay with", "caption"));
        auto *modeRow = new QHBoxLayout;
        modeRow->setSpacing(6);
        auto *modes = new QButtonGroup(&d);
        const QStringList modeNames = {"Cash", "Card", "Wallet"};
        for (int i = 0; i < 3; ++i)
        {
            auto *b = W::button(modeNames[i], "tab"); // text only: an icon would vanish on the selected (blue) button
            b->setCheckable(true);
            b->setMinimumHeight(42);
            modes->addButton(b, i);
            modeRow->addWidget(b, 1);
        }
        using Payment = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode;
        const auto preferredPayment = AppState::instance().settings().defaultPayment;
        const int initialMode = preferredPayment == Payment::Card ? 1 : preferredPayment == Payment::Wallet ? 2 : 0;
        modes->button(initialMode)->setChecked(true);
        right->addLayout(modeRow);
        right->addSpacing(4);

        // page 0: cash received, page 1: bank / wallet
        auto *pages = new QStackedWidget;
        auto *cashPage = new QWidget;
        auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(cashPage);
        cv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        cv->setSpacing(8);
        cv->addWidget(W::label("Amount received", "caption"));
        auto *tender = new QDoubleSpinBox;
        tender->setPrefix("PKR ");
        tender->setDecimals(2);
        tender->setRange(0, 100000000);
        tender->setGroupSeparatorShown(true);
        tender->setAlignment(Qt::AlignRight);
        tender->setMinimumHeight(52);
        tender->setStyleSheet("font-size:22px; font-weight:800;");
        tender->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
        cv->addWidget(tender);
        auto *quick = new QHBoxLayout;
        quick->setSpacing(6);
        bool replaceOnFirstKey = true; // the pre-filled exact amount is replaced by the first note the cashier adds
        bool updating = false;
        auto *exact = W::button("Exact", "soft");
        exact->setMinimumHeight(36);
        quick->addWidget(exact, 1);
        for (int add : {500, 1000, 5000})
        {
            auto *b = W::button(QString("+%L1").arg(add), "soft");
            b->setMinimumHeight(36);
            b->setToolTip("Adds to the amount received");
            QObject::connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&, add]
                             {
                const double base = replaceOnFirstKey ? 0.0 : tender->value();
                replaceOnFirstKey = false;
                tender->setValue(base + add); });
            quick->addWidget(b, 1);
        }
        cv->addLayout(quick);
        cv->addStretch();
        pages->addWidget(cashPage);

        auto *gatePage = new QWidget;
        auto *gv = new QVBoxLayout(gatePage);
        gv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        gv->setSpacing(8);
        auto *gateCap = W::label("Bank / terminal", "caption");
        gv->addWidget(gateCap);
        auto *gate = new QComboBox;
        gate->setEditable(true);
        gate->setMinimumHeight(42);
        gv->addWidget(gate);
        gv->addStretch();
        pages->addWidget(gatePage);
        right->addWidget(pages, 1);

        // change to return (green) / still short (red) / charged to card or wallet (blue)
        auto *balBox = new QFrame;
        auto *bh = new QHBoxLayout(balBox);
        bh->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
        auto *balCap = W::label("Change");
        auto *balVal = W::label("PKR 0.00");
        bh->addWidget(balCap);
        bh->addStretch();
        bh->addWidget(balVal);
        right->addWidget(balBox);
        cols->addLayout(right, 1);
        d.body()->addLayout(cols);

        // ============================================================ footer
        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel", "flat");
        auto *ok = W::button("Pay", "primary", "check");
        ok->setMinimumHeight(42);
        ok->setMinimumWidth(190);
        ok->setDefault(true); // Enter pays
        d.footer()->addStretch();
        d.footer()->addWidget(cancel);
        d.footer()->addWidget(ok);
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);

        // ============================================================ behaviour
        auto fillGateways = [&]
        {
            gate->clear();
            if (modes->checkedId() == 2)
            {
                gate->addItems({"JazzCash", "Easypaisa", "SadaPay", "NayaPay"});
                gateCap->setText("Wallet");
            }
            else
            {
                gate->addItems({"HBL - POS Terminal 04", "Meezan Bank - POS", "UBL - POS Terminal", "Allied Bank - POS"});
                gateCap->setText("Bank / terminal");
            }
            gate->setCurrentIndex(0);
        };

        auto refresh = [&]
        {
            q = Pricing::quote(req.lines, pct->value());
            gross->setText(W::money(q.gross));
            itemDisc->setText(W::money(-q.itemDiscount));
            itemRow->setVisible(q.itemDiscount > 0.005);   // only lines that apply are shown
            seasonK->setText(QString("Seasonal discount (%1%)").arg(q.seasonalPercent, 0, 'g', 4));
            seasonal->setText(W::money(-q.seasonal));
            seasonRow->setVisible(!q.seasonName.isEmpty());
            seasonRow->setToolTip(q.seasonName);
            special->setText(W::money(-q.special));
            tax->setText(W::money(q.tax));
            payable->setText(W::money(q.payable));

            const int mode = modes->checkedId();
            pages->setCurrentIndex(mode == 0 ? 0 : 1);
            QString bg = "#e3f6ec", fg = "#0b6b3f";
            bool enough = true;
            if (mode == 0)
            {
                const double diff = tender->value() - q.payable;
                enough = diff >= -0.005;
                balCap->setText(enough ? "Change to return" : "Still short");
                balVal->setText(W::money(enough ? qMax(0.0, diff) : -diff));
                if (!enough)
                {
                    bg = "#fde7e6";
                    fg = "#ba1a1a";
                }
            }
            else
            {
                balCap->setText(mode == 1 ? "Charge to card" : "Charge to wallet");
                balVal->setText(W::money(q.payable));
                bg = "#e6edff";
                fg = "#1d46b4";
            }
            balBox->setStyleSheet(QString("QFrame{background:%1; border-radius:10px;} QLabel{background:transparent; color:%2;}").arg(bg, fg));
            balCap->setStyleSheet(QString("font-size:14px; font-weight:700; color:%1;").arg(fg));
            balVal->setStyleSheet(QString("font-size:22px; font-weight:800; color:%1;").arg(fg));
            ok->setText("Pay " + W::money(q.payable));
            ok->setEnabled(mode == 0 ? enough : !gate->currentText().trimmed().isEmpty());
        };

        QObject::connect(pct, qOverload<double>(&QDoubleSpinBox::valueChanged), &d, [&](double)
                         {
            // while the amount is still the pre-filled exact amount it follows the new total
            const double before = q.payable;
            const Pricing::Quote nq = Pricing::quote(req.lines, pct->value());
            if (replaceOnFirstKey && qAbs(tender->value() - before) < 0.005)
            {
                updating = true;
                tender->setValue(nq.payable);
                updating = false;
            }
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(tender, qOverload<double>(&QDoubleSpinBox::valueChanged), &d, [&](double)
                         {
            if (!updating)
                replaceOnFirstKey = false;
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(exact, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            replaceOnFirstKey = true;
            updating = true;
            tender->setValue(q.payable);
            updating = false;
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(modes, &QButtonGroup::idClicked, &d, [&](int)
                         {
            fillGateways();
            refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(gate, &QComboBox::currentTextChanged, &d, [&](const QString &) { refresh(); });
        QObject::connect(ok, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::accept);

        fillGateways();
        updating = true;
        tender->setValue(q.payable); // exact amount pre-filled: pressing Enter completes a cash sale at once
        updating = false;
        refresh();
        if (initialMode == 0)
        {
            tender->setFocus();
            tender->selectAll();
        }
        else
            gate->setFocus();
        if (d.exec() != QDialog::Accepted)
            return false;

        const int id = modes->checkedId();
        res.mode = modeNames[id];
        res.merchant = id == 0 ? QString() : gate->currentText().trimmed();
        res.tendered = id == 0 ? tender->value() : 0;
        res.specialPercent = Pricing::clampSpecial(pct->value());
        return true;
    }
}

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/Pricing.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "Pricing.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include <QtMath>

namespace
{
    double round2(double v) { return qRound64(v * 100.0) / 100.0; }
}

namespace Pricing
{
    double clampSpecial(double percent)
    {
        if (percent < 0)
            return 0;
        return qMin(percent, UiStore::kSpecialDiscountCapPercent);
    }

    Quote quote(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent, const QDate &on)
    {
        Quote q;
        q.specialPercent = clampSpecial(specialPercent);
        const Season *season = UiStore::instance().activeSeason(on);
        if (season)
        {
            q.seasonalPercent = season->percent;
            q.seasonName = season->name;
        }
        for (const auto &l : lines)
        {
            const double base = l.price - l.discount; // per unit, after the product's own discount
            double sUnit = 0;
            if (season) // requirement 15: the active season applies to the whole bill
                sUnit = round2(base * season->percent / 100.0);
            const double xUnit = round2((base - sUnit) * q.specialPercent / 100.0);
            q.gross += l.price * l.qty;
            q.itemDiscount += l.discount * l.qty;
            q.seasonal += sUnit * l.qty;
            q.special += xUnit * l.qty;
            q.tax += l.tax * l.qty;
            q.units += l.qty;
            ++q.lines;
        }
        q.payable = q.gross - q.itemDiscount - q.seasonal - q.special + q.tax;
        return q;
    }

    QVector<ReceiptLine> fold(const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines, double specialPercent, const QDate &on)
    {
        const double sp = clampSpecial(specialPercent);
        const Season *season = UiStore::instance().activeSeason(on);
        QVector<ReceiptLine> out = lines;
        for (auto &l : out)
        {
            const double base = l.price - l.discount;
            double sUnit = 0;
            if (season) // requirement 15: the active season applies to the whole bill
                sUnit = round2(base * season->percent / 100.0);
            const double xUnit = round2((base - sUnit) * sp / 100.0);
            l.discount = round2(l.discount + sUnit + xUnit);
        }
        return out;
    }

    void apply(OrderRecord &o, const Quote &q)
    {
        o.subtotal = q.gross;
        o.discount = q.totalDiscount();
        o.tax = q.tax;
        o.total = q.payable;
        o.seasonal = q.seasonal;
        o.special = q.special;
        o.seasonalPercent = q.seasonalPercent;
        o.specialPercent = q.specialPercent;
        o.seasonName = q.seasonName;
    }
}

// "New Product" / "Edit Product" dialog (manager): a simple form on the left, the barcode on the right.
// The barcode is generated automatically from the chosen category; saving goes through AppState::addProduct /
// updateProduct, which write Product.txt (with the Category Id) and the barcode PNG.
#include "BarcodeArt.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "UiStore.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QButtonGroup>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDir>
#include <QDoubleSpinBox>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QGridLayout>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLabel>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QMarginsF>
#include <QMessageBox>
#include <QPageLayout>
#include <QPageSize>
#include <QPainter>
#include <QPdfWriter>
#include <QPixmap>
#include <QPushButton>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QStandardItemModel>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace
{
    // Saves a one-page A4 sheet (3 x 8 labels) with this barcode; returns false when the user cancels or it fails.
    bool saveLabelSheet(QWidget *parent, const QString &code, const QString &name)
    {
        const QString suggested = QDir(AppState::instance().dataDir()).filePath("labels-" + code + ".pdf");
        const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(parent, "Save barcode label sheet", suggested, "PDF (*.pdf)");
        if (path.isEmpty())
            return false;
        QPdfWriter pdf(path);
        pdf.setPageSize(QPageSize(QPageSize::A4));
        pdf.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(10, 10, 10, 10), QPageLayout::Millimeter);
        pdf.setResolution(300);
        pdf.setCreator("Syntro-Check");
        QPainter p(&pdf);
        if (!p.isActive())
            return false;
        const QImage img = BarcodeArt::render(code, 4, 120, true);
        QFont f = p.font();
        f.setPixelSize(34);
        f.setBold(true);
        p.setFont(f);
        const int cols = 3, rows = 8;
        const QRect page = p.viewport();
        const int cw = page.width() / cols, ch = page.height() / rows;
        for (int r = 0; r < rows; ++r)
            for (int c = 0; c < cols; ++c)
            {
                const QRect cell = QRect(c * cw, r * ch, cw, ch).adjusted(12, 12, -12, -12);
                p.drawText(QRect(cell.left(), cell.top(), cell.width(), 44), Qt::AlignCenter, name.left(26));
                const QRect box = cell.adjusted(0, 48, 0, 0);
                const QSize s = img.size().scaled(box.size(), Qt::KeepAspectRatio);
                p.drawImage(QRect(box.center().x() - s.width() / 2, box.top(), s.width(), s.height()), img);
            }
        p.end();
        return true;
    }
}

namespace Dialogs
{
    bool editProduct(QWidget *parent, const ProductInfo *existing)
    {
        auto &app = AppState::instance();
        auto &store = UiStore::instance();
        const bool isNew = (existing == nullptr);
        const ProductInfo cur = existing ? *existing : ProductInfo();

        Ui::Modal d(parent, isNew ? "plus" : "edit", isNew ? "New Product" : "Edit Product",
                    isNew ? "Choose a category and fill in the details - the barcode is created for you."
                          : "Barcode and category stay the same.",
                    QString(), 780);

        // small helpers: a caption above a field, and a money box without spin arrows
        auto field = [](const QString &caption, QWidget *w)
        {
            auto *box = new QVBoxLayout;
            box->setSpacing(5);
            box->addWidget(W::label(caption, "caption"));
            box->addWidget(w);
            return box;
        };
        auto money = [](double value)
        {
            auto *s = new QDoubleSpinBox;
            s->setDecimals(2);
            s->setRange(0, 10000000);
            s->setValue(value);
            s->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
            s->setMinimumHeight(38);
            return s;
        };

        auto *cols = new QHBoxLayout;
        cols->setSpacing(18);

        // ------------------------------------------------ form (left)
        auto *form = new QVBoxLayout;
        form->setSpacing(14);

        auto *catBox = new QComboBox;
        catBox->setMinimumHeight(38);
        const QString curCat = cur.categoryId.isEmpty() ? cur.ean.left(2) : cur.categoryId;
        for (const auto &c : store.categories())
            catBox->addItem(c.id + "  ·  " + c.name, c.id); // the Category Id becomes the barcode prefix
        if (!isNew && catBox->findData(curCat) < 0 && curCat.size() == 2)
            catBox->addItem(curCat + "  ·  (not in PCategory.txt)", curCat);
        if (!isNew)
        {
            catBox->setCurrentIndex(qMax(0, catBox->findData(curCat)));
            catBox->setEnabled(false); // the category is part of the barcode, which is fixed
        }
        form->addLayout(field("Category", catBox));

        auto *name = new QLineEdit(cur.name);
        name->setPlaceholderText("e.g. Olpers Full Cream Milk 1.5L");
        name->setMinimumHeight(38);
        form->addLayout(field("Product name", name));

        auto *row1 = new QHBoxLayout;
        row1->setSpacing(12);
        auto *acr = new QLineEdit(cur.sku);
        acr->setMaxLength(8);
        acr->setPlaceholderText("Up to 8 letters");
        acr->setMinimumHeight(38);
        row1->addLayout(field("Acronym", acr), 1);
        auto *unitRow = new QHBoxLayout;
        unitRow->setSpacing(6);
        auto *countBtn = W::button("Count", "tab");
        auto *weightBtn = W::button("Weight", "tab");
        auto *unitGroup = new QButtonGroup(&d);
        for (auto *b : {countBtn, weightBtn})
        {
            b->setCheckable(true);
            b->setMinimumHeight(38);
            unitGroup->addButton(b);
            unitRow->addWidget(b, 1);
        }
        (cur.unit == "weight" ? weightBtn : countBtn)->setChecked(true);
        auto *unitW = new QWidget;
        unitW->setLayout(unitRow);
        unitRow->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        row1->addLayout(field("Sold by", unitW), 1);
        form->addLayout(row1);

        auto *row2 = new QHBoxLayout;
        row2->setSpacing(12);
        auto *price = money(cur.price);
        auto *tax = money(cur.tax);
        auto *disc = money(cur.discount);
        row2->addLayout(field("Price (PKR, no tax)", price), 1);
        row2->addLayout(field("Tax (PKR)", tax), 1);
        row2->addLayout(field("Discount (PKR)", disc), 1);
        form->addLayout(row2);

        auto *row3 = new QHBoxLayout;
        row3->setSpacing(12);
        auto *stock = new QSpinBox;
        stock->setRange(0, 1000000);
        stock->setValue(cur.stock);
        stock->setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox::NoButtons);
        stock->setMinimumHeight(38);
        row3->addLayout(field(isNew ? "Opening stock" : "Stock", stock), 1);
        row3->addStretch(2);
        form->addLayout(row3);
        form->addStretch();
        cols->addLayout(form, 3);

        // ------------------------------------------------ barcode (right)
        auto *card = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(card);
        cv->setContentsMargins(16, 14, 16, 14);
        cv->setSpacing(8);
        cv->addWidget(W::label("Barcode", "caption"));
        auto *preview = new QLabel;
        preview->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        preview->setMinimumHeight(92);
        preview->setStyleSheet("background:white; border-radius:8px;");
        cv->addWidget(preview);
        auto *codeLbl = W::label("", "mono");
        codeLbl->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        codeLbl->setStyleSheet("font-size:20px; font-weight:800; letter-spacing:3px;");
        cv->addWidget(codeLbl);
        auto *catLbl = W::label("", "muted");
        catLbl->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        catLbl->setWordWrap(true);
        cv->addWidget(catLbl);
        cv->addStretch();
        auto *sheet = W::button("Print labels", "flat", "print");
        cv->addWidget(sheet, 0, Qt::AlignHCenter);
        card->setMinimumWidth(250);
        cols->addWidget(card, 2);
        d.body()->addLayout(cols);

        auto *errLbl = W::label("", "muted");
        errLbl->setWordWrap(true);
        errLbl->setStyleSheet("color:#ba1a1a; font-weight:700; background:#ffdad6; border-radius:8px; padding:8px 10px;");
        errLbl->hide();
        d.body()->addWidget(errLbl);

        // ------------------------------------------------ behaviour
        QString code = isNew ? QString() : cur.ean;
        auto showCode = [&]
        {
            const bool have = code.size() == 8;
            codeLbl->setText(have ? code : "--------");
            const Category *c = store.category(have ? code.left(2) : catBox->currentData().toString());
            catLbl->setText(have ? "Starts with category " + code.left(2) + (c ? " (" + c->name + ")" : QString()) : QString());
            sheet->setEnabled(have);
            if (have)
                preview->setPixmap(QPixmap::fromImage(BarcodeArt::render(code, 2, 70, false)));
            else
                preview->setText("Pick a category");
        };
        auto generate = [&] // new products: a fresh barcode for the chosen category, no button needed
        {
            if (!isNew)
                return;
            code = app.nextBarcode(catBox->currentData().toString());
            errLbl->setVisible(code.isEmpty());
            if (code.isEmpty())
                errLbl->setText("This category has no free barcodes left.");
            showCode();
        };
        auto suggestAcronym = [&]
        {
            QString s = name->text().toUpper();
            s.remove(QRegularExpression("[^A-Z0-9]"));
            return s.left(8);
        };
        bool acrTouched = !isNew;
        QObject::connect(acr, &QLineEdit::textEdited, &d, [&](const QString &) { acrTouched = true; });
        QObject::connect(name, &QLineEdit::textChanged, &d, [&](const QString &)
                         {
            if (!acrTouched)
                acr->setText(suggestAcronym()); });
        QObject::connect(catBox, qOverload<int>(&QComboBox::currentIndexChanged), &d, [&](int) { generate(); });
        QObject::connect(sheet, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            if (code.size() == 8)
                saveLabelSheet(&d, code, name->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? acr->text() : name->text().trimmed()); });

        auto *cancel = W::button("Cancel", "flat");
        auto *save = W::button(isNew ? "Add Product" : "Save Changes", "primary", "check");
        save->setMinimumHeight(40);
        save->setDefault(true); // Enter saves
        d.footer()->addStretch();
        d.footer()->addWidget(cancel);
        d.footer()->addWidget(save);
        QObject::connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, &QDialog::reject);
        QObject::connect(save, &QPushButton::clicked, &d, [&]
                         {
            ProductInfo p = cur;
            p.name = name->text().trimmed();
            p.sku = acr->text().trimmed().toUpper();
            p.unit = weightBtn->isChecked() ? "weight" : "count";
            p.price = price->value();
            p.tax = tax->value();
            p.discount = disc->value();
            p.stock = stock->value();
            p.ean = code;
            p.categoryId = code.left(2); // requirement 2: the Category Id is stored in Product.txt
            QString err;
            const bool ok = isNew ? app.addProduct(p, &err) : app.updateProduct(p, &err);
            if (!ok)
            {
                errLbl->setText(err.isEmpty() ? QString("The product could not be saved.") : err);
                errLbl->show();
                return;
            }
            d.accept(); });

        if (isNew)
            generate();
        else
            showCode();
        name->setFocus();
        return d.exec() == QDialog::Accepted;
    }
}

ProductsPage::ProductsPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout* root;
    pageShell(this, root);
    root->setSpacing(8);
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader({}, "Product Catalog", "Find products, inspect barcodes, and manage the catalog."));
    auto* card = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(card);
    v->setContentsMargins(16, 16, 16, 12);
    v->setSpacing(8);
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout;
    h->addWidget(W::label("Registered products", "h1"));
    m_count = W::badge("", "blue");
    h->addWidget(m_count);
    h->addStretch();
    v->addLayout(h);
    auto* toolbar = new QHBoxLayout;
    toolbar->setSpacing(8);
    m_search = new QLineEdit;
    m_search->setPlaceholderText("Search products by name, ID, acronym, or barcode…");
    m_search->setToolTip("Search by name, acronym, product ID, or barcode.");
    m_search->setClearButtonEnabled(true);
    m_search->setAccessibleName("Search products");
    m_search->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_search->addAction(Theme::icon("search", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    toolbar->addWidget(m_search, 1);
    m_issues = W::button("", "soft", "warn");
    m_issues->setMinimumHeight(40);
    connect(m_issues, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this] { Dialogs::loadIssues(this); });
    toolbar->addWidget(m_issues);
    m_newBtn = W::button("New Product", "primary", "plus");
    m_newBtn->setMinimumHeight(40);
    m_newBtn->setVisible(false);
    connect(m_newBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ProductsPage::addNew);
    toolbar->addWidget(m_newBtn);
    v->addLayout(toolbar);
    m_table = makeTable({ "ID", "ACRONYM", "BARCODE", "PRODUCT", "UNIT", "PRICE", "TAX", "DISC.", "STOCK", "STATUS", "ACTIONS" });
    m_table->setProperty("managementTable", true);
    m_table->setAlternatingRowColors(true);
    m_table->setAccessibleName("Registered products");
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setMinimumSectionSize(42);
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(3, QHeaderView::Interactive);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(3, 196);
    for (int c : {0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 6, 7, 8})
        m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(c, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(9, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(9, 104);
    m_table->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(10, QHeaderView::Fixed);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(10, 202);
    m_table->setWordWrap(false);
    v->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    root->addWidget(card, 1);
    connect(m_search, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, [this]
        { rebuild(); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &ProductsPage::rebuild);
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::setSearch(const QString& text) { m_search->setText(text); }

void ProductsPage::rebuild()
{
    const QString q = m_search->text().trimmed();
    auto& st = AppState::instance();
    const QVector<ProductInfo> rows = q.isEmpty() ? st.products() : st.searchText(q);
    m_count->setText(QString("%1 products").arg(rows.size()));
    const int nIssues = st.productIssues().size();
    m_issues->setVisible(nIssues > 0);
    m_issues->setText(QString("Data issues (%1)").arg(nIssues));
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(rows.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < rows.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& p = rows[r];
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 44);
        const int limit = st.settings().lowStockThreshold;
        m_table->setItem(r, 0, item(QString::number(p.id)));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, item(p.sku, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "#004ac6", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 2, item(p.ean));
        auto* name = item(p.name, Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true);
        name->setToolTip(p.name);
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, name);
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, item(p.unit));
        m_table->setItem(r, 5, item(W::money(p.price), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, "", true));
        m_table->setItem(r, 6, item(W::money(p.tax), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));
        m_table->setItem(r, 7, item(W::money(p.discount), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter));
        const bool low = p.stock <= limit;
        m_table->setItem(r, 8, item(QString::number(p.stock), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter, low ? "#ba1a1a" : "", low));
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 9, wrapW(W::badge(p.stock == 0 ? "Out" : low ? "Low stock" : "In stock", p.stock == 0 ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green")));
        auto* d = W::button("Details", "mini");
        auto* cell = new QWidget;
        auto* cl = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        cl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 4, 0);
        cl->setSpacing(6);
        cl->addWidget(d);
        const QString ean = p.ean;
        const int pid = p.id;
        if (RolePolicy::canEditCatalogue(m_role)) // manager: edit the product in the same dialog as "New Product"
        {
            auto* e = W::button("Edit", "mini", "edit");
            cl->addWidget(e);
            const ProductInfo copy = p;
            connect(e, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, copy]
                {
                    // open after the click handler returns: saving rebuilds this table (and this button)
                    QTimer::singleShot(0, this, [this, copy]
                        {
                            if (Dialogs::editProduct(this, &copy))
                                emit toast("Product " + copy.sku + " updated in Product.txt");
                        });
                });
        }
        if (!m_viewOnly) // Manager views products only: no "Add" button
        {
            auto* b = W::button("Add", "mini");
            b->setEnabled(p.stock > 0);
            cl->addWidget(b);
            connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, ean]
                { emit addToOrder(ean); });
        }
        cl->addStretch();
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 10, cell);
        connect(d, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, pid]
            { Dialogs::productDescription(this, pid); });
    }
}
void ProductsPage::setViewOnly(bool on)
{
    m_viewOnly = on;
    m_table->setRowCount(0); // drop the old rows (with their buttons) before rebuilding
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::setRole(UserRole role)
{
    m_role = role;
    m_newBtn->setVisible(RolePolicy::canEditCatalogue(role));
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    rebuild();
}

void ProductsPage::addNew()
{
    if (RolePolicy::canEditCatalogue(m_role) && Dialogs::editProduct(this, nullptr))
        emit toast("Product saved to Product.txt");
}

#include "RecordOrderPage.h"

class ElidedLabel final : public QLabel
{
public:
    ElidedLabel()
    {
        setTextFormat(Qt::PlainText);
        setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Ignored, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
    }

    void setFullText(const QString &text)
    {
        m_fullText = text;
        setAccessibleName(text);
        updateText();
    }

protected:
    void resizeEvent(QResizeEvent *event) override
    {
        QLabel::resizeEvent(event);
        updateText();
    }

private:
    void updateText()
    {
        const auto visible = fontMetrics().elidedText(m_fullText, Qt::ElideRight, contentsRect().width());
        setText(visible);
        setToolTip(visible == m_fullText ? QString() : m_fullText);
    }

    QString m_fullText;
};

namespace
{
    QWidget* twoLine(const QString& top, const QString& bottom)
    {
        auto* w = new QWidget;
        auto* v = new QVBoxLayout(w);
        v->setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4);
        v->setSpacing(1);
        auto* t = new ElidedLabel;
        t->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700; font-size:13.5px;");
        t->setFullText(top);
        auto* b = new ElidedLabel;
        b->setObjectName("muted");
        b->setFullText(bottom);
        v->addWidget(t);
        v->addWidget(b);
        return w;
    }
    QTableWidgetItem* cell(const QString& text, const QString& color = "", bool bold = false)
    {
        auto* i = new QTableWidgetItem(text);
        i->setToolTip(text);
        i->setTextAlignment(Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        if (!color.isEmpty())
            i->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor(color)));
        if (bold)
        {
            QFont f = i->font();
            f.setBold(true);
            i->setFont(f);
        }
        return i;
    }
}

RecordOrderPage::RecordOrderPage(QWidget* parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    auto* root = W::pageLayout(this);
    root->setContentsMargins(10, 6, 10, 6);
    root->setSpacing(0);
    root->addWidget(buildBarcodeStrip());
    root->addWidget(buildEditBanner());
    root->addWidget(buildCustomerStrip());
    root->addWidget(buildParkedStrip());
    auto* body = new QHBoxLayout;
    body->setSpacing(0);
    body->addWidget(buildProductPane(), 27);
    body->addWidget(buildCart(), 73);
    root->addLayout(body, 1);
    root->addWidget(buildSummary());

    setTabOrder(m_manual, m_addBtn);
    setTabOrder(m_addBtn, m_descBtn);

    ScanAudio::instance().warmUp();

    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::scanned, this, [this](const QString& ean)
        { processBarcode(ean, true, true); });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::barcodeDetected, this, [this](const QString& ean) {
        const auto product = AppState::instance().findByCode(ean);
        if (product) m_feed->showResult(ean + "  -  " + product->name, true);
        else m_feed->showResult(ean + "  -  not in Product.txt", false);
        AppState::instance().emitScan(ean);
        });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::scanError, this, [this](const QString& error)
        {
            setCameraActive(false);
            emit toast(error);
        });
    loadDemoDraft();

    // keyboard: F2 hold, F10 checkout, F4 description, F9 camera (only while this page is visible)
    auto key = [this](const QKeySequence& k)
        {
            auto* s = new QShortcut(k, this);
            s->setContext(Qt::WidgetWithChildrenShortcut);
            return s;
        };
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F2)), &QShortcut::activated, this, &RecordOrderPage::holdOrder);
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F10)), &QShortcut::activated, this, &RecordOrderPage::checkout);
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F4)), &QShortcut::activated, this, &RecordOrderPage::describeCurrent);
    connect(key(QKeySequence(Qt::Key_F9)), &QShortcut::activated, m_camToggle, &QPushButton::click);

    // on-hold chips: redraw when the list changes and every 30 s (the "4m ago" text)
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::parkedChanged, this, &RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked);
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked);
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::seasonsChanged, this, &RecordOrderPage::updateTotals);
    auto* ageTimer = new QTimer(this);
    ageTimer->setInterval(30000);
    connect(ageTimer, &QTimer::timeout, this, &RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked);
    ageTimer->start();
    rebuildParked();
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildBarcodeStrip()
{
    auto* strip = W::card(this, "strip");
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(strip);
    h->setContentsMargins(16, 6, 16, 6);
    h->setSpacing(10);

    auto* label = W::label("Barcode", "h1");
    h->addWidget(label);
    m_manual = new QLineEdit;
    m_manual->setObjectName("checkoutBarcode");
    m_manual->setPlaceholderText("Scan or type a barcode, then press Enter");
    m_manual->setAccessibleName("Barcode");
    m_manual->setMaxLength(12);
    m_manual->setMinimumHeight(42);
    m_manual->addAction(Theme::icon("barcode", Theme::outline, 16), QLineEdit::LeadingPosition);
    label->setBuddy(m_manual);
    h->addWidget(m_manual, 1);
    connect(m_manual, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, [this]
        { processBarcode(m_manual->text(), false, true); });

    auto* browse = W::button("Browse products", "soft", "products");
    m_camToggle = W::button("Camera  [F9]", "soft", "camera");
    for (auto* button : {browse, m_camToggle})
    {
        button->setMinimumHeight(40);
        h->addWidget(button);
    }
    connect(browse, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::quickLookup);
    connect(m_camToggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        { setCameraActive(m_cameraPanel->isHidden()); });
    return strip;
}

    m_pName->setObjectName("h1");
    detail->addWidget(m_pName);
    m_pMeta = W::label("Scan or enter a barcode.", "muted");
    m_pMeta->setWordWrap(true);
    detail->addWidget(m_pMeta);
    auto* amounts = new QHBoxLayout;
    m_pPrice = W::label("", "checkoutPrice");
    m_pStock = W::label("", "checkoutStock");
    amounts->addWidget(m_pPrice);
    amounts->addWidget(m_pStock, 0, Qt::AlignRight);
    detail->addLayout(amounts);
    pv->addWidget(info);

    m_addBtn = W::button("Add to order", "primary", "cart");
    m_addBtn->setMinimumHeight(36);
    m_addBtn->setEnabled(false);
    m_descBtn = W::button("Description  [F4]", "flat", "info");
    m_descBtn->setEnabled(false);
    pv->addWidget(m_addBtn);
    pv->addWidget(m_descBtn);
    connect(m_descBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::describeCurrent);
    connect(m_addBtn, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            if (m_preview)
                addToCart(*m_preview);
            focusInput();
        });
    v->addWidget(pc, 0, Qt::AlignTop);

    m_cameraPanel = W::card(col);
    auto* cv = new QVBoxLayout(m_cameraPanel);
    cv->setContentsMargins(10, 5, 10, 5);
    m_feed = new CameraFeedWidget;
    m_feed->setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Ignored, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
    m_feed->setFixedHeight(90);
    cv->addWidget(m_feed);
    m_cameraPanel->hide();
    v->addWidget(m_cameraPanel);

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildCart()
{
    auto* oc = W::card(this);
    auto* ov = new QVBoxLayout(oc);
    ov->setContentsMargins(0, 14, 0, 10);
    ov->setSpacing(6);

    auto* head = new QHBoxLayout;
    head->setContentsMargins(18, 0, 14, 0);
    head->setSpacing(10);
    head->addWidget(W::label("Cart", "h1"));
    m_itemsBadge = W::badge("0 items", "grey");
    head->addWidget(m_itemsBadge);
    head->addStretch();
    auto* clear = W::button("Clear", "flat", "trash");
    head->addWidget(clear);
    ov->addLayout(head);
    connect(clear, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::newDraft);

    m_table = Ui::makeTable({"PRODUCT", "UNIT PRICE", "QTY", "DISCOUNT", "LINE TOTAL", ""});
    m_table->setAccessibleName("Current order");
    m_table->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::NoSelection);
    auto* hh = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Fixed);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(0, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(1, 95);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(2, 116);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(3, 95);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(4, 104);
    m_table->setColumnWidth(5, 42);
    m_table->horizontalHeaderItem(4)->setToolTip("Includes item discounts and tax. Order discounts appear in the summary.");
    m_table->setWordWrap(false);
    ov->addWidget(m_table, 1);
    return oc;
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildSummary()
{
    auto* tc = W::card(this, "totals");
    tc->setObjectName("checkoutSummary");
    auto* th = new QHBoxLayout(tc);
    th->setContentsMargins(14, 8, 14, 8);
    th->setSpacing(12);
    auto row = [&](const QString& k, QLabel*& val)
        {
            auto* r = new QVBoxLayout;
            r->setSpacing(2);
            r->addWidget(W::label(k, "muted"));
            val = W::label("PKR 0.00");
            val->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700; font-size:14px;");
            r->addWidget(val);
            th->addLayout(r);
        };
    row("Subtotal", m_gross);
    row("Discounts", m_disc);
    row("Tax", m_tax);
    th->addStretch(1);

    auto* tb = W::card(tc, "totalBox");
    auto* tv = new QVBoxLayout(tb);
    tv->setContentsMargins(14, 5, 14, 5);
    tv->setSpacing(0);
    auto* cap = W::label("NET PAYABLE", "caption");
    tv->addWidget(cap);
    m_total = W::label("PKR 0.00", "checkoutPayable");
    m_total->setStyleSheet("font-size:28px; font-weight:800;");
    tv->addWidget(m_total);
    th->addWidget(tb);
    m_hold = W::button("Hold  [F2]", "soft", "pause");
    m_hold->setMinimumHeight(44);
    m_hold->setToolTip("Put this order on hold and start a new one (one press, no questions)");
    m_checkout = W::button("Checkout  [F10]", "primary", "check");
    m_checkout->setMinimumHeight(44);
    th->addWidget(m_hold);
    th->addWidget(m_checkout);
    connect(m_hold, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::holdOrder);
    connect(m_checkout, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &RecordOrderPage::checkout);
    return tc;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- behaviour
void RecordOrderPage::focusInput()
{
    m_manual->setFocus();
    m_manual->selectAll();
}

void RecordOrderPage::simulateScan()
{
    setCameraActive(true);
}

void RecordOrderPage::setCameraActive(bool active)
{
    m_cameraPanel->setVisible(active);
    m_camToggle->setText(active ? "Stop camera  [F9]" : "Camera  [F9]");
    m_feed->setRunning(active);
}

void RecordOrderPage::quickLookup()
{
    QStringList items;
    for (const auto& p : AppState::instance().products())
        items << QString("%1  -  %2").arg(p.ean, p.name);
    bool ok = false;
    const QString pick = QInputDialog::getItem(this, "Browse products", "Choose a product:", items, 0, false, &ok);
    if (ok)
        showCode(pick.section("  -  ", 0, 0), false);
    focusInput();
}

void RecordOrderPage::showCode(const QString& codeIn, bool fromScanner)
{
    processBarcode(codeIn, fromScanner, false);
}

void RecordOrderPage::processBarcode(const QString& codeIn, bool fromScanner, bool playAudio)
{
    const QString code = codeIn.trimmed();
    if (code.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Enter a barcode first");
        return;
    }
    const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
    if (!p)
    {
        setPreview(std::nullopt);
        emit toast("No product found for \"" + code + "\"");
        return;
    }
    m_feed->setCode(p->ean);
    setPreview(p);
    m_manual->clear();
    // A camera scan, or a complete barcode typed / sent by a USB scanner (which "types" the code and presses
    // Enter), goes straight into the cart. Typing a product ID or acronym only shows it, to check before adding.
    if ((fromScanner || code == p->ean) && AppState::instance().settings().autoAddScans)
    {
        if (!addToCart(*p))
            return;
    }
    if (playAudio && p->stock + m_stockCredit.value(p->id) > 0)
        ScanAudio::instance().playSuccess();
}

QVector<QPair<QString, int>> RecordOrderPage::addScannedBatch(const QVector<QPair<QString, int>>& items)
{
    QVector<QPair<QString, int>> accepted;
    int addedUnits = 0;
    int adjustedCount = 0;
    QString lastError;
    std::optional<ProductInfo> lastAdded;

    for (const auto& entry : items)
    {
        const QString code = entry.first.trimmed();
        const int requested = entry.second;
        if (code.isEmpty() || requested <= 0)
            continue;

        const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
        if (!p)
        {
            ++adjustedCount;
            lastError = QString("No product found for \"%1\"").arg(code);
            continue;
        }

        int have = 0;
        for (const auto& l : m_cart)
            if (l.p.sku == p->sku)
                have = l.qty;

        const int available = qMax(0, p->stock + m_stockCredit.value(p->id) - have);
        const int addQty = qMin(requested, available);
        const QString problem = stockIssue(p->id, have, requested);
        if (addQty != requested || !problem.isEmpty())
        {
            ++adjustedCount;
            lastError = problem;
            if (addQty <= 0 || !stockIssue(p->id, have, addQty).isEmpty())
                continue;
        }

        bool found = false;
        for (auto& l : m_cart)
        {
            if (l.p.sku == p->sku)
            {
                l.qty += addQty;
                found = true;
                break;
            }
        }
        if (!found)
            m_cart.push_back({ *p, addQty });

        accepted.push_back({code, addQty});
        addedUnits += addQty;
        lastAdded = *p;
    }

    if (lastAdded)
    {
        m_feed->setCode(lastAdded->ean);
        setPreview(lastAdded);
        m_manual->clear();
        rebuildTable();
        focusInput();
    }

    if (addedUnits > 0 && adjustedCount == 0)
    {
        emit toast(QString("Added %1 product%2 (%3 unit%4) to checkout")
                       .arg(accepted.size())
                       .arg(accepted.size() == 1 ? "" : "s")
                       .arg(addedUnits)
                       .arg(addedUnits == 1 ? "" : "s"));
    }
    else if (addedUnits > 0)
    {
        emit toast(QString("Added %1 unit%2 · %3 item%4 need review in the scanner")
                       .arg(addedUnits)
                       .arg(addedUnits == 1 ? "" : "s")
                       .arg(adjustedCount)
                       .arg(adjustedCount == 1 ? "" : "s"));
    }
    else if (!lastError.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(lastError);
    }
    return accepted;
}

void RecordOrderPage::setPreview(const std::optional<ProductInfo>& p)
{
    m_preview = p;
    m_addBtn->setEnabled(p.has_value() && p->stock + m_stockCredit.value(p->id) > 0);
    m_descBtn->setEnabled(p.has_value() && p->id > 0);
    m_pPrice->setVisible(p.has_value());
    m_pStock->setVisible(p.has_value());
    if (!p)
    {
        m_pName->setFullText("No product selected");
        m_pPrice->clear();
        m_pMeta->setText("Scan or enter a barcode.");
        m_pMeta->setToolTip({});
        m_pStock->clear();
        W::setBadge(m_pBadge, "—", "grey");
        return;
    }
    m_pName->setFullText(p->name);
    m_pPrice->setText(W::money(p->price));
    m_pMeta->setText(QString("%1  •  %2").arg(p->ean, p->unit));
    m_pMeta->setToolTip(QString("Tax %1 · Discount %2").arg(W::money(p->tax), W::money(p->discount)));
    const bool low = p->stock <= AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold;
    m_pStock->setText(QString("%1 in stock").arg(p->stock));
    m_pStock->setProperty("lowStock", low);
    Theme::repolish(m_pStock);
    W::setBadge(m_pBadge, p->stock == 0 ? "Out of stock" : low ? "Low stock" : "In stock",
        p->stock == 0 ? "red" : low ? "amber" : "green");

}

bool RecordOrderPage::addToCart(const ProductInfo& p, int qty)
{
    int have = 0;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        if (l.p.sku == p.sku)
            have = l.qty;
    const QString problem = stockIssue(p.id, have, qty);
    if (!problem.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(problem);
        return false;
    }
    bool found = false;
    for (auto& l : m_cart)
        if (l.p.sku == p.sku)
        {
            l.qty += qty;
            found = true;
        }
    if (!found)
        m_cart.push_back({ p, qty });
    rebuildTable();
    emit toast(QString("Added %1 × %2").arg(qty).arg(p.name));
    return true;
}

void RecordOrderPage::changeQty(int row, int delta)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_cart.size())
        return;
    auto& l = m_cart[row];
    const int nq = l.qty + delta;
    if (nq < 1)
    {
        removeRow(row);
        return;
    }
    const QString problem = stockIssue(l.p.id, l.qty, delta);
    if (delta > 0 && !problem.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(problem);
        return;
    }
    const auto* stepper = m_table->cellWidget(row, 2);
    const auto* focused = QApplication::focusWidget();
    const QString control = focused && stepper && stepper->isAncestorOf(focused) ? focused->objectName() : QString();
    l.qty = nq;
    rebuildTable();
    if (!control.isEmpty())
        if (auto* button = m_table->cellWidget(row, 2)->findChild<QPushButton*>(control))
            button->setFocus();
}

void RecordOrderPage::removeRow(int row)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_cart.size())
        return;
    m_cart.removeAt(row);
    rebuildTable();
    focusInput();
}

void RecordOrderPage::rebuildTable()
{
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setRowCount(m_cart.size());
    for (int r = 0; r < m_cart.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto& l = m_cart[r];
        const double gross = l.p.price * l.qty;
        const double disc = l.p.discount * l.qty;
        const double tax = l.p.tax * l.qty;
        m_table->setRowHeight(r, 54);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 0, twoLine(l.p.name, l.p.sku + "  •  " + l.p.unit));
        m_table->setItem(r, 1, cell(W::money(l.p.price), "", true));
        auto* stp = new QtyStepper(l.qty);
        for (auto* button : stp->findChildren<QPushButton*>())
        {
            const QString action = button->text() == "+" ? "Increase quantity" : "Decrease quantity";
            button->setObjectName(action);
            button->setAccessibleName(action + " for " + l.p.name);
        }
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 2, stp);
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::minus, this, [this, r]
            { changeQty(r, -1); });
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::plus, this, [this, r]
            { changeQty(r, +1); });
        m_table->setItem(r, 3, cell(disc > 0 ? W::money(-disc) : "—", disc > 0 ? "#0b7a36" : "#8a94ab"));
        m_table->setItem(r, 4, cell(W::money(gross - disc + tax), "#2554d8", true));
        auto* del = new QPushButton;
        del->setProperty("kind", "flat");
        del->setIcon(Theme::icon("trash", Theme::error, 18));
        del->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        del->setToolTip("Remove");
        del->setAccessibleName("Remove " + l.p.name);
        auto* wrap = new QWidget;
        auto* wl = new QHBoxLayout(wrap);
        wl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        wl->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        wl->addWidget(del);
        m_table->setCellWidget(r, 5, wrap);
        connect(del, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, r]
            { removeRow(r); });
    }
    updateTotals();
}

void RecordOrderPage::updateTotals()
{
    const auto quote = Pricing::quote(cartLines(), 0);
    const int items = quote.units;
    W::setBadge(m_itemsBadge, QString("%1 item%2").arg(items).arg(items == 1 ? "" : "s"), items ? "blue" : "grey");
    m_gross->setText(W::money(quote.gross));
    m_disc->setText(W::money(-quote.totalDiscount()));
    m_disc->setToolTip(QString("Item discounts %1 · Seasonal discounts %2")
        .arg(W::money(quote.itemDiscount), W::money(quote.seasonal)));
    m_tax->setText(W::money(quote.tax));
    m_total->setText(W::money(quote.payable));
    m_total->setMinimumWidth(qMax(176, m_total->sizeHint().width() + 4));
    m_checkout->setEnabled(items > 0);
    m_hold->setEnabled(items > 0 && m_editingId.isEmpty()); // a paid order being edited cannot go on hold
}

void RecordOrderPage::loadDemoDraft()
{
    // Start with an empty order; products come from Product.txt.
    rebuildTable();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
}

void RecordOrderPage::newDraft()
{
    if (!m_editingId.isEmpty())
        UiStore::instance().revokeApproval(m_editingId);
    const QString held = m_heldId;
    startFresh(held.isEmpty() ? "New order started" : "New order started (order " + held + " stays on hold)");
}

void RecordOrderPage::startFresh(const QString& message)
{
    setEditMode(QString(), QString());
    m_heldId.clear();
    m_cart.clear();
    m_customer->clear();
    m_contact->clear();
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    rebuildParked();
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + AppState::instance().nextOrderId());
    focusInput();
    if (!message.isEmpty())
        emit toast(message);
}

QString RecordOrderPage::stockIssue(int id, int have, int add) const
{
    if (add <= 0)
        return QString();
    const int credit = m_stockCredit.value(id);
    const int need = have + add - credit; // units that must still come out of the current stock
    if (need <= 0)
        return QString();
    return AppState::instance().stockProblem(id, 0, need);
}

void RecordOrderPage::checkout()
{
    if (m_cart.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("The order is empty");
        return;
    }
    OrderRecord o;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        o.lines.push_back({ l.p.name, l.p.sku, l.qty, l.p.price, l.p.tax, l.p.discount, l.p.id });
    o.recompute();

    Dialogs::PaymentRequest req;
    req.orderId = m_sessionId->text().mid(1);
    req.customer = m_customer->text();
    req.contact = m_contact->text();
    req.lines = o.lines;
    Dialogs::PaymentResult res;
    if (!Dialogs::payment(this, req, res))
    {
        focusInput();
        return;
    }
    const QString mode = res.mode, merchant = res.merchant;
    const double tendered = res.tendered;
    const auto quote = Pricing::quote(o.lines, res.specialPercent);
    // Lines keep only the product's own discount (BillDetail.txt); the seasonal and special amounts go to
    // Bill.txt as their own fields (set on `o` by Pricing::apply and passed to Billing by AppState::addOrder).
    Pricing::apply(o, quote);

    o.customer = m_customer->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? "Walk-in Customer" : m_customer->text().trimmed();
    o.contact = m_contact->text().trimmed().isEmpty() ? "-" : m_contact->text().trimmed();
    o.payment = mode;
    o.merchant = merchant;
    o.tendered = tendered;
    o.status = "Paid";
    o.shift = "POS #04 • Shift A";
    QString checkoutError;
    const QString editedId = m_editingId;
    const QString heldId = m_heldId;
    // new order, a resumed on-hold order (same Order ID), or an edited paid order (same Order ID and Bill ID)
    const bool saved = editedId.isEmpty() ? AppState::instance().addOrder(o, &checkoutError, heldId)
                                          : AppState::instance().replaceOrder(editedId, o, &checkoutError);
    if (!saved)
    {
        emit toast(checkoutError);
        return;
    }
    if (!editedId.isEmpty())
        UiStore::instance().revokeApproval(editedId);

    Dialogs::receipt(this, o);
    startFresh(!editedId.isEmpty() ? "Order " + editedId + " updated (bill " + o.billId + ") - " + W::money(o.total)
                                   : "Order " + o.id + " paid - " + W::money(o.total));
}
// ---- placeholder bodies for the Increment 3 actions (the redesigned screen replaces them) ----
void RecordOrderPage::setRole(UserRole role) { m_role = role; }
void RecordOrderPage::describeCurrent()
{
    if (m_preview && m_preview->id > 0)
    {
        Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_preview->id);
        focusInput();
    }
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- on hold (parked orders)
QVector<ReceiptLine> RecordOrderPage::cartLines() const
{
    QVector<ReceiptLine> lines;
    for (const auto& l : m_cart)
        lines.push_back({ l.p.name, l.p.sku, l.qty, l.p.price, l.p.tax, l.p.discount, l.p.id });
    return lines;
}

QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildParkedStrip()
{
    auto* strip = W::card(this, "strip");
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(strip);
    h->setContentsMargins(14, 6, 14, 6);
    h->setSpacing(10);
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("pause", Theme::primary, 18));
    m_parkTitle = W::label("ON HOLD", "caption");
    h->addWidget(m_parkTitle);

    auto* scroll = new QScrollArea;
    scroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
    scroll->setFrameShape(QFrame::NoFrame);
    scroll->setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAlwaysOff);
    scroll->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAsNeeded);
    scroll->setFixedHeight(46);
    scroll->setStyleSheet("QScrollArea, QScrollArea > QWidget > QWidget { background: transparent; border: none; }");
    auto* inner = new QWidget;
    m_parkChips = new QHBoxLayout(inner);
    m_parkChips->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    m_parkChips->setSpacing(8);
    scroll->setWidget(inner);
    h->addWidget(scroll, 1);
    m_parkedList = scroll;
    m_parkEmpty = W::label("No held orders", "muted");
    h->addWidget(m_parkEmpty, 1);
    return strip;
}

void RecordOrderPage::rebuildParked()
{
    // drop the old chips
    while (QLayoutItem* it = m_parkChips->takeAt(0))
    {
        if (QWidget* w = it->widget())
        {
            w->hide();
            w->deleteLater();
        }
        delete it;
    }
    const auto parked = UiStore::instance().parked();
    int shown = 0;
    for (const auto& p : parked)
        shown += p.code != m_heldId;
    m_parkTitle->setText(QString("ON HOLD (%1)").arg(shown));
    m_parkedList->setVisible(shown != 0);
    m_parkEmpty->setVisible(shown == 0);
    const QDateTime now = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
    for (const auto& p : parked)
    {
        if (p.code == m_heldId)
            continue; // it is in the cart right now
        const qint64 secs = p.parkedAt.secsTo(now);
        const QString ago = secs < 60 ? QString("just now")
            : secs < 3600 ? QString("%1m ago").arg(secs / 60)
            : QString("%1h ago").arg(secs / 3600);
        const QString text = QString("#%1 · %2").arg(p.code, p.customer.left(18));
        auto* chip = W::button(text, "soft", "arrow-right");
        chip->setMinimumHeight(36);
        chip->setToolTip(QString("Resume %1 · %2 items · %3 · %4")
            .arg(p.customer).arg(p.itemCount()).arg(W::money(p.total()), ago));
        const QString code = p.code;
        connect(chip, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, code] { resumeParked(code); });
        auto* del = W::button("", "flat", "trash");
        del->setToolTip("Discard this held order");
        del->setAccessibleName("Discard held order " + code);
        connect(del, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, code]
            {
                QString err;
                if (UiStore::instance().discardParked(code, &err))
                    emit toast("Held order " + code + " discarded");
                else
                    emit toast(err);
            });
        auto* cell = new QWidget;
        auto* cl = new QHBoxLayout(cell);
        cl->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        cl->setSpacing(2);
        cl->addWidget(chip);
        cl->addWidget(del);
        m_parkChips->addWidget(cell);
    }
    m_parkChips->addStretch();
}

void RecordOrderPage::holdOrder()
{
    if (!m_editingId.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Finish or cancel the edit of order " + m_editingId + " first - it cannot be put on hold");
        return;
    }
    if (m_cart.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Nothing to hold - the order is empty");
        return;
    }
    QString err;
    const QString code = UiStore::instance().park(m_customer->text(), m_contact->text(), cartLines(), m_heldId, &err);
    if (code.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast(err);
        return;
    }
    startFresh("Order " + code + " put on hold"); // ready for the next customer's first scan
}

void RecordOrderPage::resumeParked(const QString& code)
{
    if (code == m_heldId)
        return; // already in the cart
    ParkedOrder po;
    if (!UiStore::instance().takeParked(code, po))
    {
        emit toast("That held order is no longer available");
        return;
    }
    if (!m_editingId.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Finish or cancel the edit of order " + m_editingId + " first");
        return;
    }
    // A cart that is not empty is put on hold first (swap), so nothing is lost and no question is asked.
    QString swapped;
    if (!m_cart.isEmpty())
    {
        QString err;
        swapped = UiStore::instance().park(m_customer->text(), m_contact->text(), cartLines(), m_heldId, &err);
        if (swapped.isEmpty())
        {
            emit toast(err);
            return;
        }
    }
    m_heldId = code;
    m_sessionId->setText("#" + code);

    m_cart.clear();
    int adjusted = 0;
    for (const auto& line : po.lines)
    {
        std::optional<ProductInfo> prod;
        for (const auto& p : AppState::instance().products())
            if (line.productId > 0 ? p.id == line.productId : p.sku == line.acronym)
            {
                prod = p;
                break;
            }
        if (!prod)
        {
            ++adjusted;
            continue;
        }
        int qty = line.qty;
        while (qty > 0 && !AppState::instance().stockProblem(prod->id, 0, qty).isEmpty())
            --qty; // stock changed while the order was on hold
        if (qty != line.qty)
            ++adjusted;
        if (qty > 0)
            m_cart.push_back({ *prod, qty });
    }
    m_customer->setText(po.customer == "Walk-in Customer" ? QString() : po.customer);
    m_contact->setText(po.contact == "-" ? QString() : po.contact);
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    rebuildParked();
    focusInput();

    QString msg = "Resumed order " + code;
    if (!swapped.isEmpty())
        msg += "  (previous order held as " + swapped + ")";
    if (adjusted > 0)
        msg += QString("  -  %1 line%2 changed because of stock").arg(adjusted).arg(adjusted == 1 ? "" : "s");
    emit toast(msg);
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- editing an existing order (requirement 3)
QWidget* RecordOrderPage::buildEditBanner()
{
    m_editBanner = new QFrame;
    m_editBanner->setObjectName("editBanner");
    auto* h = new QHBoxLayout(m_editBanner);
    h->setContentsMargins(14, 8, 10, 8);
    h->setSpacing(10);
    h->addWidget(W::iconLabel("edit", QColor("#8a5a00"), 18));
    m_editText = W::label("");
    m_editText->setWordWrap(true);
    m_editText->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
    h->addWidget(m_editText, 1);
    auto* cancel = W::button("Cancel edit", "soft", "undo");
    h->addWidget(cancel);
    connect(cancel, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            const QString id = m_editingId;
            if (!id.isEmpty())
                UiStore::instance().revokeApproval(id);
            startFresh("Edit of order " + id + " cancelled - nothing was changed"); });
    m_editBanner->hide();
    return m_editBanner;
}

void RecordOrderPage::setEditMode(const QString& orderId, const QString& text)
{
    m_editingId = orderId;
    m_stockCredit = orderId.isEmpty() ? QMap<int, int>() : AppState::instance().stockHeldBy(orderId);
    m_editText->setText(text);
    m_editBanner->setVisible(!orderId.isEmpty());
    if (!orderId.isEmpty())
        m_sessionId->setText("#" + orderId);
}

void RecordOrderPage::loadOrderForEdit(const QString& orderId)
{
    auto& app = AppState::instance();
    OrderRecord order;
    bool found = false;
    for (const auto& o : app.orders())
        if (o.id == orderId) { order = o; found = true; break; }
    if (!found)
    {
        emit toast("Order " + orderId + " was not found");
        return;
    }
    if (order.status == "Archived")
    {
        emit toast("Order " + orderId + " is archived and cannot be edited");
        return;
    }
    if (order.status != "Paid")
    {
        resumeParked(orderId); // on-hold / in-progress: straight back into the cart, one click
        return;
    }
    if (app.editDaysLeft(order) <= 0)
    {
        emit toast("Order " + orderId + " can no longer be edited (7 days after payment are over)");
        return;
    }
    UiStore::Approval approval;
    if (!UiStore::instance().approvalFor(orderId, &approval))
    {
        emit toast("A manager's approval is needed to edit the paid order " + orderId);
        return;
    }
    if (m_editingId == orderId)
        return; // already open

    // a cart that is not empty is put on hold first, so nothing is lost
    QString swapped;
    if (!m_cart.isEmpty() && m_editingId.isEmpty())
    {
        QString err;
        swapped = UiStore::instance().park(m_customer->text(), m_contact->text(), cartLines(), m_heldId, &err);
        if (swapped.isEmpty())
        {
            emit toast(err);
            return;
        }
    }
    m_heldId.clear();

    m_cart.clear();
    for (const auto& line : order.lines)
    {
        ProductInfo p;
        bool inCatalogue = false;
        if (line.productId > 0)
            for (const auto& c : app.products())
                if (c.id == line.productId) { p = c; inCatalogue = true; break; }
        if (inCatalogue)
            p.discount = line.discount; // keep the discount the order was sold with
        else // manual line: keep what the bill says
        {
            p.id = 0;
            p.sku = line.acronym;
            p.name = line.name;
            p.unit = "count";
            p.price = line.price;
            p.tax = line.tax;
            p.discount = line.discount;
            p.stock = 1000000;
        }
        m_cart.push_back({ p, line.qty });
    }
    m_customer->setText(order.customer == "Walk-in Customer" ? QString() : order.customer);
    m_contact->setText(order.contact == "-" ? QString() : order.contact);
    setEditMode(orderId, QString("Editing paid order #%1 (bill %2)  •  approved by %3 (%4)  •  change items, then Checkout [F10] to save")
                             .arg(orderId, order.billId.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : order.billId, approval.manager, approval.reason));
    setPreview(std::nullopt);
    rebuildTable();
    rebuildParked();
    focusInput();
    emit toast(swapped.isEmpty() ? "Order " + orderId + " opened for editing"
                                 : "Order " + orderId + " opened (previous order held as " + swapped + ")");
}

#include "ReportsPage.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include "billing/BillRepository.h"
#include "common/RecordText.h"
#include <QDateEdit>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>

#include <QMarginsF>

#include <algorithm>

using namespace Ui;

namespace
{
    QString cap(const QString &s) { return s.isEmpty() ? s : s.left(1).toUpper() + s.mid(1).toLower(); }
    bool isCash(const OrderRecord &o) { return o.payment.compare("cash", Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0; }
    QString quantity(double value)
    {
        return QString::fromStdString(barcodehub::common::formatQuantity(value));
    }

    QDateTime reportTime(const OrderRecord &order)
    {
        return order.status == "Paid" || order.status == "Archived" ? order.paymentTime() : order.time;
    }

    std::vector<barcodehub::billing::Bill> savedBills(const AppState &app)
    {
        std::vector<barcodehub::billing::Bill> bills;
        if (!app.dataDir().isEmpty())
        {
            const auto path = QDir(app.dataDir()).filePath("Bill.txt");
            if (!barcodehub::billing::BillRepository(path.toStdWString()).loadAll(bills))
                bills.clear();
        }
        return bills;
    }

    const char *kTabNames[] = {"Incomplete Orders", "Completed Orders", "Cash Sales", "All Sales", "Top 10 Sold",
                               "Top 10 Remaining Stock"};
    const char *kPeriodNames[] = {"All", "Daily", "Weekly", "Monthly", "Yearly"};

    void clearLayout(QLayout *l)
    {
        while (QLayoutItem *it = l->takeAt(0))
        {
            if (QWidget *w = it->widget())
            {
                w->hide();
                w->deleteLater();
            }
            delete it;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================ page
ReportsPage::ReportsPage(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout *root;
    pageShell(this, root, true);
    root->setSpacing(8);

    auto *pdf = W::button("Export / Print PDF", "primary", "print");
    pdf->setToolTip("Export the current report to PDF (Ctrl+P)");
    root->addWidget(sectionHeader({}, "Business Reports & Analytics",
                                  "Audit orders, sales, and product turnover.", {csv, pdf}));
    connect(csv, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ReportsPage::exportCsv);
    connect(pdf, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ReportsPage::exportPdf);
    auto *print = new QShortcut(QKeySequence("Ctrl+P"), this);
    print->setContext(Qt::WidgetWithChildrenShortcut);
    connect(print, &QShortcut::activated, this, &ReportsPage::exportPdf);

    // ---- tabs

        connect(b, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, i] { setTab(i); });

    ph->addStretch();

    auto *range = new QHBoxLayout;
    range->setSpacing(8);
    range->addWidget(W::label("CUSTOM RANGE", "caption"));

    m_toEdit = dateEdit(QDate::currentDate(), "dd MMM yyyy", 34);
    m_fromEdit->setAccessibleName("Report start date");
    m_toEdit->setAccessibleName("Report end date");
    range->addWidget(m_fromEdit);
    range->addWidget(W::label("to", "muted"));
    range->addWidget(m_toEdit);
    auto *apply = W::button("Filter", "primary", "filter");
    range->addWidget(apply);
    range->addStretch();
    periodLayout->addLayout(range);
    connect(apply, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            m_from = m_fromEdit->date();
            m_to = m_toEdit->date();
            if (m_to < m_from)
                std::swap(m_from, m_to);
            setPeriod(Custom);
        });
    root->addWidget(m_periodBar);

    m_outstandingNotice = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto *notice = new QVBoxLayout(m_outstandingNotice);
    notice->setContentsMargins(12, 8, 12, 8);
    notice->setSpacing(2);
    notice->addWidget(W::label("OUTSTANDING ORDERS · ALL DATES", "caption"));
    notice->addWidget(W::label("Open and held orders are shown regardless of when they were created.", "muted"));
    root->addWidget(m_outstandingNotice);

    // ---- ledger + side panel
    auto *body = new QHBoxLayout;
    body->setSpacing(8);
    auto *ledger = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto *lv = new QVBoxLayout(ledger);
    lv->setContentsMargins(12, 12, 12, 12);
    lv->setSpacing(8);
    auto *lh = new QVBoxLayout;
    lh->setSpacing(3);
    m_ledgerTitle = W::label("", "h1");

    m_ledgerSub->setWordWrap(true);

    m_table = makeTable({"-"});
    m_table->setProperty("managementTable", true);
