    m_footNote->setStyleSheet("color:#c9d6f5; background:transparent;");
    fh->addWidget(m_footLabel);
    fh->addWidget(m_footValue);
    fh->addWidget(m_footNote, 1);
    lv->addWidget(foot);
    body->addWidget(ledger, 7);

    m_side = W::card(this, "subCard");
    auto *sv = new QVBoxLayout(m_side);
    sv->setContentsMargins(12, 12, 12, 12);
    sv->setSpacing(8);
    sv->addWidget(W::label("Top sold staples", "h1"));
    auto *sideDescription = W::label("Units sold in the selected period", "muted");
    sideDescription->setWordWrap(true);
    sv->addWidget(sideDescription);
    auto *listHost = new QWidget;
    m_sideList = new QVBoxLayout(listHost);
    m_sideList->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
    m_sideList->setSpacing(8);
    sv->addWidget(listHost);
    sv->addStretch();
    body->addWidget(m_side, 3);
    root->addLayout(body, 1);

    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, &ReportsPage::refresh);
    m_periodButtons[All]->setChecked(true);
    setTab(Incomplete);
}

void ReportsPage::refresh() { render(); }

void ReportsPage::setTab(int tab)
{
    m_tab = tab;
    for (int i = 0; i < m_tabButtons.size(); ++i)
        m_tabButtons[i]->setChecked(i == tab);
    m_periodBar->setVisible(tab != Incomplete); // outstanding orders are shown whatever their date
    m_outstandingNotice->setVisible(tab == Incomplete);
    render();
}

void ReportsPage::setPeriod(int period)
{
    m_period = period;
    const QDate today = QDate::currentDate();
    switch (period)
    {
    case Daily: m_from = today; m_to = today; break;
    case Weekly: m_from = today.addDays(-(today.dayOfWeek() - 1)); m_to = today; break;
    case Monthly: m_from = QDate(today.year(), today.month(), 1); m_to = today; break;
    case Yearly: m_from = QDate(today.year(), 1, 1); m_to = today; break;
    case Custom: break; // m_from / m_to were set by the Filter button
    default: m_from = QDate(); m_to = QDate(); break;
    }
    m_fromEdit->setDate(m_from.isValid() ? m_from : today);
    m_toEdit->setDate(m_to.isValid() ? m_to : today);
    for (int i = 0; i < m_periodButtons.size(); ++i)
        m_periodButtons[i]->setChecked(i == period);
    render();
}

QString ReportsPage::periodText() const
{
    if (m_period == All || !m_from.isValid())
        return "All dates";
    if (m_from == m_to)
        return m_from.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy");
    return m_from.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy") + " to " + m_to.toString("dd-MMM-yyyy");
}

// ============================================================================ data
QVector<ReportsPage::TopItem> ReportsPage::topSold(int limit, bool cashOnly) const
{
    auto &app = AppState::instance();
    // The checkout view model uses integer quantities. Read the saved bill lines
    // through the existing repository so a weight sale keeps its decimal quantity.
    const auto bills = savedBills(app);
    QHash<QString, const barcodehub::billing::Bill *> billsById;
    for (const auto &bill : bills)
        billsById.insert(QString::fromStdString(bill.id), &bill);

    QVector<TopItem> items;
    QHash<QString, qsizetype> byAcronym;
    auto addLine = [&](const QString &acronym, const QString &name, double qty, double value)
    {
        const auto key = acronym.toUpper();
        if (!byAcronym.contains(key))
        {
            byAcronym.insert(key, items.size());
            items.push_back({name, acronym});
        }
        auto &item = items[byAcronym.value(key)];
        item.units += qty;
        item.lineValue += value;
    };
    for (const auto &o : app.orders())
    {
        if (o.status != "Paid" || (cashOnly && !isCash(o)))
            continue;
        const QDate d = o.paymentTime().date();
        if (m_from.isValid() && (d < m_from || d > m_to))
            continue;
        if (const auto *bill = billsById.value(o.billId); bill && !bill->lines.empty())
        {
            for (const auto &line : bill->lines)
            {
                const auto acronym = QString::fromStdString(line.productAcronym);
                addLine(acronym, acronym, line.quantity, (line.unitPrice - line.discountPerUnit) * line.quantity);
            }
        }
        else
            for (const auto &line : o.lines)
                addLine(line.acronym, line.name, line.qty, (line.price - line.discount) * line.qty);
    }
    for (auto &t : items)
        if (const auto p = app.findByCode(t.acronym))
        {
            t.stock = p->stock;
            t.name = p->name;
        }
    std::stable_sort(items.begin(), items.end(), [](const TopItem &a, const TopItem &b) { return a.units > b.units; });
    if (limit > 0 && items.size() > limit)
        items.resize(limit);
    return items;
}

ReportsPage::Report ReportsPage::build(int tab, const QHash<QString, double> &paidUnits) const
{
    auto &app = AppState::instance();
    const auto &all = app.orders();
    const int low = app.settings().lowStockThreshold;
    auto inRange = [&](const OrderRecord &o)
    {
        if (!m_from.isValid())
            return true;
        const QDate d = reportTime(o).date();
        return d >= m_from && d <= m_to;
    };
    const QString dt = "dd MMM yyyy HH:mm";
    Report r;
    double sum = 0, disc = 0;
    int n = 0;

    switch (tab)
    {
    case Incomplete:
    {
        r.title = "Incomplete Orders";
        r.subtitle = "Open and held orders · All dates";
        r.headers = {"ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS", "STATUS", "AMOUNT"};
        r.right = {false, false, false, false, false, true};
        r.accent = {0, 5};
        int hold = 0;
        for (const auto &o : all)
            if (o.status == "In Progress" || o.status == "On Hold")
            {
                r.rows.push_back({"#" + o.id, o.time.toString(dt), o.customer, o.summary(), o.status, W::money(o.total)});
                sum += o.total;
                ++n;
                if (o.status == "On Hold")
                    ++hold;
            }
        r.tiles = {{"INCOMPLETE ORDERS", "orders", QString::number(n), "not paid, not archived"},
                   {"OUTSTANDING AMOUNT", "bills", W::money(sum), "total of all incomplete bills"},
                   {"ON HOLD", "pause", QString::number(hold), QString("%1 in progress").arg(n - hold)}};
        r.footLabel = "Outstanding total:";
        r.footValue = W::money(sum);
        r.footNote = QString("%1 order%2").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s");
        break;
    }
    case Completed:
    {
        r.title = "Completed Orders";
        r.subtitle = "Paid and archived orders  •  " + periodText();
        r.headers = {"ORDER ID", "DATE & TIME", "CUSTOMER", "PAYMENT", "ITEMS", "STATUS", "AMOUNT"};
        r.right = {false, false, false, false, false, false, true};
        r.accent = {0, 6};
        int archived = 0;
        for (const auto &o : all)
        {
            if ((o.status != "Paid" && o.status != "Archived") || !inRange(o))
                continue;
            const double units = paidUnits.value(o.billId, o.itemCount());
            r.rows.push_back({"#" + o.id, reportTime(o).toString(dt), o.customer, cap(o.payment), quantity(units), o.status, W::money(o.total)});
            if (o.status == "Paid")
            {
                sum += o.total;
                ++n;
            }
            else
                ++archived;
        }
        r.tiles = {{"COMPLETED (PAID)", "check", QString::number(n), periodText()},
                   {"TOTAL AMOUNT", "bills", W::money(sum), "paid orders only"},
                   {"ARCHIVED", "history", QString::number(archived), "Excluded from paid total"}};
        r.footLabel = "Paid total:";
        r.footValue = W::money(sum);
        r.footNote = QString("%1 paid  •  %2 archived (not counted)").arg(n).arg(archived);
        break;
    }
    case CashSales:
    case AllSales:
    {
        const bool cash = (tab == CashSales);
        r.title = cash ? "Cash Sales Ledger" : "All Sales Ledger";
        r.subtitle = (cash ? "Paid cash orders · " : "All paid payment methods · ") + periodText();
        r.showTopSold = true;
        if (cash)
        {
            r.headers = {"DATE & TIME", "ORDER ID", "CUSTOMER", "ITEMS", "AMOUNT", "DISCOUNT"};
            r.right = {false, false, false, false, true, true};
            r.accent = {1, 4};
        }
        else
        {
            r.headers = {"DATE & TIME", "ORDER ID", "CUSTOMER", "PAYMENT", "ITEMS", "AMOUNT", "DISCOUNT"};
            r.right = {false, false, false, false, false, true, true};
            r.accent = {1, 5};
        }
        int cashN = 0, cardN = 0, walletN = 0;
        for (const auto &o : all)
        {
            if (o.status != "Paid" || !inRange(o))
                continue;
            if (cash && !isCash(o))
                continue;
            if (isCash(o)) ++cashN;
            else if (o.payment.compare("card", Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0) ++cardN;
            else if (o.payment.compare("wallet", Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0) ++walletN;
            const QString units = quantity(paidUnits.value(o.billId, o.itemCount()));
            if (cash)
                r.rows.push_back({o.paymentTime().toString(dt), "#" + o.id, o.customer, units,
                                  W::money(o.total), W::money(-o.discount)});
            else
                r.rows.push_back({o.paymentTime().toString(dt), "#" + o.id, o.customer, cap(o.payment), units,
                                  W::money(o.total), W::money(-o.discount)});
            sum += o.total;
            disc += o.discount;
            ++n;
        }
        r.tiles = {{cash ? "TOTAL CASH SALES" : "TOTAL SALES", "cash", W::money(sum), QString("%1 order%2").arg(n).arg(n == 1 ? "" : "s")},
                   cash ? Tile{"AVERAGE CASH BILL", "bills", W::money(n ? sum / n : 0.0), "per cash order"}
                        : Tile{"PAYMENT MIX", "card", QString("%1 / %2 / %3").arg(cashN).arg(cardN).arg(walletN), "cash / card / wallet"},
                   {"DISCOUNTS GRANTED", "percent", W::money(disc), "Item, seasonal, and special discounts"}};
        r.footLabel = cash ? "Cash sales total:" : "Sales total:";
        r.footValue = W::money(sum);
        r.footNote = QString("Discounts given: %1  •  %2 paid").arg(W::money(disc)).arg(n);
        break;
    }
    case TopSold:
    {
        r.title = "Top 10 Most Sold Items";
        r.subtitle = "Descending by units sold  •  " + periodText();
        r.headers = {"RANK", "PRODUCT", "ACRONYM", "UNITS SOLD", "LINE VALUE", "STOCK LEFT", "STATUS"};
        r.right = {false, false, false, true, true, true, false};
        r.accent = {2, 3};
        const auto items = topSold(10);
        double units = 0;
        int lowCount = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < items.size(); ++i)
        {
            const auto &t = items[i];
            const QString status = t.stock < 0 ? "Not in catalogue" : t.stock == 0 ? "Out of stock" : t.stock <= low ? "Low stock" : "OK";
            if (t.stock >= 0 && t.stock <= low)
                ++lowCount;
            r.rows.push_back({QString::number(i + 1), t.name, t.acronym, quantity(t.units), W::money(t.lineValue),
                              t.stock < 0 ? QString("—") : QString::number(t.stock), status});
            units += t.units;
            sum += t.lineValue;
        }
        r.tiles = {{"BEST SELLER", "star", items.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : items[0].acronym,
                    items.isEmpty() ? QString("No sales in this period") : QString("%1 units sold").arg(quantity(items[0].units))},
                   {"UNITS SOLD (TOP 10)", "cart", quantity(units), "Across ranked products"},
                   {"NEED RESTOCK", "warn", QString::number(lowCount), "top sellers with low or no stock"}};
        r.footLabel = "Top ten line value:";
        r.footValue = W::money(sum);
        r.footNote = "After item discounts, before tax and bill-level discounts";
        break;
    }
    case TopRemaining:
    {
        r.title = "Top 10 Items by Remaining Stock";
        r.subtitle = "Ranked by current stock · Sales: " + periodText();
        r.headers = {"RANK", "PRODUCT", "ACRONYM", "STOCK LEFT", "SOLD (PERIOD)", "ADVICE"};
        r.right = {false, false, false, true, true, false};
        r.accent = {2, 3};
        QVector<ProductInfo> products = app.products();
        std::stable_sort(products.begin(), products.end(), [](const ProductInfo &a, const ProductInfo &b) { return a.stock > b.stock; });
        if (products.size() > 10)
            products.resize(10);
        const auto sold = topSold(0);
        int stockSum = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < products.size(); ++i)
        {
            const auto &p = products[i];
            double s = 0;
            for (const auto &t : sold)
                if (t.acronym.compare(p.sku, Qt::CaseInsensitive) == 0)
                    s = t.units;
            const QString advice = p.stock <= 0 ? "Out of stock"
                                 : p.stock <= low ? "Review for restock" : "Stock sufficient";
            r.rows.push_back({QString::number(i + 1), p.name, p.sku, QString::number(p.stock), quantity(s), advice});
            stockSum += p.stock;
        }
        r.tiles = {{"MOST STOCKED", "inventory", products.isEmpty() ? QString("-") : products[0].sku,
                    products.isEmpty() ? QString() : QString("%1 units on hand").arg(products[0].stock)},
                   {"UNITS ON HAND (TOP 10)", "products", QString::number(stockSum), "held by the ten items"},
                   {"CATALOGUE", "barcode", QString::number(app.products().size()), "products in Product.txt"}};
        r.footLabel = "Units on hand (top ten):";
        r.footValue = QString::number(stockSum);
        r.footNote = "Stock quantities are current snapshots";
        break;
    }
    }
    return r;
}

// ============================================================================ view
void ReportsPage::render()
{
    QHash<QString, double> paidUnits;
    if (m_tab == Completed || m_tab == CashSales || m_tab == AllSales)
        for (const auto &bill : savedBills(AppState::instance()))
        {
            if (bill.lines.empty())
                continue;
            double units = 0;
            for (const auto &line : bill.lines)
                units += line.quantity;
            paidUnits.insert(QString::fromStdString(bill.id), units);
        }
    m_report = build(m_tab, paidUnits);
    const Report &r = m_report;

    clearLayout(m_tiles);
    for (const auto &t : r.tiles)
    {
        QLabel *value = nullptr, *sub = nullptr;
        auto *tile = W::card(this, "reportMetric");
        auto *layout = new QHBoxLayout(tile);
        layout->setContentsMargins(14, 8, 10, 8);
        layout->setSpacing(10);
        layout->addWidget(W::iconLabel(t.icon, Theme::readable(Theme::primary), 24), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        auto *labels = new QVBoxLayout;
        labels->setSpacing(2);
        labels->addWidget(W::label(t.caption, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "bigValue");
        sub = W::label("", "muted");
        sub->setWordWrap(true);
        labels->addWidget(value);
        labels->addWidget(sub);
        layout->addLayout(labels, 1);
        value->setText(t.value);
        sub->setText(t.sub);
        m_tiles->addWidget(tile, 1);
    }

    m_ledgerTitle->setText(r.title);
    m_ledgerSub->setText(r.subtitle);
    m_table->setRowCount(0);
    m_table->setColumnCount(r.headers.size());
    m_table->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(r.headers);
    auto *header = m_table->horizontalHeader();
    header->setMinimumSectionSize(52);
    header->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Interactive);
    header->setStretchLastSection(false);
    const bool narrow = r.showTopSold;
    for (int column = 0; column < r.headers.size(); ++column)
    {
        const auto &name = r.headers[column];
        const int width = name == "PRODUCT" || name == "CUSTOMER" ? (narrow ? 142 : 168)
                         : name == "DATE & TIME" ? 133
                         : name == "RANK" ? 56
                         : name == "ITEMS" && narrow ? 60
                         : name == "ITEMS" && m_tab == Incomplete ? 174
                         : name == "ADVICE" ? 150 : 104;
        m_table->setColumnWidth(column, width);
        m_table->horizontalHeaderItem(column)->setTextAlignment(
            (r.right.value(column) ? Qt::AlignRight : Qt::AlignLeft) | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        if (name == "PRODUCT" || (name == "CUSTOMER" && !narrow))
            header->setSectionResizeMode(column, QHeaderView::Stretch);
        if (name == "AMOUNT" || name == "DISCOUNT" || name == "LINE VALUE")
            header->setSectionResizeMode(column, QHeaderView::ResizeToContents);
    }
    header->setStretchLastSection(narrow);
    m_table->setRowCount(r.rows.size());
    for (int row = 0; row < r.rows.size(); ++row)
    {
        m_table->setRowHeight(row, 40);
        for (int c = 0; c < r.headers.size() && c < r.rows[row].size(); ++c)
        {
            if (r.headers[c] == "STATUS")
            {
                const auto &status = r.rows[row][c];
                const auto kind = status == "Low stock" ? QString("amber")
                                 : status == "Out of stock" ? QString("red")
                                 : status == "OK" ? QString("green") : W::statusBadgeKind(status);
                m_table->setCellWidget(row, c, wrapW(W::badge(status, kind)));
                continue;
            }
            const bool accent = r.accent.contains(c);
            const Qt::Alignment a = (r.right.value(c) ? Qt::AlignRight : Qt::AlignLeft) | Qt::AlignVCenter;
            auto *cell = item(r.rows[row][c], a, accent ? "#004ac6" : "", accent);
            cell->setToolTip(r.rows[row][c]);
            m_table->setItem(row, c, cell);
        }
    }
    m_footLabel->setText(r.footLabel);
    m_footValue->setText(r.footValue);
    m_footNote->setText(r.footNote);
    m_empty->setVisible(r.rows.isEmpty());

    // top sold staples (side panel)
    m_side->setVisible(r.showTopSold);
    if (r.showTopSold)
    {
        clearLayout(m_sideList);
        const auto items = topSold(5, m_tab == CashSales);
        const double maxUnits = items.isEmpty() ? 1.0 : qMax(1.0, items.first().units);
        if (items.isEmpty())
            m_sideList->addWidget(W::label("No sales in this period.", "muted"));
        for (int i = 0; i < items.size(); ++i)
        {
            const auto &t = items[i];
            auto *rowW = new QWidget;
            auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(rowW);
            v->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
            v->setSpacing(3);
            auto *name = W::label(t.name);
            name->setStyleSheet("font-weight:700;");
            name->setWordWrap(true);
            v->addWidget(name);
            auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
            h->setSpacing(8);
            h->addWidget(W::label(QString("%1 sold").arg(quantity(t.units)), "muted"));
            v->addLayout(h);
            auto *bar = new QProgressBar;
            bar->setRange(0, 1000);
            bar->setValue(qRound(t.units * 1000 / maxUnits));
            bar->setTextVisible(false);
            bar->setFixedHeight(8);
            bar->setObjectName("reportRankingBar");
            v->addWidget(bar);
            m_sideList->addWidget(rowW);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================================ export
void ReportsPage::exportCsv()
{
    const Report r = m_report;
    auto field = [](QString value) { return csvField(value.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace('\r', '\n')); };
    QStringList rows;
    QStringList head;
    for (const auto &h : r.headers)
        head << csvField(h);
    rows << head.join(',');
    for (const auto &row : r.rows)
    {
        QStringList cells;
        for (const auto &c : row)
            cells << field(c);
        rows << cells.join(',');
    }
    rows << QString();
    rows << csvField(r.footLabel) + "," + csvField(r.footValue);
    rows << csvField("Report") + "," + csvField(r.title);
    rows << csvField("Scope") + "," + csvField(r.subtitle);
    rows << csvField("Notes") + "," + csvField(r.footNote);
    QString name = r.title.toLower();
    name.replace(' ', '-');
    if (writeCsv(this, "report-" + name + ".csv", rows))
        emit toast("Report exported as CSV");
}

void ReportsPage::exportPdf()
{
    const Report r = m_report;
    QString name = r.title.toLower();
    name.replace(' ', '-');
    const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(this, "Export / Print report as PDF", "report-" + name + ".pdf", "PDF (*.pdf)");
    if (path.isEmpty())
        return;
    QString html = QString("<h2 style='margin-bottom:2px'>%1</h2><p style='color:#555'>%2 &nbsp;|&nbsp; exported %3</p>"
                           "<table width='100%' border='1' cellspacing='0' cellpadding='5' style='border-collapse:collapse'><thead><tr>")
                       .arg(r.title.toHtmlEscaped(), r.subtitle.toHtmlEscaped(), QDateTime::currentDateTime().toString("dd-MMM-yyyy HH:mm"));
    for (const auto &h : r.headers)
        html += "<th bgcolor='#dce9ff' align='left'>" + h.toHtmlEscaped() + "</th>";
    html += "</tr></thead>";
    for (const auto &row : r.rows)
    {
        html += "<tr>";
        for (int c = 0; c < row.size(); ++c)
            html += QString("<td align='%1'>%2</td>").arg(r.right.value(c) ? "right" : "left", row[c].toHtmlEscaped());
        html += "</tr>";
    }
    if (r.rows.isEmpty())
        html += QString("<tr><td colspan='%1'>No records in this report.</td></tr>").arg(r.headers.size());
    html += QString("</table><p><b>%1 %2</b> &nbsp; %3</p>")
                .arg(r.footLabel.toHtmlEscaped(), r.footValue.toHtmlEscaped(), r.footNote.toHtmlEscaped());
    QSaveFile file(path);
    if (!file.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly))
    {
        emit toast("Could not open the report PDF for writing");
        return;
    }
    {
        QPdfWriter pdf(&file);
        pdf.setPageSize(QPageSize(QPageSize::A4));
        pdf.setPageOrientation(QPageLayout::Landscape);
        pdf.setPageMargins(QMarginsF(12, 12, 12, 12), QPageLayout::Millimeter);
        pdf.setCreator("Syntro-Check");
        pdf.setTitle(r.title);
        QTextDocument doc;
        doc.setHtml(html);
        doc.print(&pdf);
    }
    emit toast(file.size() > 0 && file.commit() ? "Report saved as PDF" : "Could not save the report PDF");
}

#include "ScanAudio.h"
#include "AppState.h"

#include <QCoreApplication>
#include <QFile>
#include <QUrl>
#include <QtGlobal>
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstring>

#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
#include <QAudioDevice>
#include <QMediaDevices>
#include <QSoundEffect>
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
#include <QDir>
#include <QTemporaryFile>
#ifndef NOMINMAX
#define NOMINMAX
#endif
#define WIN32_LEAN_AND_MEAN
#include <windows.h>
#include <mmsystem.h>
#endif

static void initScanAudioResources()
{
    Q_INIT_RESOURCE(scan_audio);
}

namespace
{
    constexpr int kSampleRate = 44100;
    constexpr int kDurationMs = 110;
    constexpr int kFadeInMs = 8;
    constexpr int kFadeOutMs = 24;
    constexpr double kBaseFreqHz = 1864.66;
    constexpr double kHarmonicFreqHz = 3729.32;
    constexpr double kPeakAmplitude = 0.40;
    constexpr double kPi = 3.14159265358979323846;

    void appendLe32(QByteArray &out, std::uint32_t value)
    {
        const char bytes[4] = {
            static_cast<char>(value & 0xFFu),
            static_cast<char>((value >> 8) & 0xFFu),
            static_cast<char>((value >> 16) & 0xFFu),
            static_cast<char>((value >> 24) & 0xFFu)
        };
        out.append(bytes, 4);
    }

    void appendLe16(QByteArray &out, std::uint16_t value)
    {
        const char bytes[2] = {
            static_cast<char>(value & 0xFFu),
            static_cast<char>((value >> 8) & 0xFFu)
        };
        out.append(bytes, 2);
    }
}

ScanAudio &ScanAudio::instance()
{
    static ScanAudio s;
    return s;
}

ScanAudio::ScanAudio()
{
    m_lastPlayTimer.invalidate();
}

ScanAudio::~ScanAudio()
{
    shutdown();
}

void ScanAudio::warmUp()
{
    ensureInitialized();
}

bool ScanAudio::playSuccess()
{
    return playIfEnabled(AppState::instance().settings().beepOnScan);
}

bool ScanAudio::playIfEnabled(bool enabled)
{
    const qint64 elapsed = m_lastPlayTimer.isValid() ? m_lastPlayTimer.elapsed() : -1;
    if (!shouldPlay(enabled, elapsed, m_minIntervalMs))
        return false;

    m_lastPlayTimer.restart();
    emit feedbackTriggered();
    return triggerBackendPlayback();
}

bool ScanAudio::shouldPlay(bool beepEnabled, qint64 elapsedSinceLastMs, qint64 minIntervalMs)
{
    if (!beepEnabled)
        return false;
    if (elapsedSinceLastMs >= 0 && minIntervalMs > 0 && elapsedSinceLastMs < minIntervalMs)
        return false;
    return true;
}

QByteArray ScanAudio::buildSuccessToneWav()
{
    const int numSamples = (kSampleRate * kDurationMs) / 1000;
    const int fadeInSamples = (kSampleRate * kFadeInMs) / 1000;
    const int fadeOutSamples = (kSampleRate * kFadeOutMs) / 1000;
    const std::uint32_t dataSize = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(numSamples * sizeof(std::int16_t));

    QByteArray wav;
    wav.reserve(44 + static_cast<int>(dataSize));
    wav.append("RIFF", 4);
    appendLe32(wav, 36u + dataSize);
    wav.append("WAVE", 4);
    wav.append("fmt ", 4);
    appendLe32(wav, 16u);
    appendLe16(wav, 1u); // PCM
    appendLe16(wav, 1u); // mono
    appendLe32(wav, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(kSampleRate));
    appendLe32(wav, static_cast<std::uint32_t>(kSampleRate * 2));
    appendLe16(wav, 2u);  // block align
    appendLe16(wav, 16u); // bits per sample
    wav.append("data", 4);
    appendLe32(wav, dataSize);

    for (int i = 0; i < numSamples; ++i)
    {
        const double t = static_cast<double>(i) / kSampleRate;
        double env = 1.0;
        if (i < fadeInSamples)
        {
            env = 0.5 * (1.0 - std::cos(kPi * static_cast<double>(i) / fadeInSamples));
        }
        else if (i >= numSamples - fadeOutSamples)
        {
            const int k = i - (numSamples - fadeOutSamples);
            env = 0.5 * (1.0 + std::cos(kPi * static_cast<double>(k + 1) / fadeOutSamples));
        }

        const double wave = 0.92 * std::sin(2.0 * kPi * kBaseFreqHz * t)
                          + 0.08 * std::sin(2.0 * kPi * kHarmonicFreqHz * t);
        const double scaled = std::clamp(wave * env * kPeakAmplitude * 32767.0, -32767.0, 32767.0);
        const auto sample = static_cast<std::int16_t>(std::lround(scaled));
        appendLe16(wav, static_cast<std::uint16_t>(sample));
    }

    return wav;
}

bool ScanAudio::isAvailable() const
{
    const_cast<ScanAudio *>(this)->ensureInitialized();
    const_cast<ScanAudio *>(this)->refreshDeviceAvailability();
    return m_available;
}

QString ScanAudio::backendName() const
{
#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
    return QStringLiteral("QtMultimedia (QSoundEffect)");
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
    return QStringLiteral("Windows Multimedia (PlaySoundW)");
#else
    return QStringLiteral("Unavailable");
#endif
}

void ScanAudio::setMinIntervalMs(qint64 ms)
{
    m_minIntervalMs = std::max<qint64>(0, ms);
}

void ScanAudio::resetDebounce()
{
    m_lastPlayTimer.invalidate();
}

void ScanAudio::ensureInitialized()
{
    if (m_initialized || m_shuttingDown)
        return;
    QCoreApplication *app = QCoreApplication::instance();
    if (!app || QCoreApplication::closingDown())
        return;

    m_initialized = true;
    initScanAudioResources();

    // The POS leaves and re-enters the event loop on logout. Keep audio reusable for the next sign-in.
    connect(app, &QObject::destroyed, this, &ScanAudio::shutdown, Qt::DirectConnection);

    QFile wavFile(QStringLiteral(":/audio/scan_beep.wav"));
    if (wavFile.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly))
        m_wavBuffer = wavFile.readAll();
    if (m_wavBuffer.isEmpty())
        m_wavBuffer = buildSuccessToneWav();

#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
    m_mediaDevices = std::make_unique<QMediaDevices>();
    connect(m_mediaDevices.get(), &QMediaDevices::audioOutputsChanged,
            this, &ScanAudio::refreshDeviceAvailability);

    m_effect = std::make_unique<QSoundEffect>();
    m_effect->setLoopCount(1);
    m_effect->setVolume(0.85f);
    connect(m_effect.get(), &QSoundEffect::statusChanged, this, [this] {
        if (m_effect && m_effect->status() == QSoundEffect::Error)
        {
            m_available = false;
            if (!m_warnedUnavailable)
            {
                m_warnedUnavailable = true;
                qWarning("ScanAudio: QSoundEffect failed to load bundled scan tone.");
            }
        }
        else
        {
            refreshDeviceAvailability();
        }
    });
    m_effect->setSource(QUrl(QStringLiteral("qrc:/audio/scan_beep.wav")));
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
    // WinMM does not support SND_MEMORY | SND_ASYNC. Persist the embedded WAV
    // to a temporary file and play by filename, asynchronously.
    m_temporaryWav = std::make_unique<QTemporaryFile>(
        QDir::tempPath() + QStringLiteral("/syntro_scan_XXXXXX.wav"));
    if (m_temporaryWav->open() &&
        m_temporaryWav->write(m_wavBuffer) == m_wavBuffer.size() &&
        m_temporaryWav->flush())
    {
        m_temporaryWavPath = m_temporaryWav->fileName();
        m_temporaryWav->close(); // Let the Windows sound API open it by filename.
    }
    else
    {
        qWarning("ScanAudio: could not prepare the embedded WAV for Windows playback.");
        m_temporaryWav.reset();
    }
#endif

    refreshDeviceAvailability();
}

void ScanAudio::shutdown()
{
    m_shuttingDown = true;
    m_available = false;
    m_initialized = false;
#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
    m_effect.reset();
    m_mediaDevices.reset();
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
    PlaySoundW(nullptr, nullptr, 0);
    m_temporaryWav.reset(); // Deletes the temporary WAV on shutdown.
    m_temporaryWavPath.clear();
#endif
    m_wavBuffer.clear();
}

void ScanAudio::refreshDeviceAvailability()
{
    if (!m_initialized || m_shuttingDown)
    {
        m_available = false;
        return;
    }

#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
    if (!m_effect)
    {
        m_available = false;
        return;
    }
    const auto outputs = QMediaDevices::audioOutputs();
    if (outputs.isEmpty())
    {
        m_available = false;
        return;
    }
    const QAudioDevice defaultOut = QMediaDevices::defaultAudioOutput();
    if (!defaultOut.isNull() && m_effect->audioDevice() != defaultOut)
        m_effect->setAudioDevice(defaultOut);
    m_available = (m_effect->status() != QSoundEffect::Error);
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
    m_available = (waveOutGetNumDevs() > 0) && !m_temporaryWavPath.isEmpty();
#else
    m_available = false;
#endif
}

bool ScanAudio::triggerBackendPlayback()
{
    ensureInitialized();
    refreshDeviceAvailability();
    if (!m_available)
    {
        if (!m_warnedUnavailable)
        {
            m_warnedUnavailable = true;
            qWarning("ScanAudio: No audio output device available; scan feedback will remain silent.");
        }
        return false;
    }

#ifdef WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT
    if (!m_effect)
        return false;
    if (m_effect->isPlaying())
        m_effect->stop();
    m_effect->play();
    return true;
#elif defined(WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
    if (m_temporaryWavPath.isEmpty())
        return false;
    const BOOL played = PlaySoundW(reinterpret_cast<LPCWSTR>(m_temporaryWavPath.utf16()),
                                   nullptr, SND_FILENAME | SND_ASYNC | SND_NODEFAULT);
    if (!played)
        qWarning("ScanAudio: Windows rejected the scan tone playback request.");
    return played != FALSE;
#else
    return false;
#endif
}

#include "ScannerPage.h"

using namespace Ui; // makeTable, item, wrapW, pageShell, writeCsv (see UiKit.h)

namespace
{
    class ElidedLineLabel final : public QLabel
    {
    public:
        explicit ElidedLineLabel(const QString &text, const char *objectName = nullptr, const QString &style = QString())
        {
            setTextFormat(Qt::PlainText);
            setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy::Ignored, QSizePolicy::Fixed);
            if (objectName)
                setObjectName(objectName);
            if (!style.isEmpty())
                setStyleSheet(style);
            m_full = text;
            setAccessibleName(text);
            updateElided();
        }

    protected:
        void resizeEvent(QResizeEvent *event) override
        {
            QLabel::resizeEvent(event);
            updateElided();
        }

    private:
        void updateElided()
        {
            const QString elided = fontMetrics().elidedText(m_full, Qt::ElideRight, contentsRect().width());
            setText(elided);
            setToolTip(elided == m_full ? QString() : m_full);
        }

        QString m_full;
    };

    double unitGrossWithTax(const ProductInfo &p)
    {
        return p.price - p.discount + p.tax;
    }

    QWidget *makeChecklistProductCell(const ScannerPage::ScanEntry &entry)
    {
        auto *w = new QWidget;
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout(w);
        v->setContentsMargins(2, 4, 2, 4);
        v->setSpacing(1);

        auto *top = new ElidedLineLabel(entry.product.name, nullptr, QStringLiteral("font-weight:700; font-size:13px;"));
        const QString subtitle = entry.product.stock <= 0
            ? QString("%1  •  %2  •  Out of stock").arg(entry.product.ean, W::money(entry.product.price))
            : QString("%1  •  %2  •  Line %3")
                  .arg(entry.product.ean,
                       W::money(entry.product.price),
                       W::money(unitGrossWithTax(entry.product) * entry.qty));
        auto *bottom = new ElidedLineLabel(
            subtitle,
            entry.product.stock <= 0 ? "errorText" : "muted",
            QStringLiteral("font-size:11.5px;"));
        v->addWidget(top);
        v->addWidget(bottom);
        return w;
    }
}

    m_checklist->setObjectName("scannerChecklist");

    m_checklist->setProperty("managementTable", true);
    m_checklist->setAlternatingRowColors(true);

    m_checklist->setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView::SelectRows);
    m_checklist->setWordWrap(false);
    auto *hh = m_checklist->horizontalHeader();
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Fixed);
    hh->setSectionResizeMode(1, QHeaderView::Stretch);
    m_checklist->setColumnWidth(0, 42);

    m_last->setWordWrap(true);
    details->addWidget(m_last);
    m_productDetails = W::label("", "muted");
    m_productDetails->setWordWrap(true);
    details->addWidget(m_productDetails);
    sv->addWidget(product);

    auto *summaryBox = new QFrame;
    summaryBox->setObjectName("field");
    auto *sbLayout = new QHBoxLayout(summaryBox);
    sbLayout->setContentsMargins(10, 7, 10, 7);
    sbLayout->setSpacing(8);
    m_selectedSummary = W::label("0 selected", "muted");
    m_selectedTotal = W::label("PKR 0.00", "h2");
    sbLayout->addWidget(m_selectedSummary, 1);
    sbLayout->addWidget(m_selectedTotal, 0, Qt::AlignRight);
    m_selectionBar = summaryBox;
    sv->addWidget(summaryBox);

    m_describe = W::button("Show description", "soft", "search");
    m_describe->setEnabled(false);
    sv->addWidget(m_describe);

    m_send = W::button("Add checked to checkout", "primary", "cart");
    m_send->setObjectName("scannerAddChecked");
    m_send->setEnabled(false);
    sv->addWidget(m_send);

    h->addWidget(side, 5);
    root->addLayout(h, 1);

    connect(m_toggle, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            const bool starting = !m_feed->running();
            m_feed->setRunning(starting);
            if (!starting || m_feed->running())
                updateCameraDisplay();
        });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::scanError, this, [this](const QString &message)
        {
            updateCameraDisplay(true);
            emit toast(message);
        });
    connect(m_feed, &CameraFeedWidget::barcodeDetected, this, [this](const QString &code)
        {
            const auto product = AppState::instance().findByCode(code);
            if (product)
            {
                const bool added = show(*product);
                m_feed->showResult(code + "  -  " + product->name, added);
                if (added)
                    ScanAudio::instance().playSuccess();
            }
            else
            {
                m_feed->showResult(code + "  -  not in Product.txt", false);
                emit toast("Barcode " + code + " not found in Product.txt");
            }
        });

    auto lookup = [this]
        {
            const QString query = m_manual->text().trimmed();
            if (query.isEmpty())
            {
                emit toast("Enter a barcode first");
                return;
            }
            const auto p = AppState::instance().findByCode(query);
            if (!p)
            {
                emit toast("No product found for \"" + query + "\"");
                return;
            }
            if (show(*p))
                ScanAudio::instance().playSuccess();
            m_manual->clear();
        };
    connect(go, &QPushButton::clicked, this, lookup);
    connect(m_manual, &QLineEdit::returnPressed, this, lookup);

    connect(m_selectAll, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ScannerPage::selectAll);
    connect(m_deselectAll, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ScannerPage::deselectAll);
    connect(m_clearAll, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ScannerPage::clearHistory);
    connect(m_send, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &ScannerPage::sendCheckedToOrder);
    connect(m_describe, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
        {
            if (m_lastId > 0)
            {
                Dialogs::productDescription(this, m_lastId);
                emit described(m_lastId);
            }
        });
    connect(m_checklist, &QTableWidget::currentCellChanged, this,
        [this](int currentRow, int, int, int) { setRowSelected(currentRow); });
    connect(&AppState::instance(), &AppState::changed, this, [this]
        {
            for (auto &e : m_entries)
            {
                if (const auto latest = AppState::instance().findByCode(e.product.ean))
                {
                    e.product = *latest;
                    if (e.product.stock <= 0)
                        e.checked = false;
                }
                else
                {
                    e.product.stock = 0;
                    e.checked = false;
                }
            }
            rebuildChecklist();
        });
    connect(&UiStore::instance(), &UiStore::seasonsChanged, this, &ScannerPage::updateSelectionSummary);

    rebuildChecklist();
}

void ScannerPage::updateCameraDisplay(bool unavailable)
{
    const bool running = m_feed->running() && !unavailable;
    const QString state = unavailable ? "Camera unavailable" : running ? "Camera started" : "Camera off";
    W::setBadge(m_cameraStatus, state, unavailable ? "amber" : running ? "blue" : "grey");
    m_cameraMessage->setText(state);
    m_cameraStack->setCurrentIndex(running ? 1 : 0);
    m_toggle->setText(running ? "Stop camera" : "Start camera");
    m_toggle->setIcon(Theme::icon(running ? "stop" : "play", Qt::white, 16));
}

void ScannerPage::recordScan(const ProductInfo &p)
{
    show(p);
}

bool ScannerPage::show(const ProductInfo &p)
{
    m_feed->setCode(p.ean);
    m_lastEan = p.ean;
    m_lastId = p.id;

    int existingIndex = -1;
    for (int i = 0; i < m_entries.size(); ++i)
    {
        if (m_entries[i].product.id == p.id && m_entries[i].product.ean == p.ean)
        {
            existingIndex = i;
            break;
        }
    }

    bool added = p.stock > 0;
    if (existingIndex >= 0)
    {
        ScanEntry updated = m_entries.takeAt(existingIndex);
        updated.product = p;
        updated.lastScanned = QTime::currentTime();
        if (p.stock > 0)
        {
            if (updated.qty < p.stock)
                ++updated.qty;
            else
            {
                added = false;
                emit toast(QString("Maximum stock (%1) reached for %2").arg(p.stock).arg(p.name));
            }
            updated.checked = true;
        }
        else
        {
            updated.checked = false;
        }
        m_entries.prepend(updated);
    }
    else
    {
        ScanEntry entry;
        entry.product = p;
        entry.qty = 1;
        entry.checked = (p.stock > 0);
        entry.lastScanned = QTime::currentTime();
        m_entries.prepend(entry);
    }

    rebuildChecklist();
    m_checklist->selectRow(0);
    setRowSelected(0);
    // Show description only on request; opening a modal on every camera frame is disruptive.
    return added;
}

void ScannerPage::rebuildChecklist()
{
    m_checklist->setRowCount(0);
    m_checklist->setRowCount(m_entries.size());

    for (int r = 0; r < m_entries.size(); ++r)
    {
        const auto &e = m_entries[r];
        m_checklist->setRowHeight(r, 52);

        auto *cb = new QCheckBox;
        cb->setStyleSheet("QCheckBox { spacing: 0px; padding: 0px; }");
        cb->setFixedSize(20, 20);
        cb->setAccessibleName("Select " + e.product.name);
        cb->setChecked(e.checked && e.product.stock > 0);
        cb->setEnabled(e.product.stock > 0);
        if (e.product.stock <= 0)
            cb->setToolTip("Out of stock — cannot be added to checkout");
        connect(cb, &QCheckBox::toggled, this, [this, r](bool on)
            {
                if (r >= 0 && r < m_entries.size())
                {
                    m_entries[r].checked = on && (m_entries[r].product.stock > 0);
                    updateSelectionSummary();
                }
            });
        m_checklist->setCellWidget(r, 0, wrapW(cb, Qt::AlignCenter));
        m_checklist->setCellWidget(r, 1, makeChecklistProductCell(e));

        auto *stp = new QtyStepper(e.qty);
        const auto buttons = stp->findChildren<QPushButton *>();
        for (auto *button : buttons)
        {
            const bool isPlus = (button->text() == "+");
            const QString action = isPlus ? "Increase quantity" : "Decrease quantity";
            button->setObjectName(action);
            button->setAccessibleName(action + " for " + e.product.name);
            if (isPlus)
                button->setEnabled(e.product.stock > 0 && e.qty < e.product.stock);
            else
                button->setEnabled(e.qty > 1);
        }
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::minus, this, [this, r] { changeEntryQty(r, -1); });
        connect(stp, &QtyStepper::plus, this, [this, r] { changeEntryQty(r, +1); });
        m_checklist->setCellWidget(r, 2, stp);

        auto *del = W::button("", "flat", "trash");
        del->setToolTip("Remove from list");
        del->setAccessibleName("Remove " + e.product.name);
        connect(del, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, r] { removeEntry(r); });
        m_checklist->setCellWidget(r, 3, wrapW(del, Qt::AlignCenter));
    }

    if (m_entries.isEmpty())
    {
        m_lastEan.clear();
        m_lastId = 0;
        m_last->setText("No scans yet");
        m_productDetails->clear();
        m_describe->setEnabled(false);
    }
    else
    {
        m_describe->setEnabled(m_lastId > 0);
    }

    updateSelectionSummary();
}

void ScannerPage::updateSelectionSummary()
{
    int totalItems = m_entries.size();
    int totalUnits = 0;
    int checkableItems = 0;
    int checkedItems = 0;
    int checkedUnits = 0;
    QVector<ReceiptLine> checkedLines;

    for (const auto &e : m_entries)
    {
        totalUnits += e.qty;
        if (e.product.stock > 0)
            ++checkableItems;
        if (e.checked && e.product.stock > 0 && e.qty > 0)
        {
            ++checkedItems;
            checkedUnits += e.qty;
            const auto &p = e.product;
            checkedLines.push_back({p.name, p.sku, e.qty, p.price, p.tax, p.discount, p.id});
        }
    }

    if (totalItems == 0)
        W::setBadge(m_countBadge, "0 items", "grey");
    else
        W::setBadge(m_countBadge,
                    QString("%1 product%2 · %3 unit%4")
                        .arg(totalItems)
                        .arg(totalItems == 1 ? "" : "s")
                        .arg(totalUnits)
                        .arg(totalUnits == 1 ? "" : "s"),
                    "blue");

    m_selectAll->setEnabled(checkedItems < checkableItems);
    m_deselectAll->setEnabled(checkedItems > 0);
    m_clearAll->setEnabled(!m_entries.isEmpty());

    m_selectedSummary->setText(checkedItems == 0
        ? QString("No items selected")
        : QString("Checked: %1 product%2 (%3 unit%4)")
              .arg(checkedItems)
              .arg(checkedItems == 1 ? "" : "s")
              .arg(checkedUnits)
              .arg(checkedUnits == 1 ? "" : "s"));
    m_selectedTotal->setText(W::money(Pricing::quote(checkedLines).payable));

    m_send->setEnabled(!m_viewOnly && checkedUnits > 0);
    m_send->setText(checkedUnits > 0
        ? QString("Add checked to checkout (%1)").arg(checkedUnits)
        : QString("Add checked to checkout"));
}

void ScannerPage::changeEntryQty(int row, int delta)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_entries.size())
        return;

    auto &entry = m_entries[row];
    const int nextQty = entry.qty + delta;
    if (nextQty < 1)
        return;
    if (delta > 0 && entry.product.stock > 0 && nextQty > entry.product.stock)
    {
        emit toast(QString("Only %1 in stock for %2").arg(entry.product.stock).arg(entry.product.name));
        return;
    }

    entry.qty = nextQty;
    if (delta > 0 && entry.product.stock > 0)
        entry.checked = true;
    rebuildChecklist();
    m_checklist->selectRow(row);
}

void ScannerPage::removeEntry(int row)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_entries.size())
        return;
    m_entries.removeAt(row);
    rebuildChecklist();
    if (!m_entries.isEmpty())
    {
        const int nextRow = std::min(row, static_cast<int>(m_entries.size()) - 1);
        m_checklist->selectRow(nextRow);
        setRowSelected(nextRow);
    }
}

void ScannerPage::setRowSelected(int row)
{
    if (row < 0 || row >= m_entries.size())
        return;
    const auto &p = m_entries[row].product;
    m_lastEan = p.ean;
    m_lastId = p.id;
    m_last->setText(p.name);
    m_productDetails->setText(QString("%1  ·  %2  ·  %3 in stock")
                                  .arg(p.ean, W::money(p.price)).arg(p.stock));
    m_describe->setEnabled(p.id > 0);
}

void ScannerPage::selectAll()
{
    for (auto &e : m_entries)
        e.checked = (e.product.stock > 0);
    rebuildChecklist();
}

void ScannerPage::deselectAll()
{
    for (auto &e : m_entries)
        e.checked = false;
    rebuildChecklist();
}

void ScannerPage::clearHistory()
{
    m_entries.clear();
    rebuildChecklist();
}

void ScannerPage::sendCheckedToOrder()
{
    if (m_viewOnly)
        return;
    QVector<QPair<QString, int>> batch;

    for (const auto &e : m_entries)
    {
        if (e.checked && e.qty > 0 && e.product.stock > 0)
            batch.push_back({e.product.ean, e.qty});
    }

    if (batch.isEmpty())
    {
        emit toast("Select at least one in-stock product to add to checkout");
        return;
    }

    emit sendBatchToOrder(batch);
}

void ScannerPage::removeTransferredItems(const QVector<QPair<QString, int>> &items)
{
    for (const auto &item : items)
    {
        const auto entry = std::find_if(m_entries.begin(), m_entries.end(), [&item](const ScanEntry &candidate)
            { return candidate.product.ean == item.first; });
        if (entry == m_entries.end())
            continue;
        entry->qty -= item.second;
        if (entry->qty <= 0)
            m_entries.erase(entry);
    }
    rebuildChecklist();
    if (!m_entries.isEmpty())
        setRowSelected(0);
}

void ScannerPage::setViewOnly(bool on)
{
    m_viewOnly = on;
    m_send->setVisible(!on);
    m_selectionBar->setVisible(!on);
    m_selectAll->setVisible(!on);
    m_deselectAll->setVisible(!on);
    m_checklist->setColumnHidden(0, on);
    updateSelectionSummary();
}

#include "SettingsPage.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "DarkModeManager.h"
#include "ScanAudio.h"
#include "UiKit.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDesktopServices>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QFileInfo>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QLineEdit>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTabBar>
#include <QTabWidget>
#include <QUrl>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace
{
    void addSetting(QVBoxLayout *page, const QString &title, const QString &description, QWidget *control)
    {
        auto *card = W::card(nullptr, "field");
        auto *row = new QHBoxLayout(card);
        row->setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12);
        row->setSpacing(16);
        auto *text = new QVBoxLayout;
        text->setSpacing(4);
        auto *name = W::label(title, "h2");
        name->setBuddy(control);
        control->setAccessibleName(title);
        text->addWidget(name);
        if (!description.isEmpty())
        {
            auto *help = W::label(description, "muted");
            help->setWordWrap(true);
            text->addWidget(help);
        }
        row->addLayout(text, 1);
        row->addWidget(control, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        page->addWidget(card);
    }
}

SettingsPage::SettingsPage(QWidget *parent, UserRole role) : QWidget(parent)
{
    QVBoxLayout *root;
    Ui::pageShell(this, root, true);
    root->setSpacing(12);
    root->addWidget(Ui::sectionHeader({}, "Terminal Settings", "Configure the way this terminal handles checkout, scanning, and receipts."));

    auto *tabs = new QTabWidget;
    tabs->setObjectName("terminalSettingsTabs");
    tabs->tabBar()->setDrawBase(false);
    auto addTab = [tabs](const QString &title, const QString &description)
    {
        auto *panel = W::card(nullptr, "subCard");
        tabs->addTab(panel, title);
        auto *layout = new QVBoxLayout(panel);
        layout->setContentsMargins(16, 16, 16, 16);
        layout->setSpacing(12);
        auto *intro = W::label(description, "muted");
        intro->setWordWrap(true);
        layout->addWidget(intro);
        return layout;
    };

    auto *checkout = addTab("Checkout", "Choose the payment option selected when checkout opens.");
    auto *payment = new QComboBox;
    using Payment = barcodehub::order::PaymentMode;
    payment->addItem("Cash", int(Payment::Cash));
    payment->addItem("Card", int(Payment::Card));
    payment->addItem("Wallet", int(Payment::Wallet));
    addSetting(checkout, "Default payment method", "The cashier can select a different method for each sale.", payment);
    checkout->addStretch();

    auto *scanner = addTab("Scanner", "Set camera scan behavior and check confirmation audio.");
    auto *autoAdd = new QCheckBox("Enabled");
    addSetting(scanner, "Add scans directly to the cart", "A camera scan, or a full barcode from a USB scanner / typed with Enter, adds the item to the cart at once.", autoAdd);
    auto *beep = new QCheckBox("Enabled");
    addSetting(scanner, "Beep on successful scan", "Play confirmation audio when a scan is accepted.", beep);
    auto *testBeep = W::button("Play test sound", "soft");
    addSetting(scanner, "Scan confirmation sound", "Preview the sound through the terminal's current audio output.", testBeep);
    connect(testBeep, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this]
            { emit toast(ScanAudio::instance().playIfEnabled(true) ? "Test sound requested" : "Could not play the test sound"); });
    scanner->addStretch();

    auto *inventory = addTab("Inventory", "Set the stock level used for low-stock indicators.");
    auto *low = new QSpinBox;
    low->setRange(0, 1000);
    low->setSuffix(" units");
    addSetting(inventory, "Low-stock limit", "Products at or below this quantity are flagged. This setting leaves quantities unchanged.", low);
    inventory->addStretch();

    auto *appearance = addTab("Appearance", "Choose a theme for the entire terminal.");
    auto *theme = new QComboBox;
    theme->addItem("Light mode", false);
    theme->addItem("Dark mode", true);
    addSetting(appearance, "Color theme", "This uses the same theme preference as the top-bar toggle.", theme);
    appearance->addStretch();

    auto *receipts = addTab("Receipts", "Set the defaults for bills saved as PDF.");
    auto *paper = new QComboBox;
    paper->addItem("A5", false);
    paper->addItem("A4", true);
    addSetting(receipts, "PDF paper size", {}, paper);
    auto *folder = new QLineEdit;
    folder->setAccessibleName("Receipt export folder");
    folder->setPlaceholderText("Documents folder");
    auto *browse = W::button("Browse…", "soft");
    auto *folderRow = new QHBoxLayout;
    folderRow->addWidget(folder, 1);
    folderRow->addWidget(browse);
    auto *folderCard = W::card(nullptr, "field");
    auto *folderLayout = new QVBoxLayout(folderCard);
    folderLayout->setContentsMargins(14, 12, 14, 12);
    folderLayout->setSpacing(8);
    auto *folderLabel = W::label("Default export folder", "h2");
    folderLabel->setBuddy(folder);
    folderLayout->addWidget(folderLabel);
    folderLayout->addLayout(folderRow);
    auto *folderHelp = W::label("Leave blank to use Documents. You can pick another location when saving a bill.", "muted");
    folderHelp->setWordWrap(true);
    folderLayout->addWidget(folderHelp);
    receipts->addWidget(folderCard);
    connect(browse, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, folder]
            {
                const auto chosen = QFileDialog::getExistingDirectory(this, "Choose receipt export folder", folder->text());
                if (!chosen.isEmpty())
                    folder->setText(QDir::toNativeSeparators(chosen));
            });
    receipts->addStretch();

    if (RolePolicy::canOpen(role, Pages::Inventory))
    {
        auto *data = addTab("Data", "Check which catalogue and transaction files this terminal is using.");
        const QString dataPath = AppState::instance().dataDir();
        auto *open = W::button("Open data folder", "soft");
        open->setEnabled(!dataPath.isEmpty());
        addSetting(data, "Active data folder", dataPath.isEmpty() ? QString("No data directory configured.")
                                                               : QDir::toNativeSeparators(dataPath), open);
        connect(open, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [this, dataPath]
                {
                    if (!QFileInfo(dataPath).isDir() || !QDesktopServices::openUrl(QUrl::fromLocalFile(dataPath)))
                        emit toast("Could not open the data folder");
                });
        data->addStretch();
    }

    root->addWidget(tabs);
    auto *buttons = new QHBoxLayout;
    auto *status = W::label({}, "muted");
    auto *discard = W::button("Discard changes", "soft");
    auto *apply = W::button("Apply", "primary", "check");
    apply->setMinimumWidth(112);
    buttons->addWidget(status, 1);
    buttons->addWidget(discard);
    buttons->addWidget(apply);
    root->addLayout(buttons);
    root->addStretch();

    auto &appearanceManager = barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance();
    auto updateButtons = [=, &appearanceManager]
    {
        const auto &saved = AppState::instance().settings();
        const bool changed = low->value() != saved.lowStockThreshold || autoAdd->isChecked() != saved.autoAddScans ||
                             beep->isChecked() != saved.beepOnScan || payment->currentData().toInt() != int(saved.defaultPayment) ||
                             paper->currentData().toBool() != saved.receiptA4 ||
                             QDir::fromNativeSeparators(folder->text().trimmed()) != saved.receiptFolder ||
                             theme->currentData().toBool() != appearanceManager.isDarkMode();
        status->setText(changed ? "Unsaved changes" : "Preferences saved on this terminal");
        apply->setEnabled(changed);
        discard->setEnabled(changed);
    };
    auto reset = [=, &appearanceManager]
    {
        const auto &saved = AppState::instance().settings();
        low->setValue(saved.lowStockThreshold);
        autoAdd->setChecked(saved.autoAddScans);
        beep->setChecked(saved.beepOnScan);
        payment->setCurrentIndex(payment->findData(int(saved.defaultPayment)));
        paper->setCurrentIndex(paper->findData(saved.receiptA4));
        folder->setText(QDir::toNativeSeparators(saved.receiptFolder));
        theme->setCurrentIndex(theme->findData(appearanceManager.isDarkMode()));
        updateButtons();
    };

    for (auto *combo : {payment, paper, theme})
        connect(combo, &QComboBox::currentIndexChanged, this, updateButtons);
    connect(low, &QSpinBox::valueChanged, this, updateButtons);
    connect(autoAdd, &QCheckBox::toggled, this, updateButtons);
    connect(beep, &QCheckBox::toggled, this, updateButtons);
    connect(folder, &QLineEdit::textChanged, this, updateButtons);
    connect(&appearanceManager, &barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::themeChanged, this,
            [theme, updateButtons](bool dark)
            {
                theme->setCurrentIndex(theme->findData(dark));
                updateButtons();
            });
    connect(discard, &QPushButton::clicked, this, reset);
    connect(apply, &QPushButton::clicked, this, [=, this, &appearanceManager]
            {
                Settings settings = AppState::instance().settings();
                settings.lowStockThreshold = low->value();
                settings.autoAddScans = autoAdd->isChecked();
                settings.beepOnScan = beep->isChecked();
                settings.defaultPayment = static_cast<Payment>(payment->currentData().toInt());
                settings.receiptA4 = paper->currentData().toBool();
                settings.receiptFolder = QDir::fromNativeSeparators(folder->text().trimmed());
                if (!settings.receiptFolder.isEmpty())
                {
                    const QFileInfo selected(settings.receiptFolder);
                    if (!selected.isAbsolute() || !selected.isDir())
                    {
                        tabs->setCurrentWidget(receipts->parentWidget());
                        folder->setFocus();
                        emit toast("Choose an existing receipt export folder");
                        return;
                    }
                    settings.receiptFolder = QDir::cleanPath(settings.receiptFolder);
                }
                if (!settings.save())
                {
                    emit toast("Could not save terminal preferences");
                    return;
                }
                auto &app = AppState::instance();
                app.settings() = settings;
                const bool dark = theme->currentData().toBool();
                if (appearanceManager.isDarkMode() != dark)
                    appearanceManager.setDarkMode(dark);
                else
                    app.emitChanged();
                reset();
                emit toast("Settings applied");
            });
    reset();
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/ui/Theme.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "Theme.h"
#include <QPainter>
#include <cmath>
#include <QSettings>
#include <QPainterPath>
#include <QStyle>
#include <QWidget>

void Theme::repolish(QWidget *w)
{
    w->style()->unpolish(w);
    w->style()->polish(w);
    w->update();
}

QIcon Theme::icon(const QString &name, const QColor &color, int px)
{
    return QIcon(iconPixmap(name, color, px));
}

QPixmap Theme::iconPixmap(const QString &n, const QColor &c, int px)
{
    const qreal dpr = 2.0;
    QPixmap pm(int(px * dpr), int(px * dpr));
    pm.setDevicePixelRatio(dpr);
    pm.fill(Qt::transparent);
    QPainter p(&pm);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    p.scale(px / 24.0, px / 24.0);
    p.setPen(QPen(c, 1.9, Qt::SolidLine, Qt::RoundCap, Qt::RoundJoin));
    p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);

    auto line = [&](qreal x1, qreal y1, qreal x2, qreal y2)
    { p.drawLine(QPointF(x1, y1), QPointF(x2, y2)); };
    auto rect = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal w, qreal h, qreal r = 2)
    { p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(x, y, w, h), r, r); };
    auto circle = [&](qreal x, qreal y, qreal r)
    { p.drawEllipse(QPointF(x, y), r, r); };
    auto fillShape = [&](auto fn)
    {
        p.save();
        p.setBrush(c);
        fn();
        p.restore();
    };

    if (n == "dashboard")
    {
        rect(3, 3, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(13.5, 3, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(3, 13.5, 7.5, 7.5);
        rect(13.5, 13.5, 7.5, 7.5);
    }
    else if (n == "cart")
    {
        QPainterPath path;
        path.moveTo(2, 3.5);
        path.lineTo(5, 3.5);
        path.lineTo(7.2, 15);
        path.lineTo(18.5, 15);
        path.lineTo(20.5, 7);
        path.lineTo(5.6, 7);
        p.drawPath(path);
        circle(9.5, 19.3, 1.5);
        circle(16.5, 19.3, 1.5);
    }
    else if (n == "orders")
    {
        rect(5, 3, 14, 18, 2);

        line(8.5, 12, 15.5, 12);
        line(8.5, 16, 12.5, 16);
    }
    else if (n == "products")
    {

        rect(4.5, 9, 15, 11, 1.5);
        line(10, 13, 14, 13);
    }
    else if (n == "barcode")
    {
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setBrush(c);
        const qreal xs[] = {3, 5.5, 8, 10, 13, 15, 17.5, 20};
        const qreal ws[] = {1.4, 0.9, 1.8, 0.9, 1.4, 1.8, 0.9, 1.2};

            p.drawRect(QRectF(xs[i], 4, ws[i], 16));

    else if (n == "bills")
    {
        QPainterPath path;

                  { circle(12, 18.5, 1.3); });

    }

    else if (n == "logout")
    {

        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{12, 8}, {12, 12}, {15, 13.8}}));

    else if (n == "mail")
    {
        rect(3, 5.5, 18, 13, 2);

    {
        rect(3.5, 5, 17, 15.5, 2.5);

        p.drawPath(path);
    }
    else if (n == "star")
    {
        QPolygonF st;
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; ++i)
        {
            const qreal r = i % 2 == 0 ? 9 : 4;
            const qreal a = -M_PI / 2 + i * M_PI / 5;
            st << QPointF(12 + r * std::cos(a), 12.5 + r * std::sin(a));
        }
        p.drawPolygon(st);
    }
    else if (n == "drawer")
    {
        rect(3, 9, 18, 10, 2);
        line(3, 13, 21, 13);
        line(10, 16, 14, 16);
        p.drawPolyline(QPolygonF({{6, 9}, {8, 4}, {16, 4}, {18, 9}}));

}

namespace
{
    int g_dark = -1;
}

bool Theme::isDark()
{
    if (g_dark < 0)
        g_dark = QSettings(QSettings::defaultFormat(), QSettings::UserScope, "BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS")
                     .value("darkMode", false).toBool() ? 1 : 0;
    return g_dark == 1;
}

void Theme::setDark(bool on)
{
    g_dark = on ? 1 : 0;
    QSettings(QSettings::defaultFormat(), QSettings::UserScope, "BarcodeHub", "SmartPOS").setValue("darkMode", on);
}

QColor Theme::readable(const QColor &c)
{
    // In dark mode a dark colour (e.g. dark blue text) would vanish on the dark cards:
    // keep its hue but raise its lightness.
    if (!isDark() || c.lightnessF() >= 0.6)
        return c;
    return QColor::fromHslF(c.hslHueF() < 0 ? 0 : c.hslHueF(), c.hslSaturationF(), 0.72);
}

#readyPill { border-radius: 12px; }
#readyText { color: @successText@; font-weight: 700; font-size: 12px; background: transparent; }
)");

    // Resolve named roles so text, fills, and borders can vary independently.
    const struct
    {
        const char *token;
        QColor light;
        QColor dark;
    } colors[] = {
        {"@canvas@", surface, darkSurface},
        {"@panel@", white, darkPanel},
        {"@panelLow@", surfaceLow, "#202b42"},
        {"@text@", onSurface, darkText},
        {"@muted@", onSurfaceVariant, darkMuted},
        {"@outline@", outline, "#667895"},
        {"@divider@", outlineVariant, darkDivider},
        {"@sidebarTop@", sidebar, "#131e37"},
        {"@sidebarBottom@", "#0d1b32", "#0c1326"},
        {"@navActive@", "#2754b5", "#264c9f"},
        {"@navTail@", "#424263", "#393953"},
        {"@dashboardWash@", "#e8edfb", "#152139"},
        {"@heroStart@", "#17254b", "#152047"},
        {"@heroMid@", "#2554bb", "#244696"},
        {"@heroViolet@", "#574974", "#46395d"},
        {"@heroWarm@", "#a95842", "#824437"},
        {"@hover@", surfaceContainer, "#28364e"},
        {"@pressed@", surfaceHigh, "#354662"},
        {"@navy@", sidebar, sidebar},
        {"@white@", white, white},
        {"@brand@", primary, primary},
        {"@brandHover@", "#2049bd", "#2049bd"},
        {"@brandPressed@", "#193c9f", "#193c9f"},
        {"@accent@", primary, "#a7bfff"},
        {"@focus@", primary, "#91b0ff"},
        {"@softBg@", primaryContainer, "#233458"},
        {"@softBorder@", "#c5d3ee", "#486295"},
        {"@selection@", "#dce7ff", "#304772"},
        {"@softPressed@", "#d1dfff", "#344a75"},
        {"@successBg@", "#e5f5ed", "#17372d"},
        {"@successBorder@", "#b3ddc7", "#406957"},
        {"@successText@", "#125437", "#a4e1bf"},
        {"@dangerBg@", "#fff0ef", "#42272e"},
        {"@dangerBorder@", "#bf716c", "#a96870"},
        {"@dangerText@", "#9a251e", "#ffb8b3"},
        {"@dangerHover@", "#ffe5e2", "#533039"},
        {"@dangerPressed@", "#ffd9d5", "#61363f"},
        {"@warningBg@", "#fff4d7", "#3a2e1b"},
        {"@warningBorder@", "#ead29a", "#775c33"},
        {"@warningText@", "#674600", "#f7d48a"},
    };

    const bool dark = isDark();
    for (const auto &color : colors)
        sheet.replace(QLatin1String(color.token), (dark ? color.dark : color.light).name());
    return sheet;
}

#include "UiKit.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include <QCalendarWidget>
#include <QDateEdit>
#include <QEvent>
#include <QFile>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QHBoxLayout>
#include <QHeaderView>
#include <QScrollArea>
#include <QShowEvent>
#include <QTableWidget>
#include <QTextCharFormat>
#include <QTextStream>
#include <QVBoxLayout>

namespace Ui
{
    QTableWidget *makeTable(const QStringList &headers)
    {
        auto *t = new QTableWidget(0, headers.size());
        t->setHorizontalHeaderLabels(headers);
        t->verticalHeader()->hide();
        t->setShowGrid(false);
        t->setEditTriggers(QAbstractItemView::NoEditTriggers);
        t->setSelectionBehavior(QAbstractItemView::SelectRows);
        t->setSelectionMode(QAbstractItemView::SingleSelection);
        t->setFocusPolicy(Qt::NoFocus);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setSectionResizeMode(QHeaderView::Stretch);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setHighlightSections(false);
        t->horizontalHeader()->setDefaultAlignment(Qt::AlignLeft | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        t->setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt::ScrollBarAsNeeded);
        t->setHorizontalScrollMode(QAbstractItemView::ScrollPerPixel);
        return t;
    }

    QTableWidgetItem *item(const QString &s, Qt::Alignment a, const QString &color, bool bold)
    {
        auto *i = new QTableWidgetItem(s);
        i->setTextAlignment(a);
        if (!color.isEmpty())
            i->setForeground(Theme::readable(QColor(color)));
        if (bold)
        {
            QFont f = i->font();
            f.setBold(true);
            i->setFont(f);
        }
        return i;
    }

    QWidget *wrapW(QWidget *w, Qt::Alignment a)
    {
        auto *c = new QWidget;
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(6, 0, 6, 0);
        h->setAlignment(a | Qt::AlignVCenter);
        h->addWidget(w);
        return c;
    }

    QString csvField(const QString &s)
    {
        if (!s.contains(',') && !s.contains('"') && !s.contains('\n'))
            return s;
        QString q = s;
        q.replace('"', "\"\"");
        return '"' + q + '"';
    }

    bool writeCsv(QWidget *parent, const QString &suggested, const QStringList &rows)
    {
        const QString path = QFileDialog::getSaveFileName(parent, "Export CSV", suggested, "CSV (*.csv)");
        if (path.isEmpty())
            return false;
        QFile f(path);
        if (!f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Text))
            return false;
        QTextStream out(&f);
        for (const auto &r : rows)
            out << r << '\n';
        return true;
    }

    QWidget *pageShell(QWidget *page, QVBoxLayout *&body, bool scrollable)
    {
        if (!scrollable)
        {
            body = W::pageLayout(page);
            return page;
        }

        auto *outer = W::pageLayout(page);
        outer->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        auto *scroll = new QScrollArea;
        scroll->setWidgetResizable(true);
        auto *content = new QWidget;
        body = W::pageLayout(content);
        body->setSizeConstraint(QLayout::SetMinimumSize);
        scroll->setWidget(content);
        outer->addWidget(scroll);
        return content;
    }

    QDateEdit *dateEdit(const QDate &date, const QString &displayFormat, int minimumHeight)
    {
        auto *edit = new QDateEdit(date);
        edit->setCalendarPopup(true);
        edit->setDisplayFormat(displayFormat);
        edit->setMinimumHeight(minimumHeight);
        auto *calendar = edit->calendarWidget();
        calendar->setWeekdayTextFormat(Qt::Saturday, QTextCharFormat());
        calendar->setWeekdayTextFormat(Qt::Sunday, QTextCharFormat());
        return edit;
    }

    QWidget *sectionHeader(const QString &eyebrow, const QString &title, const QString &subtitle,
                           const QList<QWidget *> &actions)
    {
        auto *w = new QWidget;
        w->setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, false);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(w);
        h->setContentsMargins(4, 4, 4, 4);
        h->setSpacing(10);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(1);
        if (!eyebrow.isEmpty())
            v->addWidget(W::label(eyebrow, "eyebrow"));
        v->addWidget(W::label(title, "pageH1"));
        if (!subtitle.isEmpty())
        {
            auto *s = W::label(subtitle, "pageDesc");
            s->setWordWrap(true);
            v->addWidget(s);
        }
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        for (QWidget *a : actions)
            h->addWidget(a, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        return w;
    }

    QFrame *statTile(const QString &caption, const QString &icon, QLabel *&value, QLabel *&sub, const QString &tint)
    {
        auto *c = W::card(nullptr);
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(c);
        h->setContentsMargins(16, 12, 16, 12);
        h->setSpacing(10);
        auto *v = new QVBoxLayout;
        v->setSpacing(2);
        v->addWidget(W::label(caption, "caption"));
        value = W::label("", "bigValue");
        sub = W::label("", "muted");
        v->addWidget(value);
        v->addWidget(sub);
        h->addLayout(v, 1);
        QString top = "#eaf1ff", bottom = "#d9e6ff";
        QColor fg = Theme::primary;
        if (tint == "green") { top = "#e2fbec"; bottom = "#c9f3d9"; fg = Theme::tertiary; }
        else if (tint == "amber") { top = "#fff4d6"; bottom = "#ffe8a8"; fg = QColor("#8a5a00"); }
        else if (tint == "red") { top = "#ffe9e6"; bottom = "#ffd0ca"; fg = Theme::error; }
        h->addWidget(W::iconTile(icon, fg, top, bottom, 38), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        return c;
    }

    QLabel *kbd(const QString &text)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(text);
        l->setObjectName("kbd");
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *dotLabel(const QString &text, const QColor &dot)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(QString("<span style='color:%1'>&#9679;</span>&nbsp;%2").arg(dot.name(), text));
        l->setTextFormat(Qt::RichText);
        return l;
    }

    QPushButton *pageButton(const QString &text, bool current)
    {
        auto *b = W::button(text, current ? "primary" : "tab");
        b->setFixedHeight(32);
        return b;
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------------ Modal
    Modal::Modal(QWidget *parent, const QString &icon, const QString &title, const QString &subtitle,
                 const QString &tag, int width)
        : QDialog(parent, Qt::Dialog | Qt::FramelessWindowHint)
    {
        setAttribute(Qt::WA_TranslucentBackground);
        setModal(true);
        auto *outer = new QVBoxLayout(this);
        outer->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 18); // room for the card's shadow
        auto *card = new Card;
        card->setObjectName("modalCard");
        outer->addWidget(card);
        auto *cv = new QVBoxLayout(card);
        cv->setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0);
        cv->setSpacing(0);

        auto *head = new QFrame;
        head->setObjectName("modalHead");
        auto *hh = new QHBoxLayout(head);
        hh->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 16, 14);
        hh->setSpacing(12);
        hh->addWidget(W::iconTile(icon, QColor("white"), "#4a86ff", "#2554d8", 44), 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        auto *tv = new QVBoxLayout;
        tv->setSpacing(1);
        auto *tr = new QHBoxLayout;
        tr->setSpacing(8);
        m_title = W::label(title, "modalTitle");
        tr->addWidget(m_title);
        if (!tag.isEmpty())
            tr->addWidget(W::badge(tag, "blue"));
        tr->addStretch();
        tv->addLayout(tr);
        if (!subtitle.isEmpty())
        {
            auto *s = W::label(subtitle, "muted");
            s->setWordWrap(true);
            tv->addWidget(s);
        }
        hh->addLayout(tv, 1);
        auto *x = W::button("", "icon", "close");
        x->setProperty("kind", "flat");
        x->setFixedSize(34, 34);
        x->setToolTip("Close (Esc)");
        connect(x, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QDialog::reject);
        hh->addWidget(x, 0, Qt::AlignTop);
        cv->addWidget(head);

        auto *bodyW = new QWidget;
        m_body = new QVBoxLayout(bodyW);
        m_body->setContentsMargins(20, 16, 20, 16);
        m_body->setSpacing(12);
        cv->addWidget(bodyW, 1);

        m_footerBar = new QFrame;
        m_footerBar->setObjectName("modalFoot");
        m_footer = new QHBoxLayout(m_footerBar);
        m_footer->setContentsMargins(20, 12, 20, 12);
        m_footer->setSpacing(10);
        cv->addWidget(m_footerBar);

        setFixedWidth(width + 28);
    }

    void Modal::setTitle(const QString &t) { m_title->setText(t); }
    void Modal::hideFooter() { m_footerBar->hide(); }

    void Modal::showEvent(QShowEvent *e)
    {
        QDialog::showEvent(e);
        if (QWidget *host = parentWidget() ? parentWidget()->window() : nullptr)
        {
            if (!m_dim)
            {
                m_dim = new QWidget(host);
                m_dim->setObjectName("dim");
                m_dim->setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, true);
                m_dim->setStyleSheet("background: rgba(15,27,51,46%);");
            }
            m_dim->setGeometry(host->rect());
            m_dim->show();
            m_dim->raise();
            raise();
            // centre over the host window
            const QPoint c = host->mapToGlobal(host->rect().center());
            move(c.x() - width() / 2, qMax(host->mapToGlobal(QPoint(0, 0)).y() + 16, c.y() - height() / 2));
        }
    }

    void Modal::hideEvent(QHideEvent *e)
    {
        QDialog::hideEvent(e);
        if (m_dim)
        {
            m_dim->hide();
            m_dim->deleteLater();
            m_dim = nullptr;
        }
    }
}
