#include "UiStore.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "billing/SeasonRepository.h"
#include "product/CategoryStorage.h"
#include <QDir>
#include <QFile>
#include <QSet>
#include <QUrl>
#include <QSaveFile>
#include <QFileSystemWatcher>
#include <QRegularExpression>
#include <algorithm>

QString Season::statusOn(const QDate &d) const
{
    if (end < d)
        return "expired";
    if (!active)
        return "inactive"; // isActive = false: never applied, whatever the dates
    return start > d ? "scheduled" : "active";
}

int ParkedOrder::itemCount() const
{
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto &l : lines)
        n += l.qty;
    return n;
}

double ParkedOrder::total() const
{
    double t = 0;
    for (const auto &l : lines)
        t += (l.price - l.discount + l.tax) * l.qty;
    return t;
}

UiStore &UiStore::instance()
{
    static UiStore s;
    return s;
}

UiStore::UiStore()
{
    loadCategories(); // PCategory.txt (requirement 1)
    loadSeasons(); // real seasons from Seasons.txt (no demo seasons)
    reloadEditRequests();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- categories (PCategory.txt)
// The categories are kept in m_categories (a list sorted by id) and saved to PCategory.txt after every change.

// Colour used to draw a category's badge on screen (not saved in the file).
static QString categoryColor(const QString &id)
{
    const char *colors[4] = {"#2554d8", "#535e7a", "#006e4c", "#b6c4ff"};
    return colors[id.toInt() % 4];
}

// Insertion sort: puts the categories in order of their id ("01", "10", "20", ...).
static void sortById(QVector<Category> &list)
{
    for (int i = 1; i < list.size(); i++)
    {
        Category key = list[i];
        int j = i - 1;
        while (j >= 0 && list[j].id > key.id)
        {
            list[j + 1] = list[j];
            j--;
        }
        list[j + 1] = key;
    }
}

// True if text is exactly two digits.
static bool isTwoDigits(const QString &text)
{
    return text.size() == 2 && text[0].isDigit() && text[1].isDigit();
}

QString UiStore::categoriesPath() const
{
    return QDir(AppState::instance().dataDir()).filePath("PCategory.txt");
}

void UiStore::loadCategories()
{
    CategoryRepository repo(categoriesPath().toStdString());
    m_categories.clear();

    if (!repo.exists())
    {
        // First run: there is no PCategory.txt yet, so create it with a starting set of categories.
        m_categories.push_back({"01", "General Store", "GEN", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"10", "Dairy & Milk Products", "DAI", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"20", "Bakery & Fresh Bread", "BAK", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"30", "Beverages & Tea", "BEV", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"40", "Snacks & Confectionery", "SNK", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"50", "Grocery & Staples", "GRC", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"60", "Personal Care & Hygiene", "PER", ""});
        m_categories.push_back({"70", "Household & Cleaning", "HSD", ""});
        QString error;
        if (!saveCategories(m_categories, &error))
            qWarning("Could not create PCategory.txt: %s", qPrintable(error));
    }
    else
    {
        // Read the file; lines that are not valid are skipped and reported.
        std::vector<std::string> errors;
        std::vector<ProductCategory> rows = repo.load(&errors);
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)rows.size(); i++)
        {
            Category c;
            c.id = QString::fromStdString(rows[i].id);
            c.name = QString::fromStdString(rows[i].name);
            c.acronym = QString::fromStdString(rows[i].acronym);
            m_categories.push_back(c);
        }
        for (int i = 0; i < (int)errors.size(); i++)
            qWarning("%s", errors[i].c_str());
    }

    // A product whose category is missing from the file still gets a row, so it can be seen and fixed.
    const QVector<ProductInfo> &products = AppState::instance().products();
    for (int i = 0; i < products.size(); i++)
    {
        const QString id = products[i].categoryId;
        if (id != "" && category(id) == nullptr)
            m_categories.push_back({id, "Category " + id, "C" + id, ""});
    }

    sortById(m_categories);
    for (int i = 0; i < m_categories.size(); i++)
        m_categories[i].color = categoryColor(m_categories[i].id);
}

bool UiStore::saveCategories(const QVector<Category> &categories, QString *error)
{
    // Copy the screen list into the back-end type, then let CategoryRepository write the file.
    std::vector<ProductCategory> rows;
    for (int i = 0; i < categories.size(); i++)
    {
        ProductCategory row;
        row.id = categories[i].id.toStdString();
        row.name = categories[i].name.trimmed().toStdString();
        row.acronym = categories[i].acronym.trimmed().toStdString();
        rows.push_back(row);
    }
    std::string why;
    CategoryRepository repo(categoriesPath().toStdString());
    if (!repo.save(rows, &why))
    {
        if (error)
            *error = "Could not save PCategory.txt: " + QString::fromStdString(why);
        return false;
    }
    return true;
}

// Linear search for a category by id; nullptr if there is none.
const Category *UiStore::category(const QString &id) const
{
    for (int i = 0; i < m_categories.size(); i++)
        if (m_categories[i].id == id)
            return &m_categories[i];
    return nullptr;
}

// How many products use this category.
int UiStore::productCount(const QString &id) const
{
    int count = 0;
    const QVector<ProductInfo> &products = AppState::instance().products();
    for (int i = 0; i < products.size(); i++)
    {
        QString productCategory = products[i].categoryId;
        if (productCategory == "")
            productCategory = products[i].ean.left(2); // older products: take it from the barcode
        if (productCategory == id)
            count++;
    }
    return count;
}

QString UiStore::rangeText(const QString &id) const
{
    return id + "000000 - " + id + "999999";
}

// Suggests up to n ids that are not used yet (10, then every 5th: 15, 20, ...).
QStringList UiStore::freeIds(int n) const
{
    QStringList result;
    int step = 1;
    for (int i = 10; i <= 99 && result.size() < n; i += step)
    {
        const QString id = QString::number(i);
        if (category(id) == nullptr)
        {
            result.push_back(id);
            step = 5;
        }
    }
    return result;
}

// Returns "" if the category may be saved, otherwise the reason. ignoreId = the id being edited (it may keep it).
QString UiStore::validateCategory(const QString &id, const QString &name, const QString &acronym,
                                  const QString &ignoreId) const
{
    const QString acr = acronym.trimmed();
    if (!isTwoDigits(id))
        return "Category ID must be exactly 2 digits.";
    if (id == "00")
        return "Category ID 00 is not allowed (barcodes cannot start with 00). Use 01 to 99.";
    const Category *existing = category(id);
    if (existing != nullptr && id != ignoreId)
    {
        QStringList free = freeIds(1);
        return QString("Category ID \"%1\" is already in use by \"%2\". Please pick an unused 2-digit code (e.g. %3).")
            .arg(id, existing->name, free.isEmpty() ? QString("80") : free[0]);
    }
    if (name.trimmed() == "")
        return "Category name is required.";
    if (name.contains('#'))
        return "Category name cannot contain '#'.";
    if (acr == "" || acr.size() > 3 || acr.contains(' '))
        return "Acronym must be 1 to 3 characters, without spaces.";
    for (int i = 0; i < m_categories.size(); i++)
        if (m_categories[i].id != ignoreId && m_categories[i].acronym.toUpper() == acr.toUpper())
            return QString("Acronym \"%1\" is already used by \"%2\".").arg(acr.toUpper(), m_categories[i].name);
    return "";
}

// Each change below is made on a copy of the list. The copy is saved first; only if the file was written
// does it replace m_categories - so the screen and PCategory.txt always show the same categories.

bool UiStore::addCategory(const Category &c, QString *error)
{
    const QString problem = validateCategory(c.id, c.name, c.acronym);
    if (problem != "")
    {
        if (error)
            *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    Category added;
    added.id = c.id;
    added.name = c.name.trimmed();
    added.acronym = c.acronym.trimmed().toUpper();
    added.color = categoryColor(added.id);

    QVector<Category> copy = m_categories;
    copy.push_back(added);
    sortById(copy);
    if (!saveCategories(copy, error))
        return false;
    m_categories = copy;
    emit categoriesChanged();
    return true;
}

bool UiStore::updateCategory(const QString &oldId, const Category &c, QString *error)
{
    const QString problem = validateCategory(c.id, c.name, c.acronym, oldId);
    if (problem != "")
    {
        if (error)
            *error = problem;
        return false;
    }
    // The id is part of every product barcode, so it cannot change once products use it.
    if (oldId != c.id && productCount(oldId) > 0)
    {
        if (error)
            *error = "This category already has products; changing its id would break their barcodes.";
        return false;
    }

    QVector<Category> copy = m_categories;
    for (int i = 0; i < copy.size(); i++)
        if (copy[i].id == oldId)
        {
            copy[i].id = c.id;
            copy[i].name = c.name.trimmed();
            copy[i].acronym = c.acronym.trimmed().toUpper();
            copy[i].color = categoryColor(c.id);
        }
    sortById(copy);
    if (!saveCategories(copy, error))
        return false;
    m_categories = copy;
    emit categoriesChanged();
    return true;
}

bool UiStore::removeCategory(const QString &id, QString *error)
{
    // A category that products still use cannot be deleted.
    const int used = productCount(id);
    if (used > 0)
    {
        if (error)
            *error = QString("%1 product(s) still use this category.").arg(used);
        return false;
    }

    for (int i = 0; i < m_categories.size(); i++)
        if (m_categories[i].id == id)
        {
            QVector<Category> copy = m_categories;
            copy.removeAt(i);
            if (!saveCategories(copy, error))
                return false;
            m_categories = copy;
            emit categoriesChanged();
            return true;
        }
    return false; // no category with this id
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- seasons
QString UiStore::seasonLabel(const QDate &start, const QDate &end)
{
    if (start.year() != end.year())
        return start.toString("d MMM yyyy") + " - " + end.toString("d MMM yyyy");
    return start.toString("d MMM") + " - " + end.toString("d MMM yyyy");
}

QString UiStore::seasonsPath() const
{
    return QDir(AppState::instance().dataDir()).filePath("Seasons.txt");
}

void UiStore::loadSeasons()
{
    namespace bb = barcodehub::billing;
    std::vector<std::string> errors;
    const auto records = bb::SeasonRepository(seasonsPath().toStdString()).load(&errors);
    m_seasons.clear();
    int n = 0;
    for (const auto &r : records)
    {
        Season s;
        s.start = QDate::fromString(QString::fromStdString(r.startDate), Qt::ISODate);
        s.end = QDate::fromString(QString::fromStdString(r.endDate), Qt::ISODate);
        s.percent = r.percent;
        s.active = r.active;
        s.id = QString("SEA-%1-%2").arg(s.start.year()).arg(++n, 2, 10, QLatin1Char('0'));
        s.name = seasonLabel(s.start, s.end);
        m_seasons.push_back(s);
    }
    for (const auto &e : errors)
        qWarning("%s", e.c_str()); // bad lines are skipped, not fatal
}

void UiStore::saveSeasons()
{
    namespace bb = barcodehub::billing;
    std::vector<bb::SeasonRecord> records;
    for (const auto &s : m_seasons)
        records.push_back({s.start.toString(Qt::ISODate).toStdString(), s.end.toString(Qt::ISODate).toStdString(),
                           s.percent, s.active});
    std::string error;
    if (!bb::SeasonRepository(seasonsPath().toStdString()).save(records, &error))
        qWarning("Could not save Seasons.txt: %s", error.c_str());
}

void UiStore::addSeason(const Season &s)
{
    m_seasons.push_back(s);
    m_seasons.last().name = seasonLabel(s.start, s.end);
    saveSeasons();
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

void UiStore::updateSeason(const QString &id, const Season &s)
{
    for (auto &x : m_seasons)
        if (x.id == id)
        {
            x = s;
            x.id = id;
            x.name = seasonLabel(s.start, s.end);
        }
    saveSeasons();
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

void UiStore::removeSeason(const QString &id)
{
    for (int i = 0; i < m_seasons.size(); ++i)
        if (m_seasons[i].id == id)
        {
            m_seasons.removeAt(i);
            break;
        }
    saveSeasons();
    emit seasonsChanged();
}

QString UiStore::setSeasonActive(const QString &id, bool on)
{
    for (auto &x : m_seasons)
        if (x.id == id)
        {
            if (on)
                if (const QString why = validateSeason(x.start, x.end, x.percent, true, id); !why.isEmpty())
                {
                    emit seasonsChanged(); // redraw the toggle as it was
                    return why;
                }
            x.active = on;
        }
    saveSeasons();
    emit seasonsChanged();
    return QString();
}

QString UiStore::nextSeasonId() const
{
    int maxN = 0;
    for (const auto &s : m_seasons)
        maxN = qMax(maxN, s.id.section('-', 2).toInt());
    return QString("SEA-%1-%2").arg(QDate::currentDate().year()).arg(maxN + 1, 2, 10, QLatin1Char('0'));
}

const Season *UiStore::activeSeason(const QDate &d) const
{
    for (const auto &s : m_seasons)
        if (s.active && s.start <= d && d <= s.end)
            return &s;
    return nullptr;
}

QString UiStore::validateSeason(const QDate &start, const QDate &end, double percent, bool active,
                                const QString &ignoreId) const
{
    if (!start.isValid() || !end.isValid())
        return "Enter valid start and end dates.";
    if (end < start)
        return "The end date must not be before the start date.";
    if (percent <= 0 || percent > 100)
        return "Discount percentage must be more than 0 and at most 100.";
    if (active)
        for (const auto &s : m_seasons)
            if (s.id != ignoreId && s.active && s.start <= end && start <= s.end)
                return QString("These dates overlap the active season %1 (%2%). Change the dates, save it as "
                               "inactive, or switch that season off first.")
                    .arg(s.name).arg(s.percent, 0, 'g', 4);
    return QString();
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- parked orders
QVector<ParkedOrder> UiStore::parked() const
{
    QVector<ParkedOrder> out;
    for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().heldOrders())
    {
        if (o.status != "On Hold")
            continue;
        ParkedOrder p;
        p.code = o.id;
        p.customer = o.customer;
        p.contact = o.contact;
        p.parkedAt = o.time;
        p.lines = o.lines;
        out << p;
    }
    return out;
}

QString UiStore::park(const QString &customer, const QString &contact, const QVector<ReceiptLine> &lines,
                      const QString &existingId, QString *error)
{
    const QString id = AppState::instance().holdOrder(customer, contact, lines, existingId, error);
    emit parkedChanged();
    return id;
}

bool UiStore::takeParked(const QString &code, ParkedOrder &out)
{
    for (const auto &o : AppState::instance().heldOrders())
        if (o.id == code)
        {
            out.code = o.id;
            out.customer = o.customer;
            out.contact = o.contact;
            out.parkedAt = o.time;
            out.lines = o.lines;
            return true;
        }
    return false;
}

bool UiStore::discardParked(const QString &code, QString *error)
{
    const bool ok = AppState::instance().discardHeldOrder(code, error);
    emit parkedChanged();
    return ok;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------- edit requests (EditRequests.txt)
// One request per line, space separated, ending with '#':
//   orderId status requestedAt cashier reason decidedAt manager#
// Text fields are %-encoded (space = %20), so names and reasons may contain spaces; an empty field is "-".
namespace
{
    QString enc(const QString &t)
    {
        if (t.isEmpty())
            return "-";
        QString out = t;
        out.replace('%', "%25").replace(' ', "%20").replace('#', "%23").replace('\n', "%0A");
        return out;
    }
    QString dec(const QString &t)
    {
        return t == "-" ? QString() : QUrl::fromPercentEncoding(t.toUtf8());
    }
    QString when(const QDateTime &d) { return d.isValid() ? d.toString(Qt::ISODate) : "-"; }
}

QString UiStore::editRequestsPath() const
{
    return QDir(AppState::instance().dataDir()).filePath("EditRequests.txt");
}

void UiStore::reloadEditRequests()
{
    QVector<EditRequest> loaded;
    QFile f(editRequestsPath());
    if (f.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly | QIODevice::Text))
    {
        int number = 0;
        while (!f.atEnd())
        {
            ++number;
            QString line = QString::fromUtf8(f.readLine()).trimmed();
            if (line.isEmpty())
                continue;
            const QStringList t = line.endsWith('#') ? line.chopped(1).split(' ', Qt::SkipEmptyParts) : QStringList();
            static const QStringList kStatus = {"pending", "approved", "rejected", "used"};
            if (t.size() != 7 || !kStatus.contains(t[1]))
            {
                qWarning("EditRequests.txt line %d is not valid and was skipped", number);
                continue;
            }
            EditRequest r;
            r.orderId = t[0];
            r.status = t[1];
            r.requestedAt = QDateTime::fromString(t[2], Qt::ISODate);
            r.cashier = dec(t[3]);
            r.reason = dec(t[4]);
            r.decidedAt = t[5] == "-" ? QDateTime() : QDateTime::fromString(t[5], Qt::ISODate);
            r.manager = dec(t[6]);
            loaded << r;
        }
    }
    const bool changed = loaded.size() != m_requests.size() ||
                         !std::equal(loaded.begin(), loaded.end(), m_requests.begin(), [](const EditRequest &a, const EditRequest &b) {
                             return a.orderId == b.orderId && a.status == b.status && a.requestedAt == b.requestedAt;
                         });
    m_requests = loaded;
    if (!m_watcher)
    {
        m_watcher = new QFileSystemWatcher(this);
        connect(m_watcher, &QFileSystemWatcher::fileChanged, this, [this](const QString &) { reloadEditRequests(); });
        connect(m_watcher, &QFileSystemWatcher::directoryChanged, this, [this](const QString &) { reloadEditRequests(); });
        m_watcher->addPath(AppState::instance().dataDir());
    }
    if (QFile::exists(editRequestsPath()) && !m_watcher->files().contains(editRequestsPath()))
        m_watcher->addPath(editRequestsPath()); // re-added after every atomic replace
    if (changed)
        emit editRequestsChanged();
}

bool UiStore::saveEditRequests(const QVector<EditRequest> &requests, QString *error)
{
    QByteArray data;
    for (const auto &r : requests)
        data += QString("%1 %2 %3 %4 %5 %6 %7#\n")
                    .arg(r.orderId, r.status, when(r.requestedAt), enc(r.cashier), enc(r.reason), when(r.decidedAt), enc(r.manager))
                    .toUtf8();
    QSaveFile f(editRequestsPath());
    f.setDirectWriteFallback(true);
    if (f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly) && f.write(data) == data.size() && f.commit())
        return true;
    // the safe replace was refused (on Windows another program can briefly hold the file): write it directly
    QFile direct(editRequestsPath());
    if (direct.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate) && direct.write(data) == data.size())
        return true;
    if (error) *error = "Could not save EditRequests.txt: " + direct.errorString();
    return false;
}

void UiStore::setEditRequests(const QVector<EditRequest> &requests)
{
    m_requests = requests;
    if (m_watcher && QFile::exists(editRequestsPath()) && !m_watcher->files().contains(editRequestsPath()))
        m_watcher->addPath(editRequestsPath());
    emit editRequestsChanged();
}

QVector<UiStore::EditRequest> UiStore::editRequests() const
{
    QVector<EditRequest> out = m_requests;
    std::reverse(out.begin(), out.end());
    return out;
}

QVector<UiStore::EditRequest> UiStore::pendingEditRequests() const
{
    // only the latest record of each order counts; an older "pending" line that was later answered is history
    QVector<EditRequest> out;
    QSet<QString> seen;
    for (int i = m_requests.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
    {
        if (seen.contains(m_requests[i].orderId))
            continue;
        seen.insert(m_requests[i].orderId);
        if (m_requests[i].status == "pending")
            out.prepend(m_requests[i]);
    }
    return out;
}

const UiStore::EditRequest *UiStore::latestEditRequest(const QString &orderId) const
{
    for (int i = m_requests.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (m_requests[i].orderId == orderId)
            return &m_requests[i];
    return nullptr;
}

int UiStore::approvedUnusedCount() const
{
    QSet<QString> seen;
    int n = 0;
    for (int i = m_requests.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (!seen.contains(m_requests[i].orderId))
        {
            seen.insert(m_requests[i].orderId);
            n += m_requests[i].status == "approved";
        }
    return n;
}

QString UiStore::requestEdit(const QString &orderId, const QString &cashier, const QString &reason)
{
    reloadEditRequests();
    if (const EditRequest *r = latestEditRequest(orderId))
    {
        if (r->status == "pending")
            return "A request for order " + orderId + " is already waiting for a manager.";
        if (r->status == "approved")
            return "Order " + orderId + " is already approved - you can open it.";
    }
    EditRequest r;
    r.orderId = orderId;
    r.status = "pending";
    r.cashier = cashier.trimmed().isEmpty() ? QString("Cashier") : cashier.trimmed();
    r.reason = reason.trimmed().isEmpty() ? QString("Customer request") : reason.trimmed();
    r.requestedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
    QVector<EditRequest> next = m_requests;
    next << r;
    QString error;
    if (!saveEditRequests(next, &error))
        return error;
    setEditRequests(next);
    return QString();
}

QString UiStore::decideEdit(const QString &orderId, const QString &manager, bool approve)
{
    reloadEditRequests();
    QVector<EditRequest> next = m_requests;
    for (int i = next.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (next[i].orderId == orderId)
        {
            if (next[i].status != "pending")
                return "This request was already answered (" + next[i].status + ").";
            next[i].status = approve ? "approved" : "rejected";
            next[i].manager = manager.trimmed().isEmpty() ? QString("Manager") : manager.trimmed();
            next[i].decidedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
            QString error;
            if (!saveEditRequests(next, &error))
                return error;
            setEditRequests(next);
            return QString();
        }
    return "No request was found for order " + orderId + ".";
}

void UiStore::grantApproval(const QString &orderId, const QString &manager, const QString &reason)
{
    // A signed-in manager editing an order approves it himself; it is recorded like any other approval.
    reloadEditRequests();
    QVector<EditRequest> next = m_requests;
    for (int i = next.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (next[i].orderId == orderId)
        {
            if (next[i].status == "approved")
                return; // already approved and not used yet
            if (next[i].status == "pending")
            {
                // the manager opens an order a cashier asked about: that request is approved by him
                next[i].status = "approved";
                next[i].manager = manager;
                next[i].decidedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
                if (saveEditRequests(next))
                    setEditRequests(next);
                return;
            }
            break;
        }
    EditRequest r;
    r.orderId = orderId;
    r.status = "approved";
    r.cashier = manager;
    r.reason = reason;
    r.requestedAt = r.decidedAt = QDateTime::currentDateTime();
    r.manager = manager;
    next << r;
    if (saveEditRequests(next))
        setEditRequests(next);
}

bool UiStore::approvalFor(const QString &orderId, Approval *out) const
{
    const EditRequest *r = latestEditRequest(orderId);
    if (!r || r->status != "approved")
        return false;
    if (out)
        *out = Approval{r->manager, r->reason, r->decidedAt};
    return true;
}

void UiStore::revokeApproval(const QString &orderId)
{
    QVector<EditRequest> next = m_requests;
    for (int i = next.size() - 1; i >= 0; --i)
        if (next[i].orderId == orderId)
        {
            if (next[i].status != "approved")
                return;
            next[i].status = "used";
            if (saveEditRequests(next))
                setEditRequests(next);
            return;
        }
}

#include "Widgets.h"
#include "AppState.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
#include "decoder.h"

namespace
{
    class ThemeButtonIcon final : public QIconEngine
    {
    public:
        ThemeButtonIcon(const QString &name, const QColor &color) : m_name(name), m_color(color) {}

        QIconEngine *clone() const override { return new ThemeButtonIcon(m_name, m_color); }

        QPixmap pixmap(const QSize &size, QIcon::Mode mode, QIcon::State state) override
        {
            QPixmap result(size);
            result.fill(Qt::transparent);
            QPainter painter(&result);
            paint(&painter, result.rect(), mode, state);
            return result;
        }

        void paint(QPainter *painter, const QRect &rect, QIcon::Mode mode, QIcon::State) override
        {
            painter->save();
            if (mode == QIcon::Disabled)
                painter->setOpacity(painter->opacity() * 0.5);
            const int size = qMin(rect.width(), rect.height());
            painter->drawPixmap(rect, Theme::iconPixmap(m_name, Theme::readable(m_color), size));
            painter->restore();
        }

    private:
        QString m_name;
        QColor m_color;
    };
}

namespace W
{
    QFrame *card(QWidget *parent, const char *objectName)
    {
        const QString n = objectName;
        if (n == "card" || n == "strip" || n == "totals")
            return new Card(parent, false);
        if (n == "cardAlert")
            return new Card(parent, true);
        auto *f = new QFrame(parent);
        f->setObjectName(objectName);
        return f;
    }

    QVBoxLayout *pageLayout(QWidget *page)
    {
        page->setObjectName("page");
        auto *l = new QVBoxLayout(page);
        l->setContentsMargins(14, 10, 14, 6);
        l->setSpacing(0);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *iconTile(const QString &icon, const QColor &fg, const QString &top, const QString &bottom, int size)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel;
        l->setFixedSize(size, size);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        l->setStyleSheet(QString("background: qlineargradient(x1:0,y1:0,x2:0,y2:1, stop:0 %1, stop:1 %2);"
                                 "border-radius:%3px; border-bottom:3px solid rgba(0,0,0,18%);")
                             .arg(top, bottom)
                             .arg(size / 3));
        l->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, fg, size / 2));
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *label(const QString &text, const char *objectName, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(text, parent);
        if (objectName)
            l->setObjectName(objectName);
        return l;
    }

    void setBadge(QLabel *l, const QString &text, const QString &kind)
    {
        l->setText(text);
        l->setProperty("badge", kind);
        Theme::repolish(l);
    }

    QLabel *badge(const QString &text, const QString &kind, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
        setBadge(l, text, kind);
        return l;
    }

    QPushButton *button(const QString &text, const QString &kind, const QString &icon, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *b = new QPushButton(text, parent);
        if (!kind.isEmpty())
            b->setProperty("kind", kind);
        if (!icon.isEmpty())
        {
            const bool light = kind == "primary";
            const bool danger = kind == "danger";
            const QColor c = light ? QColor("white") : danger ? Theme::error
                                                              : Theme::primary;
            b->setIcon(QIcon(new ThemeButtonIcon(icon, c)));
            b->setIconSize({16, 16});
        }
        b->setCursor(Qt::PointingHandCursor);
        return b;
    }

    QLabel *iconLabel(const QString &icon, const QColor &color, int px, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setPixmap(Theme::iconPixmap(icon, color, px));
        l->setFixedSize(px, px);
        l->setAttribute(Qt::WA_TranslucentBackground);
        return l;
    }

    QLabel *dot(const QColor &color, QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *l = new QLabel(parent);
        l->setFixedSize(8, 8);
        l->setStyleSheet(QString("background:%1; border-radius:4px;").arg(color.name()));
        return l;
    }

    QFrame *hline(QWidget *parent)
    {
        auto *f = new QFrame(parent);
        f->setFixedHeight(1);
        f->setStyleSheet("background:#dce9ff; border:none;");
        return f;
    }

    QString money(double v, bool sign)
    {
        const QString s = QLocale(QLocale::English).toString(qAbs(v), 'f', 2);
        if (v < 0)
            return "-PKR " + s;
        return (sign ? "+PKR " : "PKR ") + s;
    }

    QString statusBadgeKind(const QString &status)
    {
        if (status == "Paid")
            return "green";
        if (status == "In Progress")
            return "blue";
        if (status == "On Hold")
            return "amber";
        return "grey";
    }

    QHBoxLayout *headerRow(QVBoxLayout *into, const QString &icon, const QString &title, QWidget *right)
    {
        auto *h = new QHBoxLayout;
        h->setSpacing(8);
        if (!icon.isEmpty())
            h->addWidget(iconLabel(icon, Theme::primary, 18));
        h->addWidget(label(title, "h1"));
        h->addStretch();
        if (right)
            h->addWidget(right, 0, Qt::AlignVCenter);
        into->addLayout(h);
        return h;
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ Card
Card::Card(QWidget *parent, bool alert) : QFrame(parent), m_alert(alert)
{
    setContentsMargins(8, 4, 8, 10);
    setAttribute(Qt::WA_StyledBackground, false);
}

void Card::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    const QRectF body = QRectF(rect()).adjusted(8.5, 4.5, -8.5, -10.5);
    const bool dark = Theme::isDark();

    p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
    p.setBrush(dark ? QColor(0, 0, 0, 32) : QColor(47, 61, 114, 16));
    p.drawRoundedRect(body.translated(0, 3).adjusted(-2, 0, 2, 2), 15, 15);
    const auto fill = m_alert ? (dark ? QColor("#352434") : QColor("#fff5f6"))
                              : (dark ? Theme::darkPanel : Theme::white);
    const auto border = m_alert ? (dark ? QColor("#765064") : QColor("#e7b9c1"))
                                : (dark ? Theme::darkDivider : Theme::outlineVariant);
    p.setBrush(fill);
    p.setPen(border);
    p.drawRoundedRect(body, 14, 14);
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ CameraFeedWidget
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_QT
struct CameraFeedWidget::CameraRig
{
    explicit CameraRig(const QCameraDevice &dev) : camera(dev) {}
    QCamera camera;
    QMediaCaptureSession session;
    QVideoSink sink;
    Decoder decoder;
};
#endif
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_OPENCV
struct CameraFeedWidget::CameraRig
{
    cv::VideoCapture capture;
    QTimer timer;
    Decoder decoder;
};
#endif

CameraFeedWidget::CameraFeedWidget(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    setMinimumHeight(150);
    setMouseTracking(true);
    m_resultClock.invalidate();
    // repaint while the confirmation banner is visible so it disappears on time
    auto *t = new QTimer(this);
    connect(t, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] { if (m_resultClock.isValid()) update(); });
    t->start(300);
    // the red scan line sweeps up and down by itself while the camera runs
    auto *sweep = new QTimer(this);
    connect(sweep, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] {
        if (!m_running || m_frame.isNull()) return;
        m_sweepPhase += 0.045;
        m_lineFrac = 0.5 + 0.4 * std::sin(m_sweepPhase);
        update();
    });
    sweep->start(30);
}
CameraFeedWidget::~CameraFeedWidget() = default;

// The picture is letter-boxed inside the widget; these map the aiming line to widget coordinates.
static QRect pictureRect(const QWidget *w, const QImage &img)
{
    if (img.isNull()) return w->rect();
    const QSize s = img.size().scaled(w->rect().adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1).size(), Qt::KeepAspectRatio);
    return QRect(QPoint((w->width() - s.width()) / 2, (w->height() - s.height()) / 2), s);
}

bool CameraFeedWidget::nearLine(const QPoint &p) const
{
    if (m_frame.isNull()) return false;
    const QRect pic = pictureRect(this, m_frame);
    const int y = pic.top() + static_cast<int>(pic.height() * m_lineFrac);
    return pic.contains(p) && std::abs(p.y() - y) <= 14;
}

void CameraFeedWidget::moveLine(const QPoint &p)
{
    const QRect pic = pictureRect(this, m_frame);
    m_lineFrac = qBound(0.05, double(p.y() - pic.top()) / qMax(1, pic.height()), 0.95);
    update();
}

void CameraFeedWidget::mousePressEvent(QMouseEvent *e)
{
    Q_UNUSED(e); // the line moves by itself now
}
void CameraFeedWidget::mouseMoveEvent(QMouseEvent *e)
{
    Q_UNUSED(e);
}
void CameraFeedWidget::mouseReleaseEvent(QMouseEvent *) { m_drag = false; }

void CameraFeedWidget::showResult(const QString &text, bool ok)
{
    m_result = text;
    m_resultOk = ok;
    m_resultClock.start();
    update();
}

void CameraFeedWidget::setRunning(bool on)
{
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
    if (!on) {
        m_rig.reset(); // stops the camera and releases the device
        m_running = false;
        m_frame = QImage();
        update();
        return;
    }
    if (m_running) return;

#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_QT
#if QT_VERSION >= QT_VERSION_CHECK(6, 5, 0)
    // Needed on macOS/mobile; on Windows it is simply granted.
    QCameraPermission permission;
    const auto status = qApp->checkPermission(permission);
    if (status == Qt::PermissionStatus::Denied) {
        emit scanError("Camera access is blocked. Allow it in Windows Settings > Privacy > Camera.");
        return;
    }
    if (status == Qt::PermissionStatus::Undetermined) {
        qApp->requestPermission(permission, this, [this](const QPermission &p) {
            if (p.status() == Qt::PermissionStatus::Granted) setRunning(true);
            else emit scanError("Camera permission was not granted.");
        });
        return;
    }
#endif
    const QCameraDevice device = QMediaDevices::defaultVideoInput();
    if (device.isNull()) {
        emit scanError("No camera found. Plug in a webcam (and close other apps that use it).");
        return;
    }
    m_rig = std::make_unique<CameraRig>(device);
    connect(&m_rig->sink, &QVideoSink::videoFrameChanged, this, [this](const QVideoFrame &f) { onImage(f.toImage()); });
    connect(&m_rig->camera, &QCamera::errorOccurred, this, [this](QCamera::Error, const QString &why) {
        setRunning(false);
        emit scanError(why.isEmpty() ? QString("The camera stopped working.") : why);
    });
    m_rig->session.setCamera(&m_rig->camera);
    m_rig->session.setVideoSink(&m_rig->sink);
    m_lastDetected.clear();
    m_lastEmit.invalidate();
    m_armed = true;
    m_decodeClock.start();
    m_running = true;
    m_rig->camera.start();
#endif
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA_OPENCV
    m_rig = std::make_unique<CameraRig>();
#ifdef _WIN32
    m_rig->capture.open(0, cv::CAP_DSHOW); // DirectShow: fastest to open on Windows
    if (!m_rig->capture.isOpened()) m_rig->capture.open(0);
#else
    m_rig->capture.open(0);
#endif
    if (!m_rig->capture.isOpened()) {
        m_rig.reset();
        emit scanError("Cannot open the camera. Plug in a webcam and close other apps that use it.");
        return;
    }
    m_rig->capture.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH, 640);
    m_rig->capture.set(cv::CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT, 480);
    connect(&m_rig->timer, &QTimer::timeout, this, [this] {
        if (!m_rig) return;
        cv::Mat bgr;
        if (!m_rig->capture.read(bgr) || bgr.empty()) {
            setRunning(false);
            emit scanError("The camera stopped sending pictures.");
            return;
        }
        cv::Mat rgb;
        cv::cvtColor(bgr, rgb, cv::COLOR_BGR2RGB);
        onImage(QImage(rgb.data, rgb.cols, rgb.rows, static_cast<int>(rgb.step), QImage::Format_RGB888).copy());
    });
    m_lastDetected.clear();
    m_lastEmit.invalidate();
    m_armed = true;
    m_decodeClock.start();
    m_running = true;
    m_rig->timer.start(33);
#endif
    update();
#else
    if (on)
        emit scanError("Camera support is not built in. Install the Qt Multimedia component and reconfigure CMake.");
#endif
}

#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
void CameraFeedWidget::onImage(const QImage &img)
{
    if (!m_running || !m_rig) return;
    if (img.isNull()) return;
    m_frame = img;
    update();
    if (m_decodeClock.elapsed() < 120) return; // decode ~8 times a second, show every frame
    m_decodeClock.restart();

    QImage g = img.convertToFormat(QImage::Format_Grayscale8);
    if (g.width() > 960) g = g.scaledToWidth(960, Qt::FastTransformation);
    GrayImage gray;
    gray.width = g.width();
    gray.height = g.height();
    gray.pixels.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(gray.width) * gray.height);
    for (int y = 0; y < gray.height; ++y)
        std::copy(g.constScanLine(y), g.constScanLine(y) + gray.width, gray.pixels.begin() + static_cast<long>(y) * gray.width);

    std::string code;
    if (m_rig->decoder.decodeCropping(gray, code, m_lineFrac)) {
        const QString value = QString::fromStdString(code);
        const auto h = m_rig->decoder.lastHit();
        if (h.valid) {
            const double k = static_cast<double>(img.width()) / gray.width; // back to camera-picture pixels
            m_hitBox = QRectF(QPointF(h.x0 * k, h.y0 * k), QPointF(h.x1 * k, h.y1 * k)).normalized();
        } else {
            m_hitBox = QRectF();
        }
        m_hitText = value;
        m_hitClock.start();
        // Scan once: report a barcode a single time, then wait until it leaves the picture.
        m_lastEmit.restart();
        if (m_armed) {
            m_armed = false;
            m_lastDetected = value;
            showResult(QString(), true); // green flash only, no text on the camera
            emit barcodeDetected(value);
        }
    } else if (!m_armed && m_lastEmit.isValid() && m_lastEmit.elapsed() > 1000) {
        m_armed = true; // no barcode for 1 second: ready for the next scan
    }
}
#endif

void CameraFeedWidget::setCode(const QString &code)
{
    m_code = code;
    update();
}

void CameraFeedWidget::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    const QRect r = rect().adjusted(1, 1, -1, -1);
    p.fillRect(r, QColor("#162644"));
    if (!m_frame.isNull()) {
        const QImage scaled = m_frame.scaled(r.size(), Qt::KeepAspectRatio, Qt::SmoothTransformation);
        const QPoint origin((width() - scaled.width()) / 2, (height() - scaled.height()) / 2);
        p.drawImage(origin, scaled);
        // aiming line (drag it up/down onto the barcode) + hint
        const int ly = origin.y() + static_cast<int>(scaled.height() * m_lineFrac);
        p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 60, 60, 220), 2));
        p.drawLine(origin.x() + 24, ly, origin.x() + scaled.width() - 24, ly);
        // soft glow trail behind the line
        for (int k = 1; k <= 10; ++k) {
            p.setPen(QPen(QColor(255, 60, 60, 24 - k * 2), 2));
            p.drawLine(origin.x() + 24, ly - k * 2, origin.x() + scaled.width() - 24, ly - k * 2);
        }
        p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
        p.setPen(Qt::white);
        // short hint on the live picture (elided so it never gets cut off)
        const QRect hint(origin.x() + 10, origin.y() + 8, scaled.width() - 20, 20);
        p.drawText(hint, Qt::AlignTop | Qt::AlignHCenter,
                   p.fontMetrics().elidedText("Hold the barcode on the red line", Qt::ElideRight, hint.width()));
        // "barcode read" confirmation
        if (m_resultClock.isValid() && m_resultClock.elapsed() < 3000) {
            const QColor c = m_resultOk ? QColor(34, 197, 94) : QColor(239, 68, 68);
            p.setBrush(Qt::NoBrush);
            p.setPen(QPen(c, 4));
            p.drawRect(QRect(origin, scaled.size()).adjusted(2, 2, -2, -2));
        }
        // the barcode that was just read: green box for ~2.5 s (no text)
        if (m_hitClock.isValid() && m_hitClock.elapsed() < 2500 && !m_hitText.isEmpty()) {
            const double k = static_cast<double>(scaled.width()) / m_frame.width();
            if (m_hitBox.isValid() && !m_hitBox.isEmpty()) {
                const QRectF box(origin.x() + m_hitBox.x() * k, origin.y() + m_hitBox.y() * k,
                                 m_hitBox.width() * k, m_hitBox.height() * k);
                p.setBrush(QColor(40, 200, 90, 45));
                p.setPen(QPen(QColor(40, 220, 100), 3));
                p.drawRoundedRect(box.adjusted(-6, -6, 6, 6), 6, 6);
            }
        }
    } else {
        p.setPen(QColor("#c2d0ef"));
#ifdef WITH_CAMERA
        p.drawText(r, Qt::AlignCenter, "Camera stopped — press Start camera");
#else
        p.drawText(r.adjusted(15, 0, -15, 0), Qt::AlignCenter | Qt::TextWordWrap,
                   "Camera unavailable in this build.\nInstall the Qt Multimedia component, then re-run CMake.\nTyped barcode lookup still works.");
#endif
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ RevenueChart
RevenueChart::RevenueChart(QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent) {}

void RevenueChart::setData(const QStringList &labels, const QVector<double> &actual, bool hasPaidOrders)
{
    m_labels = labels;
    m_actual = actual;
    m_hasPaidOrders = hasPaidOrders;
    QStringList values;
    for (qsizetype i = 0; i < qMin(labels.size(), actual.size()); ++i)
        if (actual[i] > 0)
            values << labels[i] + "  " + W::money(actual[i]);
    const QString summary = !hasPaidOrders ? QString("No paid orders in this period.")
                           : values.isEmpty() ? QString("Paid orders in this period total PKR 0.00.")
                                              : values.join('\n');
    setAccessibleDescription(summary);
    setToolTip(summary);
    update();
}

void RevenueChart::paintEvent(QPaintEvent *)
{
    const int count = m_actual.size();
    if (count == 0)
        return;

    QPainter p(this);
    p.setRenderHint(QPainter::Antialiasing);
    const bool dark = Theme::isDark();
    const QColor muted = dark ? Theme::darkMuted : Theme::onSurfaceVariant;
    const QColor grid = dark ? Theme::darkDivider : Theme::outlineVariant;
    const QLocale locale;
    QFont labels = font();
    labels.setPixelSize(11);
    p.setFont(labels);

    const double peak = *std::max_element(m_actual.cbegin(), m_actual.cend());
    const double tick = std::pow(10.0, std::floor(std::log10(qMax(0.01, peak) / 4.0)));
    const double maximum = peak > 0.0 ? 4.0 * tick * std::ceil(peak * 1.1 / (4.0 * tick)) : 4.0;
    const int precision = peak > 0.0 && tick < 1.0 ? 2 : 0;
    const QString maxLabel = locale.toString(maximum, 'f', precision);
    const int axisWidth = p.fontMetrics().horizontalAdvance(maxLabel) + 12;
    int labelWidth = 0;
    for (const auto &label : m_labels)
        labelWidth = qMax(labelWidth, p.fontMetrics().horizontalAdvance(label));
    const QRectF area = QRectF(rect()).adjusted(axisWidth, 12, -qMax(8, labelWidth / 2), -26);
    if (area.isEmpty())
        return;

    for (int step = 0; step <= 4; ++step)
    {
        const qreal y = area.bottom() - area.height() * step / 4.0;
        p.setPen(QPen(grid, 1, step == 0 ? Qt::SolidLine : Qt::DashLine));
        p.drawLine(QPointF(area.left(), y), QPointF(area.right(), y));
        p.setPen(muted);
        p.drawText(QRectF(0, y - 8, axisWidth - 8, 16), Qt::AlignRight | Qt::AlignVCenter,
                   locale.toString(maximum * step / 4.0, 'f', precision));
    }

    const qreal slot = area.width() / count;
    const qreal barWidth = qMin(32.0, slot * 0.62);
    const int labelStep = qMax(1, qCeil((labelWidth + 8) / slot));
    const QColor barColor = Theme::readable(Theme::primary);
    for (int i = 0; i < count; ++i)
    {
        const qreal x = area.left() + slot * i;
        if (m_actual[i] > 0)
        {
            const qreal barHeight = qMax(2.0, area.height() * m_actual[i] / maximum);
            const qreal y = area.bottom() - barHeight;
            p.setPen(Qt::NoPen);
            p.setBrush(barColor);
            p.drawRoundedRect(QRectF(x + (slot - barWidth) / 2, y, barWidth, barHeight), 4, 4);
        }
        if (i < m_labels.size() && i % labelStep == 0)
        {
            p.setPen(muted);
            p.drawText(QRectF(x + (slot - labelWidth) / 2, area.bottom() + 5, labelWidth, 16), Qt::AlignCenter, m_labels[i]);
        }
    }

    if (peak <= 0)
    {
        p.setPen(muted);
        p.drawText(area, Qt::AlignCenter, accessibleDescription());
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------ QtyStepper
QtyStepper::QtyStepper(int value, QWidget *parent) : QWidget(parent)
{
    auto *h = new QHBoxLayout(this);
    h->setContentsMargins(4, 2, 4, 2);
    h->setSpacing(6);
    auto *m = W::button("−", "step");
    auto *pl = W::button("+", "step");
    auto *v = W::label(QString::number(value), "quantityValue");
    v->setAlignment(Qt::AlignCenter);
    v->setMinimumWidth(22);
    h->addWidget(m);
    h->addWidget(v);
    h->addWidget(pl);
    connect(m, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QtyStepper::minus);
    connect(pl, &QPushButton::clicked, this, &QtyStepper::plus);
}

FILE: /Frontend/src/main.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
#include "MainWindow.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include <QApplication>
#include <QFont>
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include <QStyleFactory>

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    QApplication::setApplicationName("BarcodeHub");
    QApplication::setStyle(QStyleFactory::create("Fusion")); // identical look on every platform
    QFont f = app.font();
    f.setPointSizeF(9.5);
    app.setFont(f);
    app.setStyleSheet(Theme::styleSheet());

    // Sign in, run the POS, and return to sign-in after "Log out".
    while (true)
    {
        UserRole role = UserRole::Cashier;
        QString name;
        if (!Dialogs::login(nullptr, role, name))
            return 0; // sign-in window closed
        MainWindow w(role, name);
        w.show();
        app.exec();
        if (!w.loggedOut())
            return 0; // "Exit" closes the app
    }
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /Project Planning/Increment-1.md
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Increment 1 — Barcode Generator and Reader

---

## Overview

The first increment focuses on developing a working version of the C++ desktop application with barcode generation and reading capabilities.

The application must be able to generate a barcode from a six-digit number, display it on the screen, save it as an image, and read the generated barcode through either an uploaded image or a laptop camera.

---

## Requirements

### 1. Barcode Generation

The application must:

- Accept a six-digit numeric input from the user.
- Ensure that the first digit is non-zero.
- Generate a barcode corresponding to the provided number.
- Display the generated barcode on the application screen.
- Save the generated barcode to disk as an image.

### 2. Barcode Reading

The application must be able to read a barcode generated through the application.

**A. Image Upload**

- Allow the user to select and upload a barcode image.
- Read and decode the barcode from the selected image.
- Display the decoded six-digit number on the screen.

**B. Laptop Camera**

- Allow the user to scan a barcode using the laptop camera.
- Detect and decode the barcode from the camera feed.
- Display the decoded six-digit number on the screen.

---

## Technical Constraints

- Existing non-standard libraries must not be used for barcode generation.
- Libraries for image processing, file handling, and other supporting operations are permitted.
- The application must be developed in C++.
- All submissions must be made through GitHub.

### Farzam — UI Development & Barcode Reading

- UI Development (with Ali)
- Barcode Decoding
- Decoded Value Return Handling
- Invalid Input Handling

### Ali — UI Development & Barcode Generation

- UI Development (with Farzam)
- Barcode Reading
- Image Processing
- Input Handling
- Taking 6-Digit Number as Input

- Project Management
- Project Integration
- Error Handling
- CMake Build Configuration

- Barcode Reading (with Farzam)
- Live Scanner Implementation
- Camera Opening and Initialization
- Camera Frame Capturing
- Scanner Functionality

## Notes

- The generated barcode must represent the original six-digit input.
- Both image upload and laptop camera scanning are required reading methods.
- Further details about the products, files, and their formats will be provided after Increment 1.

# Increment 2 — Point of Sale (POS) System

## Overview

Increment 2 extends BarcodeHub from a barcode generation and reading application into a GUI-based Point of Sale (POS) system.

The system will allow cashiers to scan product barcodes, retrieve product information, manage orders, calculate bills, and record payment details.

## Objectives

- Implement a camera-based barcode reading workflow.
- Develop a GUI-based POS interface.
- Retrieve product specifications from `Product.txt`.
- Manage shopping carts and order records.
- Validate product quantities against available stock.
- Generate and save bills.
- Support different payment modes.
- Maintain modular, reusable, and maintainable code.

## Team Division & Responsibilities

### 1. Farzam Zeeshan — Product Management

**Responsibilities**
- Read and parse `Product.txt`.
- Create the `Product` class.
- Search products by barcode and product ID.
- Display product descriptions.
- Validate available product stock.

**Deliverable:** Product management module with `Product.txt` integration.

### 2. Shauzab Mukhtar — Camera & Scanner

**Responsibilities**
- Implement camera-based barcode scanning.
- Integrate the scanner with the POS workflow.
- Handle camera and barcode scanning errors.
- Reuse the barcode reading functionality developed in Increment 1.

**Deliverable:** Camera scanner module.

### 3. Muhammad Mustafa Amir — Order Management

**Responsibilities**
- Record orders and manage the shopping cart.
- Validate requested quantities against available stock.
- Generate unique 8-digit Order IDs.
- Save order records in `Order.txt`.
- Save product details and quantities in `OrderDetail.txt`.

**Deliverable:** Order management module.

### 4. Ali Abdullah — Billing & POS GUI

**Responsibilities**
- Design and implement the POS graphical interface.
- Generate bills and calculate bill totals.
- Add the Description button to the order-recording interface.
- Save generated bills in `Bill.txt`.
- Implement payment modes.

**Deliverable:** POS GUI and billing module.

## Integration Flow

The planned module integration sequence is:

1. **Product Management:** Retrieve product information and validate stock.
2. **Camera & Scanner:** Scan product barcodes and provide decoded barcode values to the POS workflow.
3. **Order Management:** Add products to the cart and manage order records.
4. **Billing:** Calculate totals, generate bills, and record payment details.

## Data File Specifications

### Product.txt

Each product occupies one line, ending with `#`. Fields are space-separated and must follow this order:

1. Product ID
2. Product barcode
3. Product acronym (maximum 8 characters)
4. Product name
5. Measurement unit (`count` or `weight`)
6. Product unit price
7. Product tax amount in PKR
8. Discount per unit
9. Available quantity
10. Barcode file name and path

### Order.txt

Each order occupies one line, ending with `#`. Fields are space-separated and follow this order:

1. 8-digit Order ID (last two digits indicate the order date)
2. Order date
3. Order time
4. Customer name
5. Customer contact (optional)
6. Order status (`in-progress`, `on-hold`, `paid`, `archived`)
7. Payment amount
8. Payment mode (`cash`, `card`, `wallet`)
9. Payment merchant name

### OrderDetail.txt

Each order detail record occupies one line and ends with `#`.

Fields:

1. Order ID
2. Product ID
3. Product barcode
4. Quantity

Each record associates a product and its quantity with an order.

### Bill.txt

Each bill occupies one line, ending with `#`. Fields are space-separated and follow this order:

1. 9-digit Bill ID
2. Customer name
3. Order ID
4. Product acronym
5. Product unit price without tax
6. Discount per unit (if applicable)
7. Product quantity
8. Total bill without tax
9. Total tax
10. Total discount
11. Total payable bill
12. Bill payment status (order status from `Order.txt`)

## Software Engineering Principles

The implementation should emphasize:

- **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP):** Each class and module should have one clear responsibility.
- **Open/Closed Principle (OCP):** Components should support extension without unnecessary modification to existing functionality.
- **Code Quality:** Keep code readable, consistent, and maintainable.
- **Reusability:** Reuse existing barcode functionality and design independent modules with clear interfaces.

# Increment 3 — Inventory, Discounts, Reporting & Advanced Billing

## Overview

Increment 3 adds product categories, inventory management, seasonal and special discounts, detailed billing, PDF bill printing, and management reports to the Point of Sale (PoS) system.

Managers can configure product categories, generate category-based barcodes, add and edit products, manage inventory, set discount seasons, and generate reports. Cashiers can record, hold, resume, and edit orders with a minimum number of clicks. Both cashiers and managers can print bills as PDF files.

## Objectives

- Introduce product categories and category-based barcode generation.
- Allow managers to add and edit products through the GUI.
- Allow managers to view and update inventory.
- Keep the available quantity of every product up to date with each order.
- Allow existing orders to be opened and edited under controlled rules.
- Store detailed bill information in `Bill.txt` and `BillDetail.txt`.
- Support seasonal discounts and special discounts.
- Print bills as PDF.
- Provide tabular management reports.
- Provide separate cashier and manager features in the user interface.
- Keep order recording fast, with a minimum number of clicks and button presses.

## Functional Requirements

### A. Product Categories & Barcode Generation

1. The manager shall be able to configure product categories through the GUI before generating barcodes.
2. A file `PCategory.txt` shall be maintained. Each line of the file, ending with `#`, is one product category.
3. The first two digits of a product barcode shall represent the product category (the Category ID).
4. A manager shall be able to generate a barcode for a product and add new products to `Product.txt`.
5. The Category ID shall be stored in `Product.txt` against each product.
6. A manager shall be able to add or edit any product.

### B. Orders & Inventory

7. An existing order can be opened and edited by a cashier.
8. An order with `paid` status can be edited within 7 days of the order payment, and only after approval by a manager. This is different from the handling of orders that are `on-hold`.
9. A manager can see the inventory of the products at any time, as a list of products with their available quantities.
10. A manager can update the inventory.
11. The available quantity of products shall be updated with every order.

### C. Billing, Discounts & Printing

12. A bill can be printed as a PDF by a cashier or a manager.
13. `Bill.txt` stores one bill per line (see Data File Specifications).
14. `BillDetail.txt` stores the bill lines showing the product information included in each bill.
15. The manager shall set seasons for special discounts by saving the start date, end date, and discount percentage of each season in `Seasons.txt`.
16. A manager can add new discounts through the user interface and set a discount active or inactive in `Seasons.txt`.
17. When a bill is generated, the system shall calculate the final amount based on the discount percentage if the current date (the date on which the bill is made) is within the dates mentioned in `Seasons.txt`. The bill shall mention this amount in the seasonal discount field.
18. A special discount, not more than 8 percent, can be applied to a bill.

### D. Reports

19. The manager shall be able to generate the reports listed in the Reports section, in tabular form.

### E. Usability & Roles

20. Recording an order shall need a minimum number of clicks and button presses. Extra clicks or button presses while scanning a product barcode are not desired.
21. Putting an order on hold shall be easy and shall need a minimum number of clicks and button presses.
22. An on-hold order shall be resumable with minimum user interaction.
23. The application shall provide features to the cashier and to the manager separately through the user interface.

## Roles & Access

| Feature | Cashier | Manager |
|---|:---:|:---:|
| Open and edit an existing order | Yes | |
| Approve the editing of a `paid` order | | Yes |
| Print a bill as PDF | Yes | Yes |
| Configure product categories | | Yes |
| Generate barcodes and add products | | Yes |
| Add or edit any product | | Yes |
| View and update inventory | | Yes |
| Set seasons and add or activate discounts | | Yes |
| Generate reports | | Yes |

## Reports

All reports are generated by the manager and shown in tabular form.

| # | Report | Content |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Incomplete orders | All orders that are not `paid` or `archived`, with the total amount of all such bills |
| 2 | Completed orders | All orders that have been completed, with the total amount of these bills |
| 3 | Cash sales | All cash sales for a selected period: daily, weekly, monthly, yearly, or a specified time period |
| 4 | All sales | All sales with the total amount and the discounts |
| 5 | Top ten most sold items | The ten most sold items in descending order, with their remaining stock |
| 6 | Top ten items by remaining stock | The ten items with the highest remaining quantity, in descending order, so that their purchase is not initiated |

## Data File Specifications

Each record occupies one line. Fields are space-separated.

### PCategory.txt

Each category occupies one line ending with `#`. Fields:

1. Category ID (2 digits; used when generating barcodes)
2. Category name
3. Category acronym

Example line:

```text
10 Beverages BEV #
```

### Product.txt

The Category ID of the product is stored in `Product.txt` against each product.

### Bill.txt

Each bill occupies one line. Fields, in this order:

1. Bill ID (9 digits, unique, cannot repeat in the file)
2. Customer name
3. Order ID (from `Order.txt`)
4. Total bill without tax
5. Total tax
6. Total applicable discount (the sum of the discounts on all bought products)
7. Total payable bill
8. Amount received
9. Balance returned
10. Bill payment status (the order status from `Order.txt`)
11. Seasonal discount
12. Special discount (not more than 8 percent)
13. Bill date
14. Bill time

### BillDetail.txt

Each record is one bill line showing a product included in a bill. Fields, in this order:

1. Bill ID (9 digits, from `Bill.txt`)
2. Product acronym
3. Product unit price without tax
4. Product quantity
5. Discount on the bought product (for example, if 10 products are bought with a discount of 5 rupees on each, this field stores 50)

### Seasons.txt

Each season occupies one line ending with `#`. Fields, in this order:

1. Start date
2. End date
3. Discount percentage
4. isActive (`true` or `false`)

## Business Rules

- The first two digits of every barcode are the Category ID of the product.
- A `paid` order can be edited only within 7 days of its payment and only after manager approval.
- The available quantity of a product is updated with every order.
- A Bill ID is a unique 9-digit number and never repeats in `Bill.txt`.
- The seasonal discount is applied when the bill date lies within the start and end dates of an active season in `Seasons.txt`.
- The special discount cannot exceed 8 percent.
- The discount stored in `BillDetail.txt` is the total discount for the product line (unit discount multiplied by quantity).

## Software Engineering Principles

The implementation should emphasize:

- **Single Responsibility Principle (SRP):** Each class and module should have one clear responsibility, for example category handling, inventory, discount calculation, and report generation as separate components.
- **Open/Closed Principle (OCP):** Components should support extension, such as new reports or new discount types, without unnecessary modification of existing functionality.
- **Code Quality:** Keep code readable, consistent, and maintainable.
- **Reusability:** Design independent modules with clear interfaces.
- **Testing:** Every module includes basic tests, and `CMakeLists.txt` is updated whenever new source files are added.
