namespace
{
    bool writeSampleData(const QString &dirPath)
    {
        QDir dir(dirPath);
        if (!dir.exists() && !dir.mkpath("."))
            return false;

        QFile products(dir.filePath("Product.txt"));
        if (!products.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate))
            return false;
        products.write(
            "1 01001020 OLPMILK Olpers Full Cream Milk 1L count 340.00 0.00 10.00 42 data/barcodes/01001020.png#\n"
            "7 01007350 EGGS12 Farm Fresh Eggs Dozen count 390.00 0.00 0.00 4 data/barcodes/01007350.png#\n"
            "8 01008418 NESTWATR Nestle Pure Life Water 1.5L count 110.00 8.00 0.00 150 data/barcodes/01008418.png#\n"
            "11 01011722 COLA15 Coca Cola 1.5L count 230.00 18.00 0.00 0 data/barcodes/01011722.png#\n");
        products.close();

        for (const char *emptyName : {"Order.txt", "OrderDetail.txt", "Bill.txt", "Seasons.txt"})
        {
            QFile f(dir.filePath(QString::fromLatin1(emptyName)));
            if (!f.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly | QIODevice::Truncate))
                return false;
            f.close();
        }
        return true;
    }
}

class ScannerChecklistTests : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT

private slots:
    void initTestCase();
    void groupedScanChecklistAndQuantitySteppers();
    void sendCheckedBatchToCollectiveCheckout();
    void stockClampingAndOutOfStockHandling();
    void mainWindowRoutesBatchToRecordOrder();
    void partialTransfersRemainAvailableForNextOrder();
    void scannerAudioForAcceptedScansOnly();
    void twoOrdersFromOneChecklist();

private:
    QTemporaryDir m_tempDataDir;
};

void ScannerChecklistTests::initTestCase()
{
    QVERIFY(m_tempDataDir.isValid());
    QVERIFY(writeSampleData(m_tempDataDir.path()));
    qputenv("BARCODEHUB_DATA", m_tempDataDir.path().toUtf8());

    auto &state = AppState::instance();
    QCOMPARE(state.products().size(), 4);
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::groupedScanChecklistAndQuantitySteppers()
{
    ScannerPage scanner;
    auto *feed = scanner.findChild<CameraFeedWidget *>();
    auto *manual = scanner.findChild<QLineEdit *>("scannerManualInput");
    auto *table = scanner.findChild<QTableWidget *>("scannerChecklist");
    auto *sendBtn = scanner.findChild<QPushButton *>("scannerAddChecked");
    QVERIFY(feed != nullptr);
    QVERIFY(manual != nullptr);
    QVERIFY(table != nullptr);
    QVERIFY(sendBtn != nullptr);
    QVERIFY(!sendBtn->isEnabled());

    // Scan Water 3 times (matching duplicate camera/manual scans) -> grouped into 1 row with qty 3
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01008418");
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01008418");
    manual->setText("01008418");
    QTest::keyClick(manual, Qt::Key_Return);
    QVERIFY(manual->text().isEmpty());

    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().size(), 1);
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().at(0).product.ean, QString("01008418"));
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().at(0).qty, 3);
    QVERIFY(scanner.entries().at(0).checked);
    QCOMPARE(table->rowCount(), 1);

    // Scan Eggs (1) and Milk (1) -> 3 grouped rows, 5 total units
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01007350");
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01001020");
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().size(), 3);
    QCOMPARE(table->rowCount(), 3);
    QCOMPARE(sendBtn->text(), QString("Add checked to checkout (5)"));
    QVERIFY(sendBtn->isEnabled());

    // Adjust Milk qty (row 0) from 1 -> 2 using the row's QtyStepper '+' button
    auto *stepper = qobject_cast<QtyStepper *>(table->cellWidget(0, 2));
    QVERIFY(stepper != nullptr);
    emit stepper->plus();
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().at(0).qty, 2);
    QCOMPARE(sendBtn->text(), QString("Add checked to checkout (6)"));

    // Uncheck row 1 (Eggs) -> only Milk (2) + Water (3) remain checked (5 units)
    auto *row1Cell = table->cellWidget(1, 0);
    QVERIFY(row1Cell != nullptr);
    auto *cb1 = row1Cell->findChild<QCheckBox *>();
    QVERIFY(cb1 != nullptr);
    cb1->setChecked(false);
    QCOMPARE(sendBtn->text(), QString("Add checked to checkout (5)"));

    // Select all restores Eggs -> 6 units
    scanner.selectAll();
    QCOMPARE(sendBtn->text(), QString("Add checked to checkout (6)"));

    // Deselect all -> disabled
    scanner.deselectAll();
    QVERIFY(!sendBtn->isEnabled());
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::sendCheckedBatchToCollectiveCheckout()
{
    ScannerPage scanner;
    RecordOrderPage recordOrder;
    AppState::instance().settings().autoAddScans = false; // collective batch must add regardless!

    connect(&scanner, &ScannerPage::sendBatchToOrder, &recordOrder,
            [&](const QVector<QPair<QString, int>> &items)
            { scanner.removeTransferredItems(recordOrder.addScannedBatch(items)); });

    const auto water = AppState::instance().findByCode("01008418");
    const auto eggs = AppState::instance().findByCode("01007350");
    const auto milk = AppState::instance().findByCode("01001020");
    QVERIFY(water && eggs && milk);

    scanner.recordScan(*water);
    scanner.recordScan(*water);
    scanner.recordScan(*water); // qty 3 @ (110 + 8) = 354
    scanner.recordScan(*eggs);  // qty 1 @ 390 = 390
    scanner.recordScan(*milk);  // qty 1 @ (340 - 10) = 330

    // Uncheck Eggs (row 1) so only Milk (1) and Water (3) are sent
    auto *table = scanner.findChild<QTableWidget *>("scannerChecklist");
    QVERIFY(table != nullptr);
    auto *eggsCb = table->cellWidget(1, 0)->findChild<QCheckBox *>();
    QVERIFY(eggsCb != nullptr);
    eggsCb->setChecked(false);

    QSignalSpy batchSpy(&scanner, &ScannerPage::sendBatchToOrder);
    scanner.sendCheckedToOrder();
    QCOMPARE(batchSpy.count(), 1);

    // Checked rows are cleared from staging; unchecked Eggs remains in checklist
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().size(), 1);
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().at(0).product.ean, QString("01007350"));
    QVERIFY(!scanner.entries().at(0).checked);

    // RecordOrderPage cart now contains Milk (1) + Water (3) = 4 units, collective net payable = 684.00
    auto *itemsBadge = recordOrder.findChild<QTableWidget *>();
    QVERIFY(itemsBadge != nullptr);
    QCOMPARE(itemsBadge->rowCount(), 2);

    auto *payable = recordOrder.findChild<QLabel *>("checkoutPayable");
    QVERIFY(payable != nullptr);
    QCOMPARE(payable->text(), QString("PKR 684.00"));
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::stockClampingAndOutOfStockHandling()
{
    ScannerPage scanner;
    const auto outOfStock = AppState::instance().findByCode("01011722");
    const auto eggs = AppState::instance().findByCode("01007350");
    QVERIFY(outOfStock && eggs);

    scanner.recordScan(*outOfStock);
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().size(), 1);
    QVERIFY(!scanner.entries().at(0).checked);

    auto *table = scanner.findChild<QTableWidget *>("scannerChecklist");
    QVERIFY(table != nullptr);
    auto *cb = table->cellWidget(0, 0)->findChild<QCheckBox *>();
    QVERIFY(cb != nullptr);
    QVERIFY(!cb->isEnabled());

    // Scanning Eggs 5 times clamps at stock 4
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i)
        scanner.recordScan(*eggs);
    QCOMPARE(scanner.entries().at(0).qty, 4);

    // If cart already holds 2 Eggs and batch requests 4, RecordOrderPage clamps addition to 2
    RecordOrderPage recordOrder;
    recordOrder.addScannedBatch({{"01007350", 2}});
    recordOrder.addScannedBatch({{"01007350", 4}});
    auto *payable = recordOrder.findChild<QLabel *>("checkoutPayable");
    QVERIFY(payable != nullptr);
    QCOMPARE(payable->text(), QString("PKR 1,560.00")); // 4 * 390
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::mainWindowRoutesBatchToRecordOrder()
{
    const bool originalDark = Theme::isDark();
    Theme::setDark(true);
    MainWindow win(UserRole::Cashier, "Cashier Test");
    auto *scanner = win.findChild<ScannerPage *>();
    QVERIFY(scanner != nullptr);

    const auto milk = AppState::instance().findByCode("01001020");
    const auto water = AppState::instance().findByCode("01008418");
    QVERIFY(milk && water);

    scanner->recordScan(*milk);
    scanner->recordScan(*water);
    scanner->sendCheckedToOrder();

    QCOMPARE(win.recordPage()->findChild<QLabel *>("checkoutPayable")->text(), QString("PKR 448.00"));
    Theme::setDark(originalDark);
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::partialTransfersRemainAvailableForNextOrder()
{
    MainWindow window(UserRole::Cashier, "Cashier Test");
    auto *scanner = window.findChild<ScannerPage *>();
    auto *order = window.recordPage();
    const auto eggs = AppState::instance().findByCode("01007350");
    QVERIFY(scanner && order && eggs);

    order->addScannedBatch({{eggs->ean, 3}});
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i)
        scanner->recordScan(*eggs);
    scanner->sendCheckedToOrder();
    QCOMPARE(scanner->entries().size(), 1);
    QCOMPARE(scanner->entries().first().qty, 3);
    QCOMPARE(order->findChild<QLabel *>("checkoutPayable")->text(), QString("PKR 1,560.00"));

    scanner->sendCheckedToOrder();
    QCOMPARE(scanner->entries().first().qty, 3);
    order->holdOrder();
    scanner->sendCheckedToOrder();
    QVERIFY(scanner->entries().isEmpty());
    QCOMPARE(order->findChild<QLabel *>("checkoutPayable")->text(), QString("PKR 1,170.00"));
    QVERIFY(!AppState::instance().heldOrders().isEmpty());
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::scannerAudioForAcceptedScansOnly()
{
    ScannerPage scanner;
    auto *feed = scanner.findChild<CameraFeedWidget *>();
    auto *manual = scanner.findChild<QLineEdit *>("scannerManualInput");
    auto &audio = ScanAudio::instance();
    auto &settings = AppState::instance().settings();
    const bool beep = settings.beepOnScan;
    QSignalSpy feedback(&audio, &ScanAudio::feedbackTriggered);
    settings.beepOnScan = true;
    audio.resetDebounce();
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("99999999");
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01011722");
    QCOMPARE(feedback.count(), 0);

    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01001020");
    QCOMPARE(feedback.count(), 1);
    audio.resetDebounce();
    manual->setText("01008418");
    QTest::keyClick(manual, Qt::Key_Return);
    QCOMPARE(feedback.count(), 2);

    settings.beepOnScan = false;
    audio.resetDebounce();
    emit feed->barcodeDetected("01008418");
    QCOMPARE(feedback.count(), 2);
    settings.beepOnScan = beep;
}

void ScannerChecklistTests::twoOrdersFromOneChecklist()
{
    auto &state = AppState::instance();
    const auto milk = state.findByCode("01001020");
    const auto water = state.findByCode("01008418");
    QVERIFY(milk && water);
    MainWindow window(UserRole::Cashier, "Cashier Test");
    auto *scanner = window.findChild<ScannerPage *>();
    auto *order = window.recordPage();
    auto *table = scanner->findChild<QTableWidget *>("scannerChecklist");
    scanner->recordScan(*milk);
    scanner->recordScan(*water);
    scanner->recordScan(*water);
    table->cellWidget(1, 0)->findChild<QCheckBox *>()->setChecked(false);
    scanner->sendCheckedToOrder();
    QCOMPARE(scanner->entries().size(), 1);
    QCOMPARE(scanner->entries().first().product.ean, milk->ean);

    auto settleCash = [order] {
        int ticks = 0;
        bool receipt = false;
        QTimer timer;
        QObject::connect(&timer, &QTimer::timeout, order, [&] {
            auto *dialog = qobject_cast<QDialog *>(QApplication::activeModalWidget());
            if (!dialog)
                return;
            if (dialog->windowTitle().startsWith("Bill - "))
            {
                receipt = true;
                dialog->accept();
                return;
            }
            for (auto *button : dialog->findChildren<QPushButton *>())
                if (button->isEnabled() && button->text().startsWith("Pay "))
                {
                    button->click();
                    return;
                }
            if (++ticks > 20)
                dialog->reject();
        });
        timer.start(50);
        order->checkout();
        return receipt;
    };

    const auto before = state.orders().size();
    QVERIFY(settleCash());
    QCOMPARE(state.orders().size(), before + 1);
    QString firstId, firstBill;
    for (const auto &saved : state.orders())
        if (saved.status == "Paid")
        {
            firstId = saved.id;
            firstBill = saved.billId;
            QCOMPARE(saved.itemCount(), 2);
        }
    QVERIFY(!firstId.isEmpty() && !firstBill.isEmpty());
    QCOMPARE(scanner->entries().size(), 1);
    scanner->selectAll();
    scanner->sendCheckedToOrder();
    QVERIFY(scanner->entries().isEmpty());
    QVERIFY(settleCash());
    state.reloadAll();
    QCOMPARE(state.orders().size(), before + 2);
    QVERIFY(!firstBill.isEmpty());
    bool secondOrderFound = false;
    for (const auto &saved : state.orders())
        if (saved.status == "Paid" && saved.id != firstId)
        {
            QVERIFY(!saved.billId.isEmpty() && saved.billId != firstBill);
            QCOMPARE(saved.itemCount(), 1);
            secondOrderFound = true;
        }
    QVERIFY(secondOrderFound);
    QCOMPARE(state.findByCode(milk->ean)->stock, milk->stock - 1);
    QCOMPARE(state.findByCode(water->ean)->stock, water->stock - 2);
}

    if (qEnvironmentVariableIsEmpty("QT_QPA_PLATFORM"))
        qputenv("QT_QPA_PLATFORM", "offscreen");
    QApplication app(argc, argv);
    ScannerChecklistTests tests;
    return QTest::qExec(&tests, argc, argv);
}

// Unit tests for the barcode decoder (pure C++, no camera): pictures are drawn in memory.
#include "decoder.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <string>

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(c) do { if (!(c)) { std::printf("FAILED line %d: %s\n", __LINE__, #c); ++failures; } } while (0)

static std::string modulesFor(const std::string &d)
{
    std::string m = "101";
    for (int i = 0; i < 4; ++i) m += Decoder::patterns[d[i] - '0'];
    m += "01010";
    for (int i = 4; i < 8; ++i)
        for (const char *p = Decoder::patterns[d[i] - '0']; *p; ++p) m += (*p == '1') ? '0' : '1';
    return m + "101";
}

// Draw the barcode `scale` pixels per module, with quiet zones, inside a larger picture.
static GrayImage draw(const std::string &digits, int scale, int margin, bool upsideDown = false, bool sideways = false, int noise = 0)
{
    std::string m = modulesFor(digits);
    if (upsideDown) m.assign(m.rbegin(), m.rend());
    const int w = static_cast<int>(m.size()) * scale + 2 * margin, h = 120;
    GrayImage g;
    g.width = sideways ? h : w;
    g.height = sideways ? w : h;
    g.pixels.assign(static_cast<std::size_t>(g.width) * g.height, 235);
    std::srand(7);
    for (int x = 0; x < w; ++x)
    {
        const int mi = (x - margin) / scale;
        const bool dark = x >= margin && mi >= 0 && mi < static_cast<int>(m.size()) && m[static_cast<std::size_t>(mi)] == '1';
        for (int y = 20; y < 100; ++y)
        {
            int v = dark ? 20 : 235;
            if (noise) v += std::rand() % (2 * noise + 1) - noise;
            v = v < 0 ? 0 : (v > 255 ? 255 : v);
            const int px = sideways ? y : x, py = sideways ? x : y;
            g.pixels[static_cast<std::size_t>(py) * g.width + px] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(v);
        }
    }
    return g;
}

int main()
{
    Decoder d;
    std::string out;
    CHECK(Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit("40001029"));
    CHECK(!Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit("40001028"));

    for (const char *code : {"40001029", "40002149", "40003511", "96385074", "00000000"})
    {
        if (!Decoder::hasValidCheckDigit(code)) continue;
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 3, 20), out) && out == code);
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 2, 30), out) && out == code);
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 5, 10), out) && out == code);
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 3, 20, true), out) && out == code);        // upside down
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 3, 20, false, true), out) && out == code); // sideways
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(draw(code, 4, 20, false, false, 18), out) && out == code); // camera noise
    }
    // barcode only in the lower part of the picture: found when the aiming line is moved onto it
    {
        GrayImage g = draw("40001029", 3, 20);
        for (int y = 0; y < 60; ++y)                       // blank out the top half of the bars
            for (int x = 0; x < g.width; ++x) g.pixels[static_cast<std::size_t>(y) * g.width + x] = 235;
        CHECK(d.decodeCropping(g, out, 0.8) && out == "40001029");
    }
    // wrong check digit: not accepted, but reported
    CHECK(!d.decodeCropping(draw("40001028", 3, 20), out));
    CHECK(d.isBadCheck());
    // blank picture
    GrayImage blank; blank.width = 200; blank.height = 100; blank.pixels.assign(20000, 200);
    CHECK(!d.decodeCropping(blank, out));
    CHECK(!d.decodeCropping(GrayImage{}, out));

    if (failures) { std::printf("%d scanner test(s) FAILED\n", failures); return 1; }
    std::printf("scanner tests OK\n");
    return 0;
}


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
FILE: /tests/season_test.cpp
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tests for Seasons.txt reading/writing (SeasonRepository).
#include "billing/SeasonRepository.h"

#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <iostream>

using barcodehub::billing::SeasonRecord;
using barcodehub::billing::SeasonRepository;

static int failures = 0;
#define CHECK(cond)                                                       \
    do                                                                    \
    {                                                                     \
        if (!(cond))                                                      \
        {                                                                 \
            std::cerr << "FAILED line " << __LINE__ << ": " #cond "\n";   \
            ++failures;                                                   \
        }                                                                 \
    } while (0)

int main()
{
    // encode: exact Seasons.txt line
    CHECK(SeasonRepository::encode({"2026-11-20", "2026-11-30", 10, true}) == "2026-11-20 2026-11-30 10.00 true#");

    // decode: a good line
    SeasonRecord s;
    CHECK(SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-01 2026-12-31 12.5 false#", s));
    CHECK(s.startDate == "2026-12-01" && s.endDate == "2026-12-31" && s.percent == 12.5 && !s.active);

    // decode: bad lines are rejected
    std::string err;
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-01 2026-12-31 10 true", s, &err));      // no '#'
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-31 2026-12-01 10 true#", s, &err));     // end before start
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-01 2026-12-31 0 true#", s, &err));      // 0 percent
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-01 2026-12-31 120 true#", s, &err));    // over 100
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-01 2026-12-31 10 yes#", s, &err));      // not true/false
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("01-12-2026 2026-12-31 10 true#", s, &err));     // wrong date format
    CHECK(!SeasonRepository::decode("2026-12-01 2026-12-31 10 true extra#", s, &err)); // too many fields

    // save + load round trip; a missing file loads as empty
    const std::string path = "season_test_Seasons.txt";
    std::remove(path.c_str());
    SeasonRepository repo(path);
    CHECK(repo.load().empty());
    CHECK(repo.save({{"2026-11-20", "2026-11-30", 10, true}, {"2027-03-20", "2027-03-27", 15, false}}));
    std::vector<std::string> errors;
    auto loaded = repo.load(&errors);
    CHECK(loaded.size() == 2 && errors.empty());
    CHECK(loaded.size() == 2 && loaded[1].percent == 15 && !loaded[1].active);

    // a bad line in the file is skipped and reported
    {
        std::ofstream f(path, std::ios::app);
        f << "garbage line#\n";
    }
    errors.clear();
    CHECK(repo.load(&errors).size() == 2 && errors.size() == 1);
    std::remove(path.c_str());

    if (failures == 0)
        std::cout << "season_test: all checks passed\n";
    return failures == 0 ? 0 : 1;
}

#include "AppState.h"
#include "DarkModeManager.h"
#include "DashboardPage.h"
#include "Dialogs.h"
#include "MainWindow.h"
#include "SettingsPage.h"
#include "Theme.h"
#include "Widgets.h"
#include "billing/BillRecordCodec.h"
#include <QCheckBox>
#include <QComboBox>
#include <QDialog>
#include <QDir>
#include <QFileDialog>
#include <QLineEdit>

#include <QSignalSpy>
#include <QSpinBox>
#include <QTabWidget>

namespace
{
    QPushButton *button(QWidget &parent, const QString &text)
    {
        for (auto *control : parent.findChildren<QPushButton *>())
            if (control->text() == text)
                return control;
        return nullptr;
    }

    template<typename T>
    T *setting(QWidget &page, const QString &name)
    {
        for (auto *control : page.findChildren<T *>())
            if (control->accessibleName() == name)
                return control;
        return nullptr;
    }

    bool writeFile(const QDir &dir, const QString &name, const QByteArray &contents)
    {
        QFile file(dir.filePath(name));
        return file.open(QIODevice::WriteOnly) && file.write(contents) == contents.size();
    }
}

class TerminalUiTests : public QObject
{
    Q_OBJECT
private slots:
    void initTestCase();
    void revenueUsesPaymentTimeForBothRoles();
    void preferencesReachCheckoutAndReceipt();
private:
    QTemporaryDir m_fixture;
};

void TerminalUiTests::initTestCase()
{
    QVERIFY(m_fixture.isValid());
    // The app uses explicit organization/application names and the default settings format.
    QSettings::setDefaultFormat(QSettings::IniFormat);
    QSettings::setPath(QSettings::IniFormat, QSettings::UserScope, m_fixture.filePath("preferences"));
    QSettings::setPath(QSettings::IniFormat, QSettings::SystemScope, m_fixture.filePath("system"));
    qputenv("BARCODEHUB_DATA", m_fixture.path().toUtf8());
    const QDir dir(m_fixture.path());
    QVERIFY(writeFile(dir, "Product.txt",
                      "1 01001020 OLPMILK Olpers Milk 1L count 340.00 0.00 10.00 42 data/barcodes/01001020.png#\n"));
    QVERIFY(writeFile(dir, "Seasons.txt", {}));
    const auto today = QDate::currentDate();
    QByteArray orders, details, bills, billDetails;
    auto add = [&](int index, QDate paid, const char *time, const char *status, int amount)
    {
        const auto orderId = QByteArray::number(26110100 + index);
        const auto billId = QByteArray::number(261101000 + index);
        const auto created = paid.addDays(-2).toString(Qt::ISODate).toUtf8();
        orders += orderId + " " + created + " 12:00:00 QA - " + status + " " + QByteArray::number(amount) + " cash - #\n";
        details += orderId + " 1 01001020 1 #\n";
        barcodehub::billing::Bill bill;
        bill.id = billId.toStdString();
        bill.orderId = orderId.toStdString();
        bill.customerName = "QA";
        bill.paymentStatus = status;
        bill.date = paid.toString(Qt::ISODate).toStdString();
        bill.time = time;
        bill.amountReceived = amount;
        bill.totals.withoutTax = bill.totals.payable = amount;
        bills += QByteArray::fromStdString(barcodehub::billing::BillRecordCodec::format(bill)) + '\n';
        billDetails += QByteArray::fromStdString(barcodehub::billing::BillRecordCodec::formatDetail(
            bill.id, {"OLPMILK", double(amount), 0, 0, 1})) + '\n';
    };
    add(1, today, "00:05:00", "paid", 330);
    add(2, today, "23:59:59", "paid", 650);
    add(3, today.addDays(-3), "10:00:00", "paid", 510);
    add(4, today, "11:10:00", "archived", 2000);
    add(5, today.addDays(-7), "18:30:00", "paid", 1500);
    QVERIFY(writeFile(dir, "Order.txt", orders));
    QVERIFY(writeFile(dir, "OrderDetail.txt", details));
    QVERIFY(writeFile(dir, "Bill.txt", bills));
    QVERIFY(writeFile(dir, "BillDetail.txt", billDetails));
    qApp->setStyleSheet(Theme::styleSheet());
}

void TerminalUiTests::revenueUsesPaymentTimeForBothRoles()
{
    for (const auto role : {UserRole::Manager, UserRole::Cashier})
    {
        MainWindow window(role, "Dashboard QA");
        window.resize(1100, 700);
        window.show();
        QVERIFY(window.goTo(MainWindow::Dashboard));
        auto *chart = window.findChild<RevenueChart *>("dashboardRevenue");
        QVERIFY(chart);
        QVERIFY(chart->isVisible());
        QCOMPARE(chart->accessibleName(), "Hourly revenue");
        const auto hourly = chart->accessibleDescription();
        QVERIFY(hourly.contains("00:00  PKR 330.00"));
        QVERIFY(hourly.contains("23:00  PKR 650.00"));
        QVERIFY(!hourly.contains("2,000") && !hourly.contains("510"));
        auto *week = button(window, "Last 7 days");
        QVERIFY(week);
        week->click();
        QCOMPARE(chart->accessibleName(), "Daily revenue");
        const auto daily = chart->accessibleDescription();
        QVERIFY(daily.contains("PKR 980.00") && daily.contains("PKR 510.00"));
        QVERIFY(!daily.contains("1,500") && !daily.contains("2,000"));
        if (qEnvironmentVariableIsSet("SYNTRO_QA_SCREENSHOTS"))
        {
            for (const bool dark : {false, true})
            {
                barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance().setDarkMode(dark);
                for (const QSize size : {QSize(1100, 700), QSize(1440, 900)})
                {
                    window.resize(size);
                    QApplication::processEvents();
                    const auto path = QDir(qEnvironmentVariable("SYNTRO_QA_SCREENSHOTS")).filePath(
                        QString("%1-%2-%3-dashboard.png").arg(RolePolicy::name(role)).arg(size.width())
                            .arg(dark ? "dark" : "light"));
                    QVERIFY(window.grab().save(path));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Empty and fully discounted periods still provide meaningful chart feedback.
    RevenueChart empty;
    empty.setData({"00:00"}, {0.0}, false);
    QVERIFY(empty.accessibleDescription().contains("No paid orders"));
    empty.setData({"00:00"}, {0.0}, true);
    QVERIFY(empty.accessibleDescription().contains("PKR 0.00"));

    DashboardPage live;
    auto *chart = live.findChild<RevenueChart *>("dashboardRevenue");
    QVERIFY(chart);
    const auto beforeSale = chart->accessibleDescription();
    OrderRecord sale;
    sale.customer = "Dashboard test sale";
    sale.payment = "Cash";
    sale.tendered = 500;
    sale.lines.push_back({"Milk", "OLPMILK", 1, 340, 0, 10, 1});
    QString error;
    QVERIFY2(AppState::instance().addOrder(sale, &error), qPrintable(error));
    QVERIFY(chart->accessibleDescription() != beforeSale);
    auto *week = button(live, "Last 7 days");
    QVERIFY(week);
    week->click();
    QVERIFY(chart->accessibleDescription().contains("PKR 1,310.00"));
}

void TerminalUiTests::preferencesReachCheckoutAndReceipt()
{
    MainWindow window(UserRole::Manager, "Settings QA");
    window.resize(1100, 700);
    window.show();
    QVERIFY(window.goTo(MainWindow::Settings));
    auto *page = window.findChild<SettingsPage *>();
    auto *tabs = page->findChild<QTabWidget *>();
    QCOMPARE(tabs->count(), 6);
    auto *payment = setting<QComboBox>(*page, "Default payment method");
    auto *autoAdd = setting<QCheckBox>(*page, "Add scans directly to the cart");
    auto *beep = setting<QCheckBox>(*page, "Beep on successful scan");
    auto *low = setting<QSpinBox>(*page, "Low-stock limit");
    auto *theme = setting<QComboBox>(*page, "Color theme");
    auto *paper = setting<QComboBox>(*page, "PDF paper size");
    auto *folder = setting<QLineEdit>(*page, "Receipt export folder");
    QVERIFY(payment && autoAdd && beep && low && theme && paper && folder);
    auto *discard = button(*page, "Discard changes");
    auto *apply = button(*page, "Apply");
    QVERIFY(discard && apply);
    QVERIFY(!apply->isEnabled());

    payment->setCurrentText("Card");
    QVERIFY(apply->isEnabled());
    discard->click();
    QCOMPARE(payment->currentText(), "Cash");

    payment->setCurrentText("Wallet");
    autoAdd->setChecked(true);
    beep->setChecked(false);
    low->setValue(45);
    theme->setCurrentText("Dark mode");
    paper->setCurrentText("A4");
    folder->setText(m_fixture.filePath("does-not-exist"));
    apply->click();
    QCOMPARE(tabs->tabText(tabs->currentIndex()), "Receipts");
    QCOMPARE(AppState::instance().settings().lowStockThreshold, 10);

    folder->setText(m_fixture.path());
    apply->click();
    QVERIFY(!apply->isEnabled());
    const auto saved = Settings::load();
    QCOMPARE(saved.lowStockThreshold, 45);
    QCOMPARE(AppState::instance().lowStock().size(), 1);
    QVERIFY(saved.autoAddScans && !saved.beepOnScan && saved.receiptA4);
    QCOMPARE(saved.receiptFolder, m_fixture.path());
    QVERIFY(saved.defaultPayment == barcodehub::order::PaymentMode::Wallet);
    QVERIFY(Theme::isDark());
    auto *topTheme = window.findChild<QPushButton *>("darkModeToggle");
    QVERIFY(topTheme);
    topTheme->click();
    QCOMPARE(theme->currentText(), "Light mode");

    Dialogs::PaymentResult result;
    Dialogs::PaymentRequest request;
    request.lines.push_back({"Milk", "OLPMILK", 1, 340, 0, 10, 1});
    bool correctWallet = false;
    QTimer::singleShot(0, &window, [&]
    {
        auto *dialog = qobject_cast<QDialog *>(QApplication::activeModalWidget());
        if (!dialog)
            return;
        auto *wallet = button(*dialog, "Wallet");
        correctWallet = wallet && wallet->isChecked();
        dialog->accept();
    });
    QVERIFY(Dialogs::payment(&window, request, result));
    QVERIFY(correctWallet);
    QCOMPARE(result.mode, "Wallet");
    QCOMPARE(result.merchant, "JazzCash");

    const QString pdf = m_fixture.filePath("test-receipt.pdf");
    QVERIFY(Dialogs::saveReceiptPdf(AppState::instance().orders().first(), pdf));
    QFile receipt(pdf);
    QVERIFY(receipt.open(QIODevice::ReadOnly));
    const auto contents = receipt.readAll();
    QVERIFY(contents.startsWith("%PDF"));
    QVERIFY(contents.contains("/MediaBox [0 0 595"));

    const bool nonNativeDialogs = QCoreApplication::testAttribute(Qt::AA_DontUseNativeDialogs);
    QCoreApplication::setAttribute(Qt::AA_DontUseNativeDialogs);
    bool usedSavedFolder = false;
    QTimer::singleShot(0, &window, [&]
    {
        auto *dialog = qobject_cast<QDialog *>(QApplication::activeModalWidget());
        auto *exportButton = dialog ? button(*dialog, "Save as PDF") : nullptr;
        if (!exportButton)
        {
            if (dialog)
                dialog->reject();
            return;
        }
        QTimer::singleShot(0, dialog, [&]
        {
            auto *file = qobject_cast<QFileDialog *>(QApplication::activeModalWidget());
            if (!file)
                return;
            usedSavedFolder = file->directory() == QDir(m_fixture.path());
            file->reject();
        });
        exportButton->click();
        dialog->reject();
    });
    Dialogs::receipt(&window, AppState::instance().orders().first());
    QCoreApplication::setAttribute(Qt::AA_DontUseNativeDialogs, nonNativeDialogs);
    QVERIFY(usedSavedFolder);
    QSignalSpy preview(page, &SettingsPage::toast);
    auto *sound = button(*page, "Play test sound");
    QVERIFY(sound);
    sound->click();
    QCOMPARE(preview.size(), 1);

    if (qEnvironmentVariableIsSet("SYNTRO_QA_SCREENSHOTS"))
        for (const bool dark : {false, true})
        {
            barcodehub::ui::DarkModeManager::instance().setDarkMode(dark);
            for (int i = 0; i < tabs->count(); ++i)
            {
                tabs->setCurrentIndex(i);
                QApplication::processEvents();
                const auto path = QDir(qEnvironmentVariable("SYNTRO_QA_SCREENSHOTS")).filePath(
                    QString("Settings-%1-%2.png").arg(tabs->tabText(i), dark ? "dark" : "light"));
                QVERIFY(window.grab().save(path));
            }
        }

    SettingsPage cashier(nullptr, UserRole::Cashier);
    auto *cashierTabs = cashier.findChild<QTabWidget *>();
    QCOMPARE(cashierTabs->count(), 5);
    QCOMPARE(setting<QComboBox>(cashier, "Default payment method")->currentText(), "Wallet");
}

QTEST_MAIN(TerminalUiTests)
#include "terminal_ui_tests.moc"

# Changelog

All notable changes to **BarcodeHub** are documented in this file.

## [3.0.0] — Increment 3: Inventory, Discounts, Reporting & Advanced Billing

### Planned

- Add product category management through the GUI, stored in `PCategory.txt`.
- Use the first two digits of every barcode as the product category.
- Store the category ID of each product in `Product.txt`.
- Allow managers to generate barcodes and to add or edit products.
- Add an inventory view and inventory updates for managers.
- Update available product quantities with every order.
- Allow existing orders to be opened and edited; paid orders only within 7 days of payment and after manager approval.
- Reduce the clicks needed to record an order, put an order on hold, and resume an on-hold order.
- Extend `Bill.txt` with amount received, balance returned, seasonal discount, special discount, bill date, and bill time.
- Introduce `BillDetail.txt` to store the product lines of each bill.
- Introduce `Seasons.txt` for seasonal discounts, and allow managers to add discounts and set them active or inactive.
- Apply the seasonal discount automatically when the bill date falls within an active season.
- Support special discounts of up to 8 percent.
- Print bills as PDF for cashiers and managers.
- Add management reports: incomplete orders, completed orders, cash sales, all sales, top ten most sold items, and top ten items by remaining stock.
- Provide separate cashier and manager features in the user interface.

### Fixed — editing orders (requirements 3 and 10)

- Editing a paid order now changes **that** order: it keeps its Order ID and Bill ID (and its payment date, so the
  7-day window does not restart). `OrderDetail.txt`, `Bill.txt` and `BillDetail.txt` are rewritten and stock moves by
  the difference only. Previously a new order was created and the original was archived.
- While a paid order is edited, the units it already took count as available, so a low-stock line can be kept or
  restored.
- On-hold orders are real orders now: saved in `Order.txt` with status `on-hold`, listed on the Orders page
  ("On Hold" tab, one-click Resume), kept after a restart, and paid under the same Order ID. The two hard-coded demo
  held orders were removed.
- Every payment or edit backs up the five record files first and puts them all back if any step fails.
- The 7-day edit window counts from the bill (payment) date and time.
- Removed the stray duplicate `Frontend/src/ui/RecordOrderPage .cpp`, which the build compiled alongside
  `RecordOrderPage.cpp`.
- New backend calls: `OrderManager::saveCartToOrder`, `BillRepository::replace`, `BillingService::rebill`;
  `discardOrder` also accepts on-hold orders. Covered by `tests/order_edit_test.cpp`.

### Changed — Discounts page follows requirements 13-15

- The Discounts page asks only for what `Seasons.txt` stores: start date, end date, discount percentage and isActive.
  The season name field and column were removed (the requirements have no name, and the file cannot keep one); a
  season is shown by its dates, e.g. "23 Nov - 30 Nov 2026".
- Two active seasons may not share a day, so a bill date always matches at most one seasonal discount (requirement 15).
  An overlapping season can still be saved as inactive.
- A season with isActive = false shows as INACTIVE (it used to show as SCHEDULED) and has its own filter tab.
- The seasonal discount applies to the whole bill; an unused per-category filter was removed (amounts are unchanged).
- Layout: the form scrolls instead of squashing its labels onto the inputs; table columns no longer clip.

### Changed — manager approval is a request the manager accepts (requirement 3)

- A cashier no longer types a manager PIN on the cashier screen. On Orders, "Request edit" on a paid order sends
  the request with a reason; the button shows "Waiting" until a manager decides.
- The manager sees "Waiting for your approval" on Orders (and a count on the Orders menu item, plus a notice at
  sign-in) and clicks Approve or Reject. After approval the cashier's button becomes "Open".
- One approval allows one edit: it is used up when the edit is saved or cancelled. A rejected request can be sent again.
- Requests are saved in `data/EditRequests.txt` (`orderId status requestedAt cashier reason decidedAt manager#`),
  so they survive a restart and are seen by every terminal using the same data folder.
- A manager editing an order still opens it directly; his approval is recorded the same way.
- Removed the demo manager ID/PIN check.

### Development Notes

- Increment 3 builds on the Point of Sale system from Increment 2.
- Requirements and open design decisions are documented in `Project-Planning/Increment-3.md`.
- Increment 3 is currently in the planning stage. None of the items listed above are implemented yet.

## [2.0.0] — Increment 2: Point of Sale System

### Planned

- Introduce a GUI-based Point of Sale (POS) workflow.
- Implement product management using `Product.txt`.
- Add product lookup by barcode and product ID.
- Display product specifications and validate available stock.
- Integrate camera-based barcode scanning with the POS.
- Implement cart and order management.
- Generate unique 8-digit Order IDs.
- Store order records in `Order.txt`.
- Store product details and quantities in `OrderDetail.txt`.
- Implement bill generation and payment calculations.
- Save generated bills in `Bill.txt`.
- Support payment modes: cash, card, and wallet.

### Development Notes

- Increment 2 extends the existing barcode generator and reader from Increment 1.
- The system is being developed using modular components to promote separation of responsibilities, reusability, and maintainability.
- Increment 2 is currently in development.

## [1.0.0] — Increment 1: Initial Project Setup

### Added

- Established the initial project structure and directory organization.
- Configured CMake as the project build system.
- Implemented the Qt Widgets-based desktop application foundation.
- Created the initial application window and navigation.
- Implemented barcode generation for six-digit numeric input.
- Implemented barcode reading functionality.
- Added the Decode Barcode interface with camera and image-upload sections.
- Introduced the initial Scan Logs interface.
- Organized backend modules for barcode generation, reading, and scanning.
- Added project documentation and contribution guidelines.

### Notes

- Established the foundational barcode generation and reading functionality.
- Increment 1 serves as the base for the POS capabilities introduced in Increment 2.

FILE: /CMakeLists.txt

cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.21)

# Windows + Ninja: Qt Creator sometimes hands CMake Visual Studio's cl.exe together with a MinGW
# Qt kit, which cannot compile. Unless an MSVC Qt is being used, switch to Qt's own MinGW g++.
if(WIN32 AND NOT CMAKE_GENERATOR MATCHES "Visual Studio"
   AND (NOT CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER OR CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER MATCHES "[cC][lL]\\.exe$")
   AND NOT "${CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH}${Qt6_DIR}${QT_DIR}" MATCHES "[mM][sS][vV][cC]")
    include("${CMAKE_CURRENT_LIST_DIR}/cmake/toolchain-qt-mingw.cmake")
endif()

project(BarcodePOS LANGUAGES CXX)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 17)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

if(MSVC)
    # Qt 6 needs a correct __cplusplus value; also read sources as UTF-8.
    add_compile_options(/Zc:__cplusplus /utf-8 /permissive-)
endif()

option(BACKEND_ONLY "Build backend and tests without Qt" OFF)

# Camera support uses Qt Multimedia (shipped with Qt; no OpenCV needed):
#   AUTO (default) - camera if the Qt Multimedia component is installed, otherwise built without it
#   ON             - camera required: configuring fails if Qt Multimedia is missing
#   OFF            - no camera (typed barcode lookup only)
set(CAMERA_MODE AUTO CACHE STRING "Camera: AUTO, ON, OFF")
set_property(CACHE CAMERA_MODE PROPERTY STRINGS AUTO ON OFF)
# Which library grabs the pictures (the barcode decoder itself is always the same pure C++ code):
#   AUTO   - OpenCV if a build matching the compiler is found, otherwise Qt Multimedia
#   QT     - Qt Multimedia only
#   OPENCV - OpenCV only (set OpenCV_DIR; it must be built for the SAME compiler as Qt:
#            the official OpenCV Windows download is MSVC-only -> use it with the MSVC kit)
set(CAMERA_BACKEND AUTO CACHE STRING "Camera library: AUTO, QT, OPENCV")
set_property(CACHE CAMERA_BACKEND PROPERTY STRINGS AUTO QT OPENCV)

# ============================================================
# BACKEND LIBRARIES
# ============================================================

# Product management
add_library(product_core STATIC
    Backend/src/product/ProductModel.cpp
    Backend/src/product/ProductStorage.cpp
    Backend/src/product/ProductValidation.cpp
    Backend/src/product/CategoryStorage.cpp
)

target_include_directories(product_core PUBLIC
    Backend/include
    Backend/include/product
)

# Order management
add_library(order_core STATIC
    Backend/src/order/Order.cpp
    Backend/src/order/OrderDetail.cpp
    Backend/src/order/OrderManager.cpp
)

target_include_directories(order_core PUBLIC
    Backend/include
)

# Common utilities
add_library(common_core STATIC

# ============================================================
# QT CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

set(QT_INSTALL_ROOT "C:/Qt"
    CACHE PATH "Qt installation root"
)

if(WIN32 AND NOT CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH AND NOT Qt6_DIR)

    file(GLOB _qt_roots
        "${QT_INSTALL_ROOT}/6.*/mingw_64"
    )

    if(_qt_roots)
        list(SORT _qt_roots
            COMPARE NATURAL
            ORDER DESCENDING
        )

        list(GET _qt_roots 0 _qt_root)
        list(PREPEND CMAKE_PREFIX_PATH "${_qt_root}")
    endif()

endif()

set(CMAKE_AUTOMOC ON)
set(CMAKE_AUTOUIC ON)
set(CMAKE_AUTORCC ON)

find_package(QT NAMES Qt6 Qt5
    REQUIRED COMPONENTS Widgets
)

find_package(Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}
    REQUIRED COMPONENTS Widgets
)

# A MinGW build of Qt cannot be compiled with MSVC (this is what "Qt requires a C++17 compiler ...
# /Zc:__cplusplus" means: the kit picked cl.exe). Stop early with the fix instead of 100 errors.
if(MSVC AND "${Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}_DIR}" MATCHES "mingw")
    message(FATAL_ERROR
        "Qt is a MinGW build (${Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}_DIR}) but the compiler is MSVC (${CMAKE_CXX_COMPILER}).\n"
        "Fix: Qt Creator > Projects > Build & Run > use the kit 'Desktop Qt 6.x.x MinGW 64-bit' "
        "(its compiler must be 'MinGW ... 64-bit', not 'Microsoft Visual C++'), delete the build folder, "
        "and configure again. Or use the 'Desktop Qt 6.x.x MSVC2022 64-bit' kit instead.")
endif()

set(CAMERA_ENABLED OFF)
set(CAMERA_LIB "")
if(NOT CAMERA_MODE STREQUAL "OFF")
    if(NOT CAMERA_BACKEND STREQUAL "QT")
        # MinGW: first look for an OpenCV config built for MinGW (x64/mingw/lib), then for loose
        # headers + libopencv_*.dll files. (The official Windows download is MSVC-only.)
        if(MINGW)
            file(GLOB_RECURSE _mingw_cfg "$ENV{USERPROFILE}/Downloads/opencv/*mingw*/OpenCVConfig.cmake"
                                         "${OpenCV_DIR}/*mingw*/OpenCVConfig.cmake" "C:/opencv/*mingw*/OpenCVConfig.cmake")
            if(_mingw_cfg AND NOT OpenCV_DIR)
                list(GET _mingw_cfg 0 _first_cfg)
                get_filename_component(_cfg_dir "${_first_cfg}" DIRECTORY)
                set(OpenCV_DIR "${_cfg_dir}" CACHE PATH "OpenCV (MinGW)" FORCE)
            endif()
        endif()
        find_package(OpenCV 4 QUIET COMPONENTS core imgproc videoio)
        if(OpenCV_FOUND)
            set(CAMERA_ENABLED ON)
            set(CAMERA_LIB OPENCV)
            message(STATUS "Camera: OpenCV ${OpenCV_VERSION} (${OpenCV_DIR})")
        elseif(MINGW)
            list(APPEND CMAKE_MODULE_PATH "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake")
            include(FindMinGWOpenCV)
            if(MINGW_OPENCV_FOUND)
                set(OpenCV_INCLUDE_DIRS "${MINGW_OPENCV_INCLUDE}")
                set(OpenCV_LIBS ${MINGW_OPENCV_LIBS})
                set(OPENCV_DLL_DIR "${MINGW_OPENCV_DLL_DIR}")
                set(CAMERA_ENABLED ON)
                set(CAMERA_LIB OPENCV)
                message(STATUS "Camera: OpenCV (MinGW files found without a CMake config)")
            endif()
        endif()
    endif()
    if(NOT CAMERA_ENABLED AND NOT CAMERA_BACKEND STREQUAL "OPENCV" AND QT_VERSION_MAJOR EQUAL 6)
        find_package(Qt6 QUIET COMPONENTS Multimedia)
        if(TARGET Qt6::Multimedia)
            set(CAMERA_ENABLED ON)
            set(CAMERA_LIB QT)
            message(STATUS "Camera: Qt Multimedia ${Qt6Multimedia_VERSION}")
        endif()
    endif()
    if(NOT CAMERA_ENABLED)
        set(_why "No usable camera library: install Qt Multimedia (Qt Maintenance Tool > Add or remove components > "
                 "Additional Libraries > Qt Multimedia), or give OpenCV_DIR an OpenCV built for THIS compiler "
                 "(the official OpenCV Windows download only works with the MSVC kit, not MinGW).")
        if(CAMERA_MODE STREQUAL "ON")
            message(FATAL_ERROR ${_why})
        else()
            message(STATUS "Camera disabled. ${_why}")
        endif()
    endif()
endif()

# Optional scan feedback. On Windows use asynchronous file-based WinMM playback.
set(SCAN_AUDIO_BACKEND "NONE")
if(WIN32)
    set(SCAN_AUDIO_BACKEND "WINMM")
elseif(QT_VERSION_MAJOR EQUAL 6)
    if(NOT TARGET Qt6::Multimedia)
        find_package(Qt6 QUIET COMPONENTS Multimedia)
    endif()
    if(TARGET Qt6::Multimedia)
        set(SCAN_AUDIO_BACKEND "QT_MULTIMEDIA")
    endif()
endif()
message(STATUS "Scan audio: ${SCAN_AUDIO_BACKEND}")

# ============================================================
# FRONTEND
# ============================================================

# Every page / dialog lives in its own file under Frontend/src/ui. New files are picked up automatically
# (re-run CMake). main.cpp, CameraWidget.cpp and POSWindow.cpp there are legacy stand-alone files.
file(GLOB UI_SOURCES CONFIGURE_DEPENDS
    Frontend/src/ui/*.cpp
)

# Legacy stand-alone files are skipped. [Mm]ain also matches the old Main.cpp test window,
# which includes CameraWidget.h (needs OpenCV) and has its own main().
list(FILTER UI_SOURCES EXCLUDE REGEX
    "/([Mm]ain|CameraWidget|POSWindow)\\.cpp$"
)

file(GLOB UI_HEADERS CONFIGURE_DEPENDS
    Frontend/include/ui/*.h
)

list(FILTER UI_HEADERS EXCLUDE REGEX
    "CameraWidget.h|POSWindow.h"
)

add_library(barcode_ui STATIC
    ${UI_SOURCES}
    ${UI_HEADERS}
    Frontend/resources/scan_audio.qrc
)

target_include_directories(barcode_ui PUBLIC
    Frontend/include/ui
    Frontend/include
    Backend/include
    Backend/include/product
)

target_link_libraries(barcode_ui PUBLIC
    Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::Widgets
    backend_core
)

target_compile_definitions(barcode_ui PRIVATE
    BARCODEHUB_SOURCE_DIR="${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}"
)

target_link_libraries(barcode_ui PUBLIC scanner_core)
if(CAMERA_ENABLED)
    target_compile_definitions(barcode_ui PUBLIC WITH_CAMERA)
    if(CAMERA_LIB STREQUAL "QT")
        target_link_libraries(barcode_ui PUBLIC Qt6::Multimedia)
        target_compile_definitions(barcode_ui PUBLIC WITH_CAMERA_QT)
    else()
        target_include_directories(barcode_ui PUBLIC ${OpenCV_INCLUDE_DIRS})
        target_link_libraries(barcode_ui PUBLIC ${OpenCV_LIBS})
        target_compile_definitions(barcode_ui PUBLIC WITH_CAMERA_OPENCV)
    endif()
endif()

if(SCAN_AUDIO_BACKEND STREQUAL "QT_MULTIMEDIA")
    if(NOT (CAMERA_ENABLED AND CAMERA_LIB STREQUAL "QT"))
        target_link_libraries(barcode_ui PUBLIC Qt6::Multimedia)
    endif()
    target_compile_definitions(barcode_ui PUBLIC WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_QT)
elseif(SCAN_AUDIO_BACKEND STREQUAL "WINMM")
    target_link_libraries(barcode_ui PRIVATE winmm)
    target_compile_definitions(barcode_ui PUBLIC WITH_SCAN_AUDIO_WINMM)
endif()

# ============================================================
# MAIN EXECUTABLE
# ============================================================

add_executable(BarcodeHub WIN32
    Frontend/src/main.cpp
)

target_link_libraries(BarcodeHub PRIVATE
    barcode_ui
)

# OpenCV camera build on Windows: copy the OpenCV runtime DLLs next to BarcodeHub.exe.
if(WIN32 AND CAMERA_LIB STREQUAL "OPENCV")
    file(GLOB _opencv_dlls
        "${OpenCV_DIR}/x64/vc16/bin/*.dll" "${OpenCV_DIR}/x64/vc17/bin/*.dll"
        "${OpenCV_DIR}/../bin/*.dll" "${OpenCV_DIR}/../../bin/*.dll"
        "${OPENCV_DLL_DIR}/*.dll" "${OPENCV_DLL_DIR}/../bin/*.dll")
    if(_opencv_dlls)
        add_custom_command(TARGET BarcodeHub POST_BUILD
            COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E copy_if_different ${_opencv_dlls} "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:BarcodeHub>"
            VERBATIM COMMENT "Copying OpenCV runtime DLLs")
    else()
        message(WARNING "No OpenCV DLLs found near ${OpenCV_DIR}; add the OpenCV bin folder to PATH to run the exe.")
    endif()
endif()

# Preserve existing records during rebuilding.
# Only copy starter data files when they do not already exist.

add_custom_command(TARGET BarcodeHub POST_BUILD
    COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND}
        "-DSOURCE_DATA=${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/data"
        "-DDEST_DATA=$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:BarcodeHub>/data"
        -P "${CMAKE_SOURCE_DIR}/cmake/seed-data.cmake"
    VERBATIM
)

# ============================================================
# WINDOWS DEPLOYMENT (OPTIONAL)
# ============================================================

# Deployment is not part of the normal build. Qt Creator sets up the
# Qt/MinGW environment when launching an application using its Qt kit.
# To copy Qt DLLs and plugins for standalone distribution, build the
# BarcodeHub_deploy target explicitly instead.

if(WIN32)
    # Prefer the deployment tool belonging to the Qt version used by this kit.
    if(TARGET Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::windeployqt)
        get_target_property(WINDEPLOYQT_EXECUTABLE
            Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}::windeployqt IMPORTED_LOCATION)
    endif()

    if(NOT WINDEPLOYQT_EXECUTABLE)
        find_program(WINDEPLOYQT_EXECUTABLE
            NAMES windeployqt
            HINTS
                "${Qt${QT_VERSION_MAJOR}_DIR}/../../../bin"
                "${QT_INSTALL_ROOT}/6.11.0/mingw_64/bin"
        )
    endif()

    if(WINDEPLOYQT_EXECUTABLE)
        set(_deploy_apps BarcodeHub)
        if(TARGET CameraDemo)
            list(APPEND _deploy_apps CameraDemo)
        endif()

        foreach(app IN LISTS _deploy_apps)
            add_custom_target(${app}_deploy
                COMMAND "${WINDEPLOYQT_EXECUTABLE}"
                    --no-translations
                    "$<TARGET_FILE:${app}>"
                DEPENDS ${app}
                VERBATIM
                COMMENT "Deploying Qt runtime for ${app}"
            )
        endforeach()
    else()
        message(STATUS
            "windeployqt not found; optional deployment targets disabled")
    endif()
endif()

if(BUILD_TESTING AND QT_VERSION_MAJOR EQUAL 6)
    find_package(Qt6 QUIET COMPONENTS Test)
    if(TARGET Qt6::Test)
        add_executable(scan_audio_tests
            tests/scan_audio_tests.cpp
        )
        target_link_libraries(scan_audio_tests PRIVATE
            barcode_ui
            Qt6::Test
        )
        add_test(NAME scan_audio COMMAND scan_audio_tests)
        add_executable(scanner_checklist_tests
            tests/scanner_checklist_tests.cpp
        )
        target_link_libraries(scanner_checklist_tests PRIVATE
            barcode_ui
            Qt6::Test
        )
        add_test(NAME scanner_checklist COMMAND scanner_checklist_tests)
        add_executable(terminal_ui_tests tests/terminal_ui_tests.cpp)
        target_link_libraries(terminal_ui_tests PRIVATE barcode_ui Qt6::Test)
        add_test(NAME terminal_ui COMMAND terminal_ui_tests)
        if(WIN32)
            get_target_property(_qt_core_loc Qt6::Core IMPORTED_LOCATION_DEBUG)
            if(NOT _qt_core_loc)
                get_target_property(_qt_core_loc Qt6::Core IMPORTED_LOCATION_RELEASE)
            endif()
            if(NOT _qt_core_loc)
                get_target_property(_qt_core_loc Qt6::Core IMPORTED_LOCATION)
            endif()
            if(_qt_core_loc)
                get_filename_component(_qt_bin_dir "${_qt_core_loc}" DIRECTORY)
                get_filename_component(_qt_root_dir "${_qt_bin_dir}" DIRECTORY)
                if(CMAKE_VERSION VERSION_GREATER_EQUAL "3.22")
                    set_tests_properties(scan_audio scanner_checklist terminal_ui PROPERTIES
                        ENVIRONMENT_MODIFICATION "PATH=path_list_prepend:${_qt_bin_dir};QT_QPA_PLATFORM=set:offscreen;QT_PLUGIN_PATH=set:${_qt_root_dir}/plugins"
                    )
                else()
                    set_tests_properties(scan_audio scanner_checklist terminal_ui PROPERTIES
                        ENVIRONMENT "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen;QT_PLUGIN_PATH=${_qt_root_dir}/plugins"
                    )
                endif()
            endif()
        else()
            set_tests_properties(scan_audio scanner_checklist terminal_ui PROPERTIES
                ENVIRONMENT "QT_QPA_PLATFORM=offscreen"
            )
        endif()
    endif()
endif()

# Ali team integration handoff

## Git checkpoint

- Original `ali` HEAD `9cb1661640af80bc7725ee6ce43360cf046c93d4`
- Shared ancestor `b70926d5735814810c928ead692753673ac80aa1`
- Incoming approved tip `cc4bc028ffedb6786bc4748cbbfce150db2d79e9`
- Safety ref `backup/ali-before-team-integration-20261125-2122`
- Tracked checkout snapshot `backup/ali-checkout-wip-20261125-2122` at `b9c60ddb4d1fc06307f9cce43a92f76ca40a424b`
- Integration branch `integration/ali-team-sync-20261125-2122`. Resolve its merge commit with `git rev-parse integration/ali-team-sync-20261125-2122`.

The original worktree was on `ali`. The two checkout source files were modified, the index was empty, and `Barcode-POS-System.txt` was untracked. No Git operation was in progress. The tracked edits still match their snapshot and the untracked file's checksum is unchanged. Ali's verified UI commits `66355f3`, `b87f898`, and `9cb1661` remain reachable.

## Integration matrix

| Source | Finding | Result |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `ali` | Three UI commits after the shared ancestor | Preserved as the first-parent history |
| `origin/main` | 17 commits beyond local `ali`, including the approved billing and order changes | Integrated in one reviewed, non-fast-forward merge |
| `origin/ali` | Advanced from `b70926d` to the same tip as `origin/main` | Covered by the main merge |
| `origin/mustafa`, `origin/farzam`, `origin/shauzab` | Each points to the same tip as `origin/main` | No branch-only work or duplicate merges |

The incoming range includes paid-order editing and approval (`79be213`), season persistence and management (`187fcbb`), and split bill records and billing work (`f1d6931`, `69c4a46`). The approved range also brings its updated tests, CMake targets, documentation, and Windows build script. External build dependencies remain the existing Qt MinGW toolchain.

## Conflict resolutions

| Files | Resolution |
| --- | --- |
| `Theme.h`, `Theme.cpp`, `Widgets.cpp` | Preserved Ali's semantic palette, painter-drawn cards and chart, and shared controls. Removed the incoming global color conversion and kept a theme-aware warning style for the order edit banner. |
| `MainWindow.cpp` | Preserved the shell, page permissions, and shortcuts. The Orders page now uses the common resume route for persisted order IDs. |
| `RecordOrderPage.cpp` | Retained the committed layout and adapted it to held IDs, paid-edit approval, stock credit, and the separate seasonal total. Fixed clipping in the populated checkout summary. |
| `DiscountsPage.cpp` | Kept the compact themed form and scrolling table with persisted dates, percent, activation, and the Inactive filter. Season labels derive from the dates because the approved file format has no campaign name. |
| `ReportsPage.cpp` | Retained the split date controls, status badges, theme, and minimum-size scrolling. Integrated the expanded report periods and sales ledger and fixed overlapping cards on tab changes. |

Other integration adjustments include using the payment timestamp for paid sales when available and changing the default card terminal label to meet the backend merchant rule. The tracked data fixtures are identical to Ali's baseline.

## Verification

- Baseline `qt-mingw` configure and `qt-mingw-debug` build passed. CTest passed 6/6.
- Final configure and build passed. CTest passed 8/8, including the added `order_edit` and `season` tests. The Windows executable opened and closed its sign-in dialog.
- Windows Qt tests exercised Cashier and Manager sign-in, permitted navigation and shortcuts, theme persistence, typed and decoded-signal barcode lookup, the scan auto-add setting, cart quantities and stock limits, and persisted hold/resume.
- On copied records, Cash, Card, and Wallet checkout saved bills with the expected tax and discounts. A manager-approved edit preserved both IDs and adjusted stock by the quantity difference. Two legacy bills remained readable after the first split-format write. Receipt dialogs and PDF files were verified.
- Product creation, editing, lookup, category-aware barcode routing, inventory reload, season persistence, and overlap validation were exercised through the application APIs on an isolated catalog.
- Running-window screenshots were inspected at 1100×700 and 1440×900 in light and dark mode. They covered the shell, populated checkout, dashboard, reports, payment, and account menu. Captures from the final run are in `C:/Users/iAT/AppData/Local/Temp/syntro-team-final-1795624610116`.

## Promotion and remaining checks

The completed Task 03 commit `2e1faca` was reconciled on the integration branch before promoting the combined history to `ali`. The committed dashboard refresh has no separate Task 04 acceptance record.

Physical barcode capture, external card or wallet devices, and printer hardware were outside the verified workflows. Payment tests covered the application's local settlement paths. Existing category edits are session-scoped. New writes migrate copied legacy bill lines into `Bill.txt` and `BillDetail.txt`. Keep a full data-directory backup before the first production write in the new format.

## Checkout reconciliation

- Merged the completed Task 03 commit into the reviewed candidate at `30a0692`. Resolved conflicts in `RecordOrderPage.cpp` and `UI_REDESIGN_PROGRESS.md`. Preserved the fixed checkout summary, barcode focus and on-demand camera alongside persisted holds, edit approvals, stock credit and bill integration. The discounts total includes the seasonal discount, with the breakdown in its tooltip.
- Qt MinGW configure and Debug build passed. CTest passed 8/8. Windows checks on copied records verified both role sign-ins, shell navigation, shortcuts, typed and decoded-signal barcode entry, hold/reload/resume, Cash/Card/Wallet payments, paid edit ID and stock behavior, and receipt PDFs. The combined checkout was visually inspected in both themes at 1100×700 and 1440×900.
- Captures are in `C:/Users/iAT/AppData/Local/Temp/syntro-combined-1795630764231`. Optical capture and external payment/printing hardware remain unverified.
- Next Task 05 checkpoint is Orders Management.

## Management screens · Task 05

Local `ali` includes `origin/main` at `09d24ce`. The initial sync `56eef61` kept the three CMake files required by the Qt MinGW preset while accepting the upstream documentation removals. A subsequent merge `51fc643` brought in the upstream project metadata and Windows wrapper script removal.

- Orders Management `0fae373` preserved search, status filters, hold/resume, bill viewing and paid-order approval.
- Product Catalog `acc7e59` kept all real catalog fields and role-specific actions, with readable product names at minimum width.
- Inventory Management `4adf35a` kept quantity edits staged through filtering, with explicit Save and Discard.
- Product Categories kept the department index, two-digit prefix validation, linked-product protection and the existing session-scoped CRUD. Fixed the displayed range endpoints. Use `git log -1 --format=%H --grep='^feat(ui): redesign product categories screen$'` to resolve this checkpoint.

Build and CTest passed at each screen checkpoint. Windows screenshots are under `C:/Users/iAT/AppData/Local/Temp/syntro-task05-1795632134317` in both themes at 1100×700 and 1440×900. Wide tables use horizontal scrolling at the minimum width. Testing used copied records. See `UI_REDESIGN_PROGRESS.md` for each verified workflow. Next increment is Barcode Scanner, Bills, Discounts, and Settings.

## Scanner checklist and audio integration

The active checkout is `Barcode-POS-System` on `ali`. Integrated the scanner audio and grouped checklist changes from `6ebdb23` and `bb9002d` together with the unfinished Windows playback fix. Windows plays the embedded WAV asynchronously through WinMM. Barcode Scanner and Record Order use the same beep setting. Unknown barcodes, unavailable stock, and checkout transfers produce no additional beep.

The checklist keeps unchecked rows and any quantities the cart could not accept. It uses the existing pricing quote for the selected total. Verified grouping, quantity controls, stock limits, partial and rejected transfers, hold/resume, and two separate paid orders from the same checklist, with distinct order and bill IDs and correct stock after reload.

Qt MinGW Debug built successfully. CTest passed 10/10. Both Qt suites also passed with the native Windows platform plugin. Windows accepted the audio playback request. Speaker audibility and optical barcode scanning still need an in-person check. Tests used temporary data files.

## Operational screens · Task 06

- Scanner workspace `1751a44` preserves the grouped checklist, beep, manual lookup, product details, and view-only behavior. Camera status follows the existing feed and shows errors when starting fails.
- Bills ledger `6b68ece` keeps paid records and the existing PDF receipt flow. Date and time show the payment timestamp with the order time as the legacy fallback.
- Seasonal Discounts `b392e5a` keeps stored date ranges, percentage and activation. The form previews the derived season label. The file format has no custom campaign name.
- Terminal Settings groups the existing threshold, auto-add and beep preferences, with Apply and the top-bar theme control preserved. Find this commit with `git log -1 --format=%H --grep='^feat(ui): modernize terminal settings$'`.

All four pages were built and run on Windows in light and dark modes at 1100×700 and 1440×900. The default executable was open, so builds used the existing `build/qt-mingw-task05` directory with the Qt MinGW preset. The baseline and all four checkpoints passed CTest 10/10. The four sets of captures are in `C:/Users/iAT/AppData/Local/Temp/syntro-inc6-1795641158885`. Wide tables can scroll at 1100px. Physical optical capture and speaker audibility remain unverified.

Next increment is Manager Reports.

## Manager Reports · Task 07

`ali` started at `0c00ddf`. The report checkpoint is `feat(ui): finalize six manager reports and analytics`. Resolve its hash with `git log -1 --format=%H --grep='^feat(ui): finalize six manager reports and analytics$'`. Scope is `ReportsPage.h/.cpp`, report-specific selectors in `Theme.cpp`, and the two handoff logs.

Archived rows are excluded from paid totals. The report reads saved bill quantities for decimal precision and ranks available stock from the current catalogue.
- Both baseline and final Qt MinGW Debug passed, with 10/10 CTest checks each. The default executable was open. Use `cmake --preset qt-mingw -B build/qt-mingw-task05` and `cmake --build build/qt-mingw-task05 --config Debug` to rebuild the updated executable in the existing alternate directory.
- The native Windows fixture run covered Manager and Cashier permissions, all six reports, date presets and inclusive ranges, outstanding and archived orders, payment mix, sales reconciliation, fractional quantities, stock rankings, and unchanged source data. CSV and landscape PDF exports passed for all reports and an empty report. Ctrl+P was exercised.
- Running-window captures and exports are under `C:/Users/iAT/AppData/Local/Temp/syntro-inc7-1795642747357/2cd94bff-d9be-4c7e-a2b9-66e13b52dbf3`. Both themes were checked at 1100×700 and 1440×900 against the references. Wide sales tables use horizontal scrolling at minimum width.

FILE: /README.md

<h1 align="center">🏷️ BarcodeHub</h1>

<p align="center">
  <b>A C++17-based Point of Sale system with barcode scanning, product management, order processing, and billing.</b>
  <br />
  Developed as an academic project for Introduction to Software Engineering at FAST-NUCES.
</p>

<p align="center">
  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/C%2B%2B-17-00599C?style=for-the-badge&logo=cplusplus&logoColor=white" />
  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Qt-6-41CD52?style=for-the-badge&logo=qt&logoColor=white" />
  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/OpenCV-5C3EE8?style=for-the-badge&logo=opencv&logoColor=white" />
  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/CMake-Build%20System-064F8C?style=for-the-badge&logo=cmake&logoColor=white" />
  <img src="https://img.shields.io/badge/Platform-Windows%20x64-0078D4?style=for-the-badge&logo=windows&logoColor=white" />
</p>

---

## 📌 Project Overview

**BarcodeHub** is a C++ desktop application being developed as a barcode-based Point of Sale (POS) system.

The project extends its initial barcode generation and reading capabilities into a GUI-based retail workflow, allowing cashiers to scan products, retrieve product information, manage orders, and generate bills. Later increments add category-based barcodes, inventory management, discounts, and management reports.

Developed incrementally, BarcodeHub emphasizes modularity, code quality, and reusability.

---

## ✨ Key Features

### v1.0.0

- 🏷️ Barcode generation for supported six-digit numeric product codes.
- 📖 Barcode reading functionality.
- 🖥️ Qt Widgets-based desktop application foundation.

### v2.0.0

- 📷 Camera-based barcode scanning.
- 📦 Product lookup and description from `Product.txt`.
- 🛒 Cart and order management.
- 📊 Product stock validation.
- 🧾 Automated bill generation.
- 💳 Cash, card, and wallet payment modes.
- 📁 File-based product, order, and billing records.
- 🎨 GUI-based Point of Sale workflow.

### v3.0.0

- 🗂️ Product category management, with the first two barcode digits representing the category.
- ➕ Manager tools to generate barcodes and to add or edit products.
- 📦 Inventory view and inventory updates, with stock updated on every order.
- ✏️ Opening and editing existing orders (paid orders only within 7 days of payment and after manager approval).
- ⚡ Fast order recording, with easy on-hold and resume.
- 🧾 Extended bills with bill details, amount received, and balance returned.
- 🌦️ Seasonal discounts from `Seasons.txt`, plus special discounts of up to 8 percent.
- 🖨️ Bill printing as PDF.
- 📈 Management reports: incomplete orders, completed orders, cash sales, all sales, top ten most sold items, and top ten items by remaining stock.
- 👥 Separate cashier and manager features.

---

## 🔄 POS Workflow

The POS workflow consists of four main modules:

1. **Product Management** — Retrieve product information and validate stock.
2. **Camera & Scanner** — Scan product barcodes using a connected camera.
3. **Order Management** — Record orders and manage product quantities.
4. **Billing** — Calculate totals, generate bills, and record payment information.

---

## 📄 Data Management

BarcodeHub uses text files to store its records:

| File | Purpose | Increment |
|---|---|---|
| `Product.txt` | Product specifications and available stock | 2 (category ID added in 3) |
| `Order.txt` | Customer and order information | 2 |
| `OrderDetail.txt` | Products and quantities associated with orders | 2 |
| `Bill.txt` | Generated bill information and payment status | 2 (extended in 3) |
| `PCategory.txt` | Product categories (ID, name, acronym) | 3 (planned) |
| `BillDetail.txt` | Product lines belonging to each bill | 3 (planned) |
| `Seasons.txt` | Seasonal discount periods and their status | 3 (planned) |

Each record follows the specified field order and uses `#` as its end-of-record marker.

---

## 🛠️ Technology Stack

| Technology | Purpose |
|---|---|
| **C++17** | Core application logic |
| **Qt 6 Widgets** | Desktop graphical interface |
| **OpenCV** | Camera capture and image processing |
| **CMake** | Build configuration |
| **MinGW-w64** | Windows C++ compilation |
| **PowerShell** | Build automation |
| **Git & GitHub** | Version control and collaboration |
| **GitHub Actions** | Automated branch synchronization |
